(vi-ri) payam - vaḷamumām. viṭuvāy - piḷappu. nalam peṟūum naṟkālam itu eṉa māṟuka. veñciṉa vēntaṉ viṉiviṭap peṟiṉ eṉṟamiyāl, vēntaṉ veṟṟi yeytiyum ciṉa mikutiyāṟ cantiṟku oruppaṭātuḷaṉ eṉpatu pōtarum.
(mē-ḷ) `ēṉōr maruṅkiṉum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, (ic ceyyuḷ) piritaṟ pakutiyākiya pācaṟip pulampal eṉiṉum nilam paṟṟi mulliyāyiṟṟu eṉṟurittaṉar, iḷam.
165. pāli
(makaṭpōkkiya tāyatu nilimi kaṇṭār colliyatu.)
		kayantali maṭappiṭi payampiṟ paṭṭeṉak
		kaḷiṟuviḷip paṭutta kampali verīi
		oyyeṉa eḻunta cevvāyk kuḻavi
		tāteru maṟukiṉ mūtūr āṅkaṇ
	5.	erumi nalāṉ peṟumuli māntum
		nāṭupala iṟanta naṉṉa rāṭṭikku
		āyamum aṇiyiḻantu aḻuṅkiṉṟu tāyum
		1iṉtōḷ tārāy iṟīiyareṉ uyireṉak
		kaṇṇum nutalum nīvit taṇṇeṉat
	10.	taṭavunili nocci variniḻal aciit
		tāḻik kuvaḷi vāṭumalar 2cūṭṭit
		tarumaṇaṟ kiṭanta pāviyeṉ
		3arumaka ḷēyeṉa muyaṅkiṉaḷ aḻumē.

(co-ḷ) 1-6. kayantali maṭap piṭi payampiṟ paṭṭeṉa. melliya taliyiyuṭiya iḷiya peṇyāṉi kuḻiyil akap paṭṭatāka, kaḷiṟu viḷippaṭutta kampali verīi - kaḷiṟṟuyāṉi kūppīṭu ceyta āravārattiṟku añci, oyyeṉa eḻunta cevvāyk kuḻavi - virinteḻunta civanta vāyiṉatāya ataṉ kaṉṟu, tātu eru maṟukiṉ mūtūr āṅkaṇ - tātākiya eruviṉiyuṭiya teruk kaḷiyuṭiya paḻiya ūrāya avviṭattu, erumi nal āṉ peṟu muli māntum - erumiyāya nalla māṭṭiṉiṉṟum peṟukiṉṟa mulippāli uṇṇum iṭamāya, nāṭu pala iṟanta naṉṉa rāṭṭikku - pala nāṭukaḷik kaṭantu ceṉṟa naṉmiyi yuṭiyāḷ poruṭṭu;
7. āyamum aṇi iḻantu aḻuṅkiṉṟu - āyamum poli viḻantu varuntukiṉṟatu;
7-13. tāyum - tāyum, iṟīiyar eṉ uyir eṉa - eṉ uyir keṭu vatāka eṉṟu nontu kūṟi, tarumaṇal kiṭanta pāvi - koṇarntu parappiya maṇalil kiṭanta makaḷatu pāviyi eṭuttu, kaṇṇum nutalum nīvi - ataṉ kaṇṇiyum neṟṟiyiyum taṭavi, taṭavu nili nocci vari niḻal taṇ eṉa acii - vaḷinta niliyiṉiyuṭiya nocciyiṉ varivariyākavuḷḷa niḻalil taṭpamuṟak kiṭatti, tāḻik kuvaḷi vāṭumalar cūṭṭi - tāḻikkaṇṇē yuḷḷa kuvaḷiyiṉ vāṭiya malaric cūṭṭi, eṉ arumakaḷē - eṉ ariya makaḷē, iṉ tōḷ tārāy eṉa muyaṅkiṉaḷ aḻum - niṉatu iṉiya tōḷit taruvāyāka eṉṟu kūṟit taḻuvi aḻāniṟpaḷ.
(muṭipu) nāṭu - pala iṟanta naṉṉarāṭṭikku āyamum aḻuṅkiṉṟu; tāyum, iṟīiyar eṉ uyir eṉak kūṟi, pāviyik kaṇṇum nutalum nīvi, niḻal acii, malar cūṭṭi, eṉ arumakaḷē iṉtōḷ tārāy eṉa muyaṅkiṉaḷ aḻum
(vi-ri) kaṟpukkaṭaṉ pūṇṭu kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟāḷākaliṉ- `naṉṉarāṭṭi' eṉṟār. āyamum aṇiyiḻan taḻuṅkiṉṟu eṉṟata ṉāl tāyatu nilimiyōṭu āyattiṉ nilimiyiyum kaṇṭār colliṉar eṉṟu koḷka. āyamum tāyum eṉṉum ummikaḷ eccapporuḷaṉa. īṉṟōṭṭārāy eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, tāy pāviyi nōkki, niṉṉi īṉṟavaḷākiya eṉ makaḷik kāṭṭu vāyāka eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷeṉak koḷḷal vēṇṭum. taṇṇeṉa muyaṅkiṉaḷ eṉṟa iyittalumām. talivi yiruntu nīr vārkkap peṟāmiyāl, tāḻik kuvaḷiyiṉ malar vāṭuvatāyiṟṟu.
(mē-ḷ) `taṉṉum avaṉum avaḷum cuṭṭi'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu (icceyyuḷ) `tāy niliyum āyattu niliyum kaṇṭōr kūṟiyavāṟuṇarka' eṉṟum `pālkeḻu kiḷavi nālvarkku murittē'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `tarumaṇaṟ kiṭanta pāviyeṉ, arumakaḷēyeṉa muyaṅkiṉaḷ aḻumē' `itu naṟṟāy maṇaṟ pāviyip peṇ pālākak kūṟit taḻīik koṇṭaḻutaliṟ pālkeḻu kiḷaviyāyiṟṟu' eṉṟum, kūṟiṉar nac.
166. marutam
(parattiyōṭu puṉal āṭiya talimakaṉ talimakaḷiṭip pukku yāṉ 
āṭiṟṟilaṉ eṉṟu cūḷuṟṟāṉ eṉpatu kēṭṭa 3paratti taṉ 
pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpac colliyatu.)
		nalmaraṅ kuḻīiya naṉimutir cāṭi
		palnāḷ aritta kōoy uṭippiṉ
		mayaṅkumaḻit tuvaliyiṉ maṟukuṭaṉ paṉikkum
		paḻampal nelliṉ vēḷūr vāyil
	5.	naṟuviri teḷitta nāṟiṇar mālip
		poṟivari iṉavaṇṭu ūtala kaḻiyum
		uyarpali peṟūum urukeḻu teyvam
		puṉiyiruṅ katuppiṉ 4nīkaṭut tōḷvayiṉ
		aṉiyēṉ āyiṉ aṇaṅkuka eṉṉeṉa
	10.	maṉiyōḷ tēṟṟum makiḻna ṉāyiṉ
		yārkol vāḻi tōḻi nerunal
		tārpūṇ kaḷiṟṟil talippuṇi taḻīi
		vatuvi īraṇip polinta nammoṭu
		putuvatu vanta kāvirik
	15.	1kōṭutōy malirniṟi āṭi yōrē.
- iṭiyaṉ neṭuṅkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 11. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
1-4. nalmaram kuḻīiya - nalla maraṅkaḷ cūḻnta (kaḷ viṟkum illil uḷḷa), pal nāḷ aritta naṉimutir cāṭi - palanāḷum vaṭikkappeṟṟa kaḷ niṟinta cāṭiyi, kōoy uṭippiṉ - mukakkum kalam uṭittiṭiṉ, mayaṅku maḻit tuvaliyiṉ maṟuku uṭaṉ paṉikkum - viraviya maḻittuḷi pōlat teruvelām tuḷikkum (ūrāya), paḻam pal nelliṉ vēḷūr vāyil - paḻiya palavaki neṟkaḷiyum uṭiya vēḷūriṉ vāyiliṭattu;
5-7. naṟuviri teḷitta nāṟu iṇar māli - naṟumaṇa nīr teḷikkap peṟṟa nāṟum kottukkaḷālāya pūmāliyi, poṟivari iṉa vaṇṭu ūtala kaḻiyum - puḷḷikaḷiyum varikaḷiyu
muṭiya kūṭṭamāya vaṇṭu añci ūtātu oḻivataṟku ētuvāṉa, uyar pali peṟūum - uyarnta palikaḷiyē peṟum, urukeḻu teyvam - accam tarum teyvam;
8-9.  puṉi irum katuppiṉ nī kaṭuttōḷvayiṉ - aṇinta kariya kūntaliyuṭiyaḷāya niṉṉāl aiyuṟap peṟṟāḷuṭaṉ, aṉiyēṉ āyiṉ aṇaṅkuka eṉ eṉa - yāṉ puṉalāṭi vantēṉāyiṉ eṉṉi varuttuvatāka eṉṟu;
10. maṉiyōḷ tēṟṟum makiḻnaṉ āyiṉ - taṉ maṉiviyi avaḷ kaṇavaṉ cūḷurittut teḷiyavippāṉāyiṉ;
11-15. nerunal - nēṟṟu, putuvatu vanta kāvirik kōṭutōy malir niṟi - putitāka vanta kāviriyiṉ kari yucciyiṉit tōyntu varum mikka veḷḷattil, tārpūṇ kaḷiṟṟil talippuṇi taḻīi - tāraṇinta kaḷiṟṟiṉip pōṉṟa puṇiyiṉ taliyiṭattit taḻuviyiruntu, vatuvi īr aṇip polinta - kūṭṭattiṟkuriya periya aṇiyāṟ polivuṟṟa, nammoṭu āṭiyōr - nammoṭu kūṭip puṉalāṭiṉōr; yārkol - yārōtāṉ?
(muṭipu) vēḷūr vāyilil uyarpali peṟum uru keḻu teyvam, nī kaṭuttōḷ vayiṉ aṉiyēṉāyiṉ eṉ aṇuṅkuka eṉa makiḻnaṉ maṉiyōḷit tēṟṟumāyiṉ, nerunal, kāviri malir niṟi nammōṭu āṭiyōr yārkol?
(vi-ri) naṉmaraṅkuḻīiya eṉpataṉpiṉ kaḷ viṟkum il eṉpatu varuvikkappaṭṭatu. naṉi - kaḷ. kōoy - kaḷ viṟkum kalam. kaḷḷum nellum mikutiyākavuṭiya vēḷūr eṉṟapaṭi. naṟuviri - paṉinīrum, cantaṉa mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kalaviyumām. palipeṟūum teyvam urukeḻu teyvam eṉa iyiyum. kaṭuttal - aiyuṟal. eṉ - eṉṉi. tēṟṟumāyiṉ eṉak kūṭṭuka. puṇi - mitakkum kaṭṭiyā
lākiya teppam; vēḻak kōlic cērttuk kaṭṭiyatumām. talippuṇi - puṇiyiṉ muṟpakuti; mintu mali kaḷiṟṟiṉ talippuṇi taḻīi' (266) eṉap piṉṉarum ivvāṟu varutal kāṇka. īraṇi - nīrāṭṭiṟkuriya aṇiyumām. polintu eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ polintu āṭiyōr eṉka. `tōṭutōy'1 eṉpatu pērāciriyar koṇṭa pāṭam.
(mē-ḷ) `pullutal mayakkum'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, illōr ceyviṉi ikaḻccik kaṇṇum eṉṉum pakutikku ic ceyyuḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, itu, `iḷiyōr kūṟṟu' eṉṟum, `kiḻavōṉ viḷiyāṭ ṭāṅkum aṟṟē'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `kōṭutōy malirniṟi āṭi yōrē' eṉap paratti piṟar alar kūṟiya vaḻik kāmañ ciṟantu pulantavāṟu kāṇka eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nac.
167. pāli
(talimakaṉ poruḷkaṭikkūṭṭiya neñciṟkuc collic celavu aḻuṅkiyatu.)
		vayaṅkumaṇi poruta vakiyami vaṉappiṉ
		pacuṅkāḻ alkul māa yōḷoṭu
		viṉivaṉap peytiya puṉipūñ cēkki
		viṇporu neṭunakart taṅki iṉṟē
	5.	iṉituṭaṉ kaḻintaṉṟu maṉṉē nāḷip
		poruntāk kaṇṇēm pulampuvan tuṟutarac
		cēkkuvaṅ kollō neñcē cātteṟintu
		atarkūṭ ṭuṇṇum aṇaṅkuṭip pakaḻik
		koṭuvil āṭavar paṭupaki verīi
	10.	 ūreḻuntu ulaṟiya pīreḻu mutupāḻ
		muruṅki mēynta peruṅki yāṉi
		verinōṅku ciṟupuṟam 4uriña olki
		iṭṭiki neṭuñcuvar viṭṭam vīḻnteṉa
		maṇippuṟāt tuṟanta marañcōr 5 māṭattu
	15.	eḻutaṇi kaṭavuḷ pōkaliṟ pulleṉṟu
		oḻukupali maṟanta meḻukāp puṉtiṇip
		1pālnāy tuṉṉiya paṟikkaṇ ciṟṟil
		kuyilkāḻ citiya maṇṭi ayilvāyk
		kūrmukac citali vēynta
	20.	pōrmaṭi nalliṟip potiyi lāṉē.
- kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 7. neñcē-,
1-5. vayaṅku maṇi poruta - paḷiṅku maṇiyiṉi yotta, vaki ami vaṉappiṉ - kūṟupāṭaminta aḻakiṉiyuṭiya, pacu kāḻ alkul māayōḷoṭu - paciya carattālāya mēkaliyi aṇinta alkuliyuṭiya māmi niṟattiṉaḷāya nam taliviyuṭaṉ, viṇ poru neṭunakar - vāṉi aḷāvum nīṇṭa māḷikiyil, viṉi vaṉappu eytiya puṉi pū cēkki  taṅki - aḻakiya vēlippāṭu aminta puṉiyappeṟṟa pūkkaḷiyuṭiya paḷḷiyiṉkaṇ taṅkutalāṉē, iṉṟu - iṉṟip pōḻtu, iṉitu uṭaṉ kaḻintaṉṟu - muḻutum iṉi
tākak kaḻintatu;
7-20. cāttu eṟintu atar kūṭṭuṇṇum - vaḻic cellum vāṇikac cāttiṉik koṉṟu avar poruḷik koḷḷi koṇṭu uṇṇum, aṇaṅku uṭi pakaḻi - varuttattic ceyyum ampiṉiyum, koṭuvil - vaḷinta villiyum uṭiya, āṭavar paṭu paki verīi - maṟavaratu mikka pakiyi añci, ūr eḻuntu ulaṟiya - kuṭi pōkap peṟṟamiyiṉ vaḷaṉ aṟṟa, pīr eḻu mutupāḻ - pīrkkup paṭarnta periya pāḻ iṭattil, muruṅki mēynta peruṅki yāṉi - muruṅkiyiṉit tiṉṟa periya kiyiṉi
yuṭiya yāṉiyiṉ, verin ōṅku ciṟupuṟam uriña - mutukiṉiṉ ṟuyarnta piṭari urāytaliṉ, olki iṭṭiki neṭuñcuvar viṭṭam vīḻnteṉa - taḷarntu ceṅkallālāya nīṇṭa cuvariluḷḷa viṭṭa maram vīḻntatāka, maṇippuṟāt tuṟanta maramcōr māṭattu - maṇippuṟā viṭṭoḻinta maram cōrnta māṭattiṉiyum, eḻutu aṇi kaṭavuḷ pōkaliṉ - eḻutappeṟṟa aḻakiya kaṭavuḷ puṟattē pōyviṭṭamiyiṉ, pul eṉṟu - polivaṟṟu, oḻuku pali maṟanta - iṭiyaṟātu nikaḻum pali maṟakkap peṟṟa, meḻukāp puṉ tiṇi - meḻukappaṭāta pulliya tiṇṇiyil, pāl nāy tuṉṉiya paṟikkaṇ ciṟu il - īṉṟaṇimiyuṭiya nāy taṅkiya paṟinta iṭattiyuṭiya ciṟṟiliyum, avviṭattē kuyil kāḻ citiya maṇṭi - iyaṟṟappaṭṭa kim maraṅkaḷ citiyumāṟu paravi, ayilvāy kūrmuka citali vēynta - vēliṉ muṉipōṉṟa kūriya mukattiṉiyuṭiya kaṟiyāṉ mūṭik koḷḷutaliṉ, pōr maṭi nalla iṟi potiyil āṉ - kūrimaṭinta nalla iṟappiṉiyu
muṭiya ampalattiṉ kaṇ;
5-7. pulampu vantu uṟutarap poruntāk kaṇṇēm - taṉimi vantaṭiyat tuyilāta kaṇkaḷi yuṭiyēmāy, nāḷi - nāḷip pōḻtil, cēkkuvam kol - taṅki iruppēmō (aḵtu iyalātaṉṟē.)
(muṭipu) neñcē! iṉṟu, neṭunakar, cēkkikkaṇ māyō ḷoṭu iṉituṭaṉ kaḻintaṉṟu maṉṉē; nāḷi, potiyiliṉ kaṇ poruntāk kaṇṇēm pulampu vantuṟutarac cēkkuvaṅ kollō?
māṭattiṉiyum ciṟṟiliyum iṟappiṉiyu muṭi potiyil eṉka.
(vi-ri) vayaṅku maṇi - paḷiṅku, kaṇṇāṭi. `vayaṅkaluḷ tuppeṟintavi pōla'1 eṉpuḻi vayaṅkal ip poruṭṭātal uṇarka. maṉ - mikutip poruṭṭu. ūr - ūriṉuḷḷārkku ākupeyar. eḻuntu - eḻutalāl. muruṅki - muruṅkiyi; muruṅki - toṭi nōkkit tiriyātu niṉṟatu: iṭṭiki - ceṅkal. pālnāy - pāliṉiyuṭiya nāy; īṉṟaṇimiyuṭiya nāy eṉṟapaṭi. kuyilutal - iyaṟṟutal.
(mē-ḷ) `karaṇattiṉamintu'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `tāṉ avaṭpiḻitta niliyiṉ kaṇṇum' eṉṉum pakutiyil icceyyuḷ neñciṉāṟ piriyak karuti varuntik kūṟiyatu eṉṟār, nac.
168. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟi vanta talimakaṉi iravukkuṟi vilakki varivu kaṭāyatu.)
		yāmam nummoṭu kaḻippi nōymikap
		paṉivār kaṇṇēm vikutum iṉiyē
		āṉṟal vēṇṭum vāṉtōy veṟpa
		3pallāṉ kuṉṟiṟ paṭuniḻaṟ cērnta
	5.	nalāṉ parappiṟ kuḻumūr āṅkaṇ
		koṭikkaṭaṉ ēṉṟa kōṭā neñciṉ
		utiyaṉ aṭṭil pōla oliyeḻuntu
		aruvi ārkkum peruvaric cilampiṉ
		īṉṟaṇi irumpiṭi taḻīik kaḷiṟutaṉ
	10.	tūṅkunaṭik kuḻavi tuyilpuṟaṅ kāppa
		oṭuṅkaḷi pulampap pōkik kaṭuṅkaṇ
		vāḷvari vayappuli kaṉmuḻi uraṟak
		kāṉavar maṭinta kaṅkul
		māṉatarc ciṟuneṟi varutal nīyē.
- kōṭṭampalattut tuñciya cēramāṉ.
(co-ḷ) 3. vāṉ tōy veṟpa - vāṉi aḷāviya maliyiyuṭiya talivaṉē!
1-2.	 yāmam nummōṭu kaḻippi - irāppoḻuti num muṭaṉ
kūṭik kaḻittu, nōy mika - piṉpu iravukkuṟiyiṉ ētam añcit tuṉpam mikutalāl, paṉivār kaṇṇēm vikutum - nīr oḻukuṅ kaṇkaḷiyuṭiyēmāy varunti yirukkiṉṟēm ākaliṉ;
3-14. pallāṉ kuṉṟil paṭuniḻal cērnta - pallāṉ kuṉṟu eṉṉum maliyiṟ poruntum niḻaliṉkaṭ cērnta, nalāṉ parappiṉ kuḻumūr āṅkaṇ - nalla āṉiriyiṉ parappiṉik koṇṭa kuḻumūriṭattē, koṭik kaṭaṉ eṉṟa kōṭā neñciṉ - īkiyāya kaṭaṉi ēṟṟuk koṇṭa kōṭṭamillāta neñciṉi yuṭiya, utiyaṉ aṭṭil pōla - utiyaṉ eṉpāṉatu aṭukkaḷi pōla, oli eḻuntu aruvi ārkkum peruvaric cilampiṉ - olimikku aruvikaḷ āravārittu iḻiyum periya variyiṉatu pakka maliyil, kaḷiṟu īṉṟaṇi irupiṭi taḻīi - kaḷiṟu īṉṟa aṇimi yuṭiya periya piṭiyiṉit taḻuvikkoṇṭu, taṉ tūṅku naṭik kuḻavi tuyil puṟam kāppa - taṉatu acinta naṭiyiṉiyuṭiya kuḻavi uṟaṅkukiṉṟa iṭattik kāttirukka, kaṭuṅkaṇ vāḷ vari vayappuli - taṟukaṇmiyuṭiya vāḷ pōṉṟa variyiṉiyuṭiya valiya puli, oṭuṅku aḷi pulampa pōki - oṭuṅkiya muḻi taṉittiṭap puṟam pōy, kal muḻi uraṟa - maliyiṉ kukiyiṭattil muḻaṅka, kāṉavar maṭinta kaṅkul - vēṭṭuvarum tuyilum naḷḷiravil, māṉ atarc ciṟu neṟi - vilaṅkukaḷ cellum vaḻiyākiya ciṟuneṟiyil, nī varutal - nī varutali, iṉi āṉṟal vēṇṭum - iṉi oḻital vēṇṭum.
(muṭipu) veṟpa! yāmam nummoṭu kaḻippi, paṉivār kaṇṇēm vikutum ākaliṉ, cilampil, kaḷiṟu kāppa, puli uraṟa kāṉavar maṭinta kaṅkulil ciṟu neṟikkaṇ nī varutali iṉi āṉṟal vēṇṭum.
(vi-ri) irāp poḻuti nummuṭaṉ kaḻikkum nāḷellām appoḻutu iṉpamāyiṉum nī varum vaḻiyiṉ ētatti niṉintu peritum tuṉpuṟukiṉṟōm. ākaliṉ, iṉi nī avvāṟu varutali oḻital vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟi, iravukkuṟi vilakki varivukaṭāyiṉā ḷeṉka. āṉṟal - amital; oḻital. pallāṉ kuṉṟu - oru maliyiṉ peyar pōlum; vallāṇ kuṉṟu  eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ vallāṇmikku iṭamākiya kuṉṟu eṉka. aṭṭilpōla - aṭṭilil oli yeḻutalpōla. oli; uṇṇavaruvārālāvatu. īṉṟaṇi - īṉṟa aṇimi. oṭuṅku aḷi - puli tuyiṉṟa iṭṭiya muḻi eṉavum, kaṉmuḻi - periya maliyiṉ kuki eṉavum koḷka. kaḷiṟu kākkumāṟu puli uraṟa eṉka. ik karuttu `ilaṅku maruppi yāṉi, kuṟumpoṟi maruṅkiṉ amartuṇi taḻīik, koṭuvari irumpuli kākkum'1 eṉṉum ceyyuḷil amin tiruttaluṅ kāṇka. māṉatarc ciṟuneṟi eṉṟamiyāl makkaḷ iyaṅkutaṟkākāta iṭukiya neṟi eṉpatu peṟṟām.
169. pāli
(talimakaṉ, iṭic curattut taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		marantali karintu nilampayam vāṭa
		2alaṅkukatir vēynta aḻaltikaḻ naṉantalip
		pulitolit tuṇṭa peruṅkaḷiṟṟu oḻiūṉ
		kalikeḻu maṟavar kāḻkkōt toḻintati
	5.	ñelikōl ciṟutī māṭṭi olitirik
		kaṭalviḷi amiḻtiṉ kaṇañcāl umaṇar
		cuṉikoḷ tīnīrc cōṟṟulik kūṭṭuñ
		curampala kaṭanta namvayiṟ paṭarntunaṉi
		pacali pāynta mēṉiyaḷ neṭituniṉintu
	10.	celkatil 3maḻukiya pulampukoḷ māli
		melviral cērttiya nutalaḷ malkik
		kayalumiḻ nīriṟ kaṇpaṉi vārap
		peruntōḷ 4 nekiḻnta cellaloṭu
		varuntumāl aḷiyaḷ tiruntiḻi tāṉē.
- toṇṭi āmūrc cāttaṉār.
(co-ḷ) neñcē!
1-8. maram tali karintu nilam payam vāṭa - maraṅkaḷ ucci kariyavum nilam vaḷam kuṉṟavum, alaṅku katir vēynta aḻal tikaḻ naṉantali - acikiṉṟa ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir cūḻappeṟṟa vemmi viḷaṅkum akaṉṟa pāli nilattē, puli tolittu uṇṭa perum kaḷiṟṟu oḻi ūṉ - puliyāṟ koṉṟu tiṉṉappeṟṟa periya yāṉiyiṉatu eñcikkiṭanta ūṉil, kalikeḻu maṟavar kāḻkōttu oḻintati - āravāram poruntiya āṟalippōr kōliṟ kōttuk (koṇṭēkiyatu pōka) eñciyati, olitiri kaṭal viḷi amiḻtiṉ kaṇam cāl umaṇar - olikkum ali poruntiya kaṭalil viḷiyum uppiṉik koṇarum kūṭṭam mikka uppu vāṇikar, ñelikōl ciṟu tī māṭṭi - tīkkaṭi kōlālākkiya ciṟu tīyil vatakki, cuṉi koḷ tī nīr cōṟṟu uli kūṭṭum -cuṉiyiṟ koṇṭa iṉiya nīrālāya cōṟṟiṉ uliyil kūṭṭi ākkum, curampala kaṭanta nam vayiṉ - pala curaneṟikaḷiyum kaṭantu vantuḷa nammiṭattu, paṭarntu - taṉ uḷḷam paṭartalāl;
8-14. tiruntu iḻi - tiruntiya aṇikaḷiyuṭiya nam talivi, celkatir maḻukiya pulampu koḷ māli - maṟiyum katir maṅkiya taṉimi koṇṭa māliyil, naṉi pacali pāynta mēṉiyaḷ - pacali mikavum paranta, mēṉiyaḷāki, neṭitu niṉintu - nīḷa niṉintu, mel viral cērttiya nutalaḷ - melliya viralkaḷic cērtta neṟṟiyiṉaḷāy, kayal umiḻ nīril kaṇ paṉi malki vāra - kayalmīṉ umiḻum nīrpōlak kaṇkaḷil nīr niṟintu oḻuka, perum tōḷ nekiḻnta cellaloṭu - periya tōḷ melintatā lāya tuṉpattuṭaṉ, varuntum - varuntuvaḷ, aḷiyaḷ - iraṅkattakkāḷ.
(muṭipu) neñcē! curampala kaṭanta namvayiṉ taṉ uḷḷam paṭartaliṉ, tiruntiḻi, māliyilē neṭitu niṉintu pacali pāynta mēṉiyaḷāy, viral cērttiya nutalaḷāy, kaṇpaṉi vārac cellaloṭu varuntum; aḷiyaḷ.
(vi-ri) karintu - kariya eṉat tirikka. payam - ēṉi vaḷaṅkaḷ. kāḻ - iruppuc calāki. kaṭalviḷi amiḻtu - uppu. kaṇam cāl umaṇar eṉṟamiyiṉ umaṇar kūṭṭamākavē varuvar eṉpatu peṟṟām. ciṉikoḷ tī nīr eṉṟamiyāl curattil nīr iṉmi peṟappaṭum. paṭarntu - paṭartalāl.
170. neytal
(talimakaḷ, kāmamikka kaḻipaṭarkiḷaviyāṟ coṟṟatu.)
		kāṉaluṅ kaḻaṟātu kaḻiyuṅ kūṟātu
		tēṉimir naṟumalarp puṉṉiyum moḻiyātu
		1oruniṉ allatu piṟitiyātum ilaṉē
		iruṅkaḻi malarnta kaṇpōl neytal
	5.	kamaḻitaḻ nāṟṟam amiḻteṉa naciit
		taṇtātu ūtiya vaṇṭiṉam kaḷiciṟantu
		2paṟiiya 3taḷarun tuṟivaṉi nīyē
		collal 4vēṇṭumāl alava palkāl
		kitiyam paṭuciṉi evvamoṭu acāam
	10.	kaṭaṟciṟu kākki kāmar peṭiyoṭu
		kōṭṭumīṉ vaḻaṅkum vēṭṭamaṭi parappiṉ
		veḷiṟāk kaṉavum naḷḷeṉ yāmattu
		niṉṉuṟu viḻumam kaḷintōḷ
		taṉṉuṟu viḻumam nīntumō eṉavē.
- maturik kaḷḷiṟ kaṭiyattāṉ veṇṇākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 8. alava - naṇṭē!
4-7. iru kaḻi malarnta - kariya nīrōṭiyil malarnta, kaṇpōl neytal kamaḻ itaḻ nāṟṟam - kaṇṇippōlum neytaṟ pūviṉ kamaḻum itaḻ nāṟṟattiṉi, amiḻtu eṉa nacii - amiḻtam pōlum eṉa virumpic ceṉṟu, taṇ tātu ūtiya - kuḷirnta tātiṉi uṇṭa, vaṇṭu iṉam kaḷi ciṟantu - vaṇṭiṉ kūṭṭam kaḷippumikku, paṟiiya taḷarum tuṟivaṉi - paṟattaṟku iyalātu cōrum tuṟiyiyuṭiya talivaṉukku;
1-2. kāṉalum kaḻaṟātu - kaṭaṟkaric cōliyum ceṉṟu tūtu kūṟātu, kaḻiyum kūṟātu - uppaṅkaḻiyum iyampātu, tēṉ imir naṟumalarp puṉṉiyum moḻiyātu - vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum naṟiya malariṉiyuṭiya puṉṉiyum moḻiyātu;
3. oru niṉ allatu piṟitu yātum ilaṉ - niṉṉiyē yaṉṟi vēṟu ettuṇiyum illēṉ;
8-14. kitiam paṭu ciṉi - tāḻi marattiṉ tāḻnta kiḷiyil, kāmar peṭiyoṭu evvamoṭu acāam - viruppam mikka peṭiyuṭaṉ varuttamuṭaṉ taḷarntirukkum, kaṭal ciṟu kākki - ciṟiya kaṭaṟ kākki, kōṭṭu mīṉ vaḻaṅkum vēṭṭam maṭi parappiṉ - cuṟāmīṉ iyaṅkum vēṭṭiyāṭutal nīṅkiya iṭattiluḷḷa, veḷ iṟā kaṉavum - veḷḷiya iṟāmīṉip paṟṟi yuṇpatākak kaṉavu kāṇum, naḷ eṉ yāmattu - iruḷ ceṟinta naṭuiravil vantu, palkāl niṉ uṟu viḻumam kaḷintōḷ - pala nāḷum niṉatu mikka tuyariṉi nīkkiyōḷ, taṉ uṟu viḻumam nīntumō eṉavē - niṉ pirivāl eytiya taṉatu mikka tuyariṉik kaṭakka vallaḷō eṉṟu;
7-8. nīyē collal vēṇṭum - at talivaṉpāṟ ceṉṟu nīyē kūṟutal vēṇṭum.
(muṭipu) alava! tuṟivaṉukkuk kāṉaluṅkaḻaṟātu; kaḻiyuṅ kūṟātu; puṉṉiyum moḻiyātu; oru niṉṉallatu piṟitu yātum ilaṉ; naḷḷeṉ yāmattu vantu palnāḷum niṉṉuṟu viḻumam kaḷintōḷ, taṉṉuṟu viḻumam nīntumō eṉa nīyē collal vēṇṭum.
(vi-ri) kāṉal, kaḻi, puṉṉi eṉpaṉa cāṉṟākat talivar eṉṉik kalantu ceṉṟārāyiṉum, avi yellām talivaṉip pirintu eytum varuttatti uṇaravallaṉa aṉmiyāṉum, orukāl uṇariṉum ceṉṟu kūṟamāṭṭātaṉa ākalāṉum nīyē tuṟivaṉukkuc collalvēṇṭum eṉat talivi alavaṉi nōkkik kūṟiṉāḷeṉka. kaṇpōl neytal eṉṟatu, `poruḷē uvamam ceytaṉar moḻiyiṉum'1 eṉpataṉāl amintatu. tuṟivaṉi - tuṟivaṉukku. kākki peṭiyoṭu veḷ iṟāk kaṉavum yāmam eṉka. kōṭṭu mīṉ vaḻaṅkum parappu eṉavum, vēṭṭamaṭi parappu eṉavum iyiyum. niṉṉuṟu viḻumam kaḷin
tōḷ eṉṟamiyāl nī avaḷatu viḻumattik kaḷiyātiruttal naṉṟi kōṟalām eṉpatu paṭa niṉṟatu. iṉi, paṟiiya taḷarum eṉpataṉi muṟṟākkik kāṉal mutaliyaṉa pōlātu vaṇṭu ceṉṟu kūṟutaṟku uriyatēṉum, atu tēṉiyuṇṭa kaḷippāl cōrntu kiṭakkum ākaliṉ nīyē collal vēṇṭum eṉṟāḷ eṉalumām. kāmam mikka kaḻipaṭar kiḷaviyāvatu talivaṉip pirintu varuntum talivi, taṉatu vēṭki mikutiyāl, kēḷātaṉavaṟṟik kēṭpaṉavāka viḷittu, talivaṉiṭattuc ceṉṟu eṉ tuṉpatti moḻiyīrō eṉṟu kūṟutalām. nīntumō eṉṟataṉāl viḻumam kaṭal eṉṟu kuṟitta vāṟām.
(u-ṟi) tātiṉi uṇṭa vaṇṭiṉam kaḷiciṟantu paṟakka māṭṭātu taḷarum tuṟivaṉ eṉṟatu, tāṉ peṟṟa vēṟu iṉpattil mayaṅkit taliviyi maṟantirukkum talivaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
kākki peṭiyoṭu yāmattu iṟāk kaṉavum eṉṟatu, talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ kūṭiyiruntu muṉpupeṟṟa iṉpatti niṉintu varuntukiṉṟāḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
(mē-ḷ) `nōyu miṉpamum'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷ, `collā marapiṉa colluvaṉavāka aḻuki paṟṟik kūṟiyatu, ivi uyartiṇiyumāyiṉ' eṉṟār, nac.
`iṉpatti veṟuttal'3 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `niṉṉuṟu viḻumaṅ kaḷintōḷ, taṉṉuṟu viḻumam nīntumō eṉavē' eṉpatu tuṉpattup pulampal eṉṉum meyppāṭu ākumeṉṟum, `muṭṭu vayiṟ kaḻaṟal'4 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, `kāṉaluṅ kaḻaṟātu.... nīyē, collal vēṇṭumāl alava' eṉpatu, tūtumuṉi viṉmi eṉṉum meyppāṭu ākumeṉṟum kūṟiṉar, pērā.
171. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟīiyatu.)
		nutalum nuṇpacappu ivarum tōḷum
		akaṉmali iṟumpiṉ āyntukoṇ ṭaṟutta
		paṇiyeḻil aḻiya vāṭum nāḷum
		niṉival mātavar paṇpeṉṟu ōvātu
	5.	iṉiyal vāḻi tōḻi puṇarvar
		ilaṅkukōl āytoṭi nekiḻap poruḷpurintu
		alantali ñemiyattu ataraṭin tirunta
		mālvaric cīṟūr maruḷpaṉ mākkaḷ
		kōḷval ēṟṟi ōci ōrmār
	10.	tiruttik koṇṭa ampiṉar nōṉcili
		eruttattu irīi iṭantoṟum paṭartaliṉ
		kīḻppaṭu tāram uṇṇā mēṟciṉip
		paḻampōl cēṟṟa tīmpuḻal uṇīiya
		karuṅkōṭṭu iruppi ūrum
	15.	peruṅki eṇkiṉ curaṉiṟan tōrē.
- kallāṭaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 5. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
6. poruḷ purintu - poruḷ īṭṭivara virumpi, ilaṅku kōl āytoṭi nekiḻa - viḷaṅkum tiraṭci poruntiya aḻakiya niṉ toṭi kaḻaṉṟu viḻa;
7-15. alan tali ñemiyattu atar aṭintirunta - vāṭiya ucciyiṉiyuṭiya ñemi maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya vaḻiyic cārntirunta, mālvaric cīṟūr - periya maliyaṭi vārattuḷḷa ciṟiya ūril, maruḷ pal mākkaḷ - maruṇṭa makkaḷāya palar, kōḷval ēṟṟi ōci ōr mār - kollutal valla karaṭi ēṟṟiṉ oliyiṉi uṇarvārāy, tiruttik koṇṭa ampiṉar - ceppam ceytu koṇṭa ampiṉa rāy, nōṉcili eruttattu irīi - valiya villit tōḷiṟ koṇṭu, iṭamtōṟum paṭartaliṉ - iṭamelām paravi varutaliṉ, kīḻp paṭutāram uṇṇā - tariyiṟ kiṭakkum uṇaviṉi uṇṇāvāy, mēl ciṉi paḻampōl cēṟṟa tīm puḻal uṇīiya - mēliṭattē kiḷikaḷiluḷḷa paḻaṅkaḷip pōla iṉikkum cāṟṟiṉiyuṭiya iṉiya tuḷiyiyuṭiya pūkkaḷi uṇṇa, karumkōṭṭu iruppi ūrum - kariya kompiṉiyuṭiya iruppi marattiṉ mēl ēṟum, perum ki eṇkiṉ curam iṟantōr - periya kiyiṉiyuṭiya karaṭikaḷi yuṭiya curaneṟiyik kaṭantu ceṉṟār.
5. puṇarvar - virivil varuvar ātalāl;
1-5. nutalum nuṇ pacappu ivarum - neṟṟiyum nuṇṇiya pacaliparakkappeṟum, tōḷum akal mali iṟumpiṉ āyntu koṇṭu aṟutta paṇi eḻil aḻiyavāṭum - tōḷum akaṉṟa maliyiluḷḷa kāṭṭil ārāyntukoṇṭu aṟutta mūṅkil tuṇṭiyotta aḻaku oḻiya vāṭṭamuṟum, nāḷum niṉival avar paṇpu eṉṟu - nāṭōṟum avar aruṭpaṇpiṉi yāṉ niṉivēṉ eṉṟu kūṟi, ōvātu iṉiyal - oḻiyātu varuntātē koḷ.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! poruḷ purintu, toṭi nekiḻac curaṉ iṟantōr puṇarvar; ātaliṉ, nutalum pacappu ivarum, tōḷum vāṭum, nāḷum niṉival eṉa iṉiyal.
mākkaḷ ōciyōrmār cili irīip paṭartaliṉ, eṇku kīḻppaṭutāram uṇṇā, puḻal uṇīiya iruppiyūrum curam eṉka.
(vi-ri) niṉival mātu: mātu, aci, alantali, vikāram. ñemiyattu: attu, cāriyi. maruḷ - karaṭiyiṉ ōciyi mutalil iṉṉateṉṟaṟiyātu maruṇṭa eṉka. tāram - paṇṭam (uṇavup poruḷ) puḻal, āku peyar. karaṭiyum marattiṉ kōṭṭil iyaṅka vallatātal aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu:
172. kuṟiñci
(tōḻi talimakaḷi iṭattu uyttuvantu talimakaṉi varivu kaṭāyatu.)
		vāraṇam uraṟum nīr tikaḻ cilampil
		piracamoṭu viriiya vayaṅkuveḷ aruvi
		iṉṉici imiḻiyaṅ kaṭuppa immeṉak
		kalmuki viṭarakañ cilampa vīḻuṅ
	5.	kāmputali maṇanta ōṅkumalic cāral
		irumpuvaṭit taṉṉa karuṅkik kāṉavaṉ
		virimalar marāam poruntik kōlterintu
		1varinutal yāṉi aruniṟat taḻutti
		ikalaṭu muṉpiṉ veṇkōṭu koṇṭutaṉ
	10.	2pulvēy kurampi pulara ūṉṟi
		muṉṟil nīṭiya 3muḻavuṟaḻ palaviṉ
		piḻimakiḻ uvakiyaṉ kiḷiyoṭu kaliciṟantu
		cānta ñekiḻiyiṉ ūṉpuḻuk kayaruṅ
		kuṉṟa nāṭaṉ aṉpili yākutal
	15.	aṟiyēṉ yāṉaḵtu aṟintaṉeṉ āyiṉ
		aṇiyiḻi uṇkaṇ āyitaḻk kuṟumakaḷ
		maṇiyēr māṇnalam citiyap
		poṉṉēr 1pacali pāviṉṟu maṉṉē.
- maturip pālāciriyar nappālaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. vāraṇam uraṟum nīrtikaḻ cilampil - yāṉi muḻaṅkum taṉmi vāynta pakka maliyil, piracamoṭu viri iyavayaṅku veḷ aruvi - tēṉuṭaṉ kalantuvarum viḷakka muṟṟa veḷḷiya aruvi, iṉici imiḻ iyam kaṭuppa - iṉṉoliyuṭaṉ muḻaṅkum mattaḷatti yoppa, im eṉa kalmuki viṭar akam cilampa vīḻum - immeṉum oliyuṭaṉ maliyiṉ kukikaḷum piḷappiṭaṅkaḷum olikka viḻum, kāmpu talimaṇanta ōṅku malic cāral - mūṅkil neruṅkiya uyarnta maliccāralil uḷḷa;
6-14. irumpu vaṭittaṉṉa karuṅkik kāṉavaṉ - irumpiṉi vārttuc ceytāṟ pōṉṟa valiya kiyiṉiyuṭiya vēṭṭuvaṉ, viri malar marāam porunti - virinta malariṉiyuṭiya veṇ kaṭampiṉic cārntu niṉṟu, kōl terintu - ampiṉi āyntu koṇṭu, varinutal yāṉi aru niṟattu aḻutti - vari poruntiya neṟṟiyiṉiyuṭiya kaḷiṟṟiṉ ariya mārpiṟ celutti, ikal aṭu muṉpiṉ veṇkōṭu koṇṭu - pakiyiṉik kollum vali yiṉiyuṭiya ataṉ veḷḷiya kompiṉik koṇṭuvantu, taṉ pulvēy kurampi pulara ūṉṟi - taṉatu ūkam pullāl vēynta kuṭiciyil pulāl nāṟṟam vīca ūṉṟutal ceytu, muṉṟil nīṭiya muḻavu uṟaḻ palaviṉ piḻi makiḻ uvakiyaṉ - ak kuṭici muṉṟilil uyarnta muḻavu pōlum palāk kaṉiyiṉiṉṟu piḻinteṭutta matuviṉi uṇṭa kaḷippiṉaṉāki, kiḷiyoṭu kali ciṟantu - cuṟṟattuṭaṉ āravārammikku, cānta ñekiḻiyiṉ ūṉpuḻukku ayarum - cantaṉa viṟakālāya tīyil ūṉuṭaṉ kūṭiya cōṟṟi aṭṭuṇṇum iṭamākiya, kuṉṟa nāṭa - kuṉṟam poruntiya nāṭṭiyuṭiya talivaṉē!
14-18. nī aṉpili ākutal aṟiyēṉ yāṉ - nī aṉpilāy ātali yāṉ muṉpu aṟintilēṉ, aḵtu aṟintaṉeṉ āyiṉ- ataṉi yāṉ aṟintuḷēṉāyiṉ, aṇi iḻi āy itaḻ uṇkaṇ kuṟumakaḷ - aḻakiya aṇikalaṉiyum aḻakiya itaḻiṉiyuṭiya miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷiyum uṭiya iḷiyaḷāya eṉ taliviyiṉ, maṇi ēr māṇ nalam citiya - nīlamaṇi pōlum ciṟanta aḻakukeṭa, poṉ nēr pacali pāiṉṟu  maṉ-poṉṉi otta pacali peritum parattal illiyākum.
(muṭipu) kuṉṟa nāṭa! nī aṉpili yākutal aṟiyēṉ: aḵtu aṟintaṉeṉ āyiṉ kuṟumakaḷ māṇalam citiya pacali maṉ pāiṉṟu. aruvi immeṉa vīḻum cāralil kāṉavaṉ yāṉikkōṭu koṇṭu kurampi ūṉṟic cānta ñekiḻiyiṉ ūṉpuḻukku ayarum kuṉṟam eṉka.
(vi-ri) nīr - cuṉi nīrumām. mukiyākiya viyar eṉṟalumām. aruvi vīḻum maliccāral eṉavum, kāmpu talimaṇanta malic cāral eṉavum taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. yāṉiyik koṉṟu ataṉ kōṭu koḷḷum valiya ki yeṉpatu tōṉṟa irumpu vaṭittaṉṉa karuṅki eṉappaṭṭatu. marāam poruntutal, yāṉi taṉ iruppiyaṟintu iriyātavāṟu patuṅki niṟṟal. iravukkuṟiyiṉ ētam añcit talivi varuntutaliyum, ūril alar eḻutaliyum karuti virintu varintu koḷḷātu kaḷavoḻukkattilēyē tāḻn tiruttal paṟṟit talivaṉi `aṉpili' eṉṟum, nī ivvāṟu oḻukuti eṉpataṉi muṉṉar aṟintiruppēṉāyiṉ, yāṉ kūṭṭattiṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭirē ṉākaliṉ, varivu nīṭṭittal kāraṇamākat talivi nalaṉcitiyap pacali paṭartal illiyākum eṉṟum tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka.
(u-ṟi) piracamoṭu viriiya aruvi viṭarakam cilampa vīḻum eṉṟatu, taliviyuṭaṉ kūṭiya talivaṉatu kaḷavoḻukkam ūreṅkum alaruṇṭāka nikaḻkiṉṟatu eṉṟavāṟu. yāṉi veṇkōṭukoṇṭu kāṉavaṉ kurampi pulara ūṉṟi uvakiyaṉ kaliciṟantu cānta ñekiḻiyiṉ ūṉpuḻuk kayarum eṉṟatu, talivaṉ ariyaḷāya taliviyi eḷiyaḷ eṉa matittuk kaḷavoḻukkamākiya iṉpattilē tarukkiyiruttal eṉṟavāṟu:
173. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.)
		aṟantalip piriyātu oḻukaluñ ciṟanta
		kēḷir kēṭupala ūṉṟalum nāḷum
		varuntā uḷḷamoṭu iruntōrkku illeṉac
		ceyviṉi purinta neñciṉar naṟunutal
	5.	miyīr ōti arumpaṭar uḻattal
		cilnāḷ tāṅkal vēṇṭum eṉṟuniṉ
		nalmāṇ elvaḷi tiruttiṉa rāyiṉ
		varuvar vāḻi tōḻi palapuri
		vārkayiṟ ṟoḻuki nōṉcuval koḷīip
	10.	pakaṭutuṟi ēṟṟattu umaṇviḷi verīi
		uḻimāṉ ampiṇi iṉaṉirin tōṭak
		kāṭukaviṉ aḻiya uriik kōṭi
		niṉṟutiṉa viḷinta ampaṇi neṭuvēyk
		kaṇviṭat teṟikkum maṇṇā muttam
	15.	kaḻaṅkuṟaḻ tōṉṟala paḻaṅkuḻit tāam
		iṉkaḷi naṟaviṉ iyaltēr naṉṉaṉ
		viṇporu neṭuvarik kavāaṉ
		poṉpaṭu maruṅkiṉ maliiṟan tōrē.
- 1muḷḷiyūrp pūtiyār.
(co-ḷ) 8. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
1-4. aṟam talippiriyātu oḻukalum - aṟaneṟiyiṉiṉṟum nīṅkātu ilvāḻkki naṭattalum, ciṟanta kēḷir kēṭu pala ūṉṟalum - ciṟappuṟṟa cuṟṟattāratu palavakiyāṉa tuṉpaṅkaḷiyum tāṅkalum, nāḷum varuntā uḷḷamoṭu iruntōrkku il eṉa - eñ ñāṉṟum (muyaṟciyāl) mey varuntāmikku ētuvāya (ūkka millāta) uḷḷamoṭu iruntōrkku illiyākum eṉṟu, ceyviṉi purinta neñciṉar - poruḷ īṭṭum viṉiyi virumpiya neñciṉarāya nan talivar;
8-18. palapuri vār kayiṟṟu oḻuki - pala purikaḷālāya nīṇṭa kayiṟṟāṟ piṇitta vaṇṭiyiṉi, pakaṭu nōṉ cuval koḷīi - pakaṭukaḷiṉ valiya piṭariyil pūṭṭi, tuṟi ēṟṟattu - ēṟṟamāṉa tuṟikaḷil ōṭṭumiṭattu, umaṇ viḷi verīi - uppu vāṇikar avaṟṟi urappum ōciyik kēṭṭu añci, uḻimāṉ ampiṇi iṉaṉ irintu oṭa - āṇmāṉum peṇmāṉum ākiya iṉaṅkaḷ keṭṭu ōṭavum, kāṭu kaviṉ aḻiya - kāṭukaḷ aḻaku keṭavum, kōṭi urii - kōṭi paravi, niṉṟu tiṉa viḷinta am paṇi neṭuvēy - nilipeṟṟu nīriṉi uṟiñcutalāl vaṟṟiya aḻakiya periya neṭiya mūṅkiliṉ, kaṇviṭat teṟikkum maṇṇāmuttam - kaṇukkaḷ piḷakkat teṟittu viḻum kaḻuvap peṟāta muttukkaḷ, kaḻaṅku uṟaḻ tōṉṟala - kaḻaṅkiṉi yotta tōṟṟam uṭiyaṉavāki, paḻaṅkuḻi tāam - (aṅkuḷḷār kaḻaṅkāṭiya) paḻiya kuḻiyilē viḻum (iṭamākiya), iṉ kaḷi naṟaviṉ iyaltēr naṉṉaṉ - iṉiya kaḷippit tarum kaḷḷiṉiyum iyaṟṟappaṭṭa tēriṉiyumuṭiya naṉṉaṉatu, viṇporu neṭuvarik kavāṉ - vāṉi aḷāviya nīṇṭa mūṅkiliyuṭiya pakka maliyiṉiyum, poṉpaṭu maruṅkiṉ mali  iṟantōr - poṉ poruntiya iṭaṅkaḷiyumuṭiya maliyiṉik kaṭantu ceṉṟōrāya nam talivar;
4-8. naṟunutal mi īr ōti - naṟiya neṟṟiyiyum kariya periya kūntaliyum uṭiyāḷē, arumpaṭar uḻattal - ariya tuṉpattāl varuntutali, cilnāḷ tāṅkal vēṇṭum eṉṟu - cilakālam poṟuttuk kōṭal vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟi, niṉ nal māṇ elvaḷi tiruttiṉarāyiṉ - niṉatu nalla māṇpuṟṟa oḷi poruntiya vaḷi yiṉit tiruttit taliyaḷi ceytaṉarātalāl, varuvar - virivil vantuṟuvar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! ceyviṉi purinta neñciṉarāy, maliyiṟantōrākiya nam talivar, arumpaṭar uḻattal ciṉṉāḷ tāṅkal vēṇṭum eṉṟu niṉ elvaḷi tiruttiṉa rātaliṉ, (virivil) varuvar.
(vi-ri) naṟunutal, ōti eṉpavaṟṟiṟku, nutali nīvutalum ōtiyik kōtutalum ceytaṉar eṉak kuṟippiṟ poruḷ koḷḷalumām. `vali muṉpiṉ'1 eṉṟa pālikkaliyil, tāḻ katuppu aṇikuvar kātalar `oḷḷiḻi tiruttuvar kātalar, oṇṇutal nīvuvar kātalar' eṉa varutal kāṇka. tiruttiṉarāyiṉ - tiruttiṉarātaliṉ eṉka. ciṉṉāḷ tāṅkal vēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟi iṅṅaṉam taliyaḷi ceytu akaṉṟārātaliṉ, avar niṉṉi niṉintu virivil vārā tiruppā rallar, nī varuntātēkoḷ eṉṟu, taliviyit tōḻi vaṟpuṟut tiṉāḷ eṉka. umaṇ - umaṇar;  ākupeyar. kōṭitiṉa eṉpatu, `uṇṭaṟkuriya vallāpporuḷi, yuṇṭaṉa pōlak kūṟalu marapē'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ummiyāl amikkappeṟum. muttam tāam kavāṉ eṉa iyiyum. kaḻaṅku uṟaḻ tōṉṟala eṉṟatu muttiṉ parumi kuṟittapaṭi.
174. mulli
(pācaṟikkaṇ talimakaṉ taṉṉeñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		iruperu vēntar māṟukoḷ viyaṉkaḷattu
		orupaṭi koṇṭu varupaṭi peyarkkuñ
		celvam uṭiyōrkku niṉṟaṉṟu viṟaleṉap
		pūkkōḷ ēya taṇṇumi vilakkic
	5.	celvēm ātal aṟiyāḷ mulli
		nērkāl mutukoṭi kuḻippanīr corintu
		kāli vāṉattuk kaṭuṅkural koṇmū
		muḻaṅkutoṟuṅ 3kiyaṟṟu oṭuṅkinap pulantu
		paḻaṅkaṇ koṇṭa pacali mēṉiyaḷ
	10.	yāṅkā kuvaḷkol tāṉē vēṅki
		4ūḻuṟu naṟuvī kaṭuppak kēḻkoḷa
		ākat tarumpiya mācaṟu cuṇaṅkiṉaḷ
		naṉmaṇal viyaliṭi naṭanta
		cilmel otukkiṉ māa yōḷē.
- maturi aḷakkar ñāḻalār makaṉār maḷḷaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. iru peru vēntar māṟu koḷ viyaṉ kaḷattu- pēraracar iruvar māṟupāṭu koṇṭu porum periya pōrkkaḷattē, oru paṭi koṇṭu varupaṭi peyarkkum - tamatu oppaṟṟa paṭikkalattikkoṇṭu etir varum paṭikaḷip piṟakkiṭac ceyyum, celvam uṭiyōrkku viṟal niṉṟaṉṟu eṉa - veṟṟiyākiya celvam uṭiyōrkku ipperumi nilipeṟṟatu eṉṟu kūṟi, pūkkōḷ ēya taṇṇumi vilakki - taṇṇumi muḻakkuṭaṉ porunta aracaṉ aḷikkum pūkkōḷiṉiyum ēlātu tavirttu, celvēm ātal aṟiyāḷ - nām taṉpāl virintu cellutali aṟiyāḷāy;
10-14. vēṅki ūḻ uṟu naṟuvī kaṭuppak kēḻ koḷa - vēṅkiyiṉ malartaluṟṟa naṟiya malari oppa naṉṉiṟam koḷḷa, ākattu arumpiya mācu aṟu cuṇaṅkiṉaḷ - mēṉiyiṟ ṟōṉṟiya kuṟṟamaṟṟa tēmaliyuṭiyaḷāya, nal maṇal viyaliṭi naṭanta - nalla maṇaliyuṭiya akaṉṟaviṭattē naṭanta, cilmel otukkiṉ māayōḷ - cilavāya melliya naṭiyiṉiyuṭiya māmi niṟattiṉiyuṭiya nam talivi;
5-10. nēr kāl mulli mutukoṭi kuḻippa - nirampiya kālkaḷiṟ paṭarnta mutiya mullikkoṭi taḷirkka, kāli vāṉattuk kaṭuṅkural koṇmū - kīḻtticiyil vāṉatteḻunta kaṭiya kuraliyuṭiya mēkam, nīr corintu muḻaṅkutoṟum - nīrip peytu muḻaṅkuntōṟum, nappulantu kiyaṟṟu oṭuṅki - nammi veṟuttuc ceyalaṟṟu oṭuṅki, paḻaṅkaṇ koṇṭa pacali mēṉiyaḷ - tuṉpiṉi eytiya pacali pāynta mēṉiyaḷāy, yāṅku ākuvaḷ kol - enniliyiṉi aṭivāḷō?
(muṭipu) neñcē! nām pūkkōḷ ēya taṇṇumi vilakkic celvēmātali aṟiyāḷāya māyōḷ, koṇmū nīr corintu muḻaṅku toṟum kiyaṟṟu oṭuṅki nammip pulantu pacali mēṉiyaḷāki yāṅkākuvaḷ kollō!
(vi-ri) varupaṭi peyarkkum celvam eṉṟamiyāl celvameṉpatu veṟṟiyi uṇarttiṟṟu. viṟal - mēṉmi. māṟṟāri veṉṟa veṟṟi vīrarkku aracaṉ poṟpū aḷittuc ciṟappuc ceyvaṉ; appūviṉip peṟutal pūkkōḷākum. taṇṇumi ēya pūkkōḷ eṉa māṟuka. pakiyi veṉṟu aracaṉ aḷikkum ciṟappiṉip peṟutaṟkum tāḻttu nillātu nām celvē
māyiṉum kuṟitta paruvam vantamiyiṉ talivi eṅṅaṉam ākuvaḷō eṉṟu talivaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc collic celavu vituppuṟukiṉṟāṉ eṉka. kāli vāṉam - ñāyiṟu tōṉṟum kīḻttici vāṉam eṉka. tāṉ avaḷip piriyum ñāṉṟu maṇaṟparappilē avaḷ kuṟuka aṭiyiṭṭu naṭanta meṉṉaṭi avaṉ neñcam viṭṭakaṉṟila tākaliṉ, `naṉmaṇal viyaliṭi naṭanta, ciṉmel lotukkiṉ māayōḷ' eṉṟāṉ.
(mē-ḷ) `ēvaṉ marapiṉ'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷ mīḷvāṉ taṉ neñciṟku urittatu eṉṟum, itaṉuḷ pūkkōḷēya taṇṇumi vilakkic celvēm eṉṟaliṉ, aracaṉāṟ ciṟappup peṟṟa talivaṉāyiṟṟu eṉṟum, `oṉṟāt tamariṉum'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ākit tōṉṟum pāṅkōr pāṅkiṉum eṉṉun tuṟikku, taṉakku ākkam ciṟanta naṭpuṭiyōrākit tōṉṟum naṭpuṭiyōrkku uṟṟuḻi yutavac cēṟaṟkaṇṇum eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, ic ceyyuḷ ataṟku utāraṇamākum eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nac.
175. pāli
(piriviṉkaṇ vaṟpuṟukkun tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu. piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc coṟṟatūumām.
		vīṅkuviḷimpu urīiya viciyami nōṉcili
		vāṅkutoṭi piḻiyā vaṉkaṇ āṭavar
		viṭutoṟum viḷikkum vevvāy vāḷi
		āṟucel vampalar uyircelap peyarppiṉ
	5.	pā ṟukiḷi payirntu paṭumuṭi kavarum
		veñcuram iṟanta kātalar neñcuṇara
		ariya vañciṉañ colliyum paṉmāṇ
		terivaḷi muṉki paṟṟiyum viṉimuṭittu
		varutum eṉṟaṉar aṉṟē tōḻi
	10.	kāliyal neṭuntērk kivaṇ ceḻiyaṉ
		ālaṅ kāṉattu amarkaṭan tuyartta
		vēliṉum palūḻ miṉṉi muraceṉa
		māiru vicumpil kaṭiyiṭi payiṟṟi
		nērkatir 3niritta nēmiyañ celvaṉ
	15.	pōraṭaṅku akalam poruntiya tārpōl
		tiruvil tēettuk kulii urukeḻu
		maṇpayam pūppap pāayt
		taṇpeyal eḻili tāḻnta pōḻtē.       - ālampēri cāttaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 9. tōḻi-,
1-8. vīṅku viḷimpu urīiya - tamatu taṭitta tōḷiṉ viḷimpiṉi uraciya, vici ami nōṉ cili - vēkam aminta valiya villil vittu, vāṅku toṭi piḻiyā vaṉkaṇ āṭavar - iḻuttu viṭum ampu kuṟi tapputal illāta taṟukaṇmiyi yuṭiya maṟavar, viṭutoṟum viḷikkum vevvāy vāḷi - eyyuntōṟum olittuc cellum koṭiya vāyiṉiyuṭiya ampu, āṟu cel vampalar uyir celap peyarppiṉ - vaḻiccellum putiyaratu uyirkeṭap pōkkutalāl, pāṟu kiḷi payirntu paṭumuṭi kavarum -paruntu taṉ kiḷiyiṉi aḻittu mikka pulāli uṇṇum, vem curam iṟanta kātalar - koṭiya cura neṟiyik kaṭantu ceṉṟa nam kātalar, neñcu uṇara - tamatu neñcu uṇara, ariya vañciṉam colliyum - kaṭiya cūḷ urittum, palmāṇ terivaḷi muṉki paṟṟiyum - paṉmuṟi ārāynta vaḷiyiṉiyuṭiya eṉatu muṉ kiyiṉip paṟṟit taliyaḷi ceytum;
10-18. taṇ peyal eḻili - taṇṇiya maḻiyip peyyum mēkam, kāl iyal neṭu tēr kivaṇ ceḻiyaṉ - kāṟṟuppōla iyalum neṭiya tēriṉiyum kivaṇmiyiṉiyumuṭiya pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, ālaṅkāṉattu amar kaṭantu uyartta - taliyālaṅ
kāṉattup pōri veṉṟu uyartta, vēliṉum pal ūḻ miṉṉi - vēṟpaṭikaḷik kāṭṭil paṉmuṟi miṉṉi, muracu eṉa māiru vicumpil kaṭiiṭi payiṟṟi - kariya periya vāṉiṉkaṇ (avaṉatu veṉṟu eṟi) muracam eṉak kaṭiya iṭiyiṉip palakālum tōṟṟuvittu, nēr katir niritta nēmi am celvaṉ - nirampiya katirkaḷiṉ oḻuṅkiṉik koṇṭa āḻiyiyuṭiya tirumāliṉ, pōr aṭaṅku akalam poruntiya tārpōl - pakivar pōr oḻitaṟkukkāraṇamāya mārpiṉiṭattē taṅkiya mālipōla, urukeḻu tiruvil tēettuk kulii - paṉṉiṟam vāynta aḻakiya villi avvicumpiṉiṭattē vaḷittu; maṇ payam pūppap pāay - nilam payaṉittarap paravi, tāḻnta pōḻtu - iṟaṅkip peyyuṅ kālattu;
8-9. viṉi muṭittu varutum eṉṟaṉar aṉṟē - tām cellum viṉiyi muṭittukkoṇṭu mīṇṭum varuvēm eṉṟāraṉṟō? (aṅṅaṉam vantilarē; eṉ ceyval!)
(muṭipu) veñcuram iṟanta kātalar, vañciṉam colliyum, muṉki paṟṟiyum, taṇpeyal eḻili tāḻnta pōḻtu varutum eṉṟaṉarē? (aṅṅaṉam vantilarē; eṉ ceyval!)
(vi-ri) viḷimpu - tōliṉ mēṟpuṟam. peyarppiṉ - peyarttalāl. avar kūṟiya vañciṉattiṟku avar neñcē cāṉṟāka amiyum eṉpāḷ, `neñcuṇara' eṉṟāḷ; `neñcattukkuṟukiya kariyilli'1 eṉap piṟacāṉṟōr kūṟutalum kāṇka. muṉki paṟṟi yeṉpataṉi vēṟu pirittu vañciṉam colli eṉpataṉōṭu kūṭṭi yurittalumām. muṉki paṟṟi vañciṉam kūṟum vaḻakkuṇmi, `nēriṟi muṉki paṟṟic, cūrara makaḷiro ṭuṟṟa cūḷē'2 eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum. kariya vāṉukku nēmiyañ celvaṉatu akalamum, avvāṉiṟ ṟōṉṟiya villukku avvakalattiṟ poruntiya tārum uvamam. tēem - iṭam, vāṉiṉ iṭam. kārpparuvat toṭakkattu varutum eṉṟavar vārāmiyāl, yāṉ eṉceyvēṉ eṉat tōḻikkut talimakaḷ kūṟiṉāḷ.
176. marutam
(tōḻi, talimakaṉi vāyiṉ maṟuttatu.)
		kaṭalkaṇ ṭaṉṉa kaṇṇakaṉ parappiṉ
		nilampaka vīḻnta vērmutir kiḻaṅkiṉ
		kaḻikaṇ ṭaṉṉa tūmpuṭit tiraḷkāl
		kaḷiṟṟuccevi yaṉṉa pācaṭi maruṅkil
	5.	kaḻunivan taṉṉa koḻumuki iṭiiṭi
		muṟuval mukattiṟ paṉmalar tayaṅkap
		pūtta tāmarip puḷḷimiḻ paḻaṉattu
		vēppunaṉi yaṉṉa neṭuṅkaṇ nīrñeṇṭu
		iritēr veṇkuruku añci ayalatu
	10.	olitta pakaṉṟi iruñcēṟṟu aḷḷal
		titaliyiṉ varippa ōṭi virintutaṉ
		īrmali maṇṇaḷic ceṟiyum ūra
		3maṉinaku vayali maraṉivar koḻuṅkoṭi
		arimalar āmpaloṭu ārtaḻi tii
	15.	viḻavāṭu makaḷiroṭu taḻūu aṇippolintu
		malarēr uṇkaṇ māṇiḻi muṉkik
		kuṟuntoṭi tuṭakkiya neṭuntoṭar viṭuttatu
		uṭaṉṟaṉaḷ pōluniṉ kātali empōl
		pulluḷik kuṭumip putalvaṟ payantu
	20.	nelluṭi neṭunakar niṉiṉṟu uṟiya
		eṉṉa kaṭattaḷō maṟṟē taṉmukattu
		eḻuteḻil citiya aḻutaṉaḷ ēṅki
		vaṭitteṉa urutta tittip palūḻ
		noṭitteṉac civanta melviral tirukupu
	25.	kūrnuṉi maḻukiya eyiṟṟaḷ
		ūrmuḻutum nuvaluniṟ kāṇiya ceṉmē.
- marutampāṭiya iḷaṅkaṭuṅkō.
(co-ḷ) 1-12. kaṭal kaṇṭaṉṉa kaṇ akaṉ parappiṉ - kaṭalik kaṇṭāṟ pōṉṟa iṭamakaṉṟa nīrpparappil, nilampaka vīḻnta vērmutir kiḻaṅkiṉ - nilam piḷakkumāṟu iṟaṅkiya vēril mutirnta kiḻaṅkiṉiyum, kaḻi kaṇṭaṉṉa tūmpu uṭi tiraḷ kāl - mūṅkilik kaṇṭālotta tuḷiyuṭiya tiraṇṭa taṇṭiṉiyum, kaḷiṟṟuc cevi aṉṉa pācaṭi maruṅkil - kaḷiṟṟiṉ cevipōṉṟa paciya iliyiṭattē, kaḻu nivantaṉṉa koḻumuki iṭiiṭi - kaḻu uyarntu tōṉṟali yotta ceḻitta moṭṭukkaḷiṉ iṭiyiṭiyē, muṟuval mukattil tayaṅkap pūtta paṉmalar - puṉcirippiṉiyuṭiya mukam pōla viḷaṅkap pūtta pala malar
kaḷiyumuṭiya, tāmari puḷ imiḻ paḻaṉattu - tāmariyiṉi
yuṭiya puṭkaḷ olikkum vayalil uḷḷa, vēppu naṉi aṉṉa neṭukaṇ nīr ñeṇṭu - vēmpiṉ arumpiṉi yotta nīṇṭa kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya nīr ñeṇṭāṉatu (parattamikkup pirintu pōyatu). iri tēr veṇ kuruku añci - iriyiṉi āyntu pārttirunta veḷḷiya nāriyiṉik kaṇṭu añci, ayalatu olitta pakaṉṟi iru cēṟṟu aḷḷal - ayaliluḷḷa taḻitta civatiyiṉiyuṭiya kariya cēṟṟup piḻampil, titaliyiṉ varippa ōṭi - tēmalpōla variyuṇṭāka ōṭicceṉṟu, virintu taṉ īrmali maṇ aḷic ceṟiyum ūra - virintu taṉatu īram mikka maṇ aḷiyuṭ patuṅkum ūraṉē!
13-18. maṉinaku maraṉ ivar vayali koḻuṅkoṭi - maṉiyiṉ kaṇ viḷaṅkum marattiṟ paṭarum vayaliyākiya koḻuviya koṭi
yiṉi, arimalar āmpaloṭu ārtaḻi tii - viḷaṅkum malarkaḷi
yuṭiya āmpaloṭu cērttuk kaṭṭiya taḻiyuṭiyi uṭuttu, viḻavu āṭu makaḷiroṭu taḻūuaṇi polintu - viḻāviṉkaṇ āṭum makaḷiroṭu taḻuvi āṭum aṇiyāṟ polivuṟṟu, malar ēr uṇkaṇ māṇ iḻi kuṟuntoṭi muṉki tuṭakkiya - malari yotta miyuṇṭa kaṇṇiṉiyum māṇpuṟṟa aṇiyiṉiyum uṭiya niṉ paratti taṉatu kuṟiya vaḷiyaṇinta muṉkiyiṉāl paṇitta, neṭu toṭar viṭuttatu - neṭiya piṇippiṉi viṭuttuc ceṉṟa aḷaviṟkē, uṭaṉṟaṉaḷ - vekuṇṭaṉaḷāy;
21-26. taṉ mukattu eḻutu eḻil citiya - taṉ mukattiṉatu eḻutuvataṟku ētuvākiya aḻaku keṭa, aḻutaṉaḷ ēṅki - aḻutu ēkka
muṟṟu, vaṭittu eṉa urutta titti - poṉṉi urukki vārttāloppa urukkoṇṭa tēmaliyum, pal ūḻ noṭitteṉa civanta melviral - paṉmuṟi muṟittuk koḷḷaliṉ civanta melliya viraliṉiyum, tirukupu kūrnuṉi maḻukiya eyiṟṟaḷ - tirukik kaṭittalāṉ kūriya muṉi maḻuṅkiya eyiṟṟiyu muṭiyaḷāy, ūr muḻutum nuvalum niṉ kāṇiya ceṉmē - ūr eṅkum alar kūṟappaṭum niṉṉik kāṇṭaṟkut tēṭic celvāḷ (ākaliṉ);
18-21. niṉ kātali - niṉ kātaliyākiya avaḷ, empōl pul uḷik kuṭumip putalvaṉ payantu - emmip pōlap pulliya uḷi pōlum kuṭumiyiyuṭiya putalvaṉip peṟṟu, nel uṭi neṭunakar niṉ iṉṟu uṟiya - nelluṭiya nīṇṭa maṉiyil niṉṉip pirintu taṅka, eṉṉa kaṭattaḷō - eṉṉa kaṭappāṭu uṭiyavaḷō?
(muṭipu) ūra! māṇiḻi taṉ muṉki toṭakkiya neṭun toṭar viṭuttatu uṭaṉṟaṉaḷāy aḻutaṉaḷ ēṅki, ūr muḻutum nuvalum niṉṉik kāṇiya ceṉmē; ākaliṉ niṉ kātaliyāya avaḷ empōṟ putalvaṟ payantu neṭunakar niṉiṉṟu uṟiya eṉṉa kaṭattaḷō?
(vi-ri) kaṭal kaṇṭaṉṉa eṉpataṉālum, tāmariyiṉ kiḻaṅku, taṇṭu, ili, muki, malar eṉṟa ivaṟṟikkuk kūṟiya uvamikaḷālum marutanilattiṉ koḻumiyum nīr mikutiyum kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. neṭuṅkaṇ - neṭitāy nivantu tōṉṟum kaṇ. vayalik koṭi maṉiyiṉkaṇ vittu vaḷarkkappaṭutalāl, maṉinaku vayali eṉappaṭṭatu. uṇ kaṇṇiṉiyum māṇiḻiyiṉiyumuṭiya niṉ kātaliyeṉak kūṭṭiyurittalumām. pōlum : oppil pōli. maṟṟu, viṉimāṟṟu. eḻutu eḻil - ōviyar pārttu eḻututaṟku ētuvākiya aḻaku. viralkaḷip palamuṟi noṭittalum, eyiṟṟiṉit tirukutalum ciṉattiṉāl nikaḻntaṉa. avaḷ kippiṇippiṉic ciṟitu viṭutta aḷaviṟkē uṭaṉṟu niṉṉit tēṭit tirivāḷ eṉṟamiyiṉ, avaḷatu poṟumiyiṉmiyiyum, yām maṉiyiṉkaṇ niṉṉi yiṉṟiyē uṟikiṉṟōm eṉṟataṉāl, tamatu poṟumiyiyum talivi pulappaṭuttiṉāḷ eṉka. nelluṭi neṭunakar eṉaviruntōm paṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta nelluṭimi kūṟiya ataṉāṉē, yām putalvaṟ payantum viruntōmpiyum niṉakku irumikkum ētuvākiya maṉiyaṟattiṉ vaḻuvā toḻukukiṉṟēm eṉṟu talivi kuṟittāḷām. putalvaṟ payantu eṉṟatu, mutumiyi eḷḷip parattiyiṟ pirintāṉ eṉap pulappaṭuttapaṭiyumām. ūr muḻutum nuvalum eṉṟamiyāl, piṟar kūṟum paḻi karuti ippoḻutu īṇṭu varukiṉṟāyaṉṟi aṉpiṉāl varukiṉṟāyalli eṉṟu kūṟit tōḻi vāyil maṟuttāḷām. ceṉmē - cellum; īṟṟumiciyukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭatu.
talivikkum taṉakkum uḷḷa orumippāṭṭiṉāl tōḻi, empōl eṉat taliviyik kūṟiṉāḷ. itaṉit talivi kūṟṟākavē koḷvatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyarkkum pērāciriyarkkum karuttātal tolkāppiya uriyāl aṟiyappaṭum.
(u-ṟi) puḷimiḻ paḻaṉattu ñeṇṭu kurukañci iruñ cēṟṟaḷḷal titaliyiṉ varippa ōṭi maṇṇaḷic ceṟiyum eṉṟatu, parattiyar cērikkaṇ avar nalattiṉi nukarntirunta talivaṉ alar eḻutali añci apparattiyar ceyta kuṟi, taṉ mārpilē kiṭappat taṉ maṉikkaṇ virintu varalāyiṉaṉ eṉṟapaṭi.
(mē-ḷ) `avaṉaṟi vāṟṟa aṟiyu mākaliṉ'1 eṉṉum cūttirattu, `pukaṉṟa uḷḷamoṭu..... īrattu maruṅkiṉum' eṉṉum tuṟikkaṇ, ic ceyyuḷi utāraṇamākak kāṭṭi, maṟuppāḷpōla nayantāḷāyiṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟiṉar, nac.
`iḷivē yiḻavē..... aḻuki nāṉkē'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu eḻuteḻil citiya aḻutaṉaḷ ēṅki...... niṟkāṇiya ceṉmē' eṉpatu, taṉkaṭṭōṉṟiya iḷivaral poruḷāka avalaccuvi piṟantatu eṉṟum, `teyvam añcal'3 eṉṉum cūttirattu, `vēmpu naṉiyaṉṉa... ceṟiyum ūra' eṉpatu, talimakaṉ vāyil vēṇṭac ceṉṟāṉip piṟar kūṟum paḻikku vantāy eṉṟamiyiṉ, iḵtu uḷḷa tuvarttal (eṉum meyppāṭu) āyiṟṟu eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, pērā.
177. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.)
		tolnalam citiyac cāay alkalum
		iṉṉum vārār iṉiyevaṉ ceykeṉap
		perumpulampu uṟutal ōmpumati ciṟukaṇ
		irumpiṭit taṭakki māṉa neyaruntu
	5.	oruṅkupiṇit tiyaṉṟa veṟikoḷ aimpāl
		tēṅkamaḻ veḷimalar peymmār kāṇpiṉ
		kaḻiamal cilampiṉ vaḻitali vāṭak
		katirkataṅ kaṟṟa ēkal neṟiyiṭip
		piṅkoṭip pākal ceṅkaṉi naciik
	10.	kāṉa maññik kamañcūl māppeṭi
		ayiriyāṟṟu aṭikari vayiriṉ naraluṅ
		kāṭiṟan takaṉṟōr nīṭiṉa rāyiṉum
		vallē varuvar pōlum veṉvēl
		iliniṟam peyara ōcci māṟṟōr
	15.	malimaruḷ yāṉi maṇṭamar oḻitta
		kaḻalkāl paṇṇaṉ kāviri vaṭavayiṉ
		niḻalkayam taḻīiya neṭuṅkāl māviṉ
		taḷirēr ākam takipeṟa mukinta
		aṇaṅkuṭi vaṉamulit tāayaniṉ
	20.	1cuṇaṅkiṭi varittal toyyili niṉintē.
- 2 ceyalūr iḷampoṉ cāttaṉ koṟṟaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 6-12. tōḻi-, kāṇpu iṉ kaḻi amal cilampiṉ vaḻi talivāṭa - kāṇṭaṟku iṉiya mūṅkil niṟinta pakkamaliyi luḷḷa curapuṉṉiyiṉ ucci vāṭumpaṭi, katir katam kaṟṟa - ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir ciṉattip payiṉṟa, ē kal neṟiyiṭi - perukiya kaṟkaḷ poruntiya curaneṟiyil, piṅkoṭip pākal ceṅkaṉi nacii - paciya pākaṟkoṭiyiṉ civanta paḻattiṉi virumpi, kāṉa maññi kamam cūl mā peṭi - kāṭṭiluḷḷa mayiliṉ niṟinta karuviṉik koṇṭa kariya peṭi, ayiri yāṟṟu aṭikari vayiriṉ naralum, ayiriyāṟṟiṉatu aṭikariyiṉkaṇ ūtukompeṉa olikkum, kāṭu iṟantu akaṉṟōr nīṭiṉar āyiṉum - kāṭṭiṉit tāṇṭic ceṉṟa talivar (kuṟitta paruvaṅ kaṭantu) tāḻttu vārā
tiruppiṉum;
3-6. ciṟukaṇ iru piṭi taṭa ki māṉa - ciṟiya kaṇṇiṉi yuṭiya periya peṇyāṉiyiṉ periya kiyiṉi oppa, oruṅku piṇittu iyaṉṟa ney aruntu veḷikoḷ aimpāl - cērakkaṭṭi viṭṭa ney pūcappeṟum naṟunāṟṟamuṭiya kūntaṟkaṇṇē, tēm kamaḻ veṟimalar peymmār - tēṉ maṇakkum naṟiya malarip peytaliyum;
13-20. veṉvēl iliniṟam peyara ōcci - veṉṟi vēli ilimuṉi niṟam māṟic cenniṟamaṭiyac celutti, māṟṟōr mali maruḷ yāṉi maṇṭu amar oḻitta - pakivaruṭiya mali pōlum yāṉikaḷi mikka pōriṉkaṇ aḻitta, kaḻalkāl paṇṇaṉ- vīrakkaḻal taritta kāliṉiyuṭiya paṇṇaṉatu, kāviri vaṭavayiṉ - kāviriyiṉ vaṭapāluḷḷa, niḻal kayam taḻīiya neṭukāl māviṉ - kuḷirnta kuḷattiṉi yaṭuttuḷa neṭiya aṭi marattiṉiyuṭiya māmarattiṉ, taḷir ēr ākam takipeṟa mukinta- taḷiri yotta ākattil aḻakupeṟa arumpiya, aṇaṅku uṭi niṉ vaṉamuli tāya - varuttum iyalpuṭiya niṉ aḻakiya muliyiṟ paranta, cuṇaṅkiṭi toyyili varittal niṉintē - cuṇaṅkiṉiṭiyē toyyil eḻututaliyum niṉintu;
13. vallē varuvar - virintu vantaruḷvar ātaliṉ;
1-3. tolnalam citiyac cāay - paḻiya aḻaku citintiṭa varunti, alkalum - nāṭōṟum, iṉṉum vārār - iṉṉum vantilar, iṉi evaṉ ceyku eṉa - iṉi eṉṉa ceyvēṉ eṉṟu, perum pulampu uṟutal ōmpumati - mikka varuttam aṭitalip parikarippāyāka:
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kāṭiṟantakaṉṟōr nīṭiṉar āyiṉum, niṉatu aimpāl veṟimalar peytaliyum, niṉ vaṉamulit tāaya cuṇaṅkiṭit toyyil eḻututaliyum niṉintu vallē varuvar: ātaliṉ, nalam citiyac cāay, alkalum perum pulampu uṟutal ōmpumati.
(vi-ri) taṭakki māṉap piṇittiyaṉṟa aimpāl eṉka. arunti eṉpatu aruntu eṉat tirintateṉak koṇṭu, arunti veṟikoḷ eṉṟalumām. katam  kaṟṟa - vemmi muṟukiya eṉṟapaṭi. ē kal neṟi : ē - perukka mātali, `ē peṟṟākum'1 eṉpataṉ uriyāl aṟika; ēkal neṟi - uyarcciyiyuṭiya neṟiyeṉṟumām. `ēkal veṟpaṉ'2 eṉpatu kāṇka. peṭi vayiriṉ naraluṅ kāṭu eṉka. ayiriyāṟu tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭakkaṇṇuḷḷatōr yāṟu eṉpatu `vaṭukar perumakaṉ, pērici yerumi naṉṉāṭ ṭuḷḷati, ayiriyā ṟiṟantaṉar āyiṉum'3 eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum. pōlum : oppil pōli, malimaruḷ : maruḷ, uvamac col. kāviri vaṭavayiṉ ciṟukuṭi eṉṉum ūriluḷḷa māviṉ taḷir eṉa viritturikka. paṇṇaṉatu ūr ciṟukuṭi eṉpatu, `taṉakkeṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkuriyāḷaṉ, paṇṇaṉ ciṟukuṭip paṭappi'4 eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum.
178. kuṟiñci
(tōḻi varivu malintu colliyatu.)
		vayirattu aṉṉa viyēntu maruppiṉ
		vetirvēr aṉṉa parūumayirp paṉṟi
		paṟikkaṇ aṉṉa niṟiccuṉi paruki
		nīlattu aṉṉa akalilic cēmpiṉ
	5.	piṇṭam aṉṉa koḻuṅkiḻaṅku māntip
		piṭimaṭin taṉṉa kaṉmici ūḻiḻipu
		yāṟucērn taṉṉa ūṟunīrp paṭāarp
		pimputal naḷiciṉik kurukirun taṉṉa
		vaṇpiṇi aviḻnta veṇkū tāḷattu
	10.	alaṅkukuli yalari tīṇṭit tātukap
		poṉṉuri kaṭṭaḷi kaṭuppak kāṇvarak
		kiḷiamal ciṟutiṉi viḷikural mēyntu
		kaṇiṉitu paṭukkum naṉmali nāṭaṉoṭu
		uṇarntaṉi puṇarnta nīyum niṉtōḷ
	15.	paṇikkaviṉ aḻiyātu tuṇippuṇarn teṉṟum
		tavalil ulakattu uṟiiyarō tōḻi
		elliyum iravum eṉṉātu kalleṉak
		koṇṭal vāṉmaḻi poḻinta vikaṟit
		taṇpaṉi aṟciram tamiyōrk kariteṉak
	20.	kaṉaviṉum 1pirivaṟi yalaṉē ataṉtali
		muṉtāṉ kaṇṭa ñāṉṟiṉum
		piṉperitu aḷikkuntaṉ paṇpi ṉāṉē.
- paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 16. tōḻi-,
1-13. vayirattu aṉṉa vi ēntu maruppiṉ - vayiratti otta kūriya mēṉōkkiya kōṭṭiṉiyum, vetirvēr aṉṉa parūu mayirppaṉṟi - mūṅkil vēriṉi otta parutta mayiriṉiyumuṭiya paṉṟiyāṉatu, paṟikkaṇ aṉṉa niṟic cuṉi paruki - paṟiyiṉ kaṇṇiṉi otta niṟinta caṉi nīrik kuṭittu, nīlattu aṉṉa akal ilic cēmpiṉ - nīlamaṇiyi yotta niṟattiṉiyuṭiya akaṉṟa iliyiṉiyuṭiya cēmpiṉ, piṇṭam aṉṉa koḻu kiḻaṅku mānti - piṇṭittu vittāṟpōṉṟa vaḷaviya kiḻaṅkiṉi niṟiyat tiṉṟu, piṭi maṭintaṉṉa kalmici - peṇyāṉi kiṭan
tālotta kalliṉmītu, ūḻ iḻipu - varuttamiṉṟi muṟiyāka iṟaṅki
vantu, yāṟu cērntaṉṉa ūṟunīr paṭā ar - yāṟṟiṉi aṭuttiruppatu pōṉṟa nīr ūṟum iṭattiluḷḷa ciṟutūṟākiya, pim putal naḷiciṉi kuruku iruntaṉṉa - paciya putariluḷḷa ceṟinta kiḷikaḷil veṇṇāriyiruntālotta, vaṇpiṇi aviḻnta veṇ
kūtāḷattu alaṅku kuli alari tīṇṭi - veḷḷiya kūtaḷañ ceṭiyiṉ aciyum kottiluḷḷa vaḷamporuntiya muki virinta malariṉip porunti, tātu uka - ataṉ pūmpoṭi mēlē utirtalāl, poṉ uri kaṭṭaḷi kaṭuppak kāṇvara - poṉ urittu māṟṟu aṟiyum kaṭṭaḷik kalliṉi oppa aḻakupeṟat tōṉṟi, kiḷi amal ciṟutiṉi viḷikural mēyntu - kiḷittal mikka ciṟiya tiṉiyiṉ viḷinta katiriṉi mēyntu, kaṇ iṉitu paṭukkum nalmali nāṭaṉoṭu - iṉitu kaṇṇuṟaṅkum iṭamākiya nalla mali nāṭṭiyuṭiyāṉoṭu;
14-16. uṇarntaṉi puṇarnta nīyum - ūṭip piṉ avvūṭali nīṅkip puṇarntiṭum nīyum, niṉ tōḷ paṇikkaviṉ aḻiyātu - niṉatu tōḷiṉ mūṅkilpōlum aḻaku oḻiyātu, eṉṟum tuṇi puṇarntu - niṉ talivaṉuṭaṉ eṉṟum cērntu vāḻntu piṉ, taval il ulakattu uṟiiyar - eṉṟuṅ keṭāta maṟumi yulakattut taṅkivāḻ vīrāka;
17-20. elliyum iravum eṉṉātu - pakalum iravum eṉṟillātu, kal eṉa koṇṭal vāṉ maḻi poḻinta vikaṟi - kalleṉum oliyuṭaṉ mēkam periya maḻiyic corinta viṭiyaṟ kālattē, taṇpaṉi aṟciram - kuḷirnta paṇiyiyuṭiya aṟcirak kālam, tamiyōrkku aritu eṉa - taṉittiruppōrkkut tāṅkaṟkari tākumeṉavuṇarntu, kaṉaviṉum pirivu aṟiyalaṉ - kaṉaviṉ kaṇṇum piritali aṟiyāṉ;
20-22. ataṉ tali - ataṉmēlum, taṉ paṇpiṉāṉ - taṉatu naṟkuṇattiṉāl, muṉ tāṉkaṇṭa ñāṉṟiṉum, mutaṉmutal tāṉ kaṇṭa nāḷiṉum, piṉ peritu aḷikkum - piṉ enta nāḷiṉum mikavum aruḷ ceyvāṉāvaṉ.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! paṉṟi kaṇ iṉitu paṭukkum naṉmali nāṭaṉoṭu, eṉṟum puṇarntu uṟiiyarō; aṟciram tamiyōrkku ariteṉak kaṉaviṉum pirivu aṟiyalaṉ; ataṉtali taṉ paṇpiṉālē, muṉ kaṇṭa ñāṉṟiṉum piṉ peritu aḷikkum.
(vi-ri) vayirattaṉṉa maruppu, vi maruppu, ēntu maruppu, eṉat taṉittaṉi iyiyum. kuḻintu vaṭṭamāyiruttal paṟṟic cuṉikkup paṟikkaṇ uvami kūṟappaṭṭatu. paṭāar - ciṟu tūṟu: paṭukar eṉṟumām. paṭukar - nīrnili. karuniṟamuṭiya paṉṟiyiṉ uṭampil kūtaḷattiṉ poṉṉiṟap pūmpoṭi paṭintiruttal, poṉṉiṉ māṟṟaṟiyum kariya kaṭṭaḷik kalliṟ poṉ urittāṟ
pōlum eṉa uvamamum poruḷum ottu viḷaṅkutal kāṇka. talivaṉ māṭṭut tavaṟilliyāyiṉum avaṉpāl iruppatāka ēṟaṭṭuk koṇṭu, kaḷavuk kālattum ciṟupāṉmi ūṭutalum, piṉṉar avvūṭal nīṅkik kūṭutalum talivi māṭṭuṇmiyiṉ, `uṇarntaṉi puṇarnta' eṉṟāḷ. uṇartal - ūṭal nīṅkutal. iṉi, iyaṟkip puṇarcci mutaliya kūṭṭaṅkaḷāl, talivaṉuṭiya paṇpiṉi iṉituṇarnta nīyum eṉṟurittalumām. nīyum eṉṟa ummiyāl talivaṉum eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. taval il ulakam - keṭutalillāta iṉpattiyuṭiya maṟumiyulakam. ivvulakilē taṇavāk kātaluṭaṉ kūṭi illaṟam iṉitu naṭātti, naṉmakap peṟṟuvāḻntōr, maṟumiyilum piriviṉṟi iṉpam tuyppar eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉaḷātaliṉ, eṉṟum tavalil ulakattu uṟiiyar eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷeṉka; `immi māṟi maṟumiyāyiṉum, nīyā kiyareṉ kaṇavaṉi, yāṉā kiyarniṉ ṉeñcu nērpavaḷē'1 `immi yulakat ticiyoṭum viḷaṅki, maṟumi yulakamum maṟuviṉ ṟeytupa, ceṟunarum viḻiyum ceyirtīr kāṭcic, ciṟuvarp payanta cemmalōreṉa'2 eṉpaṉa kāṇka. ivvāṟṟāl, talivaṉ variya varutali yuṇarntu tōḻi varivumalintu kūṟiṉāḷ eṉpatu peṟṟām. aṅṅaṉam talivaṉ varital orutali eṉa vaṟpuṟuttu taṟkuk kaṉaviṉum pirivu aṟiyalaṉ eṉṟum, muṉ tāṉ kaṇṭa ñāṉṟiṉum piṉ peritaḷikkum  eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. iyaṟkip puṇarcci toṭaṅki orukālik korukāl talivi māṭṭu aṉpu perukit taliyaḷi ceytu varutalik kaṇṭuḷāḷ ākaliṉ, taṉ paṇpiṉāṉē aḷikkum eṉṟaṉaḷ.
(u-ṟi) paṉṟi cuṉi parukik kiḻaṅku māntit tiṉi viḷi kural mēyntu iṉitu kaṇpaṭukkum nāṭaṉ eṉṟatu, talivaṉ tāṉ karutiya poruḷelām peṟṟut taliviyi maṇantu iṉpan tuyttu iṉitu vāḻvāṉ eṉṟapaṭiyām.
179. pāli
(pirivuṇarttiya talimakaṟkut tōḻi celavu aḻuṅkac colliyatu.)
		viṇṭōy cimiya viṟalvarik kavāaṉ
		veṇtēr ōṭuṅ kaṭaṅkāy maruṅkil
		tuṉiyeri paranta tuṉṉarum viyaṉkāṭṭuc
		ciṟukaṇ yāṉi neṭuṅki nīṭṭi
	5.	3vāṉvāy tiṟantum vaṇpuṉal peṟāatu
		kāṉpulantu kaḻiyuṅ kaṇṇakaṉ parappiṉ
		viṭuvāyc ceṅkaṇik koṭuvil āṭavar
		nalnili poṟitta kalnili atara
		4arampukoḷ pūcal kaḷiyunark kāṇāc
	10.	curañcela virumpiṉi rāyiṉ iṉṉaki
		murunteṉat tiraṇṭa muṉeyiṟṟut tuvarvāyk
		kuvaḷi nāḷmalar puriyum uṇkaṇim
		matiyēr 5vāḷnutal pulampap
		patipeyarntu uṟital ollumō numakkē.
- koṭimaṅkalattu vātuḷi naṟcēntaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-10. taliva-, viṇtōy cimiya viṟal varik kavāṉ - vāṉi aḷāviya ucciyiṉiyuṭiya perumi taṅkiya maliyiṉatu pakkamalikkaṇṇē, veṇtēr ōṭum kāy kaṭa maruṅkil - pēyt tēr ōṭāniṟkum kāynta kaṟkāṭṭiṉ pakkattē, tuṉi eri paranta tuṉ arum viyaṉkāṭṭuc ciṟukaṇ yāṉi - virinta neruppup paraviya kiṭṭutaṟkariya periya kāṭṭiluḷḷa ciṟiya kaṇṇiṉi yuṭiya yāṉi, neṭu ki nīṭṭi vāṉ vāy tiṟantum - ap pēyt tēriṉpāl taṉ neṭiya kiyiṉi nīṭṭiyum periya vāyiṉit tiṟantum, vaṇpuṉal peṟāatu kāṉ pulantu kaḻiyum kaṇ akaṉ parappiṉ - vaḷaviya nīriṉip peṟāmal kāṭṭi veṟuttuk kaḻintupōm iṭam akaṉṟa pālinilattiṉ kaṇ, viṭuvāyc ceṅkaṇik koṭuvil āṭavar - viṭutal vāynta civanta ampiṉi yuṭiya vaḷinta villikkoṇṭa maṟavaratu, nalnili poṟitta kalnili atara - nalla veṟṟi niliyi eḻutiya naṭukaṟkaḷ nili koṇṭa vaḻikaḷiyuṭiyavāya, arampu koḷ pūcal kaḷiyunar kāṇāc curamcela virumpiṉir āyiṉ - kuṟumparkaḷ ceyyum pūcali nīkkunarik kāṇāta curaneṟiyik kaṭantu cella virumpiṉīr āyiṉ;
10-14. iṉ naki muruntu eṉat tiraṇṭa muḷ eyiṟṟut tuvar vāy - iṉiya nakiyiṉiyum mayiliṟakiṉ aṭiyeṉat tiraṇṭa muṭpōlum kūriya paṟkaḷiyum civanta vāyiṉiyum, kuvaḷi nāḷ malar puriyum uṇkaṇ - kuvaḷiyiṉ putiya malari yokkum miyuṇṭa kaṇṇiṉiyum uṭiya, i mati ēr vāḷ nutal pulampa - inta matiyiṉi yotta oḷiporuntiya neṟṟiyiṉi yuṭiyāḷ varunta, pati peyarntu uṟital numakku ollumō - ippatiyi nīṅkip pōyt taṅkutal umakkup poruntuvatāmō?
(muṭipu) taliva! curamcela virumpiṉirāyiṉ, vāḷnutal pulampap patipeyarntu uṟital numakku ollumō!
veṇtēr ōṭum viyaṉ kāṭṭil, yāṉi, kin nīṭṭiyum vāy tiṟantum puṉal peṟātu pulantu kaḻivatum koṭuvil lāṭavar naṭukal nilipeṟṟa atarkaḷiyuṭiyatum arampu koḷ pūcal kaḷiyunar illātatum ākiya curam eṉka.
(vi-ri) koṭuvil āṭavar, karanti maṟavar eṉka. arampu - kuṟumpar, kāṭṭut talivar. `iṉṉaki...... vāṇutal pulampa' eṉṟatu, taliviyiṉ iṉiya nakiyum kūriya pallum tuvar vāyum uṇkaṇṇum vāṇutalum āya ivi, nummuṉ tōṉṟi nummi aḻuṅ
kuvippaṉavākaliṉ, nīrpirintuṟital ollāteṉṟāḷ eṉṟapaṭi.
180. neytal
(irantu piṉṉiṉṟa talimakaṟkuk kuṟinērnta tōḻi tali makaḷik 
kuṟinayappak kūṟiyatu. talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat 
tōḻikkuc colliyatū u  mām.)
		nakinaṉi yuṭittāl tōḻi takimikak
		kōti āyamoṭu kuvavumaṇal ēṟi
		vītati kāṉal vaṇṭal ayarak
		kataḻparit tiṇtēr kaṭii vantu
	5.	taṇkayattu amaṉṟa oṇpūṅ kuvaḷi
		arumpalit tiyaṟṟiya curumpār kaṇṇi
		piṉṉuppuṟan tāḻak koṉṉē cūṭṭi
		nalvaral iḷamuli nōkki neṭituniṉintu
		nillātu peyarntaṉaṉ oruvaṉ ataṟkē
	10.	pulavunā ṟiruṅkaḻi tuḻiip palavuṭaṉ
		puḷiṟi koṇṭa muḷḷuṭi neṭuntōṭṭut
		tāḻi maṇantu ñāḻaloṭu keḻīip
		paṭappi niṉṟa muṭattāṭ puṉṉip
		poṉṉēr nuṇtātu nōkki
	15.	eṉṉum nōkkumiv vaḻuṅkal ūrē.
- 1karuvūrk kaṇṇampāḷaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-9. tōḻi-, taki mika kōti āyamoṭu kuvavu maṇal ēṟi - aḻaku mika māliyiyuṭiya āyattāruṭaṉ (nāṉ) tiraṇṭa maṇal mēṭṭil ēṟi, vī tati kāṉal vaṇṭal ayara - malar neruṅkiya cōliyil viḷiyāṭal ceyyāniṟka, oruvaṉ - oru talivaṉ, kataḻpari tiṇtēr kaṭii vantu - virinta kutiriyiyuṭiya valiya tēriṉic celutti vantu, taṇ kayattu amaṉṟa oḷpū kuvaḷi - taṇṇiya kuḷattē niṟinta oḷḷiya pūkkaḷiyuṭiya kuvaḷiyiṉ, arumpu alittu iyaṟṟiya curumpu ār kaṇṇi - arumpiṉi virittuk kaṭṭiya vaṇṭukaḷ moykkum kaṇṇiyiṉi, piṉṉuppuṟam tāḻak koṉṉē cūṭṭi - eṉatu piṉṉalik koṇṭa mutukiṉpāl tāḻntiṭa yāṉ vēṇṭātē taliyiṟ cūṭṭi, nalvaral iḷamuli nōkki - nalla vaḷarcciyiyuṭiya iḷiya muliyip pārttu, neṭitu niṉintu nillātu peyarntaṉaṉ - ētō nīḷa niṉintu piṉ tāḻkkātu pōyiṉaṉ;
9-15. ataṟkē - avvaḷaviṟkē, i aḻuṅkal ūr - inta āravāramikka ūr, pulavu nāṟu irukaḻi tuṟii pala uṭaṉ puḷ iṟi koṇṭa - pulāl vīcum periya kaḻiyiṉit tuḻāvip palavakip puṭkaḷum oruṅkē taṅkiyirukkum, muḷ uṭi neṭu tōṭṭut tāḻi maṇantu - muḷḷuṭiya niṇṭa itaḻiyuṭiya tāḻiyic cārntu, ñāḻaloṭu keḻīi - pulinakak koṉṟi yoṭu porunti, paṭappi niṉṟa muṭattāḷ puṉṉip poṉ nēr nuṇtātu nōkki - tōṭṭattil niṟkum vaḷinta tāḷiṉi yuṭiya puṉṉiyatu poṉṉi yotta nuṇṇiya tātiṉip pārttu, eṉṉum nōkkum - eṉṉiyum nōkkā niṟkum;
1. naki naṉi uṭittu - itu mikavum nakiyiṉit taruva toṉṟākum.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! nāṉ āyamoṭu vaṇṭal ayara, oruvaṉ tēr kaṭii vantu, kaṇṇiyiṉip piṉṉuppuṟam tāḻak koṉṉē cūṭṭi iḷamuli nōkki, neṭitu niṉintu peyarntaṉaṉ: ataṟkē inta aḻuṅkalūr puṉṉit tātu nōkki eṉṉum nōkkum; itu naki naṉiyuṭittu.
(vi-ri) yāṉ virumpātirukkac ceytē cūṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpāḷ, `koṉṉē cūṭṭi' yeṉṟāḷ. piṉ avaṉōṭu taṉakku yātoru toṭarpum iṉṟeṉpāḷ, nillātu peyarntaṉaṉ eṉṟāḷ. ivvūr puṉṉiyiṉ tātu nōkki eṉṉiyum nōkkum eṉṟatu, eṉ mēṉi puṉṉiyiṉ tātu pōlum pacali paṭarnta teṉak karutukiṉṟatu eṉṟapaṭi; itu kuṟippu nuṭpamām. tōḻiyāṉavaḷ talivi kuṟi nayappak karuti, iṅṅaṉam paṭittu moḻiyāl kūṟavē, ik kāṉaliṉkaṇ iṅṅaṉam vantu celvāṉ talivaṉē yāvāṉ eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉaḷāyk kuṟi nērvāḷām eṉka.  iraṇṭāvatu tuṟiyiṉ karuttu taliviyāṉavaḷ talivaṉ variyātu pirintiruttalāl, taṉ mēṉi pacali pūttaliyum ataṉāl ūriṉkaṇ alar eḻutaliyum talimakaṉ kēṭpat tōḻikkuc colliṉāḷām eṉpatu.
181. pāli
(iṭiccurattu oḻiyak karutiya neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		tuṉṉaruṅ kāṉamun tuṇital āṟṟāy
		piṉniṉṟu peyarac cūḻntaṉi yāyiṉ
		eṉnili yurimō neñcē oṉṉār
		ōmparaṇ kaṭanta vīṅkuperun tāṉi
	5.	aṭupōr miñili ceruviṟku uṭii
		murukuṟaḻ muṉpoṭu porutukaḷañ civappa
		āay 1eyiṉaṉ vīḻnteṉa ñāyiṟṟu
		oṇkatir uruppam putiya ōrāṅku
		vampap puḷḷiṉ kampalip peruntōṭu
	10.	vicumpiṭi tūra āṭi mocintuṭaṉ
		pūviri akaṉtuṟik kaṉivicik kaṭunīrk
		kāvirip pēriyāṟṟi 2ayirkoṇṭu īṇṭi
		ekkar iṭṭa kuppi veṇmaṇal
		vippiṉ yāṇar vaḷaṅkeḻu vēntar
	15.	ñālam nāṟum nalaṅkeḻu nallici
		nāṉmaṟi mutunūl mukkaṇ celvaṉ
		āla muṟṟam kaviṉpeṟat 3tiiya
		poyki cūḻnta 4poḻiṉmaṉi makaḷir
		kicey pāvit tuṟikkaṇ iṟukkum
	20.	makara neṟṟi vāṉtōy puricic
		cikaran tōṉṟāc cēṇuyar nallil
		5pukāar naṉṉāṭ ṭatuvē pakāar
		paṇṭam nāṟum 6vaṇṭaṭar aimpāl
		paṇittakit taṭiiya kāṇpiṉ meṉtōḷ
	25.	aṇaṅkucāl arivi irunta
		maṇaṅkamaḻ maṟukiṉ maṇalperuṅ kuṉṟē.
- paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 23. neñcē-,
22-26. pakāar paṇṭam nāṟum vaṇṭu aṭar aimpāl - viṟpāratu naṟumaṇap paṇṭaṅkaḷ nāṟukiṉṟa vaṇṭukaḷ moykkum aimpakutiyāya kūntaliṉiyum, paṉittaki taṭiiya kāṇpu iṉmel tōḷ - mūṅkiliṉ takutiyiyuṭiyatāy vaḷinta kāṇṭaṟku iṉiya melliya tōḷiṉiyumuṭiya, aṇaṅkucāl arivi irunta - aḻaku mikka nam taliviyirunta, maṇam kamaḻ maṟukiṉ maṇal perum kuṉṟu - maṇam nāṟum terukkaḷiyuṭiya maṇaliyuṭiya periya kuṉṟam;
3-7. āay eyiṉaṉ - āy eyiṉaṉ eṉpāṉ, muruku uṟaḻ muṉpoṭu - murukaṉi otta valimiyoṭu niṉṟu, oṉṉār ōmpu araṇ kaṭanta vīṅku perum tāṉi - pakivar pātukākkum araṇkaḷi veṉṟu kaṭanta mikka periya cēṉikaḷiyuṭiya, aṭupōr miñili ceruviṟku - pōr aṭutalvalla miñili eṉpāṉ ceruviṉ kaṇ, kaḷam civappap porutu - kurutiyāl kaḷam civappuṟap porutu piṉ, uṭii vīḻnteṉa - tōṟṟu vīḻntaṉaṉāka;
7-10. ñāyiṟṟu oḷkatir uruppam putiya - ñāyiṟṟiṉ oḷḷiya katirkaḷiṉ veppam (avaṉ uṭaliṟpaṭātu) maṟiya, orāṅku - oru peṟṟiyē, vampap puḷḷiṉ kampalip peruntōṭu - putiya puṭkaḷiṉ oli poruntiya periya kūṭṭam, vicumpiṭi tūra āṭi - vicumpiṭam maṟiya vaṭṭamiṭṭup piṉpu, uṭaṉ mocintu - oruṅkukūṭi;
11-19. pūviri akal tuṟi - pūkkaḷ virinta akaṉṟa tuṟiyiṉi yuṭiya, kāvirip pēr yāṟṟu - kāviriyāya periya āṟṟiṉ, kaṉi vicik kaṭunīr - mikka viciyuṭaṉ kaṭukivarum nīrāṉatu, ayir koṇṭu īṇṭi - nuṇmaṇalik koṇṭu tiraḷumpaṭi, ekkar iṭṭa kuppi veṇmaṇal - mēṭākkiya veḷḷiya maṇaṟ kuviyaliyum, yāṇar vippiṉ - putu varuvāyiyumuṭiya ūrkaḷi yuṭiya, vaḷam keḻu vēntar - celvam mikka cōḻavēntarāṟ purakkap paṭum, ñālam nāṟum nalamkeḻu nalici - ulakamellām paravum naṉmi poruntiya naṟpukaḻiyuṭiya, nālmaṟi mutunūl mukkaṇ celvaṉ - nāṉku vētaṅkaḷāya paḻiya nūli aruḷiya mukkaṇṇi yuṭiya paramaṉatu, ālamuṟṟam - ālamuṟṟam eṉṉumiṭattu, kaviṉpeṟa tiiya - aḻakupeṟa iyaṟṟap peṟṟa, poyki cūḻnta poḻil - poykiyic cūḻntuḷa poḻiliṉkaṇṇē, maṉimakaḷir - ciṟṟiliḻittu viḷiyāṭum ciṟumikaḷatu, kicey pāvit tuṟikkaṇ - aḻakuṟac ceyyappeṟṟa pāvikaḷi yuṭiya tuṟiyiṉ kaṇṇē, iṟukkum - vantu taṅkuvatum;
20-22. makara neṟṟi vāṉtōy purici - makarak koṭiyiṉi ucciyiṟ koṇṭa vāṉittōyum matiliyum, cikaram tōṉṟāc cēṇ uyar nalil - muṭi aṟiyappaṭātavāṟu cēṇiṉkaṇ uyarnta nalla māṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiyatum ākiya, pukāar nal nāṭṭatuvē - kāvirip pūm paṭṭiṉattiyuṭiya nalla cōḻanāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇatākum;
1-3. tuṉ arum kāṉamum tuṇital āṟṟāy - cellutaṟku ariya kāṭṭik kaṭakkavum tuṇivuṟal ceyyāyāy, piṉ niṉṟa peyarac cūḻntaṉi āyiṉ - piṉṉē niṉṟu peyarntu mīṇṭiṭak karutiṉiyāyiṉ, eṉnili urimō - eṉatu inniliyiṉi āṇṭuc ceṉṟu avaṭku urippāyāka.
(muṭipu) neñcē! arivi irunta maṇaṟperuṅ kuṉṟu pukāar naṉṉāṭṭatu; nī kāṉam tuṇital āṟṟāy; piṉ niṉṟu peyarac  cūḻntaṉiyāyiṉ, eṉnili āṇṭuc ceṉṟu, avaṭku urippāyāka.
(vi-ri) kāṉamum - kāṭṭiṉik kaṭakkavum; ummi eccap poruṭṭu; aci niliyumām. oṉṉār, ōmparaṇ kaṭanta vīṅku peruntāṉi eṉpataṉāl miñiliyiṉ tāṉip perukkamum, murukuṟaḻ muṉpoṭu porutu eṉpataṉāl eyiṉaṉatu pōr valiyum peṟappaṭṭaṉa. kaḷañ civappap porutu eṉṟamiyāl, pakivar tāṉiyiyellām koṉṟu piṉ vīḻntami peṟṟām. kaḷañ civappa vīḻntaṉaṉ eṉak koṇṭu, pakivaṉatu vēlāl tākkuṇṭu vīḻntāṉ eṉṟurittalumām. eyiṉaṉ puḷḷiṉ pātukāvalaṉākaliṉ, ivaṉ vīḻnta pōḻtu paṟavikaḷellām ivaṉmēl ñāyiṟṟiṉ katirpaṭātu maṟittaṉa eṉpatu, `puḷḷiṟku ēma mākiya perumpeyar, veḷḷattāṉi atikaṟ (eyiṉaṟ) koṉṟu'1 eṉpataṉālum, `veḷiyaṉ vēṇmāṉ āay eyiṉaṉ.... miñiliyoṭu, naṇpakal uṟṟa ceruvil puṇkūrn, toḷvāḷ mayaṅkamar vīḻnteṉap, puḷḷoruṅku aṅkaṇ vicumpiṉ viḷaṅku ñāyiṟu, oṇkatir teṟāmic ciṟakaril kōli, niḻal ceytu uḻaṟal'2 eṉpataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum. `akavunar vēṇṭiṉ veṇ kōṭṭu, aṇṇal yāṉi īyum vaṇmakiḻ, veḷiyaṉ vēṇmāṉ āay eyiṉaṉ' eṉṟu ivaṉ kūṟap peṟutaliṉ īṇṭu `vaṇmi eyiṉaṉ' eṉṟu kāṇappaṭum pāṭamum porutta muṭittē.
eyiṉaṉ vīḻnteṉa, uruppam putiya, puḷḷiṉ peruntōṭu āṭi, mocintu, tuṟikkaṇ iṟukkum pukār naṉṉāṭu eṉka. mukkaṭ celvaṉatu ālamuṟṟattiṭattē tiiya poyki cūḻnta poḻilil makaḷir kicey pāvikaḷiyuṭiya tuṟiyeṉṟu iyiyum. vaḷaṅkeḻu vēntarāl purakkappaṭum pukār naṉṉāṭu eṉka; ālamuṟṟam eṉalumām. īṇṭi, īṇṭaveṉat tirikka. iṟivaṉ pukaḻē meyyāya pukaḻ ākalāṉum, atuvē ulakeṅkum parattaṟkuriyatākalāṉum `ñālam nāṟum nalaṅkeḻu nallici mukkaṭ celvaṉ' eṉṟār. nāṉmaṟi mukkaṇ celvaṉatu vāymoḻiyām eṉpatu,  ‘naṉṟāynta nīṇimir caṭi, mutu mutalvaṉ vāypōkā, toṉṟu purinta īriraṇṭiṉ, āṟuṇarnta oru mutunūl'3 eṉṟa
taṉāl aṟiyappaṭum. ālamuṟṟam - civapirāṉ eḻuntaruḷiyuḷḷa
tōr iṭamākum. iṟukkum pukār eṉavum puriciyiṉiyum nal illiṉiyumuṭiya pukār eṉavum taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. pakarvār eṉpatu pakāar eṉat tirintuniṉṟatu. paṇṭam - pūvum cāntum mutalāyiṉa. aṇaṅku cāl arivi - varuttutaliyuṭiya arivi eṉṟātal, teyvam pōlum arivi eṉṟātal kūṟutalumām. kuṉṟam naṉṉāṭṭatu; cūḻntaṉiyāyiṉ āṇṭuc ceṉṟu eṉ nili uriyeṉa viritturikka.
182. kuṟiñci
(tōḻi irā varuvāṉip pakal vā eṉṟatu.)
		pūṅkaṇ vēṅkip poṉṉiṇar 1milintu
		vāṅkami nōṉcili eruttat tirīit
		tīmpaḻap palaviṉ cuḷiviḷi tēṟal
		vīḷi ampiṉ iḷiyaroṭu mānti 
	5.	ōṭṭiyal piḻiyā vayanāy piṟpaṭa
		vēṭṭam pōkiya kuṟavaṉ kāṭṭa
		kuḷavit taṇputal kurutiyoṭu tuyalvara
		muḷavumāt toliccum kuṉṟa nāṭa
		araveṟi yurumō ṭoṉṟik kālvīḻttu
	10.	uravumaḻi poḻinta pāṉāṭ kaṅkul
		taṉiyi vanta vāṟuniṉin talkalum
		paṉiyoṭu kaluḻumivaḷ kaṇṇē ataṉāl
		kaṭumpakal varutal vēṇṭum teyya
		atirkural mutukali kaṟimuṟi muṉii
	15.	uyarcimi neṭuṅkōṭ ṭukaḷa ukka
		kamaḻitaḻ alari tāay vēlaṉ
		veṟiyayar viyaṉkaḷam kaṭukkum
		peruvari naṇṇiya cāra lāṉē.			- kapilar.
(co-ḷ) 1-8. pū kaṇ vēṅki poṉ iṇar milintu - aḻakiya iṭattiṉiyuṭiya vēṅki marattiṉ poṉpōlum pūṅ kottukkaḷic cūṭi, vāṅku ami nōṉcili eruttattu irīi - vaḷinta mūṅkilālāya valiya villit tōḷil irutti, tīm paḻap palaviṉ cuḷiviḷi tēṟal - iṉiya palāppaḻattiṉ cuḷiyiṉiṉṟum viḷinta tēṉi, vīḷi ampiṉ iḷiyaroṭu mānti - cīḻkki yoliyuṭaṉ cellum ampiṉiyuṭiya ēvaliḷiyaruṭaṉ niṟiyak kuṭittu, ōṭṭu iyal piḻiyā vayanāy piṟpaṭa - vilaṅkukaḷit turattip paṟṟum iyalpu tapputalillāta valiya nāy piṉṉēvara, vēṭṭam pōkiya kuṟavaṉ - vēṭṭikkuc ceṉṟa kuṟavaṉ, kāṭṭa kuḷavit taṇputal kurutiyoṭu tuyalvara - kāṭṭiluḷḷa mallikiyāya taṇṇiya putar utirattoṭu acintiṭa, muḻavu mā toliccum kuṉṟa nāṭa - muḷḷam paṉṟiyik koṉṟu vīḻttum kuṉṟukaḷiyuṭiya nāṭṭiṉ talivaṉē!
9-12. uravu maḻi aravu eṟi urumōṭu oṉṟi kāl vīḻttu - valiya mēkam araviṉi eṟintu kollum iṭiyuṭaṉ kūṭik kāliṟakkaṅ koṇṭu, poḻinta pāl nāḷ kaṅkul - nīriṉic corinta ari nāḷākiya iravil, taṉiyi vanta āṟu niṉintu - nī taṉiyiyāki vanta vaḻiyētattiṉi niṉintu - alkalum paṉiyoṭu kaluḻum ivaḷ kaṇṇē - ivaḷ kaṇ eppoḻutum nīr koṇṭu aḻāniṟkum;
12-18. ataṉāl-, atir kural mutukali kaṟimuṟi muṉii - atirum kuraliṉiyuṭiya mutiya mucukkali miḷakiṉ taḷiri uṇṭu veṟuttu, uyar cimi neṭu kōṭṭu ukaḷa - uyarnta ucciyiṉiyuṭiya nīṇṭa cikaraṅkaḷilē tāvutalāl, ukka - utirnta, kamaḻ itaḻ alari tāay - maṇam nāṟum itaḻiṉiyuṭiya pūkkaḷ parantu, vēlaṉ veṟi ayar viyaṉ kaḷam kaṭukkum - vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭum periya kaḷattiṉi okkum; peruvari naṇṇiya cāralāṉ - periya maliyi aṭuttuḷḷa cāraṟ kaṇṇē, kaṭum pakal varutal vēṇṭum - kaṭiya pakaṟ kaṇṇē nī varutal vēṇṭum.
(muṭipu) nāṭa! pāṉāṭ kaṅkul taṉiyi vantavāṟu niṉintu, ivaḷkaṇ alkalum paṉiyoṭu kaluḻum; ataṉāl peruvari naṇṇiya cāraṟkaṇṇē nī kaṭumpakal varutal vēṇṭum.
(vi-ri) putariṭattē patuṅkiyirunta muḷavumāvik kollu taliṉ putal kurutiyoṭu tuyal varutalāyiṟṟu. iṭiyoli kēṭṭu aravu uṭkumeṉṉum vaḻakkuppaṟṟi araveṟiyurum eṉappaṭṭatu. urumiṉ kaṭumi kūṟiya paṭiyumām. urumōṭoṉṟi eṉṟataṉāl āṟṟiṉatu ētam kuṟittāḷ. tuṇiyoṭum varutaṟkariya ētap pāṭuṭiya vaḻiyil naḷḷiruḷil taṉiyiyāyt taṉporuṭṭu varukiṉṟāy eṉpatuṇarntu, ivaḷ āṟṟāmi perituṭiyaḷā kiṉṟāḷ eṉpāḷ, `taṉiyi vanta āṟu niṉin talkalum paṉiyoṭu kaluḻu mivaḷ kaṇṇē' eṉṟāḷ. kaṭum pakal - naṇpakal eṉṟapaṭi. teyya: - aci.
(u-ṟi) kuṟavaṉ muḷavumāvik kolla ataṟku iṭamākiya putal kurutiyoṭu acintu tōṉṟiyateṉpatu, talivaṉ taliviyoṭu kaḷavoḻukkattil oḻuka, avvoḻukkam taliviyiṉ mēṉi vēṟupāṭṭāl tāy mutaliyōrukkup pulaṉāyiṟṟu eṉṟavāṟām.
183. pāli
(talimakaṉ kuṟitta paruva varavu kaṇṭu talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		kuvaḷi uṇkaṇ kaluḻavun tiruntiḻit
		titali alkul avvari vāṭavum
		attamār aḻuvam nattuṟan taruḷār
		ceṉṟucē ṇiṭiyar āyiṉum naṉṟum
	5.	nīṭalar eṉṟi tōḻi pāṭāṉṟu
		paṉittuṟip peruṅkaṭal 1iṟantunīr parukik
		kuvavuttiri aruntu koḷḷiya kuṭakkērpu
		vayavuppiṭi yiṉattiṉ vayiṉvayiṉ tōṉṟi
		iruṅkiḷik koṇmū oruṅkuṭaṉ tuvaṉṟik
	10.	kāli vantaṉṟāl kārē mālik
		kuḷirkoḷ piṭaviṉ kūrmuka alari
		vaṇṭuvāy tiṟakkum taṇṭā nāṟṟam
		kūtir aṟcirattu ūti tūṟṟap
		paṉiyalik kalaṅkiya neñcamoṭu
	15.	varuntuvam allamō pirinticiṉōr tiṟattē.
- karuvūrk kaliṅkattār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. tōḻi-, kuvaḷi uṇkaṇ kaluḻavum - eṉatu kuvaḷi malar pōṉṟa miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷ aḻavum, tiruntu iḻi titali alkul avvari vāṭavum - tiruntiya aṇiyiyum tēmaliyu muṭiya alkuliṉ aḻakiya vari vāṭṭamuṟavum, aruḷār nattuṟantu - aruḷ ceyyārāy nammip pirintu, attam ār aḻuvam ceṉṟu - aruneṟikaḷiyuṭiya paranta pāliyik kaṭantu ceṉṟu, cēṇiṭiyar āyiṉum - cēymikkaṇ uḷḷār āyiṉum, naṉṟum nīṭalar eṉṟi - mikavum tāḻkkātu varuvar eṉkiṉṟāy;
5-10. iruṅ kiḷik koṇmū - periya kūṭṭamāya mēkaṅkaḷ, kuvavu tiri - vaḷinta alikaḷiyum, paṉittuṟi - kuḷirnta tuṟiyiṉiyu muṭiya, peruṅkaṭal iṟantu - periya kaṭalilē mikkuc ceṉṟu, nīr paruki aruntu koḷḷiya - nīriṉip parukiyuṇṭa mikutiyi yuṭiyaṉavāy, kuṭakku ērpu - mēṟkē eḻuntu, vayavu piṭi  iṉattiṉ - cūluṟṟa peṇyāṉik kūṭṭampōla, vayiṉ vayiṉ tōṉṟi - iṭantōṟum tōṉṟi, pāṭu āṉṟu - olimikku, oruṅku uṭaṉ tuvaṉṟi - peytaṟku oru cēra neruṅka, kār kāli vantaṉṟu - kārkālam kāliyē vantatu eṉiṉum;
10-15. māli - mālip poḻutil, kuḷirkoḷ piṭaviṉ kūrmuki alari - kuḷircci poruntiya piṭāviṉatu kūriya arumpu alartaṟkuriyataṉi, vaṇṭu vāy tiṟakkum taṇṭā nāṟṟam - vaṇṭu vāyiṉi nekiḻttalāl eḻum amiyāta maṇattiṉi, kūtir aṟcirattu ūti tūṟṟa - kūtir muṉpaṉik kālaṅkaṭku uriya vāṭik kāṟṟāṉatu tūṟṟā niṟka, paṉi ali kalaṅkiya neñcamoṭu - paṉi alittalāl kalaṅkiya neñcamuṭaṉ, pirinticiṉōr tiṟattu - pirintu ceṉṟārāya talivariṉ poruṭṭu, varuntuvam allamō - nam varuntutaṟkuriyōm allēmō.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! talivar nattuṟantu cēṇiṭiyarāyiṉum nīṭalar eṉṟi; kār kāli vantaṉṟu; māli, piṭaviṉatu alariyiṉ nāṟṟatti ūti tūṟṟa paṉi alik kalaṅkiya neñcamoṭu nām varuntuvam allamō?
(vi-ri) iḻi - mēkali. nam tuṟantu - nattuṟantu eṉa vikāramāyiṟṟu. tuvaṉṟi - tuvaṉṟa eṉat tirikka. kārp paruvam vanta  tākaliṉ, talivar nīṭātu vantuviṭuvar eṉṟu kūṟi āṟṟuvitta tōḻikku, avar varuntuṇiyum nām eṅṅaṉam āṟṟiyirukkaṟ pālam eṉpāḷ, `kārkāli vantatu eṉiṉum māli  kūtir aṟcirattu ūti tūṟṟap paṉi alik kalaṅkiya neñcamoṭu varuntuvam allamō' eṉṟu talivi kūṟiṉāḷ. kārkāli vantateṉiṉum māli kūtir aṟcirattu ūti tūṟṟa eṉṟatu, ap paruvaṅkaḷ aṭuttaṭuttu virintu varum eṉpati uṇarttiyavāṟu. kārpparuvam kuṟittuc ceṉṟa talivar, apparuvam vantum iṉṉum vantilāmiyiṉ, aṭuttu varum kūtir aṟcirap paruvaṅkaḷiṟṟāṉ avar varutal eṅṅaṉam teḷiyappaṭum eṉṟāḷu māyiṟṟu.
184. mulli
(talimakaṉ viṉivayiṟ pirintu vantu eytiyaviṭattut 
tōḻi pullu makiḻvu urittatu.)
		kaṭavuṭ kaṟpoṭu kuṭikkuviḷak kākiya
		putalvaṉ payanta pukaḻmiku ciṟappiṉ
		naṉṉa rāṭṭik kaṉṟiyum eṉakkum
		iṉitā kiṉṟāl ciṟakkaniṉ āyuḷ
	5.	aruntoḻil muṭitta cemmal uḷḷamoṭu
		curumpimir malara kāṉam piṟpaṭa
		veṇpiṭavu aviḻnta vīkamaḻ puṟavil
		kuṇṭik kōṭṭa kuṟumuḷ kaḷḷip
		puṉtali putitta koḻuṅkoṭi mulli
	10.	ārkaḻal putuppū uyirppiṉ nīkkit
		teḷḷaṟal parukiya tirimaruppu eḻiṟkali
		puḷḷiyam piṇiyoṭu vatiyum āṅkaṇ
		1kōṭuṭik kiyar tuḷareṟi viṉiñar
		ariyal ārkiyar viḷimakiḻ tūṅkac
	15.	celkatir maḻukiya uruva ñāyiṟṟuc
		cekkar vāṉañ ceṉṟa poḻutil
		kaṟpāl aruviyiṉ olikkum naṟṟērt
		tārmaṇi palavuṭaṉ iyampac
		cīrmiku kuricilnī vantuniṉ ṟatuvē.
- maturi marutaṉ iḷanākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 19. cīrmiku kuricil - ciṟappumikka talivaṉē!
5-19. veṇpiṭavu aviḻnta vī kamaḻ puṟavil - veḷḷiya piṭava marattiṉ virinta malar maṇakkum mulli nilattē, kuṇṭik kōṭṭa kuṟumuḷ kaḷḷi - kuṟiya kiḷikaḷiyum kuṟiya muṭkaḷiyu muṭiya kaḷḷiyiṉ, puṉ tali putitta koḻu koṭi mulli - pulliya ucciyiṉi mūṭiya vaḷaviya mullikkoṭiyiṉ, ār kaḻal putu pū uyirppiṉ nīkki - ārkkuk kaḻaṉṟa putiya pūkkaḷi mūcceṟi talāl otukki, teḷ aṟal parukiya tiri maruppu eḻil kali - teḷinta nīrik kuṭitta muṟukkuṇṭa kōṭṭiṉiyuṭiya aḻakiya māṉ kali, puḷḷi am piṇiyoṭu vatiyum āṅkaṇ - puḷḷikaḷiyuṭiya aḻakiya piṇiyuṭaṉ taṅkum avviṭattē, kōṭu uṭik kiyar- kaḷik koṭṭiṉiyuṭiya kiyiṉarāy, tuḷar eṟi viṉiñar kaḷiyiṉi veṭṭi eṟiyum toḻiliyuṭiyavarkaḷ, ariyal ārkiyar viḷi makiḻ tūṅka - kaḷḷiṉi niṟiya uṇṭu ataṉālāya kaḷippu mikkuṟa, celkatir maḻukiya uruva ñāyiṟṟu - cellum katir vemmi kuṟinta cenniṟattiṉatāya ñāyiṟṟiyuṭiya, cekkar vāṉam ceṉṟa poḻutil - cevvāṉam paraviya kālattē, kalpāl aruviyiṉ olikkum naṟṟēr - maliyiṉpāl vīḻum aruviyip pōla olikkum nalla tēriluḷḷa, tārmaṇi pala uṭaṉ iyampa - māliyākiya maṇikaḷ palavum oruṅkē olikka, curumpu imir malara kāṉam piṟpaṭa - vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum malarkaḷiyuṭiya kāṭu piṉṉēpōka, arun toḻil muṭitta cemmal uḷḷamoṭu - ariya viṉiyi muṭitta talimi mēviya ūkkattuṭaṉ, nī vantu niṉṟatu - nī iṅku vantu niṉṟatu.
1-4. kaṭavuṭ kaṟpoṭu - teyvak kaṟpuṭaṉ, kuṭikku viḷakku ākiya - kuṭikku viḷakkam ākiya, putalvaṉ payanta pukaḻmiku ciṟappiṉ naṉṉarāṭṭikku aṉṟiyum - makaṉip peṟṟa pukaḻ mikka ciṟappiṉi yuṭiya naṉmiyiyuṭiya talivikkē yallāmalum, eṉakkum iṉitu ākiṉṟu - eṉakkum iṉimiyit taruvatākiṉṟatu; niṉ āyuḷ ciṟakka - niṉ āyuḷ ciṟantiṭuvatāka.
(muṭipu) cīrmiku kuricil! cekkar vāṉam ceṉṟa poḻutil, naṟṟēr maṇi iyampa, kāṉam piṟpaṭa, cemmal uḷḷamoṭu nī vantu niṉṟatu, naṉṉarāṭṭikku aṉṟiyum, eṉakkum iṉitā kiṉṟu; niṉ āyuḷ ciṟakka.
(vi-ri) kaṭavuṭ kaṟpu - teyvattaṉmiyiyuṭiya kaṟpu. kaṟpuṭi makaḷir vēṇṭuṅkāl maḻiyum peyvippārākaliṉ avaratu kaṟpu teyvattaṉmi yuṭiyatāyiṟṟu. `kaṟpuk kaṭampūṇṭa itteyvamallatu, poṟpuṭit teyvam yāmkaṇ ṭilamāl, vāṉam poyyātu vaḷampiḻip paṟiyātu, nīṇila vēntar koṟṟam citiyātu, pattiṉip peṇṭi rirunta nāṭu'1 eṉpatuṅ kāṇka. kaṭavuṭ kaṟpoṭu putalvaṉ payanta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya naṉṉarāṭṭi eṉṟu iyiyum. putalvaṉ kuṭiyi viḷaṅkac ceypavaṉ ākaliṉ, kuṭikku viḷakkākiya putalvaṉ eṉṟāḷ. maṉivāḻkki makaḷirkkuk kaṟpoḻukkamum, naṉmakkaṭpēṟum iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa eṉpatu, `maṅkala meṉpa maṉimāṭci maṟṟataṉ - naṉkalam naṉmakkaṭ pēṟu'2 eṉṉum poyyā moḻiyāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. `maṉikku viḷakkam maṭavār' 3 eṉpatu paṟṟik kuṭikku viḷakkākiya eṉpataṉit talivikku ēṟṟiyurittalumām. naṉṉarāṭṭi - maṉimāṭciyāya naṉmiyellām uṭiyavaḷ. kaṇavaṉ kūṟiya coṟpiḻiyātu āṟṟiyirunta arumippāṭu tōṉṟat talivi iṅṅaṉam ciṟappittuk kūṟap peṟṟāḷ. pirivāṟṟātu talivi varuntuntōṟum tāṉum uṭaṉ varunti, aritiṉ āṟṟuvittuk koṇṭiruntavaḷ ākaliṉ, nī vantu niṉṟatu eṉakkum iṉitākiṉṟatu eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ. pirinta talivaṉ mīṇṭu vantatēyaṉṟi avaṉ ariya viṉiyi muṭitta perumitat tuṭaṉ tēriṉ maṇi olikkavantu niṉṟa tōṟṟam, talivaṉatu ākkatti virumpum talivikkuk kaḻipēriṉpam payappatākaliṉ, cemmaluḷḷamoṭu maṇi iyampa nī vantu niṉṟatu iṉitākiṉṟu eṉṟāḷ. talivaṉ tēr vanta virivu tōṉṟak kāṉam piṟpaṭa eṉṟāḷ. celkatir - mēṟṟicikkaṭceṉṟu maṟiyum katir. tuḷar - kaḷi. tār maṇi - māliyākak kōtta maṇikaḷ.
talivaṉ pōnta kāṭṭiṉkaṇ mulli, kaḷḷiyiṉ puṉ taliyi maṟintiruppatu, talivi taṉ pirivāluḷatākiya varuttam puṟan tōṉṟāmal kaṟpiṉāl āṟṟiyiruttaliyum, maṟitta pūvi uyirppiṉāl nīkki nīriṉip parukiya kali piṇiyoṭu vatital, tāṉ taliviyiṉ meliviṉi nīkki avaḷuṭaṉ iṉpuṟṟu vatiyap pōvatiyum avaṉukkup pulappaṭutti maṉaveḻucci taruvaṉa eṉka.
185. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		elvaḷi ñekiḻac cāay āyiḻi
		nalleḻiṟ paṇittōḷ iruṅkaviṉ aḻiyap
		peruṅki aṟṟa neñcamoṭu nattuṟantu
		irumpiṉ iṉṉuyir uṭiyōr pōla
	5.	1valittu valliṉar kātalar vāṭal
		olikaḻi nivanta nelluṭi neṭuvetir
		kalikoḷ maḷḷarvil viciyiṉ uṭiyap
		pitaṟa vempiya kalporu parappiṉ
		vēṉil attat tāṅkaṇ vāṉulantu
	10.	aruvi āṉṟa uyarcimi maruṅkil
		peruviḻā viḷakkam pōlap palavuṭaṉ
		iliila malarnta ilavamoṭu
		niliyuyar piṟaṅkal maliiṟan tōrē.
- pālipāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō.
(co-ḷ) 5-13. vāṭal oli kaḻi nivanta nel uṭi neṭu vetir - vāṭutaluṟṟa olikkiṉṟa mēlnōkki eḻunta taṇṭiṉiyum neṟkaḷiyumuṭiya nīṇṭa mūṅkil, kalikoḷ maḷḷar vil viciyiṉ uṭiya - āravāram koṇṭa maṟavaratu villiṉiṉ ṟeḻum ampiṉ viciyāṟ piḷantiṭa, pitu aṟa vempiya kal poru parappiṉ - pacumi aṟak kāynta parukkikaḷ porukiṉṟa iṭattiṉiyuṭiya, vēṉil attattu āṅkaṇ - vēṉilāṉ uḻanta kāṭṭiṭattē, vāṉ ulantu aruvi āṉṟa uyar cimi maruṅkiṉ - mēkam peyyā toḻitaliṉ aruvikaḷ illiyākiya uyarnta cikaraṅkaḷil, peruviḻā viḷakkam pōla - periya kārttiki viḻāviṟku iṭum viḷakkukaḷip pōla, pala uṭaṉ ili ila malarnta ilavamoṭu - iliyē illaṉavāyp palavum oruṅkē malarnta ilava maraṅkaḷik koṇṭu, nili uyar piṟaṅkal mali iṟantōr - uyarnta niliyiyuṭiya pakka maliyiṉi yuṭiya perumaliyiṉit tāṇṭic ceṉṟōrāya nam talivar;
1-5. āy iḻi nal eḻil paṇittōḷ - āynteṭutta aṇikaḷiyum nalla aḻakiṉiyumuṭiya mūṅkil pōlum namatu tōḷ, elvaḷiñekiḻac cāay - oḷiporuntiya vaḷi nekiḻumpaṭi melintu, iruṅkaviṉ aḻiya - periya aḻaku keṭumāṟu, peru ki aṟṟa neñcamoṭu nam tuṟantu - periya ceyalaṟṟa neñca mōṭirukkum nammik kiviṭṭu, irumpiṉ iṉ uyir uṭiyōr pōla - irumpiṉāl iyaṉṟa iṉiya uyiri uṭiyārpōla, valittu valliṉar - valittirukka vaṉmiyuṭi yōrāyiṉar.
(muṭipu) maliyiṟantōr āya nam kātalar, nam paṇittōḷ cāayk kaviṉ aḻiya nattuṟantu irumpiṉ iṉṉuyir uṭiyōr pōla valittu valliṉar.
(vi-ri) neñcamoṭu irukka eṉa orucol viritturikka. tām eytum tuṉpam karuti iraṅkit talivar vārātiruttaliṉ irumpiṉ iṉṉuyir uṭiyōrpōla valittu valliṉar eṉṟāḷ. nammi irumpāliyaṉṟa uyiruṭiyārpōlak karuti vārātiruk kiṉṟār eṉṟurittalumām.
186. marutam
(talimakaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpa illiṭip paratti colli neruṅkiyatu.)
		vāṉam vēṇṭā vaṟaṉil vāḻkki
		nōṉñāṇ viṉiñar kōḷaṟintu īrkkum
		mīṉmutir ilañcik kalitta tāmari
		nīrmici nivanta neṭuntāḷ akalili
	5.	1iruṅkayam tuḷaṅkak kāluṟu tōṟum
		peruṅkaḷiṟṟuc ceviyiṉ alikkum ūraṉoṭu
		eḻunta kuviyō peritē naṭpē
		koḻuṅkōl vēḻattup puṇituṇi yākap
		puṉalāṭu kēṇmi aṉittē avaṉē
	10.	oṇṭoṭi makaḷir 2paṇṭiyāḻ pāṭa
		īrntaṇ muḻaviṉ eṟikuṇil vitirppat
		taṇnaṟuñ cāntam kamaḻum tōḷmaṇantu
		iṉṉum piṟaḷvayi ṉāṉē maṉiyōḷ
		emmoṭu pulakkum eṉpa 3veṉvēl
	15.	māri ampiṉ maḻittōl paḻiyaṉ
		kāviri vippiṟ pōor aṉṉeṉ
		ceṟivaḷi yuṭittalō ilaṉē 4uritiṉiṉ
		yāntaṉ pakiyēm allēm cērntōr
		tirunutal pacappa nīṅkum
	20.	koḻunaṉuñ cāluntaṉ uṭaṉuṟi pakiyē.
- paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 1-7. vāṉam vēṇṭā vaṟaṉ il vāḻkki - maḻipeytali vēṇṭāta vaṟumiyuṟuta lillāta vāḻkki yiṉiyuṭiya, nōṉ ñāṇ viṉiñar - valiya tūṇṭiṟ kayiṟṟiṉi yuṭiya mīṉ piṭippōr, kōḷ aṟintu īrkkum - (mīṉ iri kōtta muḷḷiṉip) paṟṟiyatu uṇarntu iḻukkum, mīṉ mutir ilañci - mīṉ mikka nīrniliyil, kalitta tāmari nīr mici nivanta neṭu tāḷ akal ili - taḻitta tāmariyiṉ nīrmītu uyarnta neṭiya kāmpiṉiyuṭiya akaṉṟa iliyi, irukayam tuḷaṅka kāl uṟutōṟum - periya kuḷam aliyak kāṟṟu aṭikkuntōṟum, peru kaḷiṟṟuc ceviyiṉ alikkum ūraṉoṭu - periya kaḷiṟṟi yāṉiyiṉ kātupōla acivikkum ūraṉāl, eḻunta kuviyō peritē - eṉakku uṇṭāya alarō peritākum āyiṉum;
7-9. naṭpu - avaṉ naṭpō, koḻu kōl vēḻattup puṇi tuṇi āka - ceḻitta koṟukkaṅkaḻiyālāya puṇiyiṉit tuṇiyākakkoṇṭu, puṉal āṭu kēṇmi aṉittē - puṉal viḷiyāṭum naṭpiṉ aḷaviṉatē;
9-13. avaṉē - at talivaṉṟāṉ, oḷ toṭi makaḷir paṇṭiyāḻ pāṭa - oḷḷiya toṭiyiṉiyuṭiya makaḷir paḻiya yāḻiṉip pāṭavum, īr taṇ muḻaviṉ eṟi kuṇil vitirppa - mikka taṇmi vāynta muḻaviṉik kuṟuntaṭiyāl aṭikkavum, taṇ naṟum cāntam kamaḻum tōḷ maṇantu - taṇṇiya naṟiya cantaṉam nāṟum tōḷik kūṭi, iṉṉum piṟaḷ vayiṉāṉ - iṉṉum piṟaḷiṭattēyuḷḷāṉ āvaṉ; aṅṅaṉamākavum;
13-14. maṉiyōḷ emmoṭu pulakkum eṉpa - avaṉ maṉivi emmuṭaṉ veṟukkiṉṟāḷ eṉpar;
14-17. veṉ vēl māri ampiṉ maḻit tōl paḻiyaṉ - veṟṟi poruntiya vēliyum maḻittuḷi pōṉṟa mikka ampiṉiyum mēkampōlum kariya kēṭakattiṉiyum uṭiya paḻiyaṉ eṉpāṉatu, kāviri vippiṉ pōōr aṉṉa - kāviri nāṭṭiluḷḷa pōr eṉṉum ūriṉi otta, eṉ ceṟi vaḷi uritiṉiṉ uṭittalō ilaṉ - eṉatu neruṅkiya vaḷikaḷi urimiyāl uṭitta loṉṟuñ ceytilēṉ; eṉavē,
18-20. yām taṉ pakiyēm allēm - yām avaḷukkup pakiyāvēm allēm, cērntōr tirunutal pacappa nīṅkum - taṉṉic cērntōratu aḻakiya neṟṟi pacantiṭa avarkaḷi oḻittu nīṅkum, koḻunaṉum - avaḷ kaṇavaṉ, taṉ uṭaṉ uṟipaki cālum - taṉṉuṭaṉ kūṭiyuṟiyum pakivaṉātaṟkup poruntu vāṉāvaṉ.
(muṭipu) ūraṉoṭu eṉakku eḻunta kuviyō peritē; naṭpē vēḻattup puṇi tuṇiyākap puṉalāṭu kēṇmi aṉittē; avaṉē iṉṉum piṟaḷ vayiṉāṉē; maṉiyōḷ emmoṭu pulakkum eṉpa; ceṟivaḷi uṭittalō ilēṉ; yām taṉ pakiyēm allēm; koḻunaṉ taṉ uṭaṉuṟi paki cālum.
(vi-ri) mīṉ piṭikkum viṉiñar uḻaviṉāl nel mutaliyaṉa viḷittal ilarākaliṉ avaratu vāḻkki vāṉam vēṇṭā vāḻkki eṉavum, aṉṉarāyiṉum avar piṭikkum mīṉē avarukkup periyatoru varuvāyākaliṉ, vaṟaṉil vāḻkki eṉavum kūṟap paṭṭatu. nōṉñāṇ - tūṇṭiliṉ valiya kayiṟu; atukoṇṭu viṉi ceytaliṉ avar nōṉñāṇ viṉiñar eṉappaṭṭār. kōḷ - irumpiṟkōtta iriyip paṟṟuki. iṉi, valiya kayiṟṟiyuṭiya valiyeṉak koṇṭu, aḵtu akappaṭuttami aṟintu īrkkum eṉṟurittalumām. puṇi tuṇiyāka nīrāṭuvār nīrāṭṭu muṭintavuṭaṉ ap puṇiyi viṭuttu akaṟal pōla, talivaṉ eṉpāl vantu akaṉṟaṉaṉ eṉpāḷ, naṭpu kēṇmi aṉittu eṉṟāḷ. kēṇmi - puṇiyiṉiṭattuḷatāya kēṇmi; kuṟippu moḻi. iṉi, naṭpu oru nāḷ puṇituṇiyākap puṉalāṭiya avvaḷaviṟṟē eṉṟu rittalumām; "nī veyyōḷoṭu vēḻaveṇ puṇi taḻīi ....... nerunalāṭiṉi puṉalē"1 eṉa muṉ vantamiyuṅ kāṇka. oṇṭoṭi makaḷir - viṟaliyar; parattiyarumām. talivaṉ cēripparattiyoṭu kūṭi yoḻukutali parattiyar cēriyiṉiṉ ṟeḻum yāḻoliyum muḻavōciyumē yāvarumaṟiyap pulap paṭuttum; aṅṅaṉamākavum talivi emmoṭu pulattal poruntā teṉpatu tōṉṟa, pāṭavitirppat tōḷ maṇantu piṟaḷvayiṉāṉ eṉavum, emmoṭu pulakku meṉpa eṉavum iṟparatti kūṟiṉā ḷeṉka. maṟṟum, talivaṉ taliviyiyum eṉṉiyum kiyakaṉṟu cēripparattiyarmāṭṭu maruvi yoḻukutalāl eṉakkuḷatāya veṟuppiṉi eṉ vaḷiyi uṭittuk kāṭṭiṟṟilēṉ; atu koṇṭē avaṉ eṉṉuṭaṉ uḷaṉākat talivi karutiṉāḷpōlu meṉpāḷ, "ceṟivaḷi yuṭittalō ilaṉē" eṉṟāḷ; kula makaḷirallāta alavaṟ peṇṭir mutalāyiṉār veṟuppiṉāl vaḷiyuṭittal ceyvareṉpatu, "eṉṉōṭu tiriyēṉāyiṉ..... uṭikaveṉ, nēriṟi muṉki vīṅkiya vaḷiyē"2 eṉpataṉālaṟika. ilaṉ: aṉ vikuti taṉmikkaṇ vantatu. koḻunaṉum: um : aci nili. iṉi, cēripparattiyariyum viṭuttut taliviyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu uṟivāṉeṉpatu tōṉṟat `taṉ uṭaṉuṟi paki' eṉṟāḷ; uṭaṉuṟinta koḻunaṉ pakiyātal cālum eṉak kūṭṭiyurittalumām.
187. pāli
(pirivuṇarttiya tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu. talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ talimakaṭkut tōḻi colliyatūumām.)
		tōḷpulam pakalat tuñci nammoṭu
		nāḷpala nīṭiya karantuṟi puṇarcci
		nāṇuṭi miyiṉ nīṅkic cēynāṭṭu
		arumporuḷ valitta neñcamō ṭēki
	5.	nammuyarvu uḷḷiṉar kātalar kaṟuttōr
		temmuṉi cititta kaṭumparip puravi
		vārkaḻal polinta vaṉkaṇ maḻavar
		pūntoṭi viḻaviṉ talināḷ aṉṉa
		tarumaṇal ñemiriya tirunakar muṟṟam
	10.	pulampuṟuṅ kollō tōḻi 1cēṇōṅku
		alantali ñemiyat tāḷil āṅkaṇ
		2kalcērpu irunta cilkuṭip pākkattu
		elviruntu ayara ēmattu alki
		maṉiyuṟi kōḻi aṇaltāḻpu aṉṉa
	15.	kaviyoṇ taḷira karuṅkāl yāattu
		3vēṉil veṟpil kāṉaṅ kāya
		muṉiyeḻun tōṭiya 4keṭunāṭ ṭāriṭip
		paṉiveḷiṟu aruntu piṅkaṇ yāṉi
		oṇcuṭar mutirā 5iḷaṅkatir amiyattuk
	20.	kaṇpaṭu pāyal kiyoṭuṅku acinili
		vāḷvāyc cuṟaviṉ paṉittuṟi nīnti
		nāḷvēṭṭu eḻunta nayaṉil paratavar
		vikukaṭal ampiyil tōṉṟum
		mipaṭu māmali vilaṅkiya curaṉē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 10. tōḻi-,
1-4. tōḷ pulampu akala nammoṭu tuñci - namatu tōḷ taṉimi yakala nammuṭaṉ tuyiṉṟu, nāḷ pala nīṭiya karantu uṟi puṇarcci - palanāḷ nīṭṭitta kaḷavuppuṇarcciyi, nāṇ uṭimiyiṉ nīṅki - viṉiyiṉṟi yiruttaṟku nāṇutal uṭimiyāṉē nīṅki, cēy nāṭṭu arum poruḷ valitta neñcamoṭu - tūra nāṭṭilē ariya poruḷi īṭṭat tuṇinta neñcattuṭaṉ;
14-24. maṉi uṟi kōḻi - maṉiyil vatiyuṅ kōḻiyiṉ, aṇal tāḻpu aṉṉa - toṅkukiṉṟa tāṭiyi yotta, kavi oṇtaḷira karuṅkālyāattu - kavarnta oḷḷiya taḷiriṉiyum kariya aṭiyiṉiyum uṭiya yā maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya, vēṉil veṟpil kāṉam kāya - vēṉil veppam vāynta maliyi yaṭutta kāṭu kāyntiṭa, muṉi eḻuntu ōṭiya keṭunāṭṭu ār iṭi - kāṭṭaraṇiluḷḷār kuṭiyeḻuntōṭiya pāḻ nāṭṭiṉ ariya iṭattē, paṉi veḷiṟu aruntu piṅ kaṇ yāṉi - paṉaṅkuruttit tiṉṉum paciya kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya yāṉi, oḷ cuṭar mutirā iḷam katir amiyattu - oḷḷiya cuṭar mutirāta iḷiya ñāyiṟṟiyuṭiya kāliyilē, kaṇpaṭu pāyal ki oṭuṅku aci nili - tuyilumiṭattē ceyaloṭuṅki acintu koṇṭirukkum niliyāṉatu, vāḷ vāyc cuṟaviṉ paṉi tuṟi nīnti - vāḷ pōlum vāyiṉiyuṭiya cuṟā mīṉkaḷuḷḷa kuḷirnta tuṟikaḷi nīntic ceṉṟu, nāḷ vēṭṭu eḻunta nayaṉ il paratavar - nāṭkālattē mīṉ vēṭṭi kuṟittu eḻunta nayamaṟṟa paratavaruṭiya, viku kaṭal ampiyil tōṉṟum - kaṭaliṭattē poruntiya tōṇi pōlat tōṉṟāniṟkum, mi paṭu mā mali vilaṅkiya curaṉ - mēkam poruntiya periya mali kuṟukkiṭṭa pāliyiṉ kaṇṇē;
10-13. cēṇ ōṅku alantali ñemiyattu - neṭuntūram uyarnta kāynta ucciyiṉiyuṭiya ñemi maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya, āḷ il āṅkaṇ - āḷ vaḻakkaṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil, kal cērpu irunta cilkuṭi pākkattu - maliyic cārntirunta cila kuṭikaḷiyuṭiya pākkattē, el viruntu ayara - iravil viruntu ceyya, ēmattu alki - pātukāvalākat taṅki;
4-5. ēki - toṭarntu ceṉṟu, nam uyarvu uḷḷiṉar - namatu uyarcciyik karutiṉar; (ataṉāl),
5-10. kaṟuttōr - ciṉanteḻunta pakivaratu, tevmuṉi cititta kaṭumparip puravi - pakippulam tolitta kaṭiya cela viṉi yuṭiya kutirikaḷiyuṭiya, vārkaḻal polinta - nīṇṭa kaḻalāṟ polivuṟṟa, vaṉkaṇ maḻavar - taṟukaṇmiyiṉi yuṭiya maḻavar ceyyum, pūntoṭi viḻaviṉ talināḷ aṉṉa - pūntoṭi viḻā eṉṉum viḻāviṉ mutalnāḷi yotta poliviṉi yuṭiya, taru maṇal ñemiriya tirunakar muṟṟam - koṇarntiṭṭa maṇal paranta aḻakiya maṉiyiṉ muṟṟam, pulampuṟum kollō - taṉimi yuṟumō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kātalar, nammoṭu nīṭiya karantuṟi puṇarcciyi nāṇuṭimiyiṉ nīṅki, arumporuḷ valitta neñcamoṭu, mali vilaṅkiya curattiṉkaṇṇē, pākkattē elviruntu ayara ēmattu alki, ēki nam uyarvu uḷḷiṉar; ataṉāl tirunakar muṟṟam pulampuṟuṅ kollō?
