(muṭipu) tōḻi kēḷāy! alar namakkoḻiya, (nām) aḻap pirintōr, vēṅkaṭattumpar moḻi peyar tēetta rāyiṉum nalkuvar.
(vi-ri) kaṉṟu piṭiyuṭip peruniri eṉa māṟuka. pūcal - vēṭṭam. yāṉikaḷi akappaṭuttum vēṭṭikku eḻiṉi eṉ pāṉ vārāmiyāl, ciṉaṅkoṇṭa cōḻa maṉṉaṉ, matti eṉṉum paṭit talivaṉi ēva, avaṉ ceṉṟu av veḻiṉiyip pōrilē akappaṭuttu, avaṉ pallip paṟittu vantu veṇmaṇi yeṉṉum ūriṉatu vāyiṟ katavilē patittaṉaṉ eṉpatum, avveṟṟikku aṭiyāḷamāka āṅkuḷḷa nīrttuṟiyil kal nāṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpatum ic ceyyuḷil aṟiyappaṭum varalāṟu kaḷākum. matti eṉpāṉ paratavak kuṭiyiṉaṉ eṉpatum, kaḻāar eṉṉum ūrukku uriyavaṉ eṉpatum, paratavar kōmāṉ palvēl matti, kaḻāar muṉtuṟi (akam 126) eṉpataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum. iṉip pūcaliṉ eytā toḻiya eṉpataṟku, vēṭṭiyiṟ ceṉṟiruntā ṉākaliṉ taṉ āṇiyiṉpaṭi vārātoḻiya eṉṟurittalu mām.
212. kuṟiñci
(allakuṟippaṭṭu nīṅkun talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṉi 1neruṅkic colliyatu.)
		tāvil naṉpoṉ tiiya pāvi
		viṇtavaḻ iḷaveyil koṇṭuniṉ ṟaṉṉa
		mikukaviṉ eytiya tokukural aimpāl
		kiḷiyaril nāṇal kiḻaṅkumaṇaṟ kīṉṟa
	5.	muḷiyō raṉṉa muḷḷeyiṟṟut tuvarvāy
		nayavaṉ tivaruñ cevvaḻi nalyāḻ
		iciyōrt taṉṉa iṉtīṅ kiḷavi
		aṇaṅkucāl ariviyi naciip peruṅkaḷiṟṟu
		iṉampaṭi nīril kalaṅkiya poḻutil
	10.	peṟalaruṅ kuriya ḷeṉṉāy vikalum
		iṉṉā aruñcuram nīnti nīyē
		eṉṉi yiṉṉaṟ paṭuttaṉi miṉṉuvacipu
		uravukkār kaṭuppa maṟali mintuṟṟu
		1viravumoḻik kaṭṭūr vēṇṭuvaḻik koḷīip
	15.	paṭinilā ilaṅkuṅ kaṭalmaruḷ tāṉi
		maṭṭaviḻ teriyal maṟappōrk kuṭṭuvaṉ
		porumuraṇ peṟāatu vilaṅkuciṉañ ciṟantu
		ceruccey muṉpoṭu munnīr muṟṟi
		ōṅkutirip puvam nīṅka vōṭṭiya
	20.	nīrmāṇ eḵkam niṟattucceṉ ṟaḻuntak
		kūrmataṉ aḻiyarō neñcē āṉātu
		eḷiya ḷallōḷ karuti
		viḷiyā evvan talittan tōyē.
- paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 21. neñcē-,
1-12.  tāil naṉ poṉ tiiya pāvi - valiyaṟṟa māṟṟu yarnta poṉṉāṟ ceyyappeṟṟa pāvi, viṇ tavaḻ iḷaveyil koṇṭu niṉṟaṉṉa - vāṉil paravum iḷaveyilit taṉmēṟ koṇṭu oḷitikaḻa niṉṟālotta, miku kaviṉ eytiya - mikka aḻaku poruntiyavaḷum, toku kural aimpāl - tiraṇṭa kottākiya kūntaliyum, kiḷi aril nāṇal kiḻaṅku maṇaṟku īṉṟa - kiḷitta tūṟākiya nāṇaṟ kiḻaṅku maṇaliṭattē īṉṟa, muḷi ōraṉṉa muḷ eyiṟṟut tuvar vāy - muḷiyi otta (veḷḷiya) kūriya paṟkaḷi yuṭiya civanta vāyiṉiyum, nayavaṉ tivarum nalyāḻ cevvaḻi ici ōrttaṉṉa iṉ tīm kiḷavi - yāḻ vallōṉ iyakkum nalla yāḻiṉ cevvaḻip paṇṇiṉ iciyik kēṭṭāṟpōlum mika iṉiya colliṉiyum uṭivaḷumākiya, aṇaṅku cāl ariviyi nacii - teyvattiṉ iyalpiṉi yuṭiya makaḷi virumpi, peru kaḷiṟṟu iṉam paṭi nīril kalaṅkiya poḻutil - periya kaḷiṟṟi yāṉik kūṭṭam paṭintu kalakkiya nīrippōla nī kalakkamuṟṟa poḻutu, peṟal aruṅkuriyaḷ eṉṉāy - ivaḷ peṟutaṟku ariyaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇāyāki, vikalum iṉṉā aruñcuram nīnti - nāṭōṟum iṉṉāmi yuṭiya ariya curaneṟiyik kaṭantu, nī eṉṉi iṉṉal paṭuttaṉi - nī eṉṉit tuṉpattiṉkaṇ celuttiṉi;
21-23. āṉātu eḷiyaḷ allōḷ karuti - eḷiyaḷ allaḷāya avaḷi amiyātu eṇṇi, viḷiyā evvam talit tantōy - nīṅkāta tuṉpattiṉi eṉpāṟ cērttiyuḷḷa nītāṉ;
12-21. paṭi nilā ilaṅkum kaṭal maruḷ tāṉi - paṭik kalaṉkaḷiṉ oḷi vīcum kaṭaliyotta tāṉiyiṉiyuṭiya, maṭṭu aviḻ teriyal maṟap pōrk kuṭṭuvaṉ - tēṉ oḻukum māliyiṉi yuṭiya taṟukaṇmiyuṭaṉ pōrceytalvalla kuṭṭuvaṉ, miṉṉu vicipu uravukkār kaṭuppa maṟali mintu uṟṟu - miṉṉalāl vāṉip piḷantu valimi koṇṭeḻum mēkattiyoppap pakittu valimi koṇṭu, viravu moḻik kaṭṭūr vēṇṭuvaḻik koḷīi - pala moḻiyum kalanta pācaṟiyit tāṉ vēṇṭumiṭaṅkaḷil amittu, poru muraṇ peṟātu - taṉṉuṭaṉ porum pakiyi nila ellikkuḷ kāṇāmiyiṉ, vilaṅku ciṉam ciṟantu - poṅki eḻum ciṉam mikku, ceru cey muṉpoṭu munnīr muṟṟi - pōr puriyum valiyāl kaṭali vaḷintu, ōṅkutirip puvam nīṅka ōṭṭiya - uyarnta alikaḷiyuṭiya kaṭalip piṟakkiṭumpaṭi ōṭṭiya, nīr māṇ eḵkam - māṇpuṟṟa nīrmiyuṭiya vēl, niṟattuc ceṉṟu aḻunta - niṉ mārpilē tittu aḻuntiṭa, kūr mataṉ aḻiyarō - niṉatu mikka cerukku aḻiyap peṟuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) neñcē! eṉṉi iṉṉaṟpaṭuttaṉi; viḷiyā evvam talittanta nītāṉ kuṭṭuvaṉ eḵkam niṟuttuc ceṉṟaḻunta mataṉ aḻiyarō.
(vi-ri) tā - vali; pōkkaṟṟa poṉ nekiḻcci yuṭiyatā yiruttaliṉ tāvil naṉpoṉ eṉappaṭṭatu. kaviṉ eytiya arivi eṉavum, aimpāliyum muḷḷeyiṟṟut tuvarvāyiṉiyum, tīṅkiḷavi yiṉiyumuṭiya arivi eṉavum kūṭṭuka. maṇaṟku īṉṟa : vēṟṟumi mayakkam. nayavaṉ - icinayam terintu pāṭupavaṉ. aṇaṅku - teyvattaṉmi. peṟutaṟkariyaḷ eṉpatu tōṉṟa aṇaṅkucāl arivi eṉṟāṉ. kalaṅkiya neñciṟkuc ceyvatum tavirvatum tōṉṟāvākaliṉ, `kalaṅkiya poḻutil........ eṉṉi yiṉṉaṟ paṭuttaṉi' eṉṟāṉ. maṟalutal - māṟupaṭutal, pakittal. paṭiñaruḷḷē palamoḻi pēcuvārum uḷarātaliṉ, viravu moḻik kaṭṭūr eṉṟār.
munnīr muṟṟi, ōṅkutirip puvam nīṅka ōṭṭiya eṉṟatu, kaṭal nāppaṇ pakivar uṟiyum tīvic cūḻntu, avarkku araṇākavuḷḷa kaṭal vali keṭap pōkkiya eṉṟavāṟu. ‘patiṟṟup pattil, `uṭi tirip parappiṟ paṭukaṭa lōṭṭiya'1 (46) eṉpuḻi kaṭalōṭṭiya eṉṟatu : `taṉṉuḷ vāḻvārkku araṇākiya kaṭal valiyi aḻitta eṉṟavāṟu' eṉa ataṉ paḻiya urikārar kūṟiyiruppatu kāṇka; ataṉālē kaṭal piṟakkōṭṭiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ' eṉak kuṭṭuvaṉ kūṟappaṭuvāṉ āyiṉaṉ.
iṉi, ōṭṭiya eṉpataṟkup pakivari ōṭṭiya eṉṟalumām. niṟam - mārpu; niṟattuc ceṉṟaḻunta eṉa, neñci uṟuppuṭiyatu pōl kūṟiyatu, `uṟuppuṭi yatupōl........ neñcoṭu puṇarttu'2 eṉpataṉāl amiyum.
213. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟīiyatu.)
		viṉinavil yāṉi viṟaṟpōrt toṇṭiyar
		iṉamaḻi tavaḻum ēṟṟaru neṭuṅkōṭṭu
		ōṅkuveḷ ḷaruvi vēṅkaṭat tumpark
		koykuḻi yatiral vikupular alari
	5.	curiyirum pitti curumpupaṭac cūṭi
		ikalmuṉit tarīiya ēṟuṭip peruniri
		naṉimutir naṟaviṉ nāṭpali koṭukkum
		vālniṇap pukaviṉ vaṭukar tēettu
		niḻaṟkavi ṉiḻanta nīril nīḷiṭi
	10.	aḻalavir aruñcuram neṭiya veṉṉātu
		akaṟa lāyntaṉa rāyiṉum pakalcelap
		palkatir vāṅkiya paṭucuṭar amiyattup
		perumaraṅ koṉṟa kālpuku viyaṉpuṉattu
		3erimaruḷ katira tirumaṇi yimikkum
	15.	velpōr vāṉavaṉ kollik kuṭavari
		vēyoḻuk kaṉṉa cāyiṟip paṇittōḷ
		peruṅkaviṉ citiya nīṅki āṉṟōr
		arumpeṟal ulakam amiḻtoṭu peṟiṉuñ
		ceṉṟutām nīṭalō ilarē yeṉṟuṅ
	20.	kalampeyak kaviḻnta kalattoṭit taṭakki
		valampaṭu veṉṟi vāyvāṭ cōḻar
		ilaṅkunīrk kāviri yiḻipuṉal varitta
		aṟaleṉa neṟinta kūntal
		uṟaliṉ cāyaloṭu oṉṟutal maṟantē.
- 4tāyaṅ kaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-11. (tōḻi! nam talivar,) viṉi navil yāṉi viṟal pōr toṇṭiyar - pōrttoḻil payiṉṟa yāṉikaḷi yuṭiya valiya pōr valla toṇṭiyaratu; iṉam maḻi tavaḻum ēṟṟu aruneṭu kōṭṭu - kūṭṭamāya mēkaṅkaḷ tavaḻum ēṟutaṟku ariya neṭiya ucciyiṉiṉṟu iḻiyum, ōṅku veḷ aruvi - uyarntu tōṉṟum veḷḷiya aruvikaḷiyuṭiya, vēṅkaṭattu umpar - vēṅkaṭa malikku appāluḷḷatum, koy kuḻi atiral viku pular alari - koyyappeṟṟa taḻikaḷiyuṭiya kāṭṭu mallikiyiṉ vikaṟiyil alarum pūviṉi, curi iru pitti curumpu paṭa cūṭi - curuṇṭa kariya mayiril vaṇṭu moykkac cūṭi, ikal muṉi taḻīiya ēṟu uṭi peru niri - pakivar pōrmuṉiyil veṉṟu koṇṭa ēṟukaḷiyuṭiya periya āṉirikkāka, naṉimutir naṟaviṉ nāḷ pali koṭukkum - mutirnta kaḷḷākiya naṟaviṉi viṭiyaṟkālattē paliyākac celuttum, vālniṇa pukaviṉ vaṭukar tēettu - veḷḷiya niṇac cōṟṟiṉi yuṭiya vaṭukaratu tēyattē yuḷḷa, niḻal kaviṉ iḻanta nīr il nīḷ iṭi - niḻaliṉ aḻaki iḻanta nīr illāta nīṇṭa iṭattiṉi yuṭiya, aḻal avir aru curam neṭiya eṉṉātu - tīyiṉ veppam viḷaṅkum ariya kāṭukaḷ nīṇṭaṉa eṉṟu karutāmal, akaṟal āyntaṉar āyiṉum - nammip pirintu pōtalit tuṇintaṉarāyiṉum;	
19-24. eṉṟum kalam peyak kaviḻnta kaḻaltoṭit taṭak ki - eññāṉṟum aṇikaḷip peytaṟkuk kaviḻntuḷa kaḻalum toṭiyiṉi aṇinta periya kiyiṉkaṇ, valam paṭu veṉṟi vāy vāḷ cōḻar - valimiyāṉ eytiya veṉṟiporuntiya tappāta vāḷiṉik koṇṭa cōḻaratu, ilaṅku nīrk kāviri iḻipuṉal varitta - viḷaṅkum nīriṉiyuṭiya kāviriyiṉatu vaṭinta nīr vari variyākac ceytiṭṭa, aṟal eṉa neṟinta kūntal - karumaṇal pōla vaḷinta kūntaliyum, uṟal iṉcāyaloṭu oṉṟutal maṟantu - uṟutaṟku iṉiya meṉmiyiyum poruntutali maṟantu;
11-17. pakal cela pal katir vāṅkiya paṭucuṭar amiyattu - pakaṟpoḻutu nīṅka ñāyiṟu taṉatu pala katirkaḷiyum curukkiya oḷi maḻuṅkum nērattē, peru maram koṉṟa kāl puku viyaṉ puṉattu - periya marattiṉi veṭṭit taḷḷiyatāl kāṟṟup pukalākum periya kolliyil. eri maruḷ katir tirumaṇi imikkum - tīppōṉṟa katirkaḷiyuṭiya aḻakiya maṇi miḷirum, velpōr vāṉavaṉ kollik kuṭavari - vellum pōriṉiyuṭiya cēraṉatu kolli maliyiṉ mēṟkup pakkattēyuḷḷa, vēy oḻukku aṉṉa cāy iṟippaṇit tōḷ - mūṅkiliṉ nēriya pakutiyi otta vaḷinta muṉkiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya parutta tōḷiṉ, peru kaviṉ citiya nīṅkic ceṉṟu - periya aḻaku citintiṭa nīṅkip pōy;
17-19. āṉṟōr arum peṟal ulakam amiḻtoṭu peṟiṉum- tēvaratu peṟalarum viṇṇulaki āṇṭuḷḷa amiḻtattuṭaṉ peṟuvārāyiṉum, tām nīṭalō ilar - avar tāḻntiruttal ilarāvar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! nam talivar, vēṅkaṭattumpar vaṭukar tēttu aruñcuram akaṟal āyntaṉarāyiṉum, niṉ kūntal uṟaliṉ cāyaloṭu oṉṟutal maṟantu peruṅ kaviṉ citiya nīṅkic ceṉṟu arum peṟal ulakam amiḻtoṭu peṟiṉum nīṭalō ilar.
(vi-ri) toṇṭiyar vēṅkaṭam eṉka, toṇṭiyar - toṇṭi nāṭṭiluḷḷa paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉar. niriyiṉiyum naṟaviṉiyam palikoṭukkum eṉṟalumām. āyntaṉarāyiṉum - āyntu tuṇintaṉa rāyiṉum. āṉṟōr ulakam - tēvarulakam.
214. mulli
(pācaṟikkaṇ talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		akaliru vicumpakam putiyap pāayp
		pakaluṭaṉ karanta palkatir vāṉam
		iruṅkaḷiṟṟu iṉaniri kuḷirppa vīcip
		perumpeyal aḻituḷi poḻita lāṉātu
	5.	vēntaṉum vempaki muraṇi yēntili
		viṭukatir neṭuvēl imikkum pācaṟi
		aṭupukaḻ mēvaloṭu kaṇpaṭi yilaṉē.
		amarum namvayiṉ atuvē namareṉa
		nammaṟivu 1 teḷinta pommal ōti
	10.	yāṅkā kuvaḷkol tāṉē ōṅkuviṭip
		paṭucuval koṇṭa pakuvāyt teṇmaṇi
		āpeyar kōvalar āmpalo ṭaḷiip
		piyuḷ nalyāḻ cevvaḻi vakuppa
		āruyir aṇaṅkun teḷḷici
	15.	māri māliyun tamiyaḷ kēṭṭē.
- vaṭamavaṇṇakkaṉ pēri cāttaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-4. (neñcē!) akal iru vicumpu akam putiya pāay - akaṉṟa periya vāṉiṭamellām maṟiyap parantu, pal katir pakal uṭaṉ karanta vāṉam - pala katirkaḷiyuṭiya ñāyiṟṟi muḻuvatum maṟitta mēkam, iru kaḷiṟṟu iṉa niri kuḷirppa - periya kaḷiṟṟiṉ piṭiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya tokuti kuḷirntiṭa, perum peyal aḻi tuḷi vīci poḻital āṉātu - perum peyalākiya mikka tuḷikaḷi vīcic coritali amiyātu;
5-7. vēntaṉum vempaki muraṇi - aracaṉum mikka paki
yoṭu māṟupaṭṭu, ēntu ili viṭukatir neṭuvēl imikkum pācaṟi - nimirnta iliyiṉiyuṭiya oḷiviṭukiṉṟa nīṇṭa vēl miṉṉum pācaṟik kaṇṇēyiruntu, aṭupukaḻ mēvaloṭu kaṇpaṭi ilaṉē - pakiyi vellutalāl eytum pukaḻiṉi virumpit tuyilutal ceytilaṉ;
8. amarum namvayi ṉatuvē - pōriṉ poṟi nammic cārntuḷatu;
8-9. namar eṉa - namkēḷir eṉṟu, nam - nammi, aṟivu teḷinta - taṉ aṟiviṉāl teruṇṭa, pommal ōti - polivuṟṟa kūntaliyuṭiya nam talivi;
10-15. ōṅku viṭi paṭucuval koṇṭa pakuvāyt teḷmaṇi - uyarnta ēṟṟiṉ poruntiya piṭariṟ kaṭṭiya periya vāyiṉi yuṭiya teḷinta oliyiṉatākiya maṇiyiṉ ōciyiyum, āpeyar kōvalar āmpaloṭu aḷii - ākkaḷi mīṭṭuvarum kōvalar pullāṅkuḻaluṭaṉ porunta, piyuḷ nalyāḻ cevvaḻi vakuppa - pirintārkkut tuṉpiṉit tarum nalla yāḻiṉ kaṇṇē cevvaḻip paṇṇip pāṭa, ār uyir aṇaṅkum teḷ ici - ariya uyir
kaḷi varuttum at teḷḷiya ōciyiyum, māri māliyum tamiyaḷ kēṭṭu - mārikkālattu māliyilum taṉiyaḷāyiruntu kēṭṭaliṉ, yāṅku ākuvaḷ kollō enniliyiṉi aṭivāḷō?
(muṭipu) neñcē! vāṉam perumpeyal vīci aḻituḷi poḻital āṉātu; vēntaṉum pācaṟi pukaḻ mēvaloṭu kaṇpaṭiyilaṉ; namareṉa nammit teḷinta pommaḷ ōti, maṇiyiṉ ōciyiyum teḷḷiciyiyum māri māliyilum tamiyaḷ kēṭṭu yāṅkākuvaḷ kollō.
(vi-ri) vēntaṉum vempaki muraṇip pukaḻ mēvaloṭu kaṇ paṭiyilaṉ eṉṟamiyāl, māṟṟār paṇintu tiṟi koṭukkavum, pukar viruppiṉāl aracaṉ mēlum pōr puriyak karutiyuḷḷāṉ eṉpatum, amarum namvayiṉatu eṉṟamiyāl, appōriṉi naṭattum kaṭappāṭu taṉakku uḷateṉṟum kūṟiṉāṉām. nām iṅkē ivvāṟirukka, avaṭku varuvēm eṉakkuṟitta kār vantu viṭṭamiyālum, ēṟṟiṉ kaḻuttiṟ kaṭṭiya maṇiyōciyum kuḻaluṭaṉ kūṭiya yāḻiciyum mālip poḻutilē tamiyaḷāyk kēṭṭalālum nam kātali ettakiya tuṉpamuṟuvaḷō eṉat talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟku iraṅkik kūṟiṉāṉeṉka. amarum nam vayiṉatu eṉṟamiyāl, talivaṉ aracaṉukkup paṭittalivaṉō aṉṟi naṭpāḷaṉō ātal vēṇṭum. namar eṉat teḷinta ōti eṉka. aṟivu - taṉ aṟiviṉālē; nām kūṟit teḷivitta urikaḷāl eṉṟumām. āmpal eṉṟatu kuḻal.
(mē-ḷ) `ēvaṉ marapiṉ ēṉōru muriyar'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ivvakappāṭṭum poruṇōkkiṉāl tūtukaṇṭu varuntik kūṟiyatu eṉpar, nac.
215. pāli
(celavuṇarttiya tōḻi talimakaḷ kuṟippaṟintu talimakaṉic celavaḻuṅkuvittatu.)
		vilaṅkiruñ cimiyak kuṉṟat tumpar
		vēṟupaṉ moḻiya tēem muṉṉi
		viṉinaciip parikkum uraṉmiku neñcamoṭu
		puṉimāṇ eḵkam valavayiṉ ēntic
	5.	celaṉmāṇpu uṟṟa numvayiṉ vallē
		valaṉāku eṉṟalum naṉṟumaṟ ṟilla
		kaṭuttatu piḻikkuva tāyiṉ toṭutta
		kiviral kavvuṅ kallāk kāṭcik
		koṭumaram piṭitta kōṭā vaṇkaṇ
	10.	vaṭinavil ampiṉ ēva lāṭavar
		āḷaḻit tuyartta añcuvaru patukkik
		kūrnutic cevvāy eruvic cēval
		paṭupiṇap pintali toṭuvaṉa kuḻīi
		mallaṉ mociviral oṟṟi maṇikoṇṭu
	15.	valvāyp pēṭikkuc coriyum āṅkaṇ
		kaḻintōrkku iraṅku neñcamoṭu
		oḻintivaṇ uṟita lāṟṟu vōrkkē.
- iṟaṅkukuṭik kuṉṟa nāṭaṉ.
(co-ḷ) 7-17. kaṭuttatu piḻikkuvatāyiṉ - kuṟitta ilakkut tappuvatāyiṉ, toṭutta kiviral kavvum - ampiṉit toṭutta kivirali vāyāṟ kavvikkeḷḷum, kallāk kāṭci - kallāta aṟiviṉiyum, koṭumaram piṭitta kōṭā vaṉkaṇ - villi ēntiya māṟāta koṭumiyiyum, vaṭi navil ampiṉ ēval āṭavar - vaṭittal ceyta ampiṉiyumuṭiya ēvalarāya maṟavar, āḷ aḻittu uyartta añcuvaru patukki - vaḻippōkum āṭkaḷik koṉṟu avarkaḷi yiṭṭu mūṭiya kaṇṭārkku accam varum kaṟkuviyalkaḷil, kūrnutic cevvāy eruvic cēval - kūriya alakiṉik koṇṭa civanta vāyiṉiyuṭiya āṇ paruntukaḷ, paṭupiṇap pintali toṭuvaṉa kuḻīi - iṟantupaṭṭa piṇaṅkaḷiṉ paciya taliyiṉit tōṇṭu vaṉavākik kūṭi, mallal mociviral oṟṟi - valiya neruṅkiya viralāl tōṇṭi, maṇi koṇṭu - kaṇmaṇiyip peyarttukkoṇṭu, valvāyp pēṭikkuc coriyum āṅkaṇ - valiya vāyiṉiyuṭiya tam pēṭikaṭkuc coriyum avviṭattē, kaḻintōrkku iraṅkum neñcamoṭu - pirintu ceṉṟārkku iraṅkiyoḻiyum neñcattuṭaṉ, oḻintu ivaṇ uṟital āṟṟuvōrkku - avar tammip pirintu iṅkē taṅkutal vallārkku;
1-5. (taliva!) vilaṅku iru cimiyak kuṉṟattu umpar - kuṟukkākavuḷḷa periya ucciyiṉiyuṭiya malikku appāluḷḷa, vēṟu palmoḻiya tēem muṉṉi - vēṟupaṭṭa pala moḻikaḷ vaḻaṅkum tēyattik koḷḷakkaruti, viṉi naciip parikkum uraṉmiku neñcamoṭu - pōrttoḻili virumpic celuttum tuṇivumikka uḷḷattuṭaṉ, puṉimāṇ eḵkam valvayiṉ ēnti - aḻakiya māṇpuṟṟa vēli valakkiyiṉ ēnti, celal māṇpu uṟṟa numvayiṉ - cellutal māṭciyuṟṟa nummiṭattē;
5-6. vallē valaṉ āka eṉṟalum naṉṟu - virintu veṟṟi mēvuka eṉṟu kūṟi viṭuttalum naṉṟākum; maṉ - yām aṅṅaṉam pirintuṟital āṟṟuvēm; allēm; eṉ ceyvēm.
(muṭipu) (taliva), eruviccēval maṇikoṇṭu pēṭikkuc coriyum āṅkaṇ kaḻintōrkku iraṅkum neñcamoṭu ivaṇ uṟital āṟṟuvōrkku, kuṉṟattu umpar tēem muṉṉi viṉi naciipparikkum neñcamoṭu celaṉ māṇpuṟṟa numvayiṉ, valaṉā keṉṟalum naṉṟu; yām aṅṅaṉam pirintuṟital āṟṟuvēm allēm; eṉ ceyvēm.
(vi-ri) tēem muṉṉi - tēyattik koḷḷak karutiyeṉka. parikkum - celuttum. valaṉāka eṉṟalum - veṟṟi uṇṭāka eṉṟu kūṟi viṭuttalum eṉa viritturikka. āka, īṟu tokkatu. maṉ, oḻiyici. tilla, acic col īṟu tirintatu. kaṭuttatu - kuṟitta ilakku; viṉippeyar. maṇi - viḻi.
(mē-ḷ) `vēntu viṉiyiyaṟki'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, muṭiyuṭi vēntarkkuriya toḻilākiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ.............. kuṟunila maṉṉariṭattum poruntum iṭaṉuṭiyatu eṉṟurittu, ataṟku utāraṇamāka `vilaṅkiruñ cimiyak kuṉṟat tumpar............ celaṉ māṇpuṟṟa' eṉṟa aṭikaḷik kāṭṭi, `vēṟupaṉ moḻiya tēettik koḷḷak karutip pōrt toḻilic celuttum uraṉmikka neñca meṉṟaliṉ, itu kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ taṉ pakivayiṉ nāṭu koḷḷac ceṉṟatām, vēntaṉ eṉap peyar kūṟāmiyiṉ' eṉṟār, nac.
216. marutam
(talimakaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpat taṉakkup 
pāṅkāyiṉārkkup paratti colliyatu.)
		nāṇkoḷ nuṇ kōliṉ mīṉkoḷ pāṇmakaḷ
		1tāṉpuṉa laṭikarip paṭutta varāal
		nārari naṟavuṇ ṭirunta tantikku
		vañci viṟakiṟ cuṭṭuvā yuṟukkun
	5.	taṇṭuṟi ūraṉ peṇṭir emmip
		peṭṭāṅku moḻipa eṉpa avvalarp
		paṭṭaṉa māyiṉ iṉiyeva ṉākiyar
		kaṭalāṭu makaḷir koyta ñāḻaluṅ
		kaḻaṉi yuḻavar kuṟṟa kuvaḷiyuṅ
	10.	kaṭimiḷip puṟaviṟ pūtta mulliyoṭu
		palliḷaṅ kōcar kaṇṇi ayarum
		mallal yāṇarc cellik kōmāṉ
		eṟiviṭat tuliyāc ceṟicuri veḷvēl
		ātaṉ eḻiṉi yaruniṟat taḻuttiya
	15.	peruṅkaḷiṟṟu evvam pōla
		varuntuvar mātavar cēriyāñ celiṉē.         - aiyūr muṭavaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-6. nāṇ koḷ nuṇkōliṉ mīṉkoḷpāṇ makaḷ- kayiṟṟiṉik koṇṭa nuṇṇiya tūṇṭiṟ kōlāl mīṉip piṭikkum pāṇaratu makaḷ, puṉal aṭikarippaṭutta varāal - puṉali aṭutta kariyil akappaṭuttiya varālmīṉi, nār ari naṟavu uṇṭu irunta tantikku - paṉṉāṭiyāl arikkappeṟṟa kaḷḷiyuṇṭu kaḷittirunta taṉ tantikku, vañci viṟakil cuṭṭu - vañcimarattiṉ viṟakiṉāṟ cuṭṭu, vāy uṟukkum - vāyil uṇpikkum, taṇtuṟi ūraṉ peṇṭir - kuḷirnta tuṟiyiṉi yuṭiya ūraṉatu peṇṭirkaḷ, emmip peṭṭāṅku moḻipa eṉpa - emmit tam maṉam virumpiyapaṭi yellām ikaḻnturippar eṉpārkaḷ;
6-7. a alar paṭṭaṉamāyiṉ - anta alar mēvappeṟṟē māyiṉ, iṉi evaṉ ākiyar - ippoḻutu yātēṉum āka;
8-16. avar cēri yām celiṉē - avartam cērikkaṇ yām celvēmāyiṉ, kaṭal āṭu makaḷir koyta ñāḻalum - kaṭalāṭum peṇṭir koytuvanta pulinakak koṉṟip pūviṉiyum, kaḻaṉi uḻavar kuṟṟa kuvaḷiyum - vayalil uḻuvōr paṟittu vanta kuvaḷip pūviṉiyum, kaṭimiḷip puṟavil pūtta mulliyoṭu - kāvaṟ kāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya mulli nilattē pūtta mullip pūvuṭaṉ cērttu, pal iḷam kōcar kaṇṇi ayarum - pala iḷiya kōcarkaḷ kaṇṇiyākak kaṭṭi viḷiyāṭum, mallalyāṇar cellik kōmāṉ - mikka vaḷam poruntiya cellūr maṉṉaṉākiya, ātaṉ eḻiṉi - ātaṉ eḻiṉi eṉpāṉ, eṟiviṭattu uliyā ceṟi curi veḷ vēl - māṟṟārmītu eṟiyumiṭattuc citiyāta curiceṟinta veḷḷiya vēli, aru niṟattu aḻuttiya perukaḷiṟṟu evvam pōla - taṉatu ariya mārpil pāyccap peṟṟa periya kaḷiṟṟiṉ tuṉpam pōla, varuntuvar - varuttamuṟuvar.
(muṭipu) taṇṭuṟi yūraṉ peṇṭir emmip peṭṭāṅku moḻipa eṉpa; avvalar paṭṭaṉamāyiṉ iṉi yātēṉum āka; avar cēri yām celiṉē, ātaṉ eḻiṉi vēl niṟattu aḻuttiya kaḷiṟṟiṉ evvampōl varuntuvar.
(vi-ri) peṇṭir - talimakaḷum avaḷ tōḻiyarum. ñāḻalum kuvaḷiyum mulliyoṭu kaṇṇi ayarum eṉa neytaṟkum mullikkum uriya pūkkaḷ marutattiṉkaṇ vantami  ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum, annilam poḻutoṭu vārā vāyiṉum, vanta nilattiṉ payatta vākum'1 eṉpataṉāl amiyum. cellik kōmāṉāya ātaṉ eḻiṉi veḷvēl aḻuttiya peruṅ kaḷiṟṟu evvam eṉka. taṉakkup pāṅkāyiṉāri uḷappaṭutti, yām celiṉ eṉṟāḷ. tāṉ, mātu eṉpaṉa acikaḷ.
217. pāli
(pirivuṇarttiya tōḻikkut talimakaḷ āṟṟāmimītūrac colliyatu.)
		2peytupulam tiṟanta poṅkal veṇmaḻi
		eḵkuṟu pañcit tuyppaṭ ṭaṉṉa
		tuvali tūval kaḻiya akalvayal
		nīṭukaḻik karumpiṉ kaṇikkāl vāṉpūk
	5.	kōṭip pūḷiyiṉ vāṭiyoṭu tuyalvarap
		pācilip potuḷiya putaltoṟum pakaṉṟi
		nīluṇ pacci niṟamaṟittu aṭicciya
		tōleṟi pāṇṭiliṉ vāliya malarak
		kōḻili avarik 1koḻumuki aviḻa
	10.	ūḻuṟu tōṉṟi ōṇpūt taḷiviṭap
		pulantoṟuṅ 2kurukiṉam naralak kalleṉa
		akaṉṟuṟi makaḷir aṇituṟantu naṭuṅka
		aṟciram vantaṉ ṟamintaṉ ṟituveṉa
		epporuḷ peṟiṉum piriyaṉmi ṉōveṉac
	15.	ceppuval vāḻiyō tuṇiyuṭi 3yīrkkeṉa
		nalkāk kātalar nalaṉuṇṭu tuṟanta
		pāḻpaṭu mēṉi nōkki nōypora
		iṇariṟupu uṭiyum neñcamoṭu puṇarvuvēṭṭu
		eyiṟutīp piṟappat tiruki
	20.	naṭuṅkutum piriyiṉyāṅ kaṭumpaṉi uḻantē.
- kaḻārk kīraṉeyiṟṟiyār.
(co-ḷ) 1-13. (tōḻi!) peytu pulam tiṟanta poṅkal veṇmaḻi - peytu piṉ ticikaḷi veḷiyākkiya poṅkiya veṇmēkam, eḵkuṟu pañci tuy paṭṭaṉṉa - kaṭiyappeṟṟa pañcāṉatu meṉmiyuṟṟāṟ pōṉṟa, tuvali tūval kaḻiya - ciṟiya tuḷiyit tūvutaliyum oḻiya, akal vayalnīṭu kaḻik karumpiṉ kaṇik kāl vāṉ pū - akaṉṟa vayaliluḷḷa nīṇṭa taṇṭiṉi
yuṭiya karumpiṉ tiraṇṭa kāmpuṭiya periya pūkkaḷ, kōṭip pūḷiyiṉ vāṭiyoṭu tuyalvara - kōṭik kālattup pūḷip pūppōla vāṭikkāṟṟāl acintiṭa, pacu ili potuḷiya putaltoṟum pakaṉṟi - paciya ili neruṅkiya putaltōṟum pakaṉṟiyāṉatu, nīl uṇ pacci niṟam maṟittu aṭicciya - nīlam ūṭṭappeṟṟa tōliṉatu niṟam maṟiyumpaṭi patitta, tōl eṟi pāṇṭiliṉ - kiṭukiṟ patitta vaṭṭak kaṇṇāṭi pōla, vāliya malara - veḷḷiyaṉavāka malara, kōḻili avari koḻumuki aviḻa - koḻutta iliyiṉiyuṭiya avariyiṉ vaḷaviya arumpukaḷ viriya, ūḻ uṟu tōṉṟi oṇpū taḷiviṭa - muṟiyāka muṟṟiya tōṉṟiyiṉ oḷḷiya pūkkaḷ piṇippu viṭa, pulam toṟum kuruku iṉam kal eṉa narala - pulaṅkaḷ tōṟum paṟavikaḷiṉ kūṭṭam kalleṉṟu olikka, akaṉṟu uṟi makaḷir aṇi tuṟantu naṭuṅka - tam talivarip pirintu taṅkiya makaḷir aḻakiḻantu naṭuṅka, aṟciram vantaṉṟu - paṉipparuvam vantuviṭṭatu (ātalāl);
13-15. amintaṉṟu itu eṉa - ip paruvam poruḷ vayiṟ pirivārkku ēṟṟa toṉṟeṉa eṇṇi, epporuḷ peṟiṉum piriyaṉmiṉ eṉa - ettuṇic ciṟanta poruḷip peṟuvatāyiṉum piriyātīr eṉṟu, tuṇiyuṭiyīrkkuc ceppuval - eṅkaṭkut tuṇiyāka
vuḷḷa numakkuc ceppuvēṉ, eṉa - eṉṟu nī nam talivarkkuk kūṟavum;
16-20. nalkāk kātalar - aruḷ ceyyāta kātalar, piriyiṉ - nammip pirintu celvarēl, yām - nām, nalaṉ uṇṭu tuṟanta pāḻpaṭu mēṉi nōkki - (avar) namatu nalattiṉi nukarntu kiviṭṭa pāḻpaṭṭa mēṉiyik kaṇṭu, nōy pora - nōy varutta, iṇar iṟupu uṭiyum neñcamoṭu - matuki iṟṟu uṭiyum neñcat tuṭaṉ, puṇarvu vēṭṭu - avar puṇartali virumpi, kaṭumpaṉi uḻantu - kaṭiya paṉiyāl varunti, eyiṟu tī piṟappat tiruki - paṟkaḷit tīyuṇṭākac kaṭittu, naṭuṅkutum - naṭuṅkuvēm.
(muṭipu) (tōḻi!) nī, aṟciram vantaṉṟu; itu amintaṉ ṟeṉa epporuḷ peṟiṉum piriyaṉmiṉō eṉat tuṇiyuṭi yīrkkuc ceppuval eṉa nalkāk kātalar piriyiṉ, yām mēṉi nōkki uṭiyum neñcamoṭu puṇarvuvēṭṭuk kaṭumpaṉi yuḻantu, tiruki, naṭuṅkutum.
tūval kaḻiya, vāṉ pū vāṭiyoṭu tuyalvara, pakaṉṟi vāliya malara, avari muki yaviḻa, tōṉṟip pū taḷiviṭa, kurukiṉam narala, makaḷir naṭuṅka, aṟciram vantaṉṟu eṉka.
(vi-ri) pācili potuḷiya putaltoṟum pakaṉṟi iṭiyiṭiyē malarntiruppatu, nīlaniṟamuṭiya tōṟkiṭukil iṭiyiṭi patitta kaṇṇāṭi pōlum eṉka. pacci - tōl. pāṇṭil - kaṇṇāṭi. tōl - tōlāl ākiya kiṭuku. `pāṇṭil niri tōl'1 eṉpataṉ uri kāṇka. aṟciram eṉṟatu īṇṭup piṉpaṉik kālatti. tuṇi yuṭiyīrkkup piriyaṉmiṉō, eṉac ceppuval eṉavum eṉṟu iyiyum. `tuṇiyuṭiyīrk keṉa' eṉṟu paḻiya patippiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa pāṭattiṉik koṇṭu, eṉavum eṉa ummi virittu urikkappaṭṭatu; `tuṇi yuṭi
yīrkkē' eṉṉum pāṭattiṟkup piriyaṉmiṉ eṉṟu talivarkku nī collumāṟu eṉakkut tuṇiyākavuḷḷa niṉakku yāṉ ceppuval eṉṟu talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟiṉāḷākak koḷka.
218. kuṟiñci
(tōḻi talimakaḷi iṭattuyttu vantu pakaṟkuṟinērnta vāypāṭṭāl talimakaṉi varivukaṭāyatu.)
		kiḷipā rāṭṭuṅ kaṭunaṭi vayakkaḷiṟu
		muḷitaru pūṭṭi vēṇṭu 2kuḷa karutta
		vāḷniṟa uruviṉ oḷiṟupu miṉṉip
		parūuvuṟip paḵṟuḷi citaṟivāṉ naviṉṟu
	5.	peruvari naḷircimi atira vaṭṭittup
		puyalēṟu uriiya viyaliruḷ naṭunāḷ
		viṟaliḻip polinta kāṇpiṉ cāyal
		taṭiit tiraṇṭaniṉ tōḷcērpu allatip
		paṭāa vākumeṅ kaṇṇeṉa nīyum
	10.	iruṇmayaṅ kiyāmat tiyavukkeṭa vilaṅki
		varivayaṅ kirumpuli vaḻaṅkunarp pārkkum
		perumali viṭarakam 1varalari teṉṉāy
		varaveḷi tāka eṇṇuti ataṉāṉ
		nuṇṇitiṟ kūṭṭiya paṭumāṇ āran
	15.	taṇṇitu kamaḻum niṉmārpu orunāḷ
		aṭiya muyaṅkē māyiṉ yāmum
		viṟaliḻi nekiḻac cāaytum atuvē
		aṉṉi yaṟiyiṉum aṟika alarvāy
		ampal mūtūr kēṭpiṉuṅ kēṭka
	20.	vaṇṭiṟi koṇṭa erimaruḷ tōṉṟiyoṭu
		oṇpū vēṅki kamaḻun
		taṇperuñ cāral pakalvan tīmē.			- kapilar.
(co-ḷ) 1-6. (taliva!) kiḷi pārāṭṭum kaṭu naṭi vayak kaḷiṟu - taṉ iṉaṅkaḷ pārāṭṭum kaṭiya celaviṉiyuṭiya valiya kaḷiṟu, muḷi tarupu ūṭṭi vēṇṭu kuḷaku arutta - mūṅkil muḷikaḷit tantu avaṟṟiṟku uṇpittu vēṇṭum taḻi
kaḷiyum uṇpikka, vāḷniṟa uruviṉ oḷiṟupu miṉṉi - vāḷiṉ niṟam pōlum uruvuṭaṉ oḷirumāṟu miṉṉi, parūu uṟip pal tuḷi citaṟi - pariya palavākiya maḻittuḷikaḷic citaṟi, vāṉ  naviṉṟu - vāṉilē payiṉṟu, peru vari naḷir cimi atira vaṭṭittu - periya maliyiṉ kuḷirnta ucci atirac cūḻntukoṇṭu, puyal - mēka māṉatu, ēṟu uriiya viyal iruḷ naṭunāḷ - iṭiyēṟṟuṭaṉ paranta mikka iruḷ koṇṭa naṭu iravil;
7-9. viṟal iḻip polinta kāṇpu iṉ cāyal - mēmpaṭṭa aṇikaḷāṟ polivuṟṟa kāṇṭaṟkiṉiya meṉmittaṉmi yiṉatāy, taṭii tiraṇṭa niṉ tōḷ cērpu allati - vaḷintu tiraṇṭa niṉ tōḷic cērntālallatu, paṭāa ākum em kaṇ eṉa - em kaṇkaḷ tuyilāvākum eṉṟu kūṟi;
9-13. nīyum iruḷ mayaṅku yāmattu - nīyum iruḷ ceṟinta naṭu yāmattē, iyavu keṭa vilaṅki - neṟi taṭumāṟalāṉ vilaki, vari vayaṅku irum puli vaḻaṅkunarp pārkkum - varikaḷ viḷaṅkum periya puli vaḻivaruvārip pārttirukkum, perumali viṭar akam vara aritu eṉṉāy - periya maliyiṉ piḷappiṉiyuṭiya iṭattil varutal ariteṉṟu niṉiyāyāy, vara eḷitāka eṇṇuti - varaṟku eḷiteṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟaṉi;
14-17. nuṇṇitil kūṭṭiya paṭu māṇ āram - nuṇmiyāka aṟintu pala maṇaṅkaḷik kalanta māṇpu poruntiya cantaṉam, taṇṇitu kamaḻum niṉ mārpu - taṇṇeṉak kamaḻum niṉ mārpiṉi, orunāḷ - orunāḷēṉum, aṭiya muyaṅkēm āyiṉ - poruntat taḻuvēmāṉāl, yāmum - yāṅkaḷum, viṟal iḻi nekiḻac cāaytum - mēmpaṭṭa aṇi nekiḻntu viḻa melivōm.
13. ataṉāl-,
17-22. atuvē - ataṉi, aṉṉi aṟiyiṉum aṟika - em aṉṉi aṟintiṭiṉum aṟintiṭuka, alar vāy ampal mūtūr kēṭpiṉum kēṭka - alar kūṟum peṇṭiratu vāyiṉiṉṟu varum paḻic coṟkaḷi mutiya emmūr kēṭpiṉum kēṭpatāka, vaṇṭu iṟi koṇṭa erimaruḷ tōṉṟiyoṭu - vaṇṭukaḷ taṅkutal koṇṭa tīyiṉi otta tōṉṟip pūvuṭaṉ, oṇpū vēṅki kamaḻum - oḷḷiya vēṅkip pū nāṟum, taṇ perum cāral - taṇṇiya periya malic cāralil, pakal vantīmē - pakalil varuvīrāka.
(muṭipu) taliva! puyal ēṟuṭaṉ uriiya naṭunāḷ nīyum niṉ tōḷ cērpu allati emkaṇ paṭāavākum eṉa, viṭarakam vara eṇṇuti; yāmum niṉmārpu orunāḷ muyaṅkēm āyiṉ iḻi nekiḻac cāytum; ataṉāl, atuvē aṉṉi aṟiyiṉum aṟika; mūtūr kēṭpiṉum kēṭka; tōṉṟiyoṭu vēṅki kamaḻum cāral pakal vantīmē.
(vi-ri) kiḷi pārāṭṭum - kiḷiyiṉip pārāṭṭum eṉṟumām. oḷiṟupu - oḷira eṉka. taṭii - paruttu eṉṟumām. cērpu- cērntāl eṉṉum poruṭṭu. nīyum eṉṉāy eṇṇuti eṉa iyiyum. aṭiya - muḻutum eṉṟumām. eṇṇuti, cāytum ataṉāl pakal vantīmē eṉak koṇṭukūṭṭi yurikka. atuvē eṉṟatu, pakal varutali. aṉṉi aṟiyiṉum aṟika, ampal mūtūr kēṭpiṉum kēṭka eṉṟatu, pakaṟ kuṟikkum iṭiyīṭu uḷatātal pulappaṭuttiyavāṟu.
(u-ṟi) "kiḷipārāṭṭum kaṭunaṭi vayakkaḷiṟu, muḷitaru pūṭṭi vēṇṭukuḷa karutta" eṉṟatu, nīyum taliviyi varintu koṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttic cuṟṟañcūḻa vāḻavēṇṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
(mē-ḷ) `mutalkaru uripporuḷ'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷi it tuṟikkē koṇṭu, itu kuṟiñcikku mutaluṅ karuvum vantu uripporuḷāṟ ciṟappeyti muṭintatu eṉṟār, nac.
219. pāli
(makaṭ pōkkiya tāy colliyatu.)
		cīrkeḻu viyaṉakarc cilampunaka viyali
		ōri yāyamoṭu pantuciṟi teṟiyiṉum
		vārā yōveṉ ṟēttip pērilip
		pakaṉṟi vāṉmalar paṉiniṟin tatupōl
	5.	pālpey vaḷḷañ cālki paṟṟi
		eṉpā ṭuṇṭaṉi yāyiṉ orukāl
		nunti pāṭum uṇṇeṉ ṟūṭṭip
		2piṟantataṟ koṇṭum ciṟantavi ceytiyāṉ
		nalampuṉin teṭuttaveṉ polantoṭik kuṟumakaḷ
	10.	aṟaṉi lāḷaṉō ṭiṟantaṉaḷ iṉiyeṉa
		maṟantamin tirā a neñcam nōvēṉ
		poṉvārn taṉṉa vivāl eyiṟṟuc
		cennāy verīiya pukaruḻi yoruttal
		poṟiyari viḷaviṉ puṉpuṟa viḷipuḻal
	15.	aḻaleṟi kōṭi tūkkaliṟ kōvalar
		kuḻaleṉa niṉiyum nīril nīḷiṭi
		maṭattaki meliyac cāay
		naṭakkuṅ kolleṉa nōval yāṉē.
- kayamaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-9. cīr keḻu viyaṉ nakar cilampu naka iyali - ciṟappupporuntiya periya maṉiyiṉkaṇ taṉ cilampu olittu viḷaṅka naṭantu, ōri āyamoṭu pantu ciṟitu eṟiyiṉum - viḷiyāṭum āya makaḷiruṭaṉ pantiṉic ciṟitu eṟintālum, vārāyō eṉṟu ētti - iṅku varuvāyāka eṉṟu aḻittup pārāṭṭi, pēr ilip pakaṉṟi vāl malar paṉi niṟintatu pōl - periya iliyiṉiyuṭiya pakaṉṟiyiṉ veḷḷiya pū paṉinīr niṟiyappeṟṟatupōla, pāl pey vaḷḷam cālki paṟṟi - pāl peyyappeṟṟa kiṇṇam niṟintataṉip paṟṟikkoṇṭu, eṉpāṭu uṇṭaṉiyāyiṉ - eṉ pakutikku itaṉi uṇṭāyāṉāl (iṉi), orukāl nunti pāṭum uṇ eṉṟu ūṭṭi - oru muṟi uṉ tantiyiṉ pakutikkum uṇpāyāka eṉṟu kūṟi yāṉ uṇpittu, piṟantataṟ koṇṭum ciṟantavi ceytu - avaḷ piṟantanāḷ toṭṭum ciṟappuk
kaḷic ceytu, yāṉ nalam puṉintu eṭutta eṉpolam toṭik kuṟumakaḷ - nāṉ iṅṅaṉam nalam pārāṭṭi vaḷartta poṉ vaḷiyal aṇinta eṉatu ciṟiya makaḷ;
10-11. aṟaṉ ilāḷaṉoṭu iṟantaṉaḷ iṉi eṉa - aṟa neṟi
yillātavaṉoṭu ippoḻutu kaṭantu ceṉṟāḷ eṉṟu, maṟantu amintu irā neñcam nōvēṉ - ciṟitum maṟantirutta lillāta neñcattāl varuntuvēṉ allēṉ;
12-18. poṉ vārntaṉṉa vi vāl eyiṟṟu - poṟ
kampiyiḻuttā lotta kūriya veḷḷiya paṟkaḷiyuṭiya, cennāy verīiya pukar uḻi oruttal - cennāyiṉi añciya puḷḷi
kaḷiyuṭiya kalimāṉ, poṟi ari viḷaviṉ pul puṟa viḷi puḻal - poṟikaḷ poruntiya ariyiṉiyuṭiya viḷaviṉkaṉiyiṉ pulliya ōṭṭil tōṉṟiya tuḷiyil, aḻal eṟi kōṭi tūkkaliṉ - veppattik koṇṭu vīcum mēlkāṟṟu nuḻintu icittaliṉ, kōvalar kuḻal eṉa niṉiyum - kōvalaratu kuḻalōci eṉa niṉintu (pātukāvalāka) āṅkuc cellum, nīr il nīḷ iṭi - nīr illāta nīṇṭa neṟiyil, maṭat taki meliyac cāay - taṉatu maṭappattiyuṭiya aḻaku keṭa melintu, naṭakkum
kol eṉa yāṉ nōval - naṭappaḷō eṉavē yāṉ nōkiṉṟēṉ.
(muṭipu) yāṉ nalam puṉinteṭutta kuṟumakaḷ, aṟaṉilāḷaṉoṭu iṉi iṟantaṉaḷ eṉa neñcam nōvēṉ; nīril nīḷiṭi meliyac cāay naṭakkum kolleṉa yāṉ nōval.
(vi-ri) maṉiyilē iyalip pantu ciṟi teṟiyiṉum vārāyō eṉṟu aḻittāḷ eṉpatu taliviyiṉ meṉmit taṉmi
yiṉiyum, pāl pey vaḷḷam paṟṟi, eṉpāṭuṇṭaṉi yāyiṉ orukāl nunti pāṭum uṇṇeṉ ṟūṭṭiṉā ḷeṉpatu, avaḷatu celvak kaḷippiyum uṇarttiyapaṭiyām. pāṭu - kūṟu, paṅku. celvak kuṭiyiṟ piṟantu uṇavu viruppiṉṟi yirukkum ciṟuvar ciṟumiyarit tāymār ivvāṟellām kūṟi uṇpittal iyalpu.
cennāy verīiya uḻi oruttal, kuḻal eṉa niṉintu pātukāvalākac cellum eṉka.
220. neytal
(iravukkuṟivantu nīṅkun talimakaṉi yetirppaṭṭut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		ūruñ cēriyum uṭaṉiyin talareḻat
		tēroṭu maṟukiyum paṇimoḻi payiṟṟiyum
		keṭāat tīyiṉ urukeḻu cellūrk
		kaṭāa yāṉik 1kuḻūuccaman tatiya
	5.	maṉmaruṅku aṟutta maḻuvāḷ neṭiyōṉ
		muṉmuyaṉ ṟaritiṉiṉ muṭitta vēḷvik
		kayiṟari yātta kāṇṭaku vaṉappiṉ
		aruṅkaṭi neṭuntūṇ pōla yāvaruṅ
		kāṇa lākā māṇeḻi lākam
	10.	uḷḷutoṟum paṉikkum neñciṉi nīyē
		neṭumpuṟa 2niliyiṉi varuntiṉi yāyiṉ
		muḻaṅkukaṭa lōtaṅ kālik koṭkum
		paḻampal nelliṉ ūṇū rāṅkaṇ
		nōlā 3irumpuḷ pōla neñcamarntu
	15.	kātaṉ māṟāk kāmar puṇarcciyiṉ
		iruṅkaḻi mukanta ceṅkōl avvali
		muṭaṅkupuṟa iṟavō ṭiṉamīṉ ceṟikkum
		neṭuṅkatirk kaḻaṉit taṇcāyk kāṉattu
		yāṇart taṇpaṇi yuṟumeṉak kāṉal
	20.	āya māynta cāyiṟip paṇittōḷ
		nalleḻil citiyā vēmañ
		colliṉit teyyayān teḷiyu māṟē.        
- maturi marutaṉiḷanākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-2. ūrum cēriyum uṭaṉ iyintu alar eḻa - ūriṉkaṇṇum cēriyiṉkaṇṇum oruṅkē cērntu alar eḻā niṟka, tēroṭu maṟukiyum paṇimoḻi payiṟṟiyum - tēroṭu vantu tirintum paṇinta moḻikaḷip palakāṟ kūṟiyum;
3-11. keṭāt tīyiṉ urukeḻu cellūr - eṉṟum nīṅkāta vēḷvit tīyiṉiyuṭiya aḻaku viḷaṅkum cellūriṉkaṇ, kaṭāa yāṉik kuḻūuc camam tatiya - matamporuntiya yāṉiyiṉ kūṭṭam pōrmuṉiyil aḻiya, maṉ maruṅku aṟutta maḻuvāḷ neṭiyōṉ - maṉṉar vaḻiyi aḻitta paracurāmaṉ, muṉ muyaṉṟu aritiṉil muṭitta vēḷvi - muṉpu aritiṉil muyaṉṟu muṭitta vēḷvikkaṇ, kayiṟu ariyātta kāṇṭaku vaṉappiṉ - kayiṟṟiṉi ariyiṟ cuṟṟiya kāṇattakka aḻakiṉiyuṭiya, arum kaṭi neṭutūṇ pōla - ariya kāvalik koṇṭa nīṇṭa vēḷvittūṇ pōla, yāvarum kāṇal ākā māṇ eḻil ākam - yāvarum kāṇutal iyalāta māṇpuṟṟa aḻakiṉiyuṭiya em taliviyiṉ mārpiṉi, uḷḷu
toṟum paṉikkum neñciṉiyāki - niṉiyuntoṟum naṭuṅkum neñciṉiyuṭiyiyāki, nīyē neṭumpuṟa niliyiṉi varuntiṉi yāyiṉ - nītāṉ puṟattilē neṭitu niṟṟali yuṭiyiyāki varuntiṉi yātaliṉ;
12-15. muḻaṅku kaṭal ōtam kāli koṭkum - muḻaṅkum kaṭaliṉ tirikaḷ kāliyil aliyum, paḻam pal nelliṉ ūṇūr āṅkaṇ - mikutiyāṉa paḻiya nelliyuṭiya ūṇu riṭattu, nōlā irum puḷpōla - oṉṟiyoṉṟu pirintirut talip poṟāta periya paṟaviyāya makaṉṟilippōla, neñcu amarntu - oruvar neñcilē oruvar porunti, kātal māṟā kāmar puṇarcciyiṉ - kātal nīṅkāta aḻakiya puṇarcciyiṉālē;
16-22. iru kaḻi mukanta ceṅkōl avvali - periya kaḻiyit tuḻavi mukanta nēriya kōlkaḷiyuṭiya aḻakiya vali, muṭaṅku puṟa iṟavōṭu iṉa mīṉ ceṟikkum - vaḷinta puṟattiṉi yuṭiya iṟā mīṉuṭaṉ ēṉimīṉ iṉaṅkaḷiyum kuvikkum, neṭu katirk kaḻaṉi taṇ cāykkāṉattu - nīṇṭa katirkaḷiyuṭiya vayalkaḷiyuṭiya taṇṇiya cāykkāṉam eṉṉum ūriṭattuḷḷa, yāṇar taṇpaṇi uṟum eṉa - aḻakiya kuḷirnta mūṅkili okkum eṉṟu, kāṉal āyam āynta - kaṭaṟ cōliyil tōḻimār āyntu pārāṭṭiya, cāy iṟip paṇittōḷ - vaḷinta muṉ kiyiṉi yuṭiya parutta ivaḷ tōḷkaḷ, nal eḻil citiyā ēmam - nalla aḻaku keṭāmikku ētuvāya pātukāvali, yām teḷiyumāṟu iṉic col - yām teḷiya uṇarumāṟu ippoḻutu nī kūṟuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) taliva! nīyē tēroṭu maṟukiyum paṇimoḻi payiṟṟiyum, neṭiyōṉ muṭitta vēḷvi neṭuntūṇ pōlak kāṇalākā eḻil ākam uḷḷutoṟum paṉikku neñciṉi yāki, neṭumpuṟa niliyiṉi varuntiṉi yātalāṉ, nōlā irumpuḷ pōlak kāmar puṇarcciyiṉāl, ivaḷ paṇit tōḷ eḻil citiyā ēmam yām teḷiyumāṟu col.
(vi-ri) cēri - ūriṉ oru pakuti, teru. `ōrūr vāḻiṉuñ cēri vārār'1 eṉpatu kāṇka. keṭāttī - nīṅkāta vēḷvit tī. urukeḻu- uṭkup poruntiya eṉṟumām. maruṅku - vaḻi. maḻuvāḷ - maḻuvākiya vāḷeṉa irupeyaroṭṭu. maḻu - paracu. paracurāmaṉ neṭumāliṉ tōṟṟamākaliṉ neṭiyōṉ eṉṟār. cellūrkkaṇ neṭiyōṉ muṭitta vēḷvittūṇpōlak kāṇalākā ākam eṉka. varuntiṉiyāyiṉ - varuntiṉi yātaliṉ eṉka. irumpuḷ - kātaliṟ ciṟanta makaṉṟil; makaṉṟiṟ paṟavikaḷ. āṇum peṇṇum ciṟitum pirintirukkappeṟātaṉa eṉpatu `pūviṭip paṭiṉum yāṇṭu kaḻintaṉṉa, nīruṟi makaṉṟiṟ puṇarcci pōlap pirivaritākiya taṇṭāk kāmam'1 eṉpataṉāl aṟika. cāykkāṉam - tiruc cāykkāṭu eṉṉum pati. teyya, acic col. nī tēroṭu vantu tirintum kuṟiyirantum cellutalāl, ūrum cēriyum alar uṇṭāka, ataṉāl, talivi iṟceṟikkappaṭṭu niṉṉāṟ kāṇṭaṟku ariyaḷāka iruttaliṉ, nī avaḷ ākatti niṉiyuntōṟum naṭuṅkum neñcuṭaṉ palakālum vantu puṟattē niṉṟu varunti vaṟitu mīḷkiṉṟaṉi; ataṉāl, taliviyiṉ tōḷum eḻil citikiṉṟatu; iṉi avvāṟu citi yāmikku ētuvākiya ēmam iṉṉateṉa yām teḷintilē mākaliṉ, ataṉit teḷiyumāṟu nī kūṟuka eṉpāḷ pōṉṟu, nī taliviyi varintu koḷḷutalē  pātukāvalākum eṉak kuṟippiṟ pulap paṭuttit, tōḻi talivaṉi varivu kaṭāyiṉāḷ eṉka.
221. pāli
(talimakaṟkup pōkkuṭaṉpaṭṭa tōḻi, talimakaṭkup 
pōkkuṭaṉpaṭac colliyatu.)
		naṉiviḷi naṟaviṉ tēṟal māntip
		puṉiviṉi nallil tarumaṇaṟ kuviip
		pommal ōti emmakaḷ maṇaṉeṉa
		vatuvi ayarntaṉar namarē ataṉāl
	5.	putuvatuvi puṉinta cēyili 2veḷvēl
		matiyuṭam paṭṭa miyaṇaṟ kāḷi
		3vāṅkuciṉi malinta tiraḷari marāattut
		tēmpāy melliṇar taḷiroṭu koṇṭuniṉ
		taṇṇaṟum mucci puṉiya avaṉoṭu
	10.	kaḻikaviṉ pōkiya maḻiyuyar naṉantalik
		kaḷiṟṟiri piḻittaliṟ kayavāy vēṅki
		kāyciṉañ ciṟantu kuḻumaliṉ verīi
		irumpiṭi yiriyuñ cōli
		aruñcuram cēṟal ayarntaṉeṉ yāṉē.
- kayamaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-4. naṉi viḷi naṟaviṉ tēṟal mānti - arumpi ṉiṉṟum uṇṭākiya kaḷḷiṉ teḷivip paruki, puṉiviṉi nal il tarumaṇal kuvii - puṉiyappaṭṭa viṉittiṟam aminta nalla maṉiyilē koṇarnta maṇalik kuvittup parappi, pommal ōti em makaḷ maṇaṉ  eṉa - polivuṟṟa kūntali yuṭiya em makaḷukku maṇam eṉṟu kūṟi, vatuvi ayarntaṉar namar - namtāy tantiyar maṇattiṟku āvaṉa ceytuḷār;
4-14. ataṉāl-, putuvatu puṉinta cēy ili veḷ vēl - putitākac ceyyappaṭṭa cevviya iliporuntiya veḷḷiya vēliyuṭiya, mati uṭampaṭṭa - uṭaṉ pōkkiṟku iyinta aṟiviṉi yuṭiya, mi aṇal kāḷi - kariya tāṭiyiṉiyuṭiya kāḷi pōṉṟa nam talivaṉ, vāṅku ciṉi malinta tiraḷ ari marāattu - vaḷinta kiḷikaḷ mikka tiraṇṭa ariyiṉiyuṭiya veṇ kaṭappa marattiṉ, tēmpāy mel iṇar taḷiroṭu koṇṭu - tēṉ pāyum melliya pūṅkottukkaḷit taḷiruṭaṉ koṇṭuvantu, niṉ taṇ naṟum mucci puṉiya - niṉatu taṇṇiya nalla mayir muṭiyiṟcūṭa, avaṉoṭu - at talivaṉuṭaṉ, kaḻikaviṉ pōkiya maḻi uyar naṉantali - mūṅkilkaḷ aḻakiḻanta maḻipeyyātu mēlēceṉṟa akaṉṟa pāliyiṭattē, kaḷiṟu iri piḻittaliṉ kayavāy vēṅki - kaḷiṟākiya iri tappi viṭṭamiyiṉ periya vāyiṉiyuṭiya vēṅki, kāy ciṉam ciṟantu kuḻumaliṉ verīi - kotikkum ciṉam mikku muḻaṅkutaliṉ añci, irumpiṭi iriyum cōli arum curam cēṟal - kariya peṇyāṉikaḷ nilikeṭṭu ōṭum kāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya ariya curaneṟiyiṟ cellutali, yāṉ ayarntaṉeṉ - yāṉ virumpi ēṟṟuk koṇṭēṉ.
(muṭipu) namar emmakaḷ maṇaṉ eṉa vatuvi ayarntaṉar! ataṉāl, matiyuṭampaṭṭa kāḷi melliṇar taḷiroṭu koṇṭu niṉ muccipuṉiya avaṉoṭu nī arum curam cēṟal, yāṉ ayarntaṉeṉ.
(vi-ri) vatuvi ayarntaṉar namar eṉṟatu, namar vēṟṟu variviṉi uṭaṉpaṭṭār eṉṟapaṭi aruñcuram cēṟal ayarntaṉeṉ eṉṟatu talivaṉuṭaṉ pōkkuṭaṉpaṭṭēṉ eṉṟavāṟu. putuvatu puṉinta cēyili veḷvēṟ kāḷi eṉṟamiyāl, avaṉ, kāṭṭiṭiyē ettuṇiyum ētamiṉṟik kākkavallaṉ eṉpatum, melliṇar taḷiroṭukoṇṭu niṉ mucci puṉiya eṉṟamiyāl, curattilē naṭantu cellum varuttam tōṉṟāvāṟu aṉpiṉāl niṉṉip pārāṭṭi aḻittēkuvaṉ eṉpatum kūṟiyavāṟām.
222. kuṟiñci
(talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat tōḻi talimakaṭkuc colliyatu.)
		vāṉuṟa nivanta nīlniṟap perumalik
		kāṉa nāṭaṉ uṟīiya 1 nōykkuṉ
		mēṉi āynalan tolitaliṉ moḻiveṉ
		muḻavumukam pularāk kalikoḷ āṅkaṇ
	5.	kaḻāarp peruntuṟi viḻaviṉ āṭum
		īṭṭeḻil polinta ēntukuvavu moympiṉ
		āṭṭa ṉatti nalaṉayan turiit
		tāḻiruṅ katuppiṉ kāviri vavvaliṉ
		mātiran tuḻii matimaruṇ ṭalanta
	10.	āti manti kātalaṟ kāṭṭip
		paṭukaṭal pukka pāṭalcāl ciṟappiṉ
		maruti yaṉṉa māṇpukaḻ peṟīiyar
		ceṉmō vāḻi tōḻi paṉṉāḷ
		uravurum ēṟoṭu mayaṅki
	15.	iravuppeyal poḻinta īrntaṇ āṟē.                     - paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 13. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
1-3. vāṉ uṟa nivanta nīl niṟap perumalik kāṉa nāṭaṉ - vāṉil mika uyarnta nīla niṟam poruntiya perumaliyiṉi aṭuttuḷḷa kāṭṭunāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya talivaṉ, uṟīiya nōykku uṉmēṉi āy nalam tolitaliṉ moḻiveṉ - aṭivitta nōyāl niṉatu mēṉiyiṉ āynta aḻaku oḻitaliṉ yāṉ itaṉik kūṟuvēṉ, kēṭpāyāka;
4-8. muḻavu mukam pularā kalikoḷ āṅkaṇ - muḻaviṉ oli māṟāta āravāram nikaḻum iṭamākiya, kaḻāarperum tuṟi viḻaviṉ āṭum - kaḻār eṉṉumiṭattuḷḷa periya tuṟiyiṭattu viḻāvil āṭiya, īṭṭu eḻil polinta ēntu kuvavu moympiṉ - tiraṇṭa aḻakāṟ polivuṟṟa nimirnta tiraṇṭa tōḷiṉiyuṭiya, āṭṭaṉ atti nalaṉ nayantu urii - āṭṭaṉatti eṉpāṉ aḻakiṉi virumpip parantuvantu, tāḻ iru katuppiṉ kāviri vavvaliṉ - tāḻntu toṅkum kariya kūntaliyuṭiya kāviri kavarntu koṇṭamiyiṉ;
9-12. mātiram tuḻii mati maruṇṭu alanta āti manti - ticiyellām taṉ kaṇavaṉit tēṭi matimayaṅki vāṭiya avaṉ maṉiyāḷ ākiya ātimantikku, kātalaṉ kāṭṭi - avaḷ kātalaṉik kāṭṭi, paṭukaṭal pukka pāṭal cāl ciṟappiṉ maruti aṉṉa - olikkum kaṭaliṟ pukku maṟinta pāṭutal aminta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya marutiyiyotta, māṇpukaḻ peṟīiyar - ciṟanta pukaḻip peṟum poruṭṭu;
13-15. pal nāḷ uravu urum ēṟoṭu mayaṅki - pala nāḷum valiya iṭiyēṟṟuṭaṉ kūṭi, iravu peyal poḻinta īr taṇ āṟu- iravellām maḻi coritaliṉ īram mikka cēṟṟu neṟiyil (nam talivaṉit tēṭi), ceṉmō - celvōmāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi vāḻi, niṉ mēṉi nalam tolitaliṉ moḻiveṉ: āṭṭaṉatti nalam nayantu kāviri vavvaliṉ, avaṉi ātimantikkuk kāṭṭik kaṭaliṟ pukka maruti yaṉṉa pukaḻ peṟīiyar, īrntaṇ āṟu ceṉmō.
(vi-ri) nōykkeṉmēṉi eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ  `tāyatti ṉaṭiyā'1 eṉṉuñ cūttira vitiyāl, tōḻi talivi uṟuppit taṉṉuṟuppākak koṇṭu kūṟiṉāḷeṉka. ātimanti kātalaṟ keṭuttu alamanta ceyti innūluḷ vēṟu ceyyuṭ kaḷilum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu; `vāṭal uḻiñcil'2 eṉṉum ceyyuḷiṉ uriyiṟ kāṇka. āṭṭaṉattiyik kāviri vavvic ceṉṟataṉi avaṉatu nalam nayantu kavarntu ceṉṟatākat taṟkuṟippēṟṟa vakiyāṟ kūṟiṉār. kāviriyip peṇṇāka uruvakittamikkēṟpa, tāḻiruṅ katuppiṉ eṉa aṭi koṭukkappaṭṭatu. kātalaṟ kāṭṭip paṭukaṭal pukka maruti eṉṟamiyāl, maruti yeṉpāḷ taṉṉuyirkku añcātu kaṭaliṟ ceṉṟu, ātimantikku avaḷ kaṇavaṉik kāṭṭit tāṉ uyir viṭuttāḷ eṉpatu pōtarum. ataṉāl avaḷ pukaḻ mikac ciṟantatāyiṉamiyiṉ, `maruti yaṉṉa māṇpukaḻ' eṉappaṭṭatu.  `maruti yaṉṉa māṇpukaḻ peṟīiyar ceṉmō' eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟiyatu nāmum talivaṉit tēṭic ceṉṟu avaṉik kāṇamāṭṭātu uyirtuṟantē māyiṉum ataṉāl viḻuppukaḻ eytuvēm eṉpāḷ pōṉṟu talimakaṉi varivu kaṭāyiṉavā ṟeṉka. celkam eṉpatu ceṉmō eṉa vikāramāyiṟṟu.
223. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.)
		pirital valliyar itunat tuṟantōr
		maṟantum amikuvar kolleṉ ṟeṇṇi
		āḻal vāḻi tōḻi kēḻal
		vaḷimaruppu uṟaḻum uḷi 3neṭum peruṅkāy
	5.	naṉimutir murukkiṉ ciṉicēr peruṅkal
		kāyciṉak kaṭuvaḷi eṭuttaliṉ  4veṅkāṭṭu
		aḻalpoḻi yāṉiyiṉ aiyeṉat tōṉṟum
		niḻalil ōmi nīril nīḷiṭi
		iṟantaṉa rāyiṉum kātalar namvayiṉ
	10.	maṟantukaṇ paṭutal yāvatu puṟantāḻpu
		ampaṇi neṭuntōḷ taṅkit tumpi
		ariyiṉaṅ kaṭukkuñ curivaṇar aimpāl
		1nuṇkē ḻaṭaṅka vārip piyuḷkeṭa
		naṉmuki atiraṟ pōtoṭu kuvaḷit
	15.	taṇnaṟuṅ kamaḻtoṭi vēyntaniṉ
		maṇṇār kūntal marīiya tuyilē.
- pālipāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō.
(co-ḷ) 1-3. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, pirital valliyar nattuṟantōr - nammip piritaṟku maṉavali eytiṉārāki aṅṅaṉam nammip pirintu ceṉṟār, maṟantum amikuvar kol eṉṟu itu eṇṇi - nammi maṟantu taṅkutalum ceyvarō eṉṟu itaṉi eṇṇi, āḻal - tuyaril aḻuntātē;
3-9. kēḻal vaḷi maruppu uṟaḻum uḷi neṭu perum kāy - paṉṟiyiṉ vaḷinta kompiṉi otta ārkkiṉiyuṭiya neṭiya periya kāyiṉiyuṭiya, naṉi mutir murukkiṉ ciṉicēr peruṅkal - arumpu mutirnta murukka marattiṉ kiḷi poruntiya periya kallāṉatu, kāyciṉa kaṭu vaḷi eṭuttaliṉ - mikka vēka
muṭiya kaṭiya kāṟṟu mūṭṭutaliṉ, vem kāṭṭu aḻal poḻi yāṉiyiṉi eṉat tōṉṟum - vemmi mikka kāṭṭil aḻalāl mūṭappeṟṟa yāṉiyippōṉṟu viyappuṟat tōṉṟum, niḻal il ōmi nīr il nīḷ iṭi - niḻalaṟṟa ōmi marattiṉiyuṭiya nīr illāta nīṇṭa curatti, iṟantaṉar āyiṉum - kaṭantu ceṉṟaṉarāyiṉum, kātalar - namatu kātalar;
9-16. puṟam tāḻpu - puṟattil tāḻntu, ampaṇi neṭutōḷ taṅki - aḻakiya mūṅkil pōṉṟa nīṇṭa tōḷil paṭintu, tumpi ari iṉam kaṭukkum - tumpi vaṇṭiṉ kūṭṭatti okkum, curivaṇar nuṇ kēḻ aimpāl - curintu vaḷinta nuṇṇiya karuniṟam vāynta kūntali, piyuḷ keṭa aṭaṅka vāri - varuttam oḻiya muḻuvatum vāri, nalmuki aṟal pōtoṭu - kāṭṭumallikiyiṉ nalla arumpuṭaṉ kūṭiya malaroṭu, taṇ naṟum kuvaḷi kamaḻ toṭi vēynta - taṇṇiya naṟiya kuvaḷimalari vittut toṭutta nāṟum māliyic cūṭṭiya, niṉ maṇ ār kūntal marīiya tuyil - niṉatu maṇṇutal aminta kūntaliṟ poruntiya tuyili, nam vayiṉ - nammiṭattē taṅkip peṟutali, maṟantu kaṇpaṭutal yāvatu - maṟantu āṅkut tuyilutal eṅṅaṉam iyalvatu?
(muṭipu) tōḻi, vāḻi, nattuṟantōr, maṟantum amikuvar kol eṉṟu itu eṇṇi āḻal; nam kātalar, nīril nīḷiṭi iṟantaṉarāyiṉum, niṉ maṇṇār kūntal marīiya tuyil maṟantu kaṇpaṭutal yāvatu?
(vi-ri) murukkiṉ cenniṟap pūkkaḷāṟ cūḻappeṟṟa kal, kāṭṭilē aḻalāṟ cūḻappeṟṟa yāṉipōlat tōṉṟu meṉṟār. kāyciṉak kaṭu vaḷi eṭuttaliṉ eṉṟatu, kāṭṭilē aḻal cūḻutaṟku ētuk kūṟiyapaṭiyām. aḻal poti yāṉi yeṉap pāṭam koḷḷalumām. tōṉṟum nīḷiṭi eṉavum, ōmi nīḷ iṭi eṉavum, nīril nīḷiṭi eṉavum taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. maṇ - kaḻuvutal; mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar - kātalar kūntal marīiya tuyili maṟantu āṇṭuk kaṇtuyil ceyyārātaliṉ, virintu varuvar eṉak kūṟit, tōḻi talimakaḷi āṟṟuvittā ḷeṉka.
224. mulli
(viṉimuṟṟiya talimakaṉ tērppākaṟkuc colliyatu.)
		celka pāka elliṉṟu poḻutē
		vallōṉ aṭaṅkukayi ṟamippak kollaṉ
		vicittuvāṅku turuttiyiṉ veyya uyirāk
		koṭunukat tiyātta taliya kaṭunaṭik
	5.	kāṟkaṭup paṉṉa kaṭuñcelal ivuḷi
		pālkaṭi nuriyiṉ parūumitap paṉṉa
		vālveṇ teviṭṭal vaḻivār nuṇakkañ
		cilampi nūliṉ uṇaṅkuvaṉa pāṟic
		cāntupular akalam maṟuppak kāṇṭakap
	10.	putunalam peṟṟa veytunīṅku puṟavil
		teṟinaṭi marikkaṇam iriya maṉiyōḷ
		aituṇaṅka valci peytumuṟuk kuṟutta
		tirimarak kuralici kaṭuppa varimaṇal
		alaṅkukatirt tikiri āḻi pōḻa
	15.	varuṅkol tōḻinam iṉṉuyirt tuṇiyeṉac
		cilkol  elvaḷi yoṭukkip palkāl
		aruṅkaṭi viyaṉakar nōkki
		varuntumāl aḷiyaḷ tiruntiḻi tāṉē.
- 1 āvūr mūlaṅkiḻār makaṉār peruntalic cāttaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 15. tōḻi-,
2-15. vallōṉ aṭaṅku kayiṟu amippa - vallōṉākiya pākaṉ aṭaṅkic celvataṟku uriya kaṭivāḷak kayiṟṟiṉip pūṭṭa, kollaṉ vicittu vāṅku turuttiyiṉ veyya uyirā - kollaṉ valittu iḻukkum turuttiyiṉippōla veppamākap perumūccu viṭṭu, koṭu nukattu yātta taliya - vaḷinta nukattiṭattē kaṭṭap peṟṟa taliyiṉavākiya, kaṭu naṭi kāl kaṭuppu aṉṉa kaṭum celal ivuḷi - kaṭiya naṭiyiṉiyum kāṟṟiṉ vēkatti yotta virinta ōṭṭattiṉiyumuṭiya kutirikaḷiṉ, pāl kaṭi nuriyiṉ parūu mitappu aṉṉa - pāl kaṭiyuṅkāl eḻum veṇṇeyiṉ periya mitappiṉi yotta, vāl veṇ teviṭṭal vaḻi vār nuṇakkam - mika veḷḷiyatāṉa vāyiṉ teviṭṭalāya piṉṉē vaḻintiṭum melliya nuri, cilampi nūliṉ nuṇaṅkuvaṉa pāṟi- cilampiyiṉ nūlpōla nuṇukuvaṉavāyc citaṟi, cāntu pular akalam maṟuppa - cantaṉam pūcip pularnta mārpil maṟuc ceyyavum, kāṇtaka putunalam peṟṟa veytu nīṅku puṟavil - aḻaku peṟap  putunalattiṉip peṟṟa vemmi nīṅkiya kāṭṭil, teṟi naṭi marikkaṇam iriya - kutittuc cellum mariyiṉaṅkaḷ añci
yōṭavum, maṉiyōḷ aitu uṇaṅku valci peytu muṟukku uṟutta - maṉivi patamākak kāynta ariciyip peytu cuṟṟutal ceyta, tirimarak kuralici kaṭuppa - cuḻalum tirikiyiṉ kuraloli okka, vari maṇal - varippaṭṭa maṇalil, alaṅku katir tikiri āḻi pōḻa - cuḻalum katiriṉiyuṭiya vaṭṭa āḻi aṟuttuk koṇṭu cellavum, nam iṉ uyirttuṇi varum kol eṉa - namatu iṉiya uyirttuṇivarāya kātalar varuvar kol eṉṟu; 
16-18. cil kōl el vaḷi oṭukki - cilavāya tiraṇṭa oḷi poruntiya vaḷiyiṉi yoṭukki, pal kāl aruṅkaṭi viyaṉ nakar nōkki - palamuṟi ariya kāvaluṭiya periya maṉiyiṉ puṟattē vantu pārttu, aḷiyaḷ tiruntu iḻi - iraṅkattakkāḷākiya tiruntiya aṇiyiṉiyuṭiya nam kātali, varuntum - varuntuvāḷ;
1. elliṉṟu poḻutē - poḻutum iravāyiṟṟu ātaliṉ, celka pāka - pākaṉē virintu nam tēr celvatāka.
(muṭipu) `tōḻi! iṉṉuyirttuṇi varuṅkol' eṉap palkāl viyaṉakar nōkki aḷiyaḷ tiruntiḻi varuntum; poḻutum elliṉṟu; pāka, (tēr) celka.
(vi-ri) celka - celuttuka eṉap piṟaviṉip poruṭṭāk kalumām. ivuḷiyiṉ teviṭṭal eṉka. teviṭṭal - vāy kutaṭṭutalāl uṇṭākum vilāḻinīr. maṟuppa - maṟucceyya. `kāṇṭakap putu nalam peṟṟa veytu nīṅku puṟavil' eṉṟamiyāl, kārpparuvam vantami peṟṟām. muṟukkuṟutta - cuḻaṟṟiviṭṭa. tirimaram - itu tiriki eṉa vaḻaṅkum. tikiriyāya āḻi eṉa irupeyaroṭṭumām. cuḻalum vaḷikaḷic ceṟittu eṉpāṉ, elvaḷi oṭukki eṉṟāṉ.
225. pāli
(poruḷ kaṭik kūṭṭiya neñciṟkut talimakaṉ colliyatu.)
		aṉpum maṭaṉuñ cāyalum iyalpum
		eṉpum nekiḻkkuṅ kiḷaviyum piṟavum
		oṉ ṟupaṭu koḷkiyoṭu ōrāṅku muyaṅki
		iṉṟē yivaṇa māki nāḷip
	5.	putalivar āṭamit tumpi kuyiṉṟa
		akalā antuḷi kōṭi mukattaliṉ
		nīrkkiyaṅ kiṉanirip piṉṟi vārkōl
		āykkuḻaṟ pāṇiyiṉ aituvan ticikkun
		tēkkamal cōlik kaṭaṟōṅ karuñcurattu
	10.	yātta tūṇit talitiṟan tavipōṟ
		pūtta iruppik kuḻipoti kuviyiṇar
		kaḻaltuḷi muttiṟ cennilattu utira
		maḻituḷi maṟanta aṅkuṭic cīṟūrc
		cēkkuvaṅ kollō neñcē pūppuṉi
	15.	puyaleṉa olivarun tāḻiruṅ kūntal
		ceṟitoṭi muṉkinaṅ kātali
		aṟivañar nōkkamum pulaviyum niṉintē.
- eyiṉanti makaṉār iḷaṅkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 14. neñcē-,
1-4. aṉpum maṭaṉum cāyalum iyalpum - aṉpum maṭaṉum meṉmiyum oḻukkamum, eṉpum nekiḻkkum kiḷaviyum - eṉpiyum nekiḻvikkum collum, piṟavum - ēṉiyavum, oṉṟupaṭu koḷkiyoṭu - taliviyuṭaṉ oṉṟupaṭṭuḷḷa naṟkoḷkiyuṭiyēmāy, ōrāṅku muyaṅki - oru peṟṟiyē taḻuvi; iṉṟu ivaṇam āki - iṉṟu ivviṭattēm āki;
4-13. nāḷi - nāḷip pōḻtil, putal ivar āṭu ami - putalkaḷ paranta iṭattēyuḷḷa aciyum mūṅkiṟkaṇṇē, tumpi kuyiṉṟa akalā am tuḷi - vaṇṭu tuḷitta kuṟukiya aḻakiya tuḷi vaḻiyē, kōṭi mukattaliṉ - mēlkāṟṟup pukuntu varutaliṉ (eḻum oli), nīrkku iyaṅku iṉa nirippiṉṟi - nīrparukac cellum kūṭṭamāya āṉirikkup piṉṉē varum, vārkōl āykkuḻal pāṇiyiṉ aitu vantu icikkum - nīṇṭa kōliṉiyuṭiya āyartam kuḻaliṉ iciyeṉa aḻakitāka vantu olikkum, tēkku amal cōli kaṭaṟu ōṅku arucurattu - tēkkumaraṅkaḷ niṟinta cōliyiṉiyuṭiya kāṭu uyarnta ariya pālivaḻiyiluḷḷa, yātta tūṇi tali tiṟantavi pōl - ampukaḷit tokutta puṭṭilkaḷ mūṭi tiṟakkap peṟṟaṉa pōṟ kāṇappeṟum, pūtta iruppik kuḻi poti kuvi iṇar - pūttuḷa iruppiyiṉ taḷir potiyap peṟṟa kuvinta (tuḷiyuṭiya) pūṅkottukkaḷ, kaḻal tuḷi muttil cennilattu utira - nūliṉiṉṟu kaḻaṉṟu viḻum tuḷi poruntiya muttippōla civanta nilattē utira, maḻi tuḷi maṟanta - maḻipeytali maṟanta iṭamāya, am kuṭi cīṟūr - aḻakiya kuṭikaḷiyuṭiya ciṟiya ūriṉ kaṇṇē;
14-17. pū puṉi puyal eṉa olivarum tāḻ iru kūntal - pūvāṟ puṉiyappeṟṟa mēkampōlat taḻitta tāḻnta kariya kūntaliṉiyum, ceṟi toṭi muṉki - neruṅkiya vaḷikaḷi yuṭiya muṉ kiyiṉiyum  uṭiya, nam kātali aṟivu añar nōkkamum pulaviyum niṉintu - nam kātaliyiṉ aṟivu kalaṅkiya pārviyiṉiyum pulaviyiṉiyum niṉintu, cēkkuvam kollō - (taṉittut) taṅkiyiruppōmō?
(muṭipu) neñcē! taliviyuṭaṉ ōrāṅku muyaṅki iṉṟē ivaṇamāki, nāḷi, aruñcurattuc cīṟūr, kātali nōkkamum pulaviyum niṉintu cēkkuvam kollō?
(vi-ri) aṉpu - oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyāmik kētuvākiya kātal, maṭaṉ - oruvar kuṟṟam oruvar aṟiyāmi. cāyal - meṉmit taṉmi. iyalpu - oḻukkam. `cālpum viyappum iyalpum'1  eṉpatiṉ uri kāṇka. piṟa eṉṟatu ceṟivu, niṟi, aṟivu mutalāyiṉavaṟṟi. oṉṟupaṭu koḷki eṉṟamiyāl ivi yellām talivaṉukkum talivikkum ottiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. iṉi, talivi, aṉpu mutaliya yāvum oṉṟiya koḷkiyi yuṭimiyiṉālē avaḷuṭaṉ ōrāṅku muyaṅki eṉṟalumām. kaṭaṟu - kaṟkāṭu. iruppiyiṉ arumpu ampukkuppi pōlumākaliṉ, tūṇittali tiṟantavipōl iruppikkuḻi poti kuviyiṇar eṉṟār. `villōr tūṇi vīṅkap peyta, appu nuṉi yēyppa arumpiya iruppi'2 eṉa muṉ vantamiyuṅ kāṇka. iruppiyiṉ kuviyiṇar muttup pōṟ cennilat tutirntu kiṭattalāl aḻaku peṟṟa kuṭiyiruppiṉi yuṭiya cīṟūr eṉka. iṉṟu nammāṟ pārāṭṭap peṟṟu makiḻcciyuṭaṉ irukkum nam kātali, nāḷi nammip pirintu oppaṉi ceyyāta kūntalum, toṭi nekiḻum kiyum, kalaṅkiya nōkkamum, ataṉāl veṟuppum uṭiyaḷ ātali niṉintu cīṟūril taṅki yiruppōmpōlum eṉṟāṉ eṉka.
226. marutam
(talimakaṟkut tōḻi vāyiṉ maṟuttatu.)
		uṇarkuveṉ alleṉ uriyalniṉ māyam
		nāṇili maṉṟa yāṇar ūra
		akaluḷ āṅkaṇ ampaki maṭivik
		kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir kurūuppuṉal muṉiyiṉ
	5.	paḻaṉap piñcāy koḻutik kaḻaṉik
		karantiyañ ceṟuviṉ veṇkuruku ōppum
		valvil eṟuḻtōṭ paratavar kōmāṉ
		palvēl matti kaḻāar muṉtuṟi
		neṭuveṇ marutoṭu vañci cāay
	10.	viṭiyal vanta perunīrk kāvirit
		toṭiyaṇi muṉki nīvey yōḷoṭu
		muṉṉā ḷāṭiya kavvi innāḷ
		valamiku muṉpiṟ pāṇaṉoṭu malitārt
		tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ uṟanti nāḷavip
	15.	pāṭiṉ teṇkiṇip pāṭukēṭ ṭañcip
		pōraṭu tāṉik kaṭṭi
		porāa tōṭiya ārppiṉum peritē.
-paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 1-2 yāṇar  ūra - putu varuvāyiyuṭiya ūraṉē! niṉ māyam uriyal - niṉ vañcaṉi potinta coṟkaḷik kūṟātē, uṇarkuveṉ alleṉ - yāṉ avaṟṟi meymmiyākak koḷvēṉ allēṉ, nāṇ ili maṉṟa - nī tēṟṟamāka nāṇam uṭiyi yalli;
3-12. akaluḷ āṅkaṇ - akaṉṟa ūriṭattē, am paki maṭivi - aḻakiya pakuppiyuṭiya taḻiyuṭiyiṉiyum, kuṟu toṭi - kuṟiya vaḷiyaliyumuṭiya makaḷir, kurūu puṉal muṉiyiṉ - viḷaṅkum puṉal viḷiyāṭṭi veṟuppiṉ, paḻaṉam piñcāy koḻuti - poykiyiluḷḷa kōrikaḷik kōti, kaḻaṉik karanti ceṟuvil veṇ kuruku ōppum - kaḻaṉiyākiya karantiyi yuṭiya ceyyiṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa veḷḷiya nāriyi ōṭṭum (iṭamāya), valvil eṟuḻ tōḷ paratavar kōmāṉ - valiya villittāṅkiya valimi poruntiya tōḷkaḷiyuṭiya paratavarkaṭkut taliva ṉākiya, palvēl matti - pala vēṟ paṭikaḷiyuṭiya matti eṉpāṉatu, kaḻāar - kaḻār eṉṉum patiyiṉ, muṉ tuṟi - tuṟiyiṉ muṟṟattē, neṭu veḷ marutoṭu vañci cāay - nīṇṭa veḷḷiya maruta marattuṭaṉ vañcimarattiyum cāyttu, viṭiyal vanta perunīrk kāviri - nāṭkāliyil vanta mikka veḷḷattiyuṭiya kāviri yiṭattē, toṭi aṇi muṉki nī veyyōḷoṭu - vaḷiyal aṇinta muṉkiyiṉiyuṭiya uṉṉāl virumpappeṟṟa paratti yoṭu, muṉṉāḷ āṭiya kavvi - nī nēṟṟu viḷiyāṭiya tāleḻunta alar;
12-17. in nāḷ - iṉṟu, valimiku muṉpiṉ pāṇaṉoṭu - āṟṟal mikka valimiyuṭiya pāṇaṉ eṉpāṉoṭu kūṭi, pōr aṭutāṉi kaṭṭi - pōr aṭutalilvalla tāṉiyiyuṭiya kaṭṭi yeṉpāṉ (pōrceytaṟku vantu), mali tār tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ uṟanti nāḷ avi - tār malinta tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ eṉpāṉatu uṟiyūriṉkaṇṇuḷḷa nāḷōlakkattiṉkaṇṇē eḻunta, pāṭu
iṉ teṇ kiṇip pāṭu kēṭṭu añci - ōci iṉiya teḷinta kiṇiyiṉatu oliyik kēṭṭu avaṉatu perumiyi yuṇarntu añci, porā atu ōṭiya ārppiṉum peritu - pōr ceyyātu ōṭiyapōtu uṇṭāya āravārattiṉum peritākum.
(muṭipu) ūra! māyam uriyal; yāṉ avaṟṟi uṇarkuveṉ alleṉ; nāṇili; kāviriyil kaḻāar muṉtuṟikkaṇṇē nī veyyōḷoṭu muṉṉāḷ āṭiya kavvi, innāḷ kaṭṭi yeṉpāṉ pāṇaṉ eṉpāṉoṭu kūṭip pōr ceytaṟku vantu veḷiyaṉ uṟanti nāḷavik kaṇṇē kiṇi yolikēṭṭu añcip porātu ōṭiya pōtuṇṭāya āravārattiṉum peritākum.
(vi-ri) māyam - parattiyi aṟiyēṉ eṉṉum poym moḻi. paki - pakuppu. oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu muraṇ eṉṟumām. pala niṟamuṭiya pūkkaḷālum taḻiyālum ceyyappaṭutaliṉ paki maṭivi eṉṟār. maṭivi - taḻiyuṭi. kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir eṉṟatu, maruta nilattu uḻavar makaḷiri. makaḷir puṉalāṭutalil veṟuppuṟiṉ ceṟuvil kurukiṉi yōṭṭi viḷiyāṭuvar eṉka. viḷi ceṟuvil karanti uḷatātal, `kāytta karanti mākkoṭi viḷivayal'1 eṉap piṟāṇṭum kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka. `palvēl matti kaḻāar'2 eṉa ivvāciriyar muṉṉarum kūṟiyuḷḷami aṟiyaṟ pāṟṟu. kiṇi - īṇṭu muracu. pāṇaṉum kaṭṭiyum mikka mey valiyum paṭivaliyum uṭiyarākiyum tittaṉ veḷiyaṉatu nāḷavikkaṇ olikkum kiṇiyoli kēṭṭal māttiriyāṉē añciyōṭiṉamiyiṉ eḷḷal paṟṟiya naki yāravāram peritāyiṟ ṟeṉka, ōṭiya ārppiṉum: peyareccam kāraṇap poruṭṭu.
227. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaṭkut tōḻi colliyatu. piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatūumām.)
		nutalpacan taṉṟē tōḷcā yiṉavē
		titali yalkul variyum vāṭiṉa
		eṉṉā kuvaḷkol ivaḷeṉap 1paṉmāṇ
		nīrmali kaṇṇoṭu neṭituniṉin toṟṟi
	5.	iṉiyal vāḻi tōḻi naṉikavuḷ
		kāyciṉañ ciṟanta vāypuku kaṭāttoṭu
		muṉṉili poṟāatu muraṇip poṉṉiṇarp
		pulikkēḻ vēṅkip pūñciṉi pulampa
		mutalpāyn tiṭṭa muḻuvali yoruttal
	10.	cennilap paṭunī ṟāṭic cerumalintu
		kaḷaṅkoḷ maḷḷariṉ muḻaṅkum attam
		paliṟantu akaṉṟaṉa rāyiṉum nilii
		nōyila rākanaṅ kātalar vāyvāḷ
		tamiḻakap paṭutta imiḻici muraciṉ
	15.	varunar variyāp perunā ḷirukkit
		tūṅkal pāṭiya ōṅkuperu nallicip
		2piṭimiti vaḻutuṇip perumpeyart taḻumpaṉ
		kaṭimatil varippiṉ ūṇūr umpar
		viḻuniti tuñcum 3vīṟupeṟu tirunakar
	20.	iruṅkaḻip paṭappi maruṅkūrp paṭṭiṉattu
		ellumiḻ āvaṇattu aṉṉa
		kalleṉ kampali ceytakaṉ ṟōrē.
- nakkīrar.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, nutal pacantaṉṟu - nutal pacantuḷatu, tōḷ cāyiṉa - tōḷkaḷ melintuviṭṭaṉa, titali alkul variyum vāṭiṉa - tēmal paranta alkuliṉ varikaḷum vāṭippōyiṉa, eṉ ākuvaḷ kol ivaḷ eṉa - iṉi ivaḷ ennili yaṭivaḷō eṉṟu, palmāṇ nīrmali kaṇṇoṭu - palapaṭiyāka nīr niṟinta kaṇṇoṭu, neṭitu niṉintu - neṭitu eṇṇi, oṟṟi - ārāyntu, iṉiyal - varuntātē;
5-12. kāy ciṉam ciṟanta - kāyum ciṉam mikkamiyāl, naṉikavuḷ vāypuku kaṭāttoṭu - naṉinta kaṉṉattiṉiṉṟum vāyiṟ pukum matanīroṭu, muṉ nili poṟā atu - taṉ muṉṉē niṟṟalik kāṇap poṟāmal, muraṇi - pakittu, poṉ iṇar puli kēḻ vēṅki - poṉṉiṟamāṉa kottukkaḷiyuṭiya puliyiṉ niṟattiṉikkoṇṭa vēṅki marattiṉatu, pū ciṉi pulampa - pūṅkompukaḷ pūkkaḷ utirtalāl taṉimiyuṟa, mutal pāyntiṭṭa - avvēṅkiyiṉ aṭimarattil pāyntiṭṭa, muḻuvali oruttal - mikka valiyiṉiyuṭiya kaḷiṟu, cennilap paṭu nīṟu āṭi- cemmaṇ nilattiṟ poruntiya puḻutiyi aḷintu, ceru malintu kaḷam koḷ maḷḷariṉ - pōr purintu kaḷattil veṟṟikoṇṭa virarpōl, muḻaṅkum attam - muḻaṅkukiṉṟa curaneṟikaḷ, pala iṟantu akaṉṟaṉar āyiṉum - palavaṟṟik kaṭantu akaṉṟu pōyiṉarāyiṉum;
12-22. vāy vāḷ - tappāta vāḷiṉiyum, tamiḻ akappaṭutta- tamiḻnāṭu muḻutiṉiyum akappaṭutti, imiḻ ici muraciṉ - olikkum pukaḻaminta muraciṉiyum, varunar variyā perunāḷirukki- pāṭi varunarkku variyāta periya nāḷōlakka irukkiyiṉiyum uṭiya, tūṅkal pāṭiya - tūṅkal eṉum pulavarāṟ pāṭappeṟṟa, ōṅku peru nalici - mika uyarnta nalla pukaḻ vāynta, piṭi miti - piṭi mitittamiyāl ākiya, vaḻutuṇip perum peyart taḻumpaṉ - vaḻutuṇaṅkāy pōlum taḻumpiṉi uṭimiyāl vaḻutuṇit taḻumpaṉ eṉṉum ciṟanta peyariṉi yuṭiyāṉatu, kaṭimatil varippiṉ ūṇūr umpar - kāval poruntiya matil elliyi yuṭiya ūṇūrkku appāluḷḷa, viḻuniti tuñcum vīṟu peṟu tirunakar - mikka poruḷ nilipeṟ ṟirukkum perumi koṇṭa aḻakiya nakarākiya, irukaḻi paṭappi maruṅkūr paṭṭiṉattu - periya uppaṅkaḻip pakkaṅkaḷiyuṭiya maruṅkūrp paṭṭiṉattu, el umiḻ āvaṇattu aṉṉa - oḷi vīcum kaṭitteruvip pōṉṟa, kal eṉ kampali ceytu akaṉṟōr - kalleṉṉum alari ivvūril eḻacceytu pirintakaṉṟōrākiya, nam kātalar - nam kātalar, nilii nōyilarāka - nōyillātavarāki nili peṟṟu vāḻvārāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! ivaḷ eṉṉākuvaḷ eṉa iṉiyal; attam iṟantakaṉṟaṉarāyiṉum maruṅkūrp paṭṭiṉattu āvaṇattaṉṉa kampali ceytu akaṉṟōr āya nam kātalar nilii nōyilarāka.
(vi-ri) iṉiyal eṉat talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka. nīrmali kaṇṇoṭu neṭitu niṉintoṟṟutal tōḻiyiṭat tuṇṭāya vēṟupāṭākum. eṉavē, icceyyuḷ, iraṇṭāvatākak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa talivi kūṟṟātaṟkup peritum poruttamām. nōyilarāka nam kātalar eṉat talivi tōḻiyi uḷappaṭuttik kūṟaliṉ, taliviyum tōḻiyum talivaṉi vāḻttiṉāreṉak koḷḷutalum amiyum. ciṟanta eṉṉum peyareccam kāraṇap
poruṭṭu. oruttal cennilap paṭunī ṟāṭiyatu eṉṟataṟ kēṟpa maḷḷar kurutiyāl uṭal civantami koḷka. oruttal pakiyi oḻitta perumitattuṭaṉ muḻaṅkutaliṉ kaḷaṅkoḷ maḷḷariṉ muḻaṅkum eṉṟār. nilii nōyilarāka - nōyilarāy nili
peṟṟu vāḻka eṉa vikuti pirittukkūṭṭi yurikka. muracu - vīramuracum, koṭi muracumām. avaṉatu vīramum vaṇmiyum tamiḻ nāṭu muḻutum parantiruntamiyiṉ tamiḻakappaṭutta ici muracu eṉṟār. vāḷiṉiyum muraciṉiyum irukkiyiṉiyum uṭiya taḻumpaṉ eṉavum, tūṅkal pāṭiya nallicit taḻumpaṉ eṉavum kūṭṭuka. tūṅkal - tūṅkal ōriyār eṉṉum pulavar. avar taḻumpaṉip pāṭiya pāṭṭuk kiṭittilatu. piṭimititta puṇṇiṉ taḻumpu eṉka. vaḻutuṇit taḻumpaṉ eṉṉum peyar taḻumpaṉ eṉṟē vaḻaṅkalumuṇṭu. `perumpuṇ, ēer taḻumpaṉ ūṇūr'1 eṉa varutaluṅ kāṇka. kaḻippaṭappi - uppu viḷiyum ceṟu.
`maṅkala moḻiyum’2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu maṅkalamoḻi eṉṟataṟkut `talivaṟkut tīṅku varumeṉṟu uṭkoṇṭu tōḻiyum taliviyum ataṟkañci avaṉi vaḻuttutalum' eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, ataṟku, `nōyilarāka naṅ kātalar' eṉṉum itaṉi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar, nac.
228. kuṟiñci
(talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat talimakaṭkuc 
colluvāḷāyt tōḻi colliyatu.)
		piracap palkiḷi yārppak kalleṉa
		variyiḻi yaruvi yāran tīṇṭit
		taṇṇeṉa naṉikkum naḷimalic cilampiṟ
		kaṇṇeṉa malarnta māyitaḻk kuvaḷik
	5.	kaṉmuki neṭuñcuṉi nammo ṭāṭip
		pakalē yiṉituṭaṉ kaḻippi yiravē
		celva rāyiṉum naṉṟumaṟ ṟilla
		vāṉkaṇ virinta pakalmaruḷ nilavil
		cūral 3miḷiya cāral ārāṟ,ṟu
	10.	ōṅkal miciya vēṅki yoḷvīp
		pulippoṟi kaṭuppat tōṉṟaliṟ kayavāy
		irumpiṭi iriyuñ cōlip
		peruṅkal yāṇarttañ ciṟu kuṭiyāṉē.
- aṇṭarmakaṉ kuṟuvaḻutiyār.
(co-ḷ) tōḻi-,
1-6. piracap palkiḷi ārppa - tēṉi uṇṇum palavāya vaṇṭiṉ iṉaṅkaḷum āravārikka, kal eṉa vari iḻi aruvi - kalleṉṉum oliyuṭaṉ variyiṉiṉṟum iṟaṅkum aruviyāṉatu. āram tīṇṭi taṇ eṉa naṉikkum - cantaṉa marattil vīḻntu ataṉit taṇṇiyatāka naṉittiṭum, naḷimali cilampil - ceṟinta pakka maliyil uḷḷa, kaṇ eṉa malarnta mā itaḻ kuvaḷi - kaṇ pōl malarnta kariya itaḻiṉiyuṭiya kuvaḷi malarkaḷi yuṭiya, kalmuki neṭu cuṉi - mali muḻiñciluḷḷa neṭiya cuṉiyil, nammoṭu āṭi - talivar nam muṭaṉ viḷiyāṭi, pakal iṉitu uṭaṉ kaḻippi - pakaṟ poḻuti iṉitāka nammuṭaṉiruntu kaḻittu;
8-13. vāṉkaṇ virinta pakal maruḷ nilavil - vāṉiṭattē virinta pakali yotta nilavoḷiyil, cūral miḷiya cāral ār āṟṟu - pirappaṅkāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya cāral poruntiya neṟiyilē, ōṅkal miciya vēṅki oḷ vī - maliyiṉ mēluḷḷavākiya vēṅki marattiṉ oḷi poruntiya malarkaḷ, pulip poṟi kaṭuppat tōṉṟaliṉ - puliyiṉ uṭampiluḷḷa puḷḷikaḷippōlat tōṉṟutaliṉ, kayavāy irupiṭi iriyum cōli - periya vāyiṉiyuṭiya kariya peṇ yāṉikaḷ añciyōṭum cōliyiṉi yuṭiya, peru kal yāṇar tam ciṟu kuṭiyāṉ - periya maliyil uḷḷa aḻakiya tamatu ciṟu kuṭiyāya ūrkku;
6-7. iravē celvar āyiṉum naṉṟu - iraviṉiṟ celvā rāyiṉum mika naṉṟākum.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! nam talivar neṭuñcuṉi nammōṭāṭip pakalē iṉitu kaḻippi, peruṅkal yāṇart tam ciṟukuṭiyāṉ iravu celvarāyiṉum naṉṟumaṉ.
(vi-ri) piracam eṉṉum aṭiyāl, kiḷi vaṇṭiṟ kāyiṟṟu. ārppa iḻi aruvi eṉka; vaṇṭukaḷ olittalālum, aruvi naṟiya cantaṉa marattit tīṇṭi vantu taṭpamuṟa naṉittalālum, kuvaḷikaḷ kaṇṇeṉa malartalālum, cevi mutaliya poṟikaḷukku iṉpam viḷikkum cuṉiyeṉac cuṉiyiṉ ciṟappik kūṟiṉāḷeṉka. pakaliṉum iraviṉum talivaṉip piriyātiruttalē tam viruppamā yiṉum, iravil nilavu veḷippāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟāl kūṭṭattiṟku iṭiyīṭu uṇṭākalāṉum, pakalil maliyiṭattuḷḷa cuṉiyil āṭutaṟkaṇ aṉṉatōr iṭiyīṭu iṉmiyāṉum, `pakalē yiṉituṭaṉ kaḻippi iravē, celva rāyiṉum naṉṟumaṉ' eṉṟāḷ. til eṉṉum iṭic col tilla eṉat tirintatu. til - viḻivup poruṭṭu. nilavu vāṉiṭamellām virintamiyāṉ malimītuḷḷa vēṅkimarattiṉ pūkkaḷ cāraṟkaṇṇuḷḷa peṇ yāṉikaṭku iravilē pulippoṟi yoppat tōṉṟiṉa eṉka.
229. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷi vaṟpuṟuttun tōḻikkut talimakaḷ vaṉpuṟi yetiraḻintu colliyatu.)
		pakalcey palkatirp parutiyañ celvaṉ
		akalvāy vāṉat tāḻi pōḻnteṉa
		nīraṟa vaṟanta nirampā nīḷiṭik
		kayantalik kuḻavik kaviyukir maṭappiṭi
	5.	kuḷakumaṟut tuyaṅkiya maruṅkul palavuṭaṉ
		pāḻūrk kurampiyil tōṉṟum āṅkaṇ
		neṭuñcēṇ iṭiya kuṉṟam pōkiya
		poyva lāḷar muyaṉṟucey perumporuḷ
		nammiṉ ṟāyiṉum muṭika valleṉap
	10.	peruntuṉi mēval nalkūr kuṟumakaḷ
		nōymalin tukutta nocivaraṟ ciṉṉīr
		pallitaḻ maḻikkaṭ pāvi māyppap
		poṉṉēr pacali ūrtarap poṟivari
		naṉmā mēṉi tolital nōkki
	15.	iṉiyal eṉṟi tōḻi ciṉiya
		pācarumpu īṉṟa cemmuki murukkiṉap
		pōtaviḻ alari koḻutit tātaruntu
		antaḷir māattu alaṅkaṉ mīmicic
		ceṅkaṇ iruṅkuyil nayavarak kūum
	20.	iṉṉiḷa vēṉilum vārār
		iṉṉē varutum eṉatteḷit tōrē.
- maturik kūlavāṇikaṉ cīttalic cāttaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 10. nalkūr kuṟumakaḷ - makaṭku nal kūrntāratu celva makaḷākiya iḷiya taliviyē!
1-10. pakal cey palkatir paruti am celvaṉ - pakalit tōṟṟuvikkum pala katirkaḷiyuṭiya ñāyiṟākiya aḻakiya celvaṉatu, akal vāy vāṉattu āḻi - akaṉṟa iṭamākiya vāṉiṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa āṇiyāya āḻi, pōḻnteṉa - ulakik kiḻittuc ceṉṟatāka, nīr aṟa vaṟanta nirampā nīḷ iṭi - nīrilliyāka vaṟantiṭṭa cellat toliyāta nīṇṭa neṟiyiṭattē, kayam tali kuḻavik kaviukir maṭappiṭi - melliya taliyiṉiyuṭiya kaṉṟiṉiyum kavinta nakattiṉiyumuṭiya iḷiya yāṉi, kuḷaku maṟuttu uyaṅkiya - taṉ kaṉṟuṇṇa vēṇṭit tāṉ taḻi uṇavi uṇṇātu maṟuttamiyiṉ vāṭiya, maruṅkul palavuṭaṉ - uṭampiṉ uṟuppukkaḷ palavaṟṟuṭaṉ, pāḻ ūr kurampiyil tōṉṟum āṅkaṇ - pāḻpaṭṭa ūriluḷḷa kuṭicikaḷ pōlat tōṉṟum av viṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya, neṭu cēṇ iṭiya kuṉṟam pōkiya - neṭiya cēymiyiluḷḷa kuṉṟaṅkaḷit tāṇṭic ceṉṟa, poy
valāḷar - poy mikkārāya nam talivar, muyaṉṟu cey perum poruḷ - muyaṉṟu īṭṭum periya poruḷ, nam iṉṟu āyiṉum val muṭika - nām illiyāyiṉum virintu kikūṭuka eṉṟu, peru tuṉi mēval - periya veṟuppu mēvaṟka;
11-15. nōy malintu ukutta noci varal ciṉṉīr - varutta mikku utirnta nuṇṇitāka varum ciṟitaḷavāya kaṇṇīr, pal itaḻ maḻi kaṇ pāvi māyppa - palavāya itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya tāmari malar pōṉṟa kuḷirnta kaṇṇil uḷḷa karumaṇiyi maṟittiṭa, poṉ ēr pacali ūrtara - poṉṉi yotta pacali parantiṭa, poṟi vari naṉ mā mēṉi tolital nōkki - puḷḷikaḷāya tēmali yuṭiya nalla ciṟanta eṉatu mēṉiyiṉ aḻaku toli talip pārttu, iṉiyal eṉṟi - varuntātē eṉkiṟāy;
15-21. tōḻi-, pacu arumpu īṉṟa ciṉiya - aḻakiya arumpu kaḷi īṉṟa kiḷikaḷiyuṭiya, cemmuki murukkiṉ a pōtu aviḻ alari koḻuti - civanta arumpukaḷiyuṭiya murukka marattiṉ aḻakiya pōtukaḷ virinta malarkaḷik kiṇṭi, tātu aruntu - avaṟṟiluḷḷa poṭikaḷi uṇṭu, am taḷir māattu alaṅkal mīmici - aḻakiya taḷirkaḷiyuṭiya māmarattiṉ aciyum kiḷikaḷiṉ ucciyilirukkum, cem kaṇ iru kuyil - civanta kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya kariya kuyil, nayavara kūum iṉ iḷa vēṉilum - iṉimi tōṉṟak kūvum iṉiya iḷavēṉiṟ kālat tilum, iṉṉē varutum eṉat teḷittōr - ippoḻutē varuvēm eṉat terivittuc ceṉṟavar, vārār - vantilarē; eṉ ceyvēṉ.
(muṭipu) `nalkūr kuṟumakaḷ! kuṉṟam pōkiya poyvalāḷar muyaṉṟu ceyporuḷ nammiṉṟāyiṉum muṭika eṉap perun tuṉi mēval; mēṉi tolital nōkki, iṉiyal' eṉṟi; tōḻi! iṉṉē varutum eṉat teḷittōr iṉṉiḷa vēṉilum vārār; eṉceyvēṉ.
(vi-ri) mutuvēṉiṟ kālattiṉ ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir muṟuku talāl kāṭṭiṉ iṭamellām nīr aṟa vaṟaṇṭamiyip, parutiyañ celvaṉuṭiya āḻi pōḻntamiyāl vaṟanta teṉṟār. āḻi - āṇi. kuḻaviyi īṉṟamiyālum uṇavu peṟāmiyālum eṉpu tōṉṟa vaṟṟiya uṭampiṉiyuṭimiyāṉ, piṭikaḷ kūricitinta kurampipōlat tōṉṟum eṉka. perumporuḷ, nammiṉṟāyiṉum muṭika eṉṟatu, nām iṟantupaṭiṉum avar ap perum poruḷip peṟṟu vāḻvārāka eṉat talivi tuṉipaṟṟik kūṟiyatākum. tōḻiyāṉavaḷ taliviyi nōkki, nī aṅṅaṉam kūṟit tuṉiyuṟātē eṉavum, mēṉi tolital nōkki iṉiyal eṉavum vaṟpuṟut tiṉāḷāka, talivi nī aṅṅaṉam vaṟpuṟuttalāṟ payaṉeṉṉa? iṉṉē varutum eṉat teḷivittuc ceṉṟavar, iḷavēṉiṟ paruvam vantum vantilarākaliṉ, yāṉ eṅṅaṉam āṟṟiyiruppēṉ eṉat tōḻikku etiraḻintu kūṟiṉāḷeṉka. pal itaḻ - pala itaḻkaḷi yuṭiya tāmarip pū. cemmuki eṉṟatu murukkiṟku iyalpāya aṭi. aruntu - arunti eṉat tirikka. aruntu kuyil eṉak kūṭṭi yurittalumām. mutuvēṉiṟ kālattē piritaluṟṟa talimakaṉ aṭuttuḷḷa kārpparuvattiṉ toṭakkattē varuvēṉ eṉak kālam kuṟittuc ceṉṟaṉaṉākaliṉ, ataṉi iṉṉē varutum eṉat teḷit tōr eṉṟāḷ. kārpparuvattu vārāmiyēyaṉṟik kūtir mutaliya iṭiyiṭṭa paruvaṅkaḷiyum kaṭantu iḷavēṉil vantum avar iṉṉum vantilar eṉṟāḷ. iḷavēṉilil māmarattiṉmītu kuyiliruntu kūvuvatu kūṭiyiruppārkku iṉpam viḷippatākaliṉ, kuyil nayavarak kūvum iṉṉiḷavēṉil eṉṟāḷ eṉka.
230. neytal
(talimakaḷik kaṇṇuṟṟu nīṅkum talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		uṟukaḻi maruṅkiṉ ōtamoṭu malarnta
		ciṟukaru neytaṟ kaṇpōṉ māmalarp
		peruntaṇ māttaḻi yirunta alkul
		aiya arumpiya cuṇaṅkiṉ viyeyiṟṟu
	5.	miyī rōti vāṇutaṟ kuṟumakaḷ
		viḷiyāṭ ṭāyamoṭu veṇmaṇal utirtta
		puṉṉi nuṇtātu poṉṉiṉ noṇṭu
		maṉipuṟan taruti yāyiṉ eṉiyatūum
		immaṉik kiḻami yemmoṭu puṇaril
	10.	tītu muṇṭō māta rāyeṉak
		kaṭumpari naṉmāṉ koṭiñci neṭuntēr
		kival pākaṉ piyeṉa iyakka
		yāntaṟ kuṟukiṉa māka ēnteḻil
		arivēy uṇkaṇ paṉivaral oṭukkic
	15.	ciṟiya iṟiñciṉaḷ taliyē
		periya evvam yāmivaṇ uṟavē.
- maturi aṟuvivāṇikaṉ iḷavēṭṭaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. uṟukaḻi maruṅkiṉ - periya uppaṅkaḻiyiṉ pakkalilē, ōtamoṭu malarnta - kaṭal nīrāl malarnta, kaṇpōl ciṟu karu neytal mā malar - kaṇṇippōṉṟa ciṟiya kariya neytalatu periya malaruṭaṉ kūṭiya, peru taṇ mā taḻi irunta alkul - periya kuḷirnta ciṟanta taḻiyuṭi poruntiya alkuliṉiyum, aiya arumpiya cuṇaṅkiṉ - meṉmiyavākiya arumpiya tēmaliyum, vi eyiṟṟu mi īr ōti vāḷ nutal - kūriya eyiṟṟiṉiyum kariya neṭiya kūntaliyum oḷi poruntiya neṟṟiyiṉiyumuṭiya, kuṟumakaḷ - iḷiya makaḷē;
6-10. mātarāy - peṇṇē, viḷiyāṭṭu āyamoṭu - nī kūṭi viḷiyāṭum niṉ āyattāruṭaṉ, puṉṉi veṇmaṇal utirtta nuṇ tātu - puṉṉimaram veḷḷiya maṇalil utirtta nuṇṇiya poṭiyiṉi, poṉṉiṉ noṇṭu - poṉṉākak koṇṭu mukantu, maṉi puṟan taruti yāyiṉ - illaṟam naṭāttuvāyāyiṉ, i maṉikkiḻami emmoṭu eṉiyatūum puṇariṉ - inta illaṟakkiḻami ciṟitēṉum emmuṭaṉ poruntuvatāyiṉ, 
tītum uṇṭō - varakkaṭavatoru tīṅkum uṇṭō, eṉa - eṉṟu kūṟi;
11-13. kaṭum pari nal māṉ koṭiñci neṭun tēr - kaṭiya celaviṉiyuṭiya nalla kutiri pūṇṭa koṭiñciyiṉiyuṭiya uyarnta tēriṉi, kival pākaṉ pi eṉa iyakka - celuttun tiṟami mikka pākaṉ melleṉac celutta, yām taṉ kuṟukiṉam āka - nām avaḷi aṇukiṉamāka;
13-16. periya evvam yām ivaṇ uṟa - yām iṅkē periya varuttatti aṭiya, ēntu eḻil ari vēy uṇ kaṇ - avaḷ aḻaku mikka cevvari paranta miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷil, paṉi varal 
oṭukki - uvakik kaṇṇīr varutali maṟittu, tali ciṟiya iṟiñciṉaḷ - taṉ taliyiṉic ciṟiya aḷavil kaviḻntaṉaḷ.
(muṭipu) kuṟumakaḷ! mātarāy! nī maṉipuṟantarutiyāyiṉ, im maṉikkiḻami emmoṭu puṇaril tītumuṇṭō eṉa, neñcē yām taṟkuṟukiṉamāka, yām periya evvam uṟa, avaḷ, kaṇpaṉivaral oṭuṅki, tali ciṟiya iṟiñciṉaḷ.
(vi-ri) kaṇpōl mā malar eṉpatu, `poruḷē yuvamam ceytaṉar moḻiyiṉum'1 eṉpataṉāl amiyum. aiya, peyareccak kuṟippu; viṉiyeccak kuṟippākki, meṉmiyavāka arumpiya eṉṟurittalumām. kuṟumakaḷ eṉpataṉi, eḻuvāyākakkoṇṭu kuṟumakaḷ iṟiñciṉaḷ eṉa muṭittalumām, noṇṭu - mukantu eṉpa taṉ marūu. maṉi puṟantarutalāvatu, maṉiyil vāḻvārip pēṇutalum aṟañceytalumām. im maṉikkiḻami emmoṭu puṇariṉ eṉpatu nī eṉṉuṭaṉ kūṭi maṉiyaṟam puritiyāyiṉ eṉṉuṅ karuttiṟṟu. tēriṟpūṭṭiya kutiri mika virintu celva tākavum, am makaḷir añci yakalātu taṉ kuṟippaṟintu melleṉac celuttiṉamiyiṉ, kival pākaṉ eṉṟāṉ. talivaṉ aṇukiya vaḻi taṉakku uṇṭākiya vēṭkiyi ataṉāl eḻum kaṇṇīr pulappaṭuttiṟṟākaliṉ, talivi nāṇiṉāl ataṉi maṟittut tali ciṟitu iṟiñciṉāḷ eṉka. avaḷ aṅṅaṉam nāṇi niṉṟa niliyāṉatu avaḷ vēṭkiyip pulappaṭutti avaḷip pirintu cellum taṉ uḷḷattil periya tuṉpatti viḷittaliṉ periya evvam yām ivaṇ uṟavē eṉṟāṉ.
(mē-ḷ) `maṟintavaṟ kāṇṭal'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattuk `kippaṭṭuk kalaṅkiṉum' eṉṉun tuṟikku itaṉik kāṭṭi, itu talivaṉ kūṟiyatu eṉṟaṉar, nac.
231. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaṭkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		ceṟuvōr cemmal vāṭṭaluñ cērntōrkku
		uṟumiṭattu uykkum utavi āṇmiyum
		illirun tamivōrk killeṉ ṟeṇṇi
		nallici valitta nāṇuṭi maṉattar
	5.	koṭuviṟ kāṉavar kaṇiyiṭat tolintōr
		paṭukaḷat tuyartta mayirttalip patukki
		kaḷḷiyam paṟantalik kaḷartoṟuṅ kuḻīi
		uḷḷunarp paṉikkum ūkkaruṅ kaṭattiṭi
		veñcuram iṟantaṉar āyiṉum neñcuruka
	10.	varuvar vāḻi tōḻi poruvar
		celcamaṅ kaṭanta cellā nallici
		vicumpivar veṇkuṭip pacumpūṇ pāṇṭiyaṉ
		pāṭupeṟu ciṟappiṟ kūṭal aṉṉaniṉ
		āṭuvaṇ ṭaraṟṟum uccit
	15.	tōṭār kūntaṉ marīi yōrē.
- maturi īḻattup pūtaṉṟēvaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 10. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
1-4. ceṟuvōr cemmal vāṭṭalum - tam pakivar ceruk kiṉi oḻittalum, cērntōrkku uṟumiṭattu uykkum utavi āṇmiyum - tammic cērntōrkku ōr ūṟu eytumiṭattu utavi ceytalāya āṇmiyum, il iruntu amivōrkku il eṉṟu eṇṇi - illiṉkaṇṇē vāḷāviruntu maṭiyāl amintiruppōrkku illi eṉṟu eṇṇi, nal ici valitta nāṇ uṭi maṉattār - nalla pukaḻik karutiya nāṇ uṭiya maṉattarāya nam talivar;
5-9. koṭu vil kāṉavar kaṇi iṭat tolintōr - koṭiya villiyuṭiya vēṭar tamatu ampiṉi viṭutalāṟ paṭṭōr, paṭukaḷattu uyartta mayirtalip patukki - viḻuntu kiṭakkum kaḷattil uyarttiya mayiriṉiyuṭiya talikaḷiyuṭiya kaṟkuviyalkaḷ, kaḷḷi paṟantali kaḷar toṟum kuḻīi - kaḷḷiyiṉi yuṭiya pāḻiṭamākiya kaḷarnilan tōṟum tiraṇṭu, uḷḷunar paṉikkum ūkku arum kaṭattiṭi - niṉippōriyum naṭuṅkac ceytaliṉ yārum cellattuṇiyāta kāṭṭiṭiyē uḷḷa, vem curam iṟantaṉarāyiṉum - koṭiya curattik kaṭantaṉar āyiṉum;
10-15. poruvar cel camam kaṭanta cellā nalici - pakivaratu etirnta pōriṉi oḻitta keṭāta nalla pukaḻiṉiyum, vicumpu ivar veṇkuṭi - vāṉiṭattē ōṅkiya veṇkuṭi yiṉiyum uṭiya, pacumpūṇ pāṇṭiyaṉ - pacumpūṭ pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpāṉatu, pāṭu peṟu ciṟappiṉ kūṭal aṉṉa niṉ - perumi yaminta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya maturiyi yotta niṉatu, āṭu vaṇṭu araṟṟum ucci - aciyum vaṇṭu olikkum ucciyiṉiyuṭiya, tōṭu ār kūntal marīiyōr - pūvitaḻ poruntiya kūntaṟ kaṇṇē poruntit tuyiṉṟōr āya avar;
9-10. neñcu uruka varuvar - ataṉi niṉintu tam neñcam urukutalāl virintu varuvar; ākaliṉ varuntātēkoḷ.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! nallici valitta nāṇuṭi maṉattar, veñcuram iṟantaṉarāyiṉum, niṉ kūntal marīiyōr āya avar neñcuruka, varuvar. ākaliṉ varuntātē koḷ.
(vi-ri) pakivari aḻittaṟkum naṭṭāri ākkaṟkum poruḷ iṉṟiyamiyātatākaliṉ, poruḷīṭṭum muyaṟciyiṉṟi illil iruppōrkku avi illi eṉṟār. poruḷ pakivari aḻittaṟkuk karuviyākumeṉpati, `ceyka poruḷic ceṟunar cerukkaṟukkum, eḵkataṉiṟ kūriya til'1 eṉpataṉāl aṟika. atu naṭṭāri ākkutaṟka uriyateṉpatu, āciriyar nakkīraṉār pāṭiya `kēḷkē ṭūṉṟavuṅ kiḷiñarāravum'2 eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉāṟ peṟappaṭum `aritāya aṟaṉeyti aruḷiyōrkkaḷittalum, peritāya pakiveṉṟu pēṇārit teṟutalum...... tarumeṉap..... poruḷ vayiṟ ceṉṟa naṅkātalar'3 eṉpatuṅ kāṇka. cemmal - talimi; īṇṭuc cerukkiṉmēṟṟu. utavi uykkum āṇmi eṉka. uvakkum eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, cērntōr uvattaṟku ētuvāya utaviyi āḷuntaṉmi eṉṟu poruḷ koḷka. nāṇ - paḻikku añcum kuṇam. tolintōr talikaḷi yuṭimiyiṉ niṉippōri naṭuṅkac ceyyum kaṭam eṉka. nam neñcu nekiḻu māṟu varuvar eṉṟurittalumām. kaṭanta pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉka; peyareccattiṉik kāraṇap poruṭṭākak koṇṭu, kaṭanta nallici eṉṟalumām. araṟṟum mucci eṉap pirittalumām.
232. kuṟiñci
(tōḻi, talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat 
talimakaṭkuc colluvāḷāyc colliyatu.)
		kāṇiṉi vāḻi tōḻi pāṉāḷ
		maḻimuḻaṅku aravaṅ kēṭṭa kaḻitiṉ
		māal yāṉi pulicettu verīi
		iruṅkal viṭarakañ cilampap peyarum
	5.	peruṅkal nāṭaṉ kēṇmi iṉiyē
		kuṉṟa vēlic ciṟukuṭi yāṅkaṇ
		maṉṟa vēṅki maṇanāḷ pūtta
		maṇiyēr arumpiṉ poṉvī tāay
		viyalaṟi varikkum muṉṟil kuṟavar
	10.	maṉimutir makaḷiroṭu kuravi tūṅkum
		ārkali viḻavukkaḷaṅ kaṭuppa nāḷum
		viravuppūm paliyoṭu virii aṉṉi
		kaṭiyuṭi viyaṉakark kāval kaṇṇi
		murukeṉa vēlaṉ tarūum
	15.	paruva mākap payantaṉṟāl namakkē,
- koṭimaṅkalattu vātuḷi naṟcēntaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, iṉi kāṇ - ippoḻutu yāṉ kūṟuvataṉi uṇarvāyāka;
1-5. pāl nāḷ - naṭu iravil, maḻi muḻaṅku aravam kēṭṭa - mēkam muḻaṅkum iṭiyoliyiṉik kēṭṭa, kaḻi tiṉ māal yāṉi - mūṅkilit tiṉṉum periya yāṉiyāṉatu, puli cettu verīi -  av voliyiṉip puliyiṉ oli eṉak karuti añci, iru kal viṭar akam cilampa peyarum - periya maliyiṉ muḻiñ ciṭam etiroli ceyyak kataṟip peyarntōṭum, peru kal nāṭaṉ kēṇmi - periya mali poruntiya nāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya talivaṉatu naṭpu;
5-11. iṉi - ikkālattu, kuṉṟavēli ciṟu kuṭi āṅkaṇ - kuṉṟaṅ kaḷāya vēliyiṉiyuṭiya cīṟūriṭattē, maṉṟa vēṅki maṇam nāḷ pūtta - maṉṟattiṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa vēṅki maraṅkaḷ maṇanāḷākiya kālattē pūtta, maṇi ēr arumpiṉ poṉvī tāay - maṇiyi yotta arumpu malarnta poṉpōṉṟa pūkkaḷ parantu, viyal aṟi varikkum muṉṟil - akaṉṟa pāṟikaḷi aḻakuṟuttum muṟṟattilē, kuṟavar - kuṟavarkaḷ, maṉi - maṉikkaṇṇuḷḷa, mutir makaḷiroṭu kuravi tūṅkum - āṭutal valla makaḷiroṭu kuravi āṭum, ārkali viḻavuk kaḷam kaṭuppa - āravāramikka viḻākkaḷatti yoppa;
11-15. aṉṉi-, nāḷum - nāṭoṟum, viravu pū paliyoṭu virii - viraviya palavakip pūkkaḷāya paliyoṭu porunti, kaṭiuṭi viyaṉ nakar kāval kaṇṇi - kāval poruntiya akaṉṟa maliyiṉkaṇ kāttalik karuti, muruku eṉa - namatu vēṟupāṭu murukaṉāl uṇṭāyateṉṟu, vēlaṉ tarūum - vēlaṉi aḻikkum, paruvam āka namakku payantaṉṟu - kālamāka namakku viḷintatu.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! kāṇ; nāṭaṉ kēṇmi, iṉi aṉṉi murukeṉa (eṇṇi) kāval kaṇṇi, paliyoṭu virii, vēlaṉit tarūum paruvamāka namakkup payantaṉṟu.
ciṟukuṭiyāṅkaṇ vēṅkiyiṉ poṉvī tāay aṟivarikkum muṉṟilil kuṟavar makaḷiroṭu kuravi tūṅkum kaḷaṅkaṭuppap pūm paliyoṭu virii eṉka.
(vi-ri) maṇi - muttu. muttukkaḷil poṉmi cemmi yuṭiyavum uḷaveṉpatu, `cantira kuruvē aṅkā rakaṉeṉa, vanta nīrmiya vaṭṭat tokutiyum'1 eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟām. arumpuṭaṉ vī tāay eṉṟalumām. kāval kaṇṇi eṉpataṟku, nammi iṟ ceṟittalik karuti eṉṟum, namakkut tīṅkuṇṭākāmal murukaṉ kāttalik karuti yeṉṟum, iruvakiyākak kōṭalum poruntum. viḻavuk kaḷam kaṭuppa eṉṉum uvamiyāl, vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭu miṭattiṟku malar citaṟikkiṭattaluṭaṉ kuravi āṭutal kōṭalumām.
233. pāli
(piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaṭkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		alamaral maḻikkaṇ malkupaṉi vāraniṉ
		alarmuli naṉiya aḻāal tōḻi
		erikavarpu uṇṭa karipuṟap perunilam
		pīṭukeḻu maruṅkiṉ ōṭumaḻi tuṟanteṉa
	5.	ūṉil yāṉi uyaṅkum vēṉil
		maṟappaṭik kutiri māṟā mintiṉ
		tuṟakkam eytiya toyyā nallici
		mutiyarp pēṇiya utiyañ cēral
		peruṅcōṟu koṭutta ñāṉṟi irumpal
	10.	kūḷic cuṟṟam kuḻīiyirun tāṅkuk
		kuṟiyavum neṭiyavuṅ kuṉṟutali maṇanta
		curaṉiṟan takaṉṟaṉa rāyiṉum mikanaṉi
		maṭaṅkā uḷḷamoṭu matimayak kuṟāap
		poruḷvayiṉ nīṭalō ilarniṉ
	15.	iruḷiṅ kūntal iṉtuyiṉ maṟantē.	         - māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-2. tōḻi-, alamaral maḻi kaṇ malku paṉi vāra - kalaṅkukiṉṟa kuḷirnta kaṇṇiṉiṉṟum niṟinta nīr oḻukaliṉ, niṉ alarmuli naṉiya aḻāal - niṉ paranta muli naṉiya aḻātē;
3-5. eri kavarpu uṇṭa - neruppuc cūḻntu erittiṭṭa, kari puṟam peru nilam - karinta iṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya periya nilatti, pīṭu keḻu maruṅkiṉ ōṭu maḻi tuṟanteṉa - vaḷam poruntiya vēṟu iṭaṅkaṭku ōṭum maḻi tuṟantiṭṭatāka, ūṉ il yāṉi uyaṅkum vēṉil - taci yoḻinta yāṉi varuntum vēṉiṟkaṇ;
6-10. maṟap paṭik kutiri māṟā mintiṉ - vīram poruntiya kutirip paṭikaḷuṭaṉ puṟakkiṭāta valiyiyuṭiya, tuṟakkam eytiya toyyā nal ici - tuṟakkattiṉi yaṭinta keṭāta nalla pukaḻiṉiyuṭiya, mutiyar pēṇiya utiyaṉ cēral - muṉṉōr
kaṭkut teṉpulak kaṭaṉ āṟṟiya utiyaṉ cēralātaṉ eṉum maṉṉaṉ, peru cōṟu koṭutta ñāṉṟi - avarkaṭkup paliyākap peruñcōṟu paṭittiṭṭa kālattē, iru pal kūḷic cuṟṟam kuḻīi iruntāṅku - kariya palavāya pēyccuṟṟaṅkaḷ ataṉi uṇpataṟkuk kūṭiyiruntāṟ pōla;
11-14. kuṟiyavum neṭiyavum kuṉṟu tali maṇanta - kuṟiyaṉavum neṭiyaṉavumāya kuṉṟukaḷ iṭantoṟumuḷḷa, curaṉ iṟantu - curattiṉik kaṭantu, mika naṉi maṭaṅkā uḷḷamoṭu - mikap peritum aṭaṅkāta uḷḷamuṭiyarāy, matimayakkuṟāa - aṟivumayaṅku taluṟṟu, poruḷvayiṉ akaṉṟaṉar āyiṉum - poruḷ īṭṭu talik karutic ceṉṟārāyiṉum;
14-15. niṉ iruḷ aiṅ kūntal iṉ tuyil maṟantu - niṉatu kariya aim pakuppāya kūntaliṟ peṟum iṉiya tuyili maṟantu, nīṭal  ilar - tāḻttiruttal ilarāvar; virintu varuvar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi aḻāal; vēṉiṟkaṇ kuṟiyavum neṭiyavum kuṉṟu tali maṇanta curaṉ iṟantu poruḷvayiṉ akaṉṟaṉa rāyiṉum niṉ kūntal iṉṟuyil maṟantu, nīṭalō ilar.
(vi-ri) peru nilattiṉkaṇ yāṉi uyaṅkum vēṉil eṉavum, kutirippaṭiyiṉiyum mintiṉiyumuṭiya utiyaṉ cēral eṉavum kūṭṭuka. iṉik kutirip paṭiyiṉiyum mintiṉiyum uṭiya mutiyar eṉṟurittalumām. utiyaṉ cēral tuṟakkameytiya taṉ muṉṉōrkkup piṇṭam ītalākiya kaṭaṉ āṟṟiyapōtu, peruñ cōṟu aḷittaṉaṉ eṉka. iṉi, peruñcōṟu koṭutta ñāṉṟu eṉpataṉip pāratap pōril irutiṟap paṭikkum cōṟaḷitta ñāṉṟu eṉṟurippārumuḷar. utiyañ cēralātaṉ eṉpāṉ pāratap pōril cōṟaḷittami, `alaṅkuḷip puravi aivaroṭu ciṉii.......... īrim patiṉmarum porutu kaḷattoḻiyap, peruñcōṟṟu mikupatam variyātu koṭuttōy'1 `ōriva rīrim patiṉmar uṭaṉ ṟeḻunta, pōriṟ peruñcōṟu pōṟṟātu tāṉaḷitta, cēraṉ'2 eṉpavaṟṟāṟ peṟappaṭumāyiṉum, īṇṭup pāratappōric cuṭṭum kuṟippu yātu miṉmiyiṉ, ivvutiyaṉ cēraṉ avaṉiṉ vēṟāyiṉāṉ eṉak koḷḷal vēṇṭum. toyyā - keṭāta. kūḷic cuṟṟam - kuṟiyavum periyavumākiya pēy kaḷiṉ cuṟṟam.
234. mulli
(talimakaṉ tērppākaṟkuc colliyatu.)
		kārpayam poḻinta nīrtikaḻ kāli
		nuṇṇayir paranta taṇṇayam maruṅkil
		niripaṟi aṉṉattu aṉṉa viriparip
		pulluḷik kalimā mellitiṟ 3koḷīiya
	5.	vaḷporuṅ kamiyap paṟṟi 4muḷkiya
		palkati rāḻi melvaḻi yaṟuppak
		kāleṉa maruḷa ēṟi nūliyaṟ
		1kaṇṇōkku oḻikkum paṇṇami neṭuntēr
		valvirin tūrmati nalvalam peṟuna
	10.	2tatartaḻi muṉiiya teṟinaṭi maṭappiṇi
		ēṟupuṇar uvakiya ūṟila ukaḷa
		añciṟi vaṇṭiṉ meṉpaṟit toḻuti
		mulli naṟumalart tātunayan tūta
		elli pōkiya pulleṉ mālip
	15.	puṟavaṭin tirunta uṟiviṉ nallūrk
		kaḻipaṭa ruḻanta paṉivā ruṇkaṇ
		naṉṉiṟam paranta pacaliyaḷ
		miṉṉēr ōtip piṉṉuppiṇi viṭavē.
- 3pēyaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 9. nalvalam peṟuna - tēr celuttutalil nalla veṟṟiyuṭiya pāka!
1-8. kār payam poḻinta nīr tikaḻ kāli - mēkam nīrip poḻinta tālāya veḷḷam viḷaṅkum kālattē, nuṇ ayir paranta taṇ ayam maruṅkiṉ - nuṇṇiya maṇal paraviya kuḷirnta nīrnili yiṭattē, niri paṟi aṉṉattu aṉṉa - variciyākap paṟattali yuṭiya aṉṉap paṟaviyi otta, viri pari puluḷi kalimā - virinta celaviṉiyuṭiya pulliya piṭarimayiriṉi yuṭiya cerukku vāynta kutirikaḷi, mellitiṉ koḷīiya vaḷpu oruṅku amiyap paṟṟi - meṉmiyuṟap pūṭṭiya kaṭivāḷa vāriṉi oru peṟṟiyamiyap paṟṟi, muḷkiya palkatir āḻi mel vaḻi aṟuppa - nilattē patinta pala āraṅkaḷiyuṭiya uruḷi meṉṉila vaḻiyi aṟuttēka, kāl eṉa maruḷa - vēkattāṟ kāṟṟeṉa viyakka, ēṟi - ēṟiyamarntu, nūl iyal kaṇ nōkku oḻikkum paṇ ami neṭuntēr - nūl neṟippaṭi iyaṉṟatum vēkattāṟ kaṇṇiṉ pārviyiṉit tavirppatum paṇṇutal amintatumāya neṭiya tēriṉi;
10-15. tatar taḻi muṉiiya teṟinaṭi maṭappiṇi - neruṅkiya taḻiyiṉi mikattiṉṟu veṟutta tuḷḷic cellum naṭiyiṉi yuṭiya iḷiya peṇmāṉ, ēṟu puṇar uvakiya ūṟu ila ukaḷa - āṇmāṉuṭaṉ kūṭiya makiḻcciyi yuṭiyavāy iṭiyūṟiṉṟit tuḷḷittiriya, am ciṟivaṇṭiṉ meṉpaṟit toḻuti - aḻakiya ciṟiyiṉiyuṭiya vaṇṭiṉ melliya paṟattali yuṭiya kūṭṭam, mullinaṟu malar tātu nayantu ūta - naṟiya mulli malaril uḷḷa pūmpoṭiyiṉi virumpi ūta, elli pōkiya pul eṉ māli - pakaṟpoḻutu kaḻinta polivaṟṟa mālikkālattē, puṟavu aṭintirunta uṟivu iṉ nal ūr - mullinilattic cārntirunta uṟitaṟku iṉitākiya nalla ūriṉ kaṇṇē;
16-18. kaḻi paṭar uḻanta paṉivār uṇkaṇ - mikka tuṉpattāl varuntiya nīrvārum miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷiyum, nal niṟam paranta pacaliyaḷ - nalla mēṉiyiṉkaṇ paraviya pacaliyiyum uṭiya nam talivi, miṉ nēr ōti - taṉatu miṉṉaliyotta kūntaliṉ, piṉṉup piṇi viṭavē - piṉṉalāya piṇippu nīṅka;
9. val virintu ūrmati - mika virintu celuttuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) nalvalam peṟuna! nallūrkkaṇ māliyil kaḻi paṭar uḻanta pacaliyaḷ ōtiyiṉ piṉṉup piṇiviṭa neṭun tēr valvirintu ūrmati.
(vi-ri) kārpayam poḻinta eṉṟataṉāl, talivaṉ kuṟitta kārpparuvam vantami peṟṟām. nuṇmaṇal paranta nīrnilikaḷiṉ pakkattē tāḻavirintu paṟakkum aṉṉam, aṅṅaṉam  paṟappatu pōl virintu tāvic cellum kutirikkut toḻil paṟṟi uvamamākum. tolkāppiyaṉār, `viṉivayiṟ pirintōṉ mīṇṭuvaru kāli, iṭiccura maruṅkiṟ ṟavirta lilli, uḷḷam pōla uṟṟuḻi yutavum, puḷḷiyaṟ kalimā vuṭimi yāṉa'1 eṉak kūṟutaluṅ kāṇka. tūya veṇṇiṟa muṭimi paṟṟip paṇpuvami viravivanta teṉalumām. kutirikku veṇṇiṟam ciṟantatāl, `pālpuri puravi nālkuṭaṉ pūṭṭi' 2  eṉpataṉāl aṟika. vaḷpu - vār. maḻiyāl nilam nekiḻntiruttaliṉ melvaḻi eṉappaṭṭatu. kaṭpulaṉ katuvalākāta virivuṭimiyiṉ, kāleṉa maruḷa eṉavum, kaṇṇōk koḻikkum eṉavum kūṟiṉār. tam pirivāl pacali paranta mēṉiyaḷāy oppaṉi ceyyātu piṇippuṟṟirukkum kūntaliyuṭiyaḷāyp paṭaruḻakkum taliviyiṉ tuṉpattip pōkkutaṟku mika virintu cēṟal vēṇṭumeṉpāṉ, `kāleṉa maruḷa valvirintu ūrmati' eṉṟāṉ. pākaṉ aṅṅaṉam celuttum āṟṟal uṭiyaṉ eṉpatu pulappaṭa, `nalvalam peṟuna' eṉa, viḷittāṉ. kārpparuvam vantuṟṟamiyāl mulli nilattuḷḷa māṉiṉaṅ kaḷum vaṇṭukaḷum naṟiya uṇaviṉiyuṇṭum, tuṇiyuṭaṉ kūṭiyum makiḻntirukka, annilattiṉ kaṇṇatākiya uṟitaṟkiṉiya ūrilirukkum talivi avaṟṟik kāṇuntoṟum āṟṟāmi mītūrntu paṭaruḻappaḷ eṉpatu tōṉṟa, maṭappiṇi uvaki ukaḷa, vaṇṭiṉ tokuti tātu nayantūta, kaḻipaṭaruḻanta pacaliyaḷ eṉṟāṉ eṉka. piṉṉup piṇi viṭa eṉṟatu, taṉ varukiyāl makiḻcciyuṟṟuk kūntali oppaṉi ceytu koḷḷa eṉṟapaṭi.
235. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷ 
āṟṟāmi mītūrat tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		amma vāḻi tōḻi poruḷpurintu
		uḷḷār kollō kātala ruḷḷiyuñ
		ciṟanta ceytiyiṉ maṟantaṉar kollō
		payaṉilaṅ kuḻiya vīcip peyalmuṉintu
	5.	viṇṭu muṉṉiya koṇṭal māmaḻi
		maṅkul aṟkamoṭu poṅkupu tuḷippa
		vāṭiyoṭu nivanta āyitaḻt tōṉṟi
		cuṭarkoḷ akalil curuṅkupiṇi yaviḻac
		curimukiḻ mucuṇṭip potiyaviḻ vāṉpū
	10.	vicumpaṇi mīṉiṟ pacumputa laṇiyak
		1kaḷvaṉ maṇṇaḷic ceṟiya akalvayaṟ
		kiḷiviri karumpiṉ kaṇikkāl vāṉpū
		māriyaṅ kurukiṉ īriya kuraṅka
		naṉikaṭuñ civappoṭu nāman tōṟṟip
	15.	paṉikaṭi koṇṭa paṇpil vāṭi
		maruḷiṉ māliyo 2ṭaruḷiṉṟi naliya
		nutaliṟi koṇṭa ayalaṟi pacaliyoṭu
		toṉṉalañ citiyac cāay
		eṉṉaḷkol aḷiyaḷ eṉṉā tōrē.
- kaḻārk kīraṉeyiṟṟiyār.
(co-ḷ) 1. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, amma - kēṭpāyāka;
4-16. payaṉ nilam kuḻiya vīci - payaṉ tarum nilaṅkaḷ nekiḻap peytu, peyal muṉintu - piṉ peytali veṟuttu, viṇṭu muṉṉiya koṇṭal mā maḻi - maliyic cērnta koṇṭa
lākiya kariyamēkam - maṅkul aṟkamoṭu poṅkupu tuḷippa - mīṇṭum iravil taṅkutaluṟṟup poṅkit tuḷikka, vāṭiyoṭu nivanta āy itaḻ tōṉṟi - vāṭikkāṟṟāl uyarnta aḻakiya itaḻiṉiyuṭiya ceṅkāntaḷ, cuṭar koḷ akalil curuṅku piṇi aviḻa- cuṭariṉik koṇṭa akalpōlac curuṅkiya arumpu viriya, curi mukiḻ mucuṇṭi poti aviḻ vāṉpū - curinta arumpiṉi yuṭiya mucuṇṭiyiṉ kaṭṭaviḻnta periya pū, vicumpu aṇi mīṉil pacum putal aṇiya - vāṉi aḻakuṟuttum mīṉkaḷip pōlap paciya putarkaḷi aḻakuṟutta, kaḷvaṉ maṇ aḷi ceṟiya - naṇṭu taṉatu maṇ vaḷiyuṭ ceṟintiṭa, akal vayal kiḷiviri karumpiṉ kaṇikkāl vāṉ pū - akaṉṟa vayalil kiḷittu virinta karumpiṉatu tiraṇṭa kāmpiṉiyuṭiya periya pū, māri am kurukiṉ īriya kuraṅka - maḻiyil naṉinta nāriyip pōla īram koṇṭaṉavāy vaḷintirukka, naṉi kaṭum civappoṭu nāmam tōṟṟi - mikka kaṭiya ciṉattiṉāl accatti uṇṭākki, paṉi kaṭi koṇṭa paṇpil vāṭi - paṉi taṅkiya naṟpaṇpillāta vāṭik kāṟṟāṉatu, maruḷiṉ māliyoṭu aruḷiṉṟi naliya - mayaṅkutali yuṭiya mālip poḻutuṭaṉ kūṭi irakkamiṉṟi nalintiṭa;
17-19. nutal iṟi koṇṭa ayal aṟi pacaliyoṭu - nutaṟ kaṇṇē taṅkutalkoṇṭa ayalōrāl aṟiyappaṭum pacali yuṭaṉ, tol nalam citiyac cāay - paḻiya mēṉi nalamkeṭa melintu, eṉṉaḷkol aḷiyaḷ eṉṉātōr - eṉṉa niliyiṉa vāyiṉaḷō iraṅkattakkāḷ eṉṟu ucāvi yaṟiyārātaliṉ;
1-3. kātalar - nam talivar, poruḷ purintu uḷḷār kollō- poruḷ tēṭutaliyē virumpi nammi niṉintilarō, uḷḷiyum - niṉintum, ciṟanta ceytiyiṉ - ceyyum viṉiyiṉ mikutiyāl, maṟantaṉar kollō - ucāvutali maṟantuviṭṭaṉarō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! paṇpil vāṭi māliyoṭu aruḷiṉṟi naliya, eṉṉaḷkol aḷiyaḷ eṉṉātōr ātaliṉ, nam kātalar nammi uḷḷār kollō, uḷḷiyum ciṟanta ceytiyiṉ maṟantaṉar kollō?
