maḻi poṅkupu tuḷippa, tōṉṟi piṇi aviḻa, mucuṇṭi vāṉpū putal aḻiya, kaḷvaṉ aḷic ceṟiya, karumpiṉ pū īriya kuraṅka, civappoṭu nāmam tōṟṟip paṉikaṭi koṇṭa vāṭi eṉka.
(vi-ri) kuḻiya - taḷirkka eṉṟumām. peyal muṉintu eṉṟamiyālum, poṅkupu tuḷippa eṉṟamiyālum, paṉikaṭi koṇṭa vāṭi eṉṟamiyālum, kūtiriṉ piṉṉarttākiya paṉip poḻutu eṉpatu pōtarum. maṅkul - vicumpu eṉṟumām. aṟkam - taṅkutal; toḻiṟ peyar. kūtirp paruvattē mēkam peyali veṟuttu vāṉiliruntu tuḷikkum iyalpiṉatu eṉpatu,  `peyalulan teḻunta poṅkal veṇmaḻi, akaliru vicumpiṟ ṟuvali kaṟpa'1    eṉpataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum. kuraṅkutal - vaḷital. civappu - vekuḷi. nām eṉṉum uric col īṟu tirintatu. talivaṉip pirintu talivi varuntutali ēṉōrkku aṟivippatu pacali yākaliṉ, `ayalaṟi pacali' eṉappaṭṭatu.
236. marutam
(āṟṟāmi vāyilākap pukka talimakaṉ nīkkattukkaṇ 
pukka tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		maṇimaruḷ malara muḷḷi amaṉṟa
		tuṇinīr ilañcik koṇṭa perumīṉ
		ariniṟak koḻuṅkuṟi 1vuviṉar mānti
		veṇṇe larinar peyarnilip piṉṟi
	5.	2iṭaṉila neritaru neṭuṅkatirp palcūṭṭu
		paṉipaṭu cāyppuṟam parippak kaḻaṉik
		karuṅkōṭṭu 3māattu alaṅkuciṉip putuppū
		mayaṅkumaḻit  tuvaliyil tāam ūraṉ
		kāmam perumi yaṟiyēṉ naṉṟum
	10.	uyarntaṉeṉ vāḻi tōḻi yalkal
		aṇikiḷar cāntiṉ ampaṭ ṭimippak
		koṭuṅkuḻi makaḷiriṉ oṭuṅkiya irukki
		aṟiyā miyiṉ aḻunta neñciṉ
		ēṟṟiyal eḻiṉaṭip polinta moympiṉ
	15.	tōṭṭiruñ curiyal maṇanta pitti
		āṭṭaṉ attiyik kāṇī rōveṉa
		nāṭṭiṉ nāṭṭiṉ ūriṉ ūriṉ
		kaṭalkoṇ ṭaṉṟeṉap puṉaloḷit taṉṟeṉak
		kaluḻnta kaṇṇaḷ kātalaṟ keṭutta
	20.	āti manti pōla
		ētañ collip pētuperi tuṟalē.
- paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 1-6. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, veṇ nel arinar - veḷḷiya nelli aṟuppōr, maṇi maruḷ malara - nīla maṇiyiṉi yotta malarkaḷiyuṭiya, muḷḷi - amaṉṟa - nīr muḷḷi niṟinta, tuṇi nīr ilañci koṇṭa - teḷinta nīriṉiyuṭiya maṭuviṟ kavarnta, peru mīṉ ari niṟam koḻu kuṟi - periya mīṉiṉ varikaḷ poruntiya niṟattiṉiyuṭiya vaḷaviya tuṇṭiṉi, vuviṉar mānti - kuviyuṇṭu, peyar nilip piṉṟi - eḻunta piṉṉark (koṇṭu pōyp pōkaṭṭa, iṭaṉ ila neṟitaru neṭum katir pal cūṭṭu - kaḷattē veṟṟiṭam aṟac ceṟintuḷa nīṇṭa katiriṉi
yuṭiya pala neṟkaṭṭukaḷi yuṭiya, paṉipaṭu cāyppuṟam parippa - paṉitaṅkiya kōriyiyuṭiya kaḷaṉelām cūḻa, kaḻaṉi - vayaṟ
puṟattēyuḷḷa, karuṅ kōṭṭu māattu - kariya kavariṉiyuṭiya māmarattiṉ, alaṅku ciṉip putu pū - aciyum kompukaḷiluḷḷa putiya pūkkaḷ, mayaṅkum maḻit tuvaliyil tāam ūraṉ - kalantu coriyum maḻittuḷi pōla utirntu parantu kiṭakkum ūraṉākiya nam talivaṉatu, kāmam perumi aṟiyēṉ - kāmam perumi ivaṟṟiṉi aṟiyātēṉ;
10-13. alkal - iravil, aṇikiḷar cāntiṉ ampaṭṭu imippa - aḻaku viḷaṅkum cāntiṉuṭaṉ aḻakiya paṭṭāṭi oḷi ceya, koṭu kuḻi makaḷiriṉ oṭuṅkiya irukki - vaḷinta kuḻiyaṇinta makaḷirippōla namtalivaṉ nāṇiyirunta irukki
yiṉik kaṇṭu, aṟiyāmiyiṉ aḻinta neñciṉ - maṭamiyāl urukappeṟṟa neñciṉēṉ āyiṉamiyiṉ;
14-21. kātalaṟkeṭutta - taṉ kaṇavaṉi iḻantamiyiṉ, kaluḻnta kaṇṇaḷ - aḻuta kaṇṇiṉaḷākiya, ātimanti pōla - āti mantiyippōla, ēṟṟu iyal eḻil naṭi - ēṟṟiṉ taṉmi
yuṭiya eḻucci poruntiya naṭiyiṉiyum, polinta moympiṉ - polivuṟṟa tōḷiṉiyum, tōṭu iru curiyal maṇanta pitti - tokuti koṇṭa kariya curuḷip poruntiya talimayiriṉiyu muṭiya, āṭṭaṉ attiyik kāṇīrō eṉa - āṭṭaṉ attiyāya eṉ talivaṉik kaṇṭīrallīrō eṉṟu, nāṭṭiṉ nāṭṭiṉ ūriṉ ūriṉ - nāṭu tōṟum ūrtōṟum ceṉṟu, kaṭal koṇṭaṉṟu eṉa puṉal oḷittaṉṟu eṉa - eṉ talivaṉik kaṭal koṇṭatu pōlum puṉalkoṇṭoḷittatu pōlumeṉak kūṟi, ētam collip pētu peritu uṟal - tuṉpati collip peritum mayaṅkutaliṉiṉṟum;
9-10. naṉṟum uyarntaṉeṉ - peritum tappiṉēṉ.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi ūraṉ kāmam perumi aṟiyēṉ, alkal makaḷiriṉ oṭuṅkiya avaḷatu irukki kaṇṭu aḻinta neñciṉ kātalaṟ keṭutta ātimantipōla ētam collip pētuṟal naṉṟum uyntaṉaṉ.
(vi-ri) piṉṟik koṇṭu pōkaṭṭa eṉa varuvitturikka. kāmam perumi: eṇṇummi vikārattāṟ ṟokkaṉa. cānti ṉoṭu paṭṭimippa eṉṟamiyāl, parattiyiṟ pirintu vantāṉ eṉpatu peṟṟām. nāṇālum accattālum oṭuṅkiya irukki eṉka. irukki kaṇṭu eṉa oru col varuvikkap paṭṭatu. neñciṉ - neñciṉēṉākaliṉ eṉa viritturikka. ātimanti, taṉ kātalaṉākiya āṭṭaṉ attiyuṭaṉ nīrāṭiyapoḻutu attiyik kāviri vavvic ceṉṟamiyiṉ, avaḷ avaṉip palaviṭaṅkaḷilum tēṭiyuḻanta varalāṟu muṉṉar `vāṉuṟa nivanta'1 eṉṉum akap pāṭṭilum paraṇarāṟ kūṟappaṭṭami aṟika. nāṭṭiṉ nāṭṭiṉ ūriṉ ūriṉ eṉṉum aṭukkukaḷ paṉmi kuṟittaṉa. koṇṭaṉṟu, oḷittaṉṟu eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷil aiyapporuṭṭākiya iṭic col viritturittuk koḷka.
(mē-ḷ) `aṇaṅkē vilaṅkē'2 eṉṉum cūttirattē, pulavi poruḷāka accam varutaṟku ippāṭṭiṉ `aṇikiḷar cāntiṉ am paṭṭimippak, koṭuṅkuḻi makaḷiriṉ oṭuṅkiya irukki' eṉṟa aṭikaḷik kāṭṭiṉar, pērā.
237. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷit tōḻi vaṟpuṟīiyatu.)
		puṉkāṟ pātiri ariniṟat tiraḷvī
		nuṇkoṭi atiraloṭu nuṇaṅkaṟal varippa
		araveyiṟṟu aṉṉa arumpumutir kuraviṉ
		tēṉimir naṟuñciṉit teṉṟal pōḻak
	5.	kuyilkural kaṟṟa vēṉilun tuyiltuṟantu
		iṉṉā kaḻiyuṅ kaṅku leṉṟuniṉ
		naṉmā mēṉi aṇinalam pulampa
		iṉital āṉṟiciṉ āyiḻi kaṉitiṟal
		centī aṇaṅkiya ceḻuniṇak koḻuṅkuṟi
	10.	meṉṟiṉip 3puṉkam utirtta maṇṭiyoṭu
		iruṅkatir alamaruṅ kaḻaṉik karumpiṉ
		viḷikaḻi piḻinta antīñ cēṟṟoṭu
		pālpey cenneṟ pācaval pakukkum
		puṉalporu putaviṉ uṟanti yeytiṉum
	15.	viṉiporu ḷākat 4tavirvilar kaṭicivantu
		aiya amartta uṇkaṇiṉ
		viyēr vāleyiṟu ūṟiya nīrē.
- tāyaṅ kaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ)  1-8. āyiḻi - aḻakiya aṇikaḷiyuṭiya taliviyē, puṉ kāl pātiri ari niṟa tiraḷ vī - pulliya kāmpiṉiyuṭiya pātiriyiṉ varikaḷ poruntiya niṟamuṭiya tiraṇṭa malar, nuṇ
koṭi atiraloṭu - melliya koṭiyākiya puṉaliyiṉ malaroṭu, nuṇaṅku aṟal varippa - nuṇṇiya maṇaliṭattē kōlañ ceyya, aravu eyiṟu aṉṉa arumpu mutir kuraviṉ - pāmpiṉ pal aṉiya arumpukaḷ mutirnta kurāviṉatu, tēṉ imir naṟu ciṉi teṉṟal pōḻa - vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum naṟiya kiḷiyil teṉṟal ūṭaṟuttuc cella, kuyil kural kaṟṟa vēṉilum - kuyil kūvutalip payiṉṟa vēṉiṟkālattum, kaṅkul - irāp poḻutāṉatu, tuyil tuṟantu iṉṉā kaḻiyum eṉṟu - tuyiliṉṟi varuntumpaṭi iṉṉātāyk kaḻiyā niṟkum eṉṟu kūṟi, niṉ nal mā mēṉi aṇi nalam pulampa - niṉatu nalla māmi niṟattiyuṭiya mēṉiyiṉ ciṟanta aḻaku oḻiya, iṉital āṉṟiciṉ - varuntutal oḻivāyāka;
8-14. kaṉi tiṟal centī aṇaṅkiya ceḻu niṇa koḻu kuṟi - ceṟinta valiya centīyil cuṭṭa vaḷam poruntiya niṇattil koḻuviya tuṇṭukaḷi, meṉ tiṉi puṉkam utirtta maṇṭi
yoṭu - melliya tiṉic cōṟṟilē corinta kalattuṭaṉ, iruṅ katir alamarum kaḻaṉi - periya neṟkatirkaḷ cuḻalkiṉṟa vayaliṭattu, karumpiṉ viḷikaḻi piḻinta - muṟṟiya karumpiṉ taṇṭiṉip piḻinta, am tīm cēṟṟoṭu - aḻakiya iṉiya cāṟu aṭṭapākuṭaṉ, pāl pey - pāliṉiyum peytu aḷinta, cennel pācaval - cennellālākiya paciya avali, pakukkum - pāṇar mutalāyi ṉārkkup pakuttuk koṭukkum, puṉal poru putaviṉ uṟanti eytiṉum - nīr mōtukiṉṟa vāyttali kaḷiyuṭiya uṟiyūriyē aṭivatāyiṉum;
15-17. kaṭi civantu aiya amartta uṇkaṇ niṉ - kaṭi civappuṟṟu viyakkumāṟu poruntiya miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya niṉatu, vi ēr vāl eyiṟu ūṟiya  nīr - aḻaku poruntiya kūriya veḷḷiya eyiṟṟiṉkaṇ ūṟiya nīriṉi, viṉi poruḷāka - (nam talivar) poruḷīṭṭum viṉi kāraṇamāka, tavirvilar - uṇṇātu tavirntiruppārallar.
(muṭipu) āyiḻi! vēṉilum kaṅkul iṉṉā kaḻiyum eṉṟu niṉ mēṉi nalam pulampa iṉital āṉṟiciṉ; nam talivar uṟanti eytiṉum niṉpāl eyiṟu ūṟiya nīriṉi viṉiporuḷākat tavirvilar.
(vi-ri) puṉkāl vī eṉak kūṭṭuka. ari - oṇmiyumām. `puṉkāl...... kuyil kuralkaṟṟa' eṉṟataṉāl īṇṭuk kuṟittatu iḷavēṉil ākum. pātiri mutaliya pūkkaḷ nīr vaṟṟiya karumaṇaliṟ citaṟi aḻaku ceytalum, teṉṟal pūtta kurā mutaliyavaṟṟi ūṭaṟuttu vantu vīcutalum, vaṇṭukaḷ olittalum, kuyil kūvutalum uṭiya iḷavēṉiṟ pōtu, kūṭiyiruppārkku mikka iṉpat tiṉic ceyvatākaliṉ, vēṉilum eṉṉum ummi uyarvu ciṟappu. aṇaṅkutal - varuttutal; īṇṭuc cuṭutal eṉṟapaṭi. maṇṭi - pāṇaruṭiya uṇkalam. puṉkam utirtta maṇṭi yoṭu eṉṟārēṉum. maṇṭiyiṉkaṇ corinta puṉkattiṉiyum pācavaliṉiyum pakukkum eṉak koḷka; iṉi, puṉkatti oḻitta maṇṭiyiṉkaṇ pācavaṟ pakukkum eṉṉalumām; itaṟku maṇṭi yoṭu eṉpatu vēṟṟumi mayakkam. viḷi karumpiṉ kaḻi eṉa māṟuka. cēṟu - cāṟu kāycciya pāku. cāṟu eṉṉalumām. pakukkum uṟanti eṉṟatu, pakuttaḷikkum vaṇmiyuṭiyār vāḻum celvam mikka uṟanti eṉṟapaṭi. poruḷāka - kāraṇamāka. ūṟiya nīr tavirvilar eṉṟatu, ataṉi uṇṇum vēṭkiyāl virintu varuvar eṉṟu kuṟippitta vāṟāyiṟṟu.
238. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟi vanta talimakaṟkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		māṉṟami yaṟiyā marampayil iṟumpiṉ
		īṉṟiḷip paṭṭa vayavuppiṇap pacitteṉa
		maṭamāṉ valci tarīiya naṭunāḷ
		iruṇmukic cilampiṉ irivēṭ ṭeḻunta
	5.	1paṉimaruḷ eruttiṟ palvari irumpōttu
		maṭakkaṇ āmāṉ mātirattu alaṟat
		taṭakkōṭṭu āmāṉ aṇṇal ēeṟu
		naṉantalik kāṉattu valampaṭat tolicci
		iruṅkal viyalaṟi civappa īrkkum
	10.	peruṅkal nāṭa piriti yāyiṉ
		maruntum uṭiyiyō maṟṟē irappōrkku
		iḻiyaṇi neṭuntēr kaḷiṟoṭu eṉṟum
		maḻicuran taṉṉa īki vaṇmakiḻk
		kaḻaltoṭit taṭakkik 2kalimāṉ naḷḷi
	15.	naḷimuki yuṭinta naṟuṅkā raṭukkattup
		pōnti muḻumutal niliiya kāntaḷ
		meṉpiṇi mukiyaviḻn talarnta
		taṇkamaḻ putumalar nāṟunaṟu nutaṟkē.			- kapilar.
(co-ḷ) 1-10. māṉṟami aṟiyā maram payil iṟumpiṉ - maraṅkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu piṉṉiyiruttal aṟiyappaṭātavāṟu neruṅki yuḷḷa kāṭṭiṉkaṇ, īṉṟu iḷippaṭṭa vayavu piṇa pacit teṉa - īṉṟu kāvaṟpaṭṭa vēṭkiyiṉiyuṭiya peṇpuli paciyuṟṟatāka, maṭamāṉ valci tarīiya - ataṟku iḷiya māṉākiya uṇavik koṇarntutara, naṭunāḷ iruḷmukic cilampiṉ irivēṭṭu eḻunta - naṭuiravil iruṇṭa kukikaḷiyuṭiya pakka maliyil iri koḷḷutali virumpip puṟappaṭṭa, paṉimaruḷ eruttiṉ pal vari iru pōttu - paṉantuṇṭiṉip pōṉṟa piṭariyiṉi yuṭiya pala varikaḷiyuṭiya periya āṇpuli, maṭakaṇ āmāṉ mātirattu alaṟa - maṭappam poruntiya kaṇṇiṉiyuṭiya kāṭṭuppacu cēymikkaṇ niṉṟu alaṟa, taṭa kōṭṭu āmāṉ aṇṇal ēṟu - vaḷinta kōṭṭiṉiyuṭiya talimi poruntiya ak kāṭṭup pacuviṉ ēṟṟiṉi, naṉatalik kāṉattu valampaṭa tolicci - akaṉṟa iṭattiṉiyuṭiya kāṭṭil valappakkam vīḻak koṉṟu, iru kal viyal aṟi civappa īrkkum - periya maliyi yaṭutta akaṉṟa pāṟikaḷ kurutiyāl civantiṭa iḻuttēkum, peru kal nāṭa - periya mali poruntiya nāṭṭiyuṭiyavaṉē!
piritiyāyiṉ - nī ivaḷip pirintu celvāyāyiṉ,
11-18. irappōrkku - pāṇar mutaliya iravalarkku, iḻi aṇi neṭu tēr - aṇikaḷ aṇinta neṭiya tēri, kaḷiṟōṭu - kaḷiṟṟuṭaṉ, eṉṟum maḻi curantaṉṉa īki- eññāṉṟum vāṉam curantaḷippatu pōṉṟu tarum īkiyiṉiyum, vaṇ makiḻ - mikka makiḻcciyiṉiyum, kaḻal toṭi - kaḻalaviṭṭa toṭiyaṇinta, taṭaki - periya kiyiṉiyum, kalimāṉ - cerukkuṟṟa kutirikaḷi yumuṭiya, naḷḷi - naḷḷi eṉpāṉatu, naḷi muki uṭinta naṟu kār aṭukkattu - ceṟinta arumpukaḷ malarnta naṟiya kariya pakkamali kaḷil, pōnti muḻumutal niliiya - paṉiyiṉ periya aṭip pakkattē nilipeṟṟuḷḷa, kāntaḷ mel piṇi muki aviḻntu alarnta - kāntaḷatu melliya piṇikkap peṟṟa arumpukaḷ itaḻ virintu malarnta, taṇ kamaḻ putumalar nāṟum naṟu nutaṟku - taṇṇitākik kamaḻum putiya malar pōla nāṟum naṟiya neṟṟiyuṭi yāḷi (iṟavātu kāttaṟku);
11. maruntum uṭiyiyō - maruntiṉiyum uṭiyiyō? maṟṟu - uṭiyi yalliyākaliṉ piriyaṟka.
(muṭipu) peruṅkal nāṭa! nī ivaḷip piritiyāyiṉ, naṟu nutaliyuṭiyāḷi (iṟavātu kāttaṟku) maruntum uṭiyiyō! maṟṟu.
(vi-ri) māṉṟami - mayaṅkiṉami. vayavu - cūl mutirnta viṭattum īṉṟaviṭattum uṇavu mutaliya paṟṟi yuṇṭām ācip perukkam. paṇi maruḷ eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ, paṭṭaṭi pōlum eṉka. paṭṭaṭi - kollaṉ ulikkaḷattu aṭikal. palvari eṉṉum aṭiyāl, pōttu, pulikkāyiṟṟu. puliyāṉatu paciyāl uyir varuntuvatāyiṉum iṭappakkattil vīḻnta vilaṅkiṉi uṇṇāmiyum, valappakkattē vīḻtti uṇṇutalum ākiya iyalpiṉiyuṭiyateṉpatu `kaṭuṅkaṭ kēḻal iṭampaṭa vīḻnteṉa, aṉṟavaṇ uṇṇātāki vaḻināḷ....... iruṅkaḷiṟ ṟoruttal nalvalam paṭukkum, pulipacittaṉṉa'1 eṉpataṉāl aṟika. `toṭaṅku viṉi
tavirā acivil nōṉṟāḷ, kiṭantuyir maṟukuvatāyiṉum iṭampaṭiṉ, vīḻkaḷiṟu miciyāp puliyiṉum'2 eṉa innūluḷ muṉṉarp pōntamiyum aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu. maṟṟu, viṉimāṟṟu. tēr kaḷiṟoṭu īyum eṉa oru col viritturikka. vaṇmakiḻ - vaṇmiyālākiya makiḻcciyumām.  naḷimukiyuṭinta - pūkkaḷiṉ ceṟinta mukiyuṭinta eṉka. naṟu nutaṟku - naṟu nutaliyuṭi
yāḷi iṟavātu kāttaṟku eṉa viritturikka. maruntum uṭiyi
yō eṉṟatu, nī piriyiṉ iṟantupaṭuvaḷ eṉṟapaṭiyām; eṉṟum piriyātirukka varintu koḷka eṉpatu karuttu.
239. pāli
(poruḷvayiṟ pirintupōkāniṉṟa talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		3aḷitō tāṉē evaṉā vatukol
		maṉṟun tōṉṟātu maraṉum māyum
		puliyeṉa ulampuñ ceṅkaṇ āṭavar
		ñeliyoṭu piṭitta vārkōl ampiṉar
	5.	ellūr eṟintu palāt taḻīiya
		viḷipaṭu pūcal veñcurattu iraṭṭum
		vēṟupal tēettu āṟupala nīntip
		puḷḷit toyyil poṟipaṭu cuṇaṅkiṉ
		oḷḷiḻi makaḷir uyarpiṟi toḻūum
	10.	pulleṉ māli yāmivaṇ oḻiya
		īṭṭaruṅ kuriya poruḷvayiṉ celiṉē
		nīṭṭuvir allirō neṭuntaki yīreṉak
		kuṟuneṭum pulavi kūṟi nammoṭu
		4nerunalun tīmpala moḻinta
	3.	ciṟunal orutti perunal lūrē.    - eyiṉanti maka ṉiḷaṅkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 12. neṭun takiyīr - perun taṉmiyuṭiyīrē!
3-7. puli eṉa ulampum  ceṅkaṇ āṭavar - pulipōla muḻaṅkum civanta kaṇṇiyuṭiya maṟavar, ñeliyoṭu piṭitta vārkōl ampiṉar - tīk koḷḷiyuṭaṉ piṭittuḷḷa nīṇṭa tiraṇṭa ampiṉa rākic ceṉṟu, el ūr eṟintu - iravil māṟṟār ūriṉip pōriṟṟolittu, pal ā taḻīiya viḷipaṭu pūcal - pala āṉiṉaṅ kaḷik kavarntamiyāl uṇṭākiya olittal mikka āravāram, vem curattu iraṭṭum - koṭiya curattē māṟi yolikkum, vēṟu pal tēettu āṟu pala nīnti - vēṟupaṭṭa moḻi vaḻaṅkum pala tēyaṅkaḷiṉ neṟikaḷ palavaṟṟik kaṭantu ceṉṟu;
8-12. puḷḷit toyyil - puḷḷi vaṭiviṉatāka eḻutappaṭṭa kōlattiṉiyum, poṟipaṭu cuṇaṅkiṉ - poṟikaḷ poruntiya tēmaliyum uṭiya, oḷ iḻi makaḷir - oḷḷiya aṇikaḷi yaṇinta maṅkiyar, uyar piṟi toḻūum pul eṉ māli - uyarnta piṟiyiṉi vaṇaṅkum polivaṟṟa mālikkālattē, yām ivaṇ oḻiya - nām iṅkē taṉittirukka, īṭṭu arum kuriya - īṭṭutaṟku arumiyāya, poruḷ vayiṉ celiṉē - poruḷīṭṭum viṉikkaṇ celliṉ, nīṭṭuvir allirō eṉa - kālam tāḻppīr allīrō eṉṟu;
13-15. kuṟuneṭu pulavi kūṟi - kuṟiyaṉavum neṭiyaṉavumākiya ūṭalurikaḷik kūṟi, nerunalum nammoṭu tīmpala moḻinta - nēṟṟum nammuṭaṉ iṉiya palavaṟṟi uritta, ciṟu nal orutti perunal ūr - iḷami poruntiya nallaḷāya nam taliviyatu periya nalla ūrāṉatu;
1-2. maṉṟum tōṉṟātu - maṉṟum tōṉṟap peṟātu; maraṉum māyum - maraṅkaḷum maṟiyappeṟum; evaṉ āvatu kol - iṉi ettaṉmiyatu ākumō? aḷitu - iraṅkattakkatu.
(muṭipu) neṭun takiyīr! vēṟu pal tēyattu āṟu pala nīnti, yām ivaṇoḻiya, poruḷ vayiṟ celiṉē, nīṭṭuvir allirō eṉa nerunalum nammoṭu pulavikūṟit tīmpala moḻinta orutti perunal ūrmaṉṟum tōṉṟātu; maraṉum māyum; evaṉāvatu kol? aḷitu.
(vi-ri) ceṅkaṇ āṭavar eṉṟatu, veṭci maṟavari. māṟṟār ūriṉit tīkkoḷuvutaṟku ñeliyum, pakivarik kōṟaṟku ampuṅ koṇṭu ceṉṟārākaliṉ, `ñeliyoṭu piṭitta vārkōlacampiṉar' eṉappaṭṭār. iravilē ceṉṟu ūriṉi aḻipparākaliṉ `el ūreṟintu' eṉṟār; `aravūr matiyiṟ karitūra ēma - iravū rerikoḷīik koṉṟu'1 eṉa varutal kāṇka. viḷipaṭu pūcal - veṭciyāruṅ karantiyārum pōrceytalāl eḻum āravāram. `ellūreṟintu pallāt taḻīiya, viḷipaṭu pūcal' eṉpataṉkaṇ, ūrkoli, ā kōḷ, pūcal māṟṟu eṉṉum veṭcittiṇiyiṉ tuṟikaḷ amintiruttal aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu. iraṭṭum āṟu eṉavum tēettu āṟu eṉavum iyiyum.  toyyil - cantaṉakkuḻampu mutaliyavaṟṟāl makaḷir mārpilum tōḷilum ceyyum kōlam. uyarpiṟi - mēl vaḷartaṟkuriya piṟi eṉṟumām. vatuviyākāta kaṉṉiyar piṟi toḻutal marapu eṉka. iḷamiyum eḻilum vāynta taliviyiṉ ūṭaluriyum talivaṉukku mikka iṉpam viḷittaṉavākaliṉ, `pulavikūṟi nammoṭu tīmpala moḻinta, ciṟunal lorutti' eṉṟāṉ. taliviyip pirinta piṟṟiñāṉṟu, iṭiccurattē taṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟukiṉṟāṉākaliṉ, nerunalum eṉṟāṉ. perunallūr - avaḷ piṟattalāṟ perumiyum naṉmiyum vāynta ūr eṉka. ūriṉkaṇ maṉṟum tōṉṟātu maraṉum māyum eṉṟumām. taliviyip pirintu avvūri viṭuttuc cellun talivaṉ, talivipāluḷḷa kātal mikutiyāl avvūriṉi nōkkiyavaṇṇam celvāṉākaliṉ, aṅṅaṉam celluṅkāl mutalil maṉṟu maṟiyavum, piṉ uyarcciyuṭiya maraṅkaḷum maṟiyaluṟṟaṉa; ataṉāl āṟṟāmi eytiya talivaṉ iṉiyum eṉakku ettaṉmiyatākuṅkol eṉṟāṉ eṉka.
240. neytal
(tōḻi, iravukkuṟivanta talimakaṟkup pakaṟkuṟi nērntatu.)
		cevvī ñāḻal 1karuṅkōṭ ṭiruñciṉit
		taṉippārppu uḷḷiya taṇpaṟi nāri
		maṇippū neytal mākkaḻi nivappa
		iṉippulam piṉṟē kāṉalum naḷikaṭal
	5.	tiriccuram uḻanta tiṇtimil viḷakkiṟ
		paṉmīṉ kūṭṭam eṉṉiyark kāṭṭiya
		entiyuñ cellumār iravē antil
		aṇaṅkuṭip paṉittuṟi kitoḻu tētti
		yāyum āyamō ṭayarum nīyun
	10	tēmpāy ōti tirunutal nīvik
		kōṅkumukit taṉṉa kuvimuli yākattu
		iṉṟuyil amarntaṉi yāyiṉ vaṇṭupaṭa
		virinta cerunti veṇmaṇaṉ muṭukkarp
		pūvēy puṉṉiyan taṇpoḻil
	15.	vāvē teyya maṇantaṉi celaṟkē.
- eḻūuppaṉṟi nākaṉ kumaraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-4. cev vī ñāḻal karu kōṭṭu iru ciṉi - civanta pūviṉiyuṭiya pulinakak koṉṟiyiṉ kariya kompiṉ periya kiḷiyil, taṉi pārppu uḷḷiya taṇ paṟi nāri - taṉiyēyirukkum taṉ kuñciṉi niṉitta taṇiyap paṟattaliyuṭiya nāri, maṇi pū neytal mā kaḻi nivappa - nīlamaṇi pōlum neytaṟ pūkkaḷiyuṭiya kariyakaḻiyiṉiṉṟum mēlē eḻuntu paṟantiṭa, kāṉalum iṉi pulam piṉṟu - kaṭaṟkariccōliyum ippoḻutu taṉimiyuṟṟatu;
4-7. naḷi kaṭal tiri curam uḻanta tiṇ timil viḷakkil - ceṟinta kaṭalākiya tiriyiyuṭiya curattil tirintu varuntiya tiṇṇiya paṭakiṉ viḷakkoḷiyil, pal mīṉ kūṭṭam eṉ aiyark kāṭṭiya - tāṉ akappaṭutta palavāya mīṉ kūṭṭaṅkaḷi eṉ aṇṇaṉmārkkuk kāṭṭutaṟku, entiyum iravu cellum - eṉ tantiyum iravil maṉikkaṇ pōtarum;
7-9. yāyum āyamōṭu - eṉ tāyum āyattāruṭaṉ, aṇaṅku uṭi paṉi tuṟi antil ki toḻutu ētti ayarum - teyva muṭiya kuḷirnta tuṟiyāya āṅkē (atteyvattik) kiyāl vaṇaṅkit tutittu ataṟkuc ciṟappuc ceyyum;
9-15. nīyum -, tēm pāy ōti - tēṉ oḻukum kūntaliyuṭiya taliviyatu, tiru nutal nīvi - aḻakiya neṟṟiyit taṭavi, kōṅku mukittaṉṉa kuvimuli ākattu - kōṅku arumpiṉā lotta kuvinta muliyiṉiyuṭiya mārpakattē, iṉ tuyil amarntaṉi yāyiṉ - iṉiya tuyili virumpiṉāyāyiṉ, maṇantaṉi celaṟku - aṅṅaṉam avaḷik kūṭic cellutaṟku, vaṇṭupaṭa virinta cerunti - vaṇṭu moykka virinta pūkkaḷi yuṭiya ceruntimaram poruntiya, veṇmaṇal muṭukkar - veḷḷiya maṇal paranta muṭukkil, pūvēy puṉṉi am taṇ poḻil - pūkkaḷ poruntiya puṉṉi maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya aḻakiya taṇṇiya cōlikkaṇ, vā - varuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) (taliva!) nāriyum nivappak kāṉal pulampiṉṟu; entiyum iravu (maṉikkaṭ) cellum; yāyum āyamoṭu ayarum; nīyum ōti ākattu iṉṟuyil amarntaṉiyāyiṉ maṇantaṉi celaṟkup puṉṉiyan taṇpoḻil vā.
(vi-ri) taṇ paṟi - mellap paṟattal. nivappa - uyarntu paṟantu cella eṉṟapaṭi. nāri nivappak kāṉal pulampiṉṟu eṉṟatu, kaṭalil akappaṭutta mīṉkaḷik kariyiṟpōkaṭṭuc cellutaṟku iṭiyūṟiṉmi kūṟiyapaṭi. nāri nivappa eṉṟārēṉum iṉampaṟṟip piṟa paṟavikaḷum koḷḷappaṭum. vēṭṭam āṭuvār curattilē cellum vaḻakku valiyāl kaṭalic curamāka uruvakit tār. enti iravil maṉikkaṭ cellum eṉpatum, yāy aṇaṅ kiṟkuc ciṟappayarum eṉpatum, iravukkuṟiyiṉ iṭiyīṭu kūṟiya vāṟu. aṇaṅku - neytaṟ ṟeyvamākiya varuṇaṉ. `vaṇṭu paṭa virinta.......... taṇpoḻil' eṉṟatu pakaṟkuṟikkuc ciṟanta kaḷam cuṭṭiyavāṟu. teyya, aci. antilum aci yeṉṉalumām.
(mē-ḷ) `māyōṉ mēya'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `aṇaṅkuṭi........... ayarum' eṉpatu, neytaṉmakaḷir kiḷiyuṭaṉ kuḻīi varuṇaṟkup paravukkaṭaṉ koṭuppatu eṉṟum, `tōḻiyiṉ muṭiyum iṭaṉumā ruṇṭē'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `pūvēy puṉṉiyan taṇpoḻil nīvā' eṉpataṉi itu tōḻi kaḷañ cuṭṭiyatu eṉṟum, `pakaṟ puṇar kaḷaṉē'3 eṉṉum cūttirattu, itu tōḻi pakaṟkuṟi urittatu eṉṟum, `poḻutum āṟum'4  eṉṉum cūttirattu, itu, pakal nī vā eṉṟal vaḻuvamiti eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nac.
241. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷ, vaṟpuṟuttun tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		tuṉiyiṉ ṟiyinta tuvarā naṭpiṉ
		iṉiyar amma avareṉa muṉiyātu
		nalkuvar nalla kūṟiṉum alkalum
		piriyāk kātaloṭu uḻiya rākiya
	5.	namarmaṉ vāḻi tōḻi yuyarmici
		mūṅkil iḷamuḷi tiraṅkak kāmpiṉ
		kaḻinaral viyalakam vempa maḻimaṟantu
		aruvi yāṉṟa veruvaru naṉantalip
		pēey veṇtērp peyalcet tōṭit
	10.	tāam paṭṭa taṉimutir peruṅkali
		pulampeyarn tuṟital cellā 5talaṅkutali
		viruntiṉ veṅkāṭṭu varunti vikum
		atta nellit tīñcuvit tiraḷkāy
		vaṭṭak kaḻaṅkil tāayt tuyttalic
	15.	cemmuka manti āṭum
		6naṉmara maruṅkiṉ maliyiṟan tōrē.
- kāvaṉ mullip pūtaṉār.
5. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
(co-ḷ) 1-5. muṉiyātu nalkuvar - eṉṉi veṟātu aruḷuvār oruvar, tuṉi iṉṟu iyinta tuvarā naṭpiṉ - veṟuppiṉṟi oṉṟiya eṉṟum piḷavupaṭāta naṭpiṉiyuṭiya, iṉiyar - iṉiya kuṇaṅkaḷi
yuṭiyar, avar eṉa - nam talivar eṉa, nalla kūṟiṉum - avaratu naṟkuṇaṅkaḷip pārāṭṭik kūṟiṉum, alkalum - eṉṟum, piriyāk kātaloṭu - nammi viṭṭup piriyāta kātaluṭaṉ, uḻiyar ākiya - nam pakkalil uḷḷār ākiya, namar - nam kātalar (ippoḻutu),
5-16. uyar mici - maliyiṉ uccikkaṇṇē yuḷḷa, mūṅkil iḷamuḷi tiraṅka - mūṅkiliṉ iḷiya muḷi vataṅkavum, kāmpiṉ kaḻi naral viyal akam vempa - mūṅkil taṇṭu olikkum periya pāṟiyiṉ iṭaṅkaḷ vempavum, maḻi maṟantu - maḻi peyyātu maṟantu pōtaliṉ, aruvi āṉṟa - aruvi illiyākiya, veruvaru naṉantali - accam varum akaṉṟa iṭattē tōṉṟiya, pēey veṇ tērpeyal cettu ōṭi - veḷḷiya pēyttēri maḻiyeṉṟu karuti ōṭi, tāam paṭṭa taṉi mutir peru kali - tākampaṭṭa taṉitta mutirnta periya kalimāṉ, pulam peyarntu uṟital cellātu-avviṭattiṉiṉṟum mīṇṭu uṟital māṭṭātu, alaṅku tali viruntiṉ vem kāṭṭu - ap pēyttēr aciyum iṭamāya putiya vevviya kāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇē, varunti vikum - varuttamuṟṟut taṅkik
kiṭakkum, attam nelli tīm cuvi tiraḷ kāy - pālinila vaḻiyil uḷḷa nelliyiṉ iṉiya cuvi poruntiya tiraṇṭa kāykaḷ ākiya, vaṭṭa kaḻaṅkil - vaṭṭamāṉa kaḻaṅkukaḷik koṇṭu, tuy tali cemmuka manti tāay āṭum - pañcu pōṉṟa mayiriyuṭiya tali
yiṉiyum civanta mukattiyumuṭiya manti tāvi viḷiyāṭum, nalmara maruṅkiṉ maliyiṟantōr - nalla maraṅkaḷ poruntiya pakkatti
yuṭiya maliyiṉik kaṭantu ceṉṟār ātaliṉ āṟṟakillēṉ.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! muṉiyātu nalkuvār oruvar, `naṭpiṉ iṉiyar nam talivar' eṉa nalla kūṟiṉum, piriyāk kātaloṭu uḻiyar ākiya namar, maliyiṟantōr; (ātaliṉ āṟṟakillēṉ eṉṟāḷ).
(vi-ri) muṉiyātu nalkuvar eṉṟatu, tōḻiyip piṟar pōṟ kūṟiyatākum. iṉi avar naṭpiṉ iṉiyar, nalkuvar eṉa nī muṉiyātu nalla kūṟiṉum eṉṟurittalumām. maṟantu - maṟattalāl. pēeyt tēr - veṇṭēr eṉavumpaṭum. tākam eṉṟatu tāam eṉa vikāramāyiṟṟu. pulam - vēṟu naṟpulam eṉṟumām. alaṅkutali - varuntutaṟ kētuvākiya iṭamumām.
242. kuṟiñci
(talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat tōḻi talimakaṭkuc 
colluvāḷāyc colliyatu.)
		arumpumutir vēṅki alaṅkal meṉciṉic
		curumpuvāy tiṟanta poṉpuri nuṇtātu
		maṇimaruḷ kalavat tuṟippa aṇimikku
		avirpoṟi maññi yāṭuñ cōlip
	5.	pintāṭ centiṉik koṭuṅkural viyaṉpuṉam
		centārk kiḷḷi nammoṭu kaṭintōṉ
		paṇputara vantami aṟiyāḷ nuṇkēḻ
		muṟipuri yeḻiṉalat teṉmakaḷ tuyarmaruṅku
		aṟital vēṇṭumeṉap palpirap pirīi
	10.	aṟiyā vēlaṉ tarīi aṉṉi
		veṟiyayar viyaṉkaḷam poliya ētti
		maṟiyuyir vaḻaṅkā aḷavic ceṉṟiyām
		celavarat tuṇinta cēṇviḷaṅku elvaḷi
		nekiḻnta muṉki nēriṟip paṇittōḷ
	15.	nalleḻil aḻivil tolkaviṉ peṟīiya
		mukiḻttuvaral iḷamuli mūḻkap pallūḻ
		muyaṅkal iyivatu maṉṉō tōḻi
		naṟikāl yātta 1naḷirmukic cilampiṉ
		perumali viṭarakam nīṭiya ciṟiyilic
	20.	cānta meṉciṉi tīṇṭi mēvatu
		piracan tūṅku cēṭcimi
		variyaka veṟpaṉ 2maṇanta mārpē.
- pēri cāttaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 17. tōḻi-,
1-7. arumpu mutir vēṅki alaṅkal mel ciṉi - arumpukaḷ mutirnta vēṅkimarattiṉ aciyum melliya kiḷikaḷil, curumpu vāy tiṟanta poṉpuri nuṇ tātu - vaṇṭukaḷ pūviṉ vāyit tiṟattalāl poṉṉi yotta nuṇṇiya poṭikaḷ, maṇi maruḷ kalavattu uṟippa - nīlamaṇiyi yotta tōkiyil utirntiṭa, aṇi mikku - aḻaku mikuntu, avir poṟi maññi āṭum cōli - viḷaṅkum poṟikaḷiyuṭiya mayil āṭum cōliyiṉ pakkattē, pintāḷ centiṉi koṭuṅ kural - paciya tāḷiṉiyuṭiya civanta tiṉiyiṉ vaḷinta katirkaḷiyuṭiya, viyaṉ puṉam - periya kolliyil varum, centārkkiḷḷi nammoṭu kaṭintōṉ - civanta irēkiyi yuṭiya kiḷikaḷi nammuṭaṉiruntu ōṭṭiya nam talivaṉatu, paṇpu tara vantami aṟiyāḷ - nalliyalpukaḷ tarutalāl vanta varuttatti aṟiyātavaḷāki;
7-12. aṉṉi - nam aṉṉi, nuṇ kēḻ muṟi puri eḻil nalattu eṉ makaḷ - nuṇṇiya niṟam poruntiya taḷiri yotta eḻucci vāynta aḻakiṉiyuṭiya eṉ makaḷatu, tuyar maruṅku aṟital vēṇṭum eṉa - tuyarvanta pariciṉi aṟital vēṇṭum eṉṟu, pal pirappu irīi - pala pirappiṉip paliyākavittu, aṟiyā vēlaṉ tarīi - uṇmi uṇaramāṭṭāta vēlaṉi aḻittu, veṟi ayar viyaṉ kaḷam poliya ētti - veṟiyāṭum periya kaḷam polivuṟumāṟu tutittu, maṟi uyir vaḻaṅkā aḷavi - āṭṭukkuṭṭiyiṉ uyirip paliyiṭā muṉṉarē;
12-15. celavarat tuṇinta - ēṟi iṟaṅkum aḷavil eṇṇic cērtta, cēṇ viḷaṅku elvaḷi - cēymikkaṇ ceṉṟu viḷaṅkum oḷiyiṉiyuṭiya vaḷi, nekiḻnta muṉ ki - kaḻaṉṟa muṉ kiyum, nēr iṟi - nēriya cantiṉiyuṭiya, paṇi tōḷ - periya tōḷum, nal eḻil aḻivil - nalla aḻaku oḻiviṉiṉṟum nīṅki, tolkaviṉ peṟīiya - paḻiya aḻakiṉip peṟumāṟu, yām ceṉṟu - yām ceṉṟu;
18-22. naṟi kāl yātta naḷir muki cilampiṉ - tēṉ kūṭukaḷ nirampiya kuḷirnta kukikaḷiyuṭiya pakka malikaḷik koṇṭa, perumali viṭar akam - periya maliyiṉ piḷappukkaḷiṭattē, nīṭiya ciṟi ili cānta mel ciṉi tīṇṭi - nīṇṭu vaḷarnta ciṟiya ilikaḷiyuṭiya cantaṉamarattiṉ melliya kiḷikaḷ tīṇṭa, mēlatu piracam tūṅkum - mēliṭattatākiya tēṉiṟāl toṅkum, cēṇ cimi variyaka veṟpaṉ - neṭiya ucciyiṉiyuṭiya mūṅkilit taṉṉakattē koṇṭa mali nāṭaṉatu, maṇanta mārpu- muṉpu kūṭiya mārpiṉi;
16-17. mukiḻttu varal iḷa muli mūḻka - arumpi varutaluṭiya iḷiya muli mūḻkiṭa, pal ūḻ muyaṅkal iyivatu - palamuṟi taḻuvutal takkatu.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! (namatu iṭumpi) kiḷḷi nammoṭu kaṭintōṉ paṇpu tara vantami aṟiyāḷ, aṉṉi tuyarmaruṅku aṟital vēṇṭumeṉa vēlaṉ tarīi, pirappu irīi, kaḷam poliya ētti, maṟiyuyir vaḻaṅkā aḷavi, yām ceṉṟu veṟpaṉ maṇanta
mārpu muli mūḻka muyaṅkal iyivatu.
(vi-ri) vāy tiṟanta - pūviṉ vāyit tiṟanta eṉa virittuk koḷka. tiṟanta eṉṉum peyareccam kāraṇapporuṭṭu. kiḷḷi nammoṭu kaṭintōṉ eṉṟatu, eḷiyaṉāy nammuṭaṉiruntu kiḷiyi ōṭṭi utavipurinta naṉmiyāḷaṉ eṉṟapaṭi. paṇpu tara vantatu eṉṟatu, avaṉatu uyarnta kuṇa voḻukkaṅkaḷāl talivikku melivuṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟum vāyilākat talivi avaṉik kātalittatu takavuṭiyatē eṉṟum, avaṉuṭaṉ iṭiyaṟātu kūṭiyirukkap peṟāmiyāl melivuṟukiṉṟāḷ eṉṟum tōḻi kūṟiyapaṭiyām. ivvuṇmiyi aṟiyāta aṉṉi taṉ makaḷuṟṟa melivu aṇaṅku mutaliyavaṟṟāl uṇṭāyatō eṉa aiyuṟṟu, ataṉi aṟintu kaḷitaṟku vēlaṉi yaḻittu veṟiyāṭ ṭeṭuttu maṟiyip paliyiṭa muntukiṉṟāḷākaliṉ, avaḷ aṅṅaṉam ceytaṟku muṉ namatu mēṉi paḻiya aḻakiṉip peṟumpaṭi, talivaṉip pallūḻ muyaṅkal vēṇṭumeṉṟu, talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattiṉiṉṟu kēṭpat, tōḻi talimakaṭkuk kūṟiṉā ḷeṉka. ataṉit talimakaṉ kēṭṭu viriya varintu koḷvā ṉātal payaṉ. aṟiyā vēlaṉ eṉṟatu uṇmiyi aṟiyāmalē, etaṉiyum teyvattāl vantateṉṟu kūṟupavaṉ eṉṟapaṭi. muṉkiyum paṇittōḷum tolkaviṉ peṟīiya eṉṟurit tāḷēṉum, mēṉi tolkaviṉ peṟīiya eṉpatu karuttākak koḷka. `paḻiya kaviṉ peṟṟamiyik kāṇiṉ, aṉṉi veṟiyāṭṭeṭuttali niṟuttuvaḷ eṉka.' celavaṟa (13) eṉṟu pāṭam koḷḷiṉ vaḷi kaḻalāvāṟu eṉṟum, malarnta mārpu eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ paranta mārpu eṉṟum poruḷ koḷka.
(u-ṟi) vēṅki nuṇtātu kalavattu uṟippa, maññi aṇimikku āṭum eṉṟatu, talivaṉatu mārpiṉ cāntam nam ākattiṟ poruntumāṟu muyaṅkutal kūṭiṉ, nām eḻil ciṟantu makiḻcciyuṟuvēm eṉṟavāṟu. uyarnta cantaṉattiṉ kiḷi tīṇṭat tēṉiṟāl toṅkum eṉṟatu, pallāṟṟāṉum oppumiyuṭiya uyarnta talivaṉum taliviyum kūṭi illaṟam purital yāvarkkum iṉpam payappatām eṉṟapaṭi.
243. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vaṟpuṟuttun tōḻikkut 
talimakaḷ āṟṟēṉ eṉpatupaṭac colliyatu.)
		avari āymalar utirat tuvariṉa
		vāṅkutuḷit tukiriṉ īṅki pūppa
		iṟaṅkupō taviḻnta īrmputal pakaṉṟi
		kaṟaṅkunuṇ tuvaliyiṉ ūruḻi yaṇiyap
	5.	peyalnīr putuvaral tavirac ciṉinērpu
		pīḷvirin tiṟiñciya piṟaṅkukatirk kaḻaṉi
		nelloli pācaval tuḻiik kalleṉak
		kaṭituvan tiṟutta kaṇṇil vāṭi
		neṭutuvan taṉiyeṉa 1 nillā tēkip
	10.	palapulan tuṟiyun tuṇiyil vāḻkki
		nam 2valan taṉmi kūṟi avar nili
		3aṟiyum āyiṉ naṉṟumaṉ tilla
		paṉivār kaṇṇē māki iṉiyatu
		namakkē evvam ākiṉṟu
	15.	aṉittāl tōḻinan tolviṉip payaṉē.
- 4 koṭiyūrkiḻār makaṉār neytaṟṟattaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 15. tōḻi-,
1-9. avari āy malar utira - avariyiṉ aḻakiya malar utiravum, tuvariṉa vāṅku tuḷi tukiriṉ - civanta vaḷinta tuḷiyiṉi yuṭiya pavaḷampōla, īṅki pūppa - iṇṭiyāṉatu pūkkavum, iṟaṅku pōtu aviḻnta īr putal pakaṉṟi - ñāyiṟu tāḻum poḻutu virinta kuḷirnta putalākiya pakaṉṟi, kaṟaṅku nuṇ tuvaliyiṉ ūr uḻi aṇiya - olikkum vāṭiyuṭaṉ varum nuṇṇiya maḻittuḷiyāl ūriṉ arukē aḻakupeṟa malaravum, peyal nīr putu varal tavira - maḻiyākiya nīr putuvatāka varutal oḻiyavum,  ciṉi nērpu pīḷ virintu iṟiñciya - kiḷittal poruntik karuvirintu eḻuntu vaḷinta, piṟaṅku katir kaḻaṉi nel oli pacu aval tuḻii - viḷaṅkum katirkaḷiyuṭiya vayaliṉ nel oli ceyyum paciya paḷḷaṅkaḷit tuḻāvi, kal eṉa kaṭitu vantu iṟutta kaṇil vāṭi - kalleṉṟa oliyuṭaṉ virintuvantu taṅkiya irakkamaṟṟa vāṭiyāṉatu, nillātu ēki - tāḻkkātu talivariṭattē virintu ceṉṟu, neṭitu vantaṉi eṉa - nī vantu neṭuṅkālam tāḻttaṉi eṉṟu;
10-12. pala pulantu uṟiyum tuṇi il vāḻkki - palavaṟṟiyum veṟuttu uṟiyum tuṇivaruṭaṉ kūṭiyirāta vāḻkkiyiṉi yuṭiya, nam valattaṉmi kūṟi - namatu valittaṉmiyik kūṟi, avar nili aṟiyum āyiṉ naṉṟu maṉ - avartam niliyiṉit tērumāyiṉ peritum naṉṟākum;
13-15. iṉi atu - ippoḻutu av vāṭiyāṉatu, paṉivār kaṇṇēm āki - nām nīr ōḻukum kaṇkaḷi yuṭiyēm āka, namakkē evvam ākiṉṟu - namakkē tuṉpam taruvatāka ākiṉṟatu, nam tolviṉip payaṉ aṉittu - namatu paḻiya tī viṉiyiṉ payaṉ attaṉmittup pōlum.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāṭi nillātu talivariṭattē ēki, nī neṭitu vantaṉiyeṉakkūṟi, pala pulantu uṟiyum tuṇiyil vāḻkki nam valattaṉmi kūṟi, avar nili aṟiyumāyiṉ naṉṟu; iṉi atu paṉivār kaṇṇēm āka, namakkē evvam ākiṉṟu; nam tolviṉippayaṉ aṉittu.
utira, pūppa, aṇiya, tavira, tuḻii, kalleṉa iṟutta vāṭi eṉka.
(vi-ri) muṉṉarē, talivaṉip pirintu varuntum nampāṟ ciṟitum irakkamiṉṟi, vāṭi nammi mēlum varuttukiṉṟatu eṉpāḷ, `kaṇṇil vāṭi' eṉṟāḷ. kaṇ - kaṇṇōṭṭam, irakkam; atu varak kaṭavatākiya kālattiṉum muṉṉarē nammi varuttutaṟ poruṭṭu vantu nilipeṟṟaḷatu eṉpāḷ, `kaṭitu vantiṟutta' eṉṟāḷ. valat taṉmiyāvatu, iṉṉalip poṟuttu uyir tāṅkiyirukkum valittaṉmiyākum. avar nili aṟiyu māyiṉ naṉṟu maṉ eṉṟatu, nammiṭattē taṅki iṉṉal viḷikkum ivvāṭi, talivar uṟiyumiṭattum iruppatāyiṉ avar ataṟku āṟṟātu varuvar eṉṉum karuttiṟṟu. aṉṟi, vāṭi yāṉatu talivarpāl tūtākac ceṉṟu avarip pirintu nām eytum tuṉpaniliyiṉi avarkku urittu avar karuttiṉi aṟintuvantu urikkumāyiṉ naṉṟu eṉṟu urittalumām. tilla - iṭiccol : īṟu tirintatu. āki - āka eṉat tirikka.
244. mulli
(viṉimuṟṟiya talimakaṉ tērppākaṟkuc colliyatu.)
		1pacipaṭu pacci neytōyt taṉṉa
		cēyuyar ciṉiya mācciṟip paṟavi
		pakaluṟi mutumaram pulampap pōki
		mukivāy tiṟanta nakivāy mulli
	5.	kaṭimakaḷ katuppiṉ nāṟik koṭimici
		vaṇṭiṉan tavirkkun taṇpatak kāli
		variṉum vārā rāyiṉum āṇṭavarkku
		iṉitukol vāḻi tōḻi yeṉattaṉ
		pallitaḻ maḻikkaṇ nallakañ civappa
	10.	aruntuya 1ruṭiyaḷ avaḷeṉa virumpip
		pāṇaṉ vantaṉaṉ tūtē nīyum
		pullār puravi valvirintu pūṭṭi
		2neṭuntēr ūrmati valava
		muṭintaṉṟu ammanām muṉṉiya viṉiyē.
- maturi...... maḷḷaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 13. valava  - pākaṉē;
1-6. paci paṭu pacci ney tōyttaṉṉa mā ciṟi paṟavi - paci poruntiya tōli neyyiṟṟōyttāṟ pōṉṟa kariya ciṟakiṉiyuṭiya vāval, pakal uṟi cēy uyar ciṉiya mutumaram pulampap pōki - pakalil taṅkiyirunta mika uyarnta kiḷikaḷi yuṭiya mutirnta maram taṉittiṭappōka, koṭi mici mukivāy tiṟanta nakivāy mulli - koṭiyiṉmītu arumpukaḷ vāytiṟantu malarnta oḷi poruntiya mulli, kaṭimakaḷ katuppiṉ nāṟi - maṇamakaḷ kūntalippōla maṇantu, vaṇṭiṉam tavirkkum - vaṇṭiṉ tokutikaḷic cellavoṭṭātu taṭukkum, taṇpatak kāli - kuḷirnta cevviyiyuṭiya kārkālattē;
7-11. tōḻi - tōḻiyē! variṉum vārār āyiṉum - nam talivar īṇṭu variṉum vārātiruppiṉum, āṇṭu avarkku iṉitu kol - avar ceṉṟa iṭam avarkku iṉiyatupōlum, vāḻi-, eṉa - eṉṟu kūṟi, taṉ pal itaḻ maḻik kaṇ - taṉatu pala itaḻiyuṭiya tāmari malarpōṉṟa kuḷirnta kaṇṇiṉ, nal akam civappa - nalla uḷḷiṭa mellām civakka, arum tuyar uṭiyaḷ - poṟuttaṟkariya tuyariṉi yuṭiyavaḷāyiṉaḷ, avaḷ eṉa - niṉ talivi eṉṟu, pāṇaṉ - pāṇaṉ, virumpit tūtu vantaṉaṉ - viruppamuṟṟut tūtāka vantaṉaṉ;
14. nām muṉṉiya viṉi muṭintaṉṟu - nām eṇṇi muyaṉṟa viṉiyum muṭivuṟṟatu;
11-13. nīyum pul ār puravi pūṭṭi - nīyum pulliṉit tiṉṟa kutiriyip pūṭṭi, neṭu tēr valvirintu ūrmati - nīṇṭa tēriṉi mika virintu celuttuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) `tōḻi, taṇpatak kāli, nam kātalar variṉum vārārāyiṉum āṇṭu avarkku iṉitukol' eṉṟu kūṟit taṉ kaṇ civappa aruntuyaruṭiyāḷ niṉ talivi eṉṟu pāṇaṉ tūtu vantaṉaṉ; valava! viṉiyum muṭintaṉṟu; nīyum tēr virintu ūrmati.
(vi-ri) pōki - pōka eṉattirikka. mācciṟip paṟavi pōka, mulli vaṇṭiṉam tavirkkum taṇ patak kāli eṉka. pōka eṉpatu nikaḻvu uṇarttiṟṟu. taṇpatakkāli - kārkālam. paṟavi pōtal kārkālattu māli eṉak koḷka. eṉṉi, `neṭunī rām pal aṭippuṟat taṉṉa, koṭumeṉ ciṟiya kūrukir paṟavi,....... pakaluṟi mutumaram pulampap pōkum, ciṟu puṉ māli' eṉa varutaliṉ, `variṉum vārā rāyiṉum āṇṭavarkku, iṉitu kol, eṉṟatu, avar vantu namatu tuṉpattip pōkkātoḻiyiṉum, avar uṟiyumiṭam avarkku iṉpam viḷippatāyiṉ naṉṟām, avvāṟu viḷikkuṅ kollō eṉṟu talivi tuyaruṟṟu moḻintāḷ eṉka. ataṉik kūṟip pāṇaṉ tūtu vantamiyāṉum, viṉi muṭintamiyāṉum virintu tēriṉi ūrmati eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka.
(mē-ḷ) `vēntu viṉiyiyaṟki'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `muṭintaṉṟu ammanām muṉṉiya viṉiyē' eṉṟaliṉ, tāṉē kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ ceṉṟatām eṉṟaṉar, nac.
245. pāli
(poruḷ kaṭikkūṭṭiya neñciṉik kaḻaṟit 
talimakaṉ collic celavaḻuṅkiyatu.)
		uyiriṉuñ ciṟanta oṇporuḷ tarumār
		naṉ ṟupuri kāṭciyar ceṉṟaṉar avareṉa
		maṉivalit toḻiyum matukiya ḷātal
		nīnaṉku aṟintaṉi yāyiṉ nīṅki
	5.	maḻipeyal maṟanta kaḻitiraṅku iyavil
		celcāttu eṟiyum 2paṇpil vāḻkki
		valvil iḷiyar talivar elluṟa
		varikiḷar paṇittōḷ vayiṟaṇi titali
		ariya lāṭṭiyar alkumaṉi varippiṉ
	10.	makiḻnoṭi peṟāa rāki naṉikavuḷ
		kāṉa yāṉi veṇkōṭu cuṭṭi
		maṉṟōṭu putalvaṉ puṉtali nīvum
		arumuṉip pākkattu alki vikuṟa
		niḻalpaṭak kaviṉṟa nīḷaṟi ilavattu
	15.	aḻalakin taṉṉa alaṅkuciṉi yoṇpūk
		kuḻalicit tumpi yārkkum āṅkaṇ
		kuṟumpoṟi yuṇaṅkun tatarveḷ eṉpu
		kaṭuṅkāl oṭṭakat talkupaci tīrkkum
		kalneṭuṅ kavaliya kāṉam nīnti
	20.	ammā arivi yoḻiyac
		ceṉmō neñcam vāraleṉ yāṉē.
- maturi marutaṉiḷa nākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 21. neñcam - neñcē!
1-4. naṉṟu puri kāṭciyar avar - naṉmiyiṉi virumpiya aṟiviṉiyuṭiyarākiya talivar, uyiriṉum ciṟanta oḷporuḷ tarumār - uyirik kāṭṭiṉum ciṟanta oḷḷiya poruḷit tarutal vēṇṭi, ceṉṟaṉar eṉa valittu - ceṉṟaṉar eṉṟu tuṇintu, maṉi oḻiyum matukiyaḷ ātal - maṉiyiṉkaṇ taṅkiyirukkum valiyuṭiyaḷ ātali, nī naṉku aṟintaṉiyāyiṉ - nī naṉṟāka aṟintuḷāyāyiṉ, nīṅki - ivaḷiṉ nīṅki;
5-21. maḻi peyal maṟanta - maḻi peytali maṟantamiyāl, kaḻitiraṅku iyavil - mūṅkilkaḷ vaṟṟiya neṟiyil, cel cāttu eṟiyum - cellum vaṇikar tiraḷi alittiṭum, paṇpu il vāḻkki - aṉpillāta vāḻkkiyiṉiyuṭiya, val vil iḷiyar talivar - valiya villiṉik koṇṭa maṟavar talivar, el uṟa - iruḷ vara, vari kiḷar paṇi tōḷ titali aṇivayiṟu - vari  viḷaṅkum parutta tōḷiṉiyum tēmal aṇinta vayiṟṟiṉiyu muṭiya, ariyalāṭṭiyar alkumaṉi varippiṉ - kaḷviṟpōr poruntiya maṉiyakattē, makiḻ noṭi peṟāar āki - kaḷḷiṟku uriya vilipporuḷ eytap peṟārāki, naṉi kavuḷ kāṉa yāṉi veṇkōṭu cuṭṭi - matattāl naṉinta kaṉṉattiṉi yuṭiya yāṉiyiṉ veḷḷiya kompiṉi eṭuttu varutal kuṟittu, maṉṟu ōṭu putalvaṉ - maṉṟiṉkaṇ ōṭi viḷiyāṭum putalvaṉuṭiya, puṉ tali nīvum - pulliya taliyit taṭavi ēvum, arumuṉi pākkattu alki - ariya pōrmuṉikaḷiyuṭiya pākkattiṭattē taṅki, viku uṟa - iruḷ kaḻiyum viṭiyal tōṉṟa, niḻalpaṭak kaviṉṟa nīḷ ari - niḻalporunta aḻakupeṟṟa nīṇṭa ariyiṉiyuṭiya, ilavattu - ilavamarattiṉatu, alaṅku ciṉi - aciyuṅ kiḷikaḷiluḷḷa, aḻal akintaṉṉa ōḷpū - eri taḷirttālotta oḷḷiya pūkkaḷil, kuḻal icittumpi ārkkum āṅkaṇ - kuḻalpōlum iciyiyuṭiya vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum iṭamākiya, kuṟumpoṟi uṇaṅkum - pāṟiyiṭattē kāyntu kiṭakkum, tatar veḷ eṉpu - cuḷḷipōlum veḷḷiya elumpukaḷ, kaṭum kāl oṭṭakattu alku paci tīrkkum - vēkam vāynta kāl kaḷiyuṭiya oṭṭakattiṉ mikka paciyiṉip pōkkum, kal neṭum kavaliya kāṉam nīnti - kaṟkaḷ poruntiya nīṇṭa kavar vaḻikaḷi yuṭiya kāṭṭiṉik kaṭantu, am mā arivi oḻiyac ceṉmō - aḻakiya māmi niṟattiyuṭiya nam talivi taṉittirukka nī cel vāyāka, yāṉ vāraleṉ - yāṉ avaḷip pirintu niṉṉuṭaṉ vārēṉ.
(muṭipu) neñcam! maṉi valittoḻiyum matukiyā ḷātal nī naṉku aṟintaṉiyāyiṉ, nīṅki, arumuṉip pākkattu alki, vikuṟa, kāṉam nīnti, arivi oḻiyac ceṉmō; yāṉ vāraleṉ.
(vi-ri) `uyiriṉuñ ciṟanta......... ceṉṟaṉaravar' eṉa eṉpatu taliviyiṉ kūṟṟit talivaṉ koṇṭu kūṟiyatu. talivaṉ poruḷ vayiṟ piriyiṉ, taṉ uyirkku ūṟupāṭum ak kāraṇattāl talivaṉ uyirkku ētamum uṇṭākum eṉpatu tōṉṟa, `uyiriṉum ciṟanta' eṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiṉāḷ. aṟaṉum iṉpamum payavāmiyē yaṉṟi, uyirkkum ūṟu viḷikkum poruḷit tēṭutaṟ poruṭṭuc celpavar ataṉi uyiriṉum ciṟantateṉak karutiṉā rāvar eṉavum, ataṉāl avar naṉṟu puri kāṭciyar allar eṉavum koḷvaḷ eṉpatum, annīratākiya namatu piriviṉāl avaḷ maṉiyiṉkaṇ taṉittu āṟṟiyirukkum valiyuṭiyaḷ allaḷ eṉpatum nī aṟintili eṉpāṉ, `uyiriṉum ciṟanta oṇporuḷ' eṉavum, `naṉṟupuri kāṭciyar' eṉavum, `matukiyaḷ' eṉavum kuṟippumoḻiyāṟ kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka. kaḷviṟkum makaḷir tam maṉiyiṉkaṇ vittu viṟparākaliṉ, `ariya lāṭṭiyar alku maṉi varippiṉ' eṉṟār. peṟā arāki eṉṟatu, vēṟu poruḷ peṟāmiyāl, eṉka.  veṇkōṭu cuṭṭip putalvaṉ puṉṟali nīvum eṉṟatu tam maṉiyiṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa, yāṉiyiṉ kōṭṭiṉik kaḷ viliyākat tarutaṟku, ataṉik koṇarumāṟu putalvaṉi ēvuvar  eṉṟapaṭi. taliyi nīvutal avaṟku maṉaveḻucci yuṇṭākku taṟporuṭṭām. talivar ovvoruvarum tam putalvaṉ puṉṟali nīvuvar eṉak koḷka. kāṉavarkaḷ viliyāka yāṉikkōṭu koṭuttali, `vēṭṭuvar....... mataṉuṭi vēḻattu veṇkōṭu koṇṭu, poṉṉuṭi niyamattup piḻinoṭi koṭukkum'1, `vēntūr yāṉi veṇ kōṭukoṇṭu, kaṭkoṭi nuṭaṅku māvaṇam pukku, aruṅkaḷ noṭimi tīrntapiṉ makiḻciṟantu'2 eṉpavaṟṟāṉaṟika. attakiya koṭiya pākkattilē, nī iravilē taṅki iruḷ kaḻinta piṉ kāṉam nīntic cel eṉṟāṉ. veḷḷeṉpu oṭṭakattu alku paci tīrkkum eṉṟatu, ataṟkum vēṟu uṇavu kiṭṭāmi kūṟiyapaṭi. oṭṭakam pāliyilum virintu cellum iyalpiṉa teṉpatu tōṉṟak, `kaṭuṅkāl oṭṭakam' eṉṟum, palanāḷ uṇavupeṟāta paciyip poṟuttirukkum iyalpiṉatu eṉpatu tōṉṟa, `oṭṭakattu alkupaci' eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭatu.
246. marutam
(tōḻi talimakaṟku vāyil maṟuttatu.)
		piṇarmōṭṭu nantiṉ pēḻvāy ēṟṟi
		katirmūkku āral 1kaḷva ṉāka
		neṭunīrp poykit tuṇiyoṭu puṇarum
		malinīr akalvayal yāṇar ūra
	5.	pōtār kūntal nīvey yōḷoṭu
		tātār kāñcit taṇpoḻil akalyāṟu
		āṭiṉi yeṉpa neruni alarē
		2kāyciṉa moympiṟ perumpeyark karikāl
		ārkali naṟaviṉ veṇṇi vāyil
	10.	cīrkeḻu maṉṉar maṟaliya ñāṭpiṉ
		imiḻici muracam porukaḷat toḻiyap
		patiṉoru vēḷiroṭu vēntar cāya
		moyvali yaṟutta ñāṉṟit
		toyyā aḻuntūr ārppiṉum peritē.                    - paraṇar.
(co-ḷ) 1-4. piṇar mōṭṭu nantiṉ pēḻvāy ēṟṟi - caruccari poruntiya vayiṟṟiṉiyum piḷanta vāyiṉiyumuṭiya āṇ caṅku, katir mūkku āral kaḷvaṉāka - kūriya mūkkiṉiyuṭiya āral mīṉ kari kūṟuvōṉāka, neṭu nīrp poyki - āḻnta nīriyuṭiya poy kiyil, tuṇiyoṭu puṇarum - peṇ caṅkiṉoṭu maṇam puṇarum, mali nīr akal vayal yāṇar ūra - nīr niṟinta akaṉṟa vayalkaḷiyuṭiya putu varuvāy mikka ūraṉē!
5-7. pōtu ār kūntal nī veyyōḷoṭu - pūkkaḷ niṟinta kūntaliyuṭiya niṉṉāl virumpappeṟṟa parattiyoṭu, tātu ār kāñcit taṇ poḻil akal yāṟu - pūntukaḷ niṟinta kuḷirnta kāñci maraccōli cūḻnta akaṉṟa āṟṟiṉkaṇṇē. neruni āṭiṉi eṉpa - nēṟṟu nīrviḷiyāṭṭayarntaṉi yeṉap palarum kūṟāniṟpar;
7-14. alarē - ataṉāleḻunta alar, kāy ciṉa moympiṉ- mikka ciṉamum valiyumuṭiya, perum peyark karikāl - periya pukaḻiṉik koṇṭa karikāl vaḷavaṉ, ārkali naṟaviṉ veṇṇi  vāyil - āravāra mikka kaḷ vaḷamuṭiya veṇṇivāyil eṉṉumiṭattē, cīrkeḻu maṉṉar maṟaliya ñāṭpil - ciṟappuvāynta pakiyaracar māṟupaṭṭeḻunta pōriṉkaṇ, imiḻ ici muracam porukaḷattu oḻiya - mikka ōciyiyuṭiya vīra muracam pōrk kaḷattē oḻintu kiṭakka, patiṉoru vēḷiroṭu vēntar cāya - vēḷir patiṉoruvaruṭaṉ iru peru vēntarum nilikeṭa, moy vali aṟutta ñāṉṟi - avartam mikka valiyik keṭutta nāḷil, toyyā aḻuntūr ārppiṉum - aḻuntūrkkaṇ eḻunta kuṟiyāta āravārattiṉum, peritu - peritāyatu.
(muṭipu) yāṇar ūra! neruni nī veyyōḷoṭu kāñcit taṇpoḻil akal yāṟu āṭiṉi eṉpa; alar perum peyark karikāl vaḷavaṉ, veṇṇi vāyiliṉkaṇ, ñāṭpil, muracam porukaḷattoḻiya vēḷiroṭu vēntar cāya avartam meyvali aṟutta ñāṉṟi, aḻuntūrileḻunta ārppiṉum peritu.
(vi-ri) kaḷvaṉ - kari kūṟupavaṉ; piṟar aṟiya veḷippaṭuttu pavaṉ eṉṟapaṭi. iccolliṉ uruvam kaḷavaṉ eṉṟum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu. poḻililum yāṟṟilum viḷiyāṭiṉiyeṉpa eṉṟurit talumām. karikāl - karikālvaḷavaṉ. vēḷir kuṟunila maṉṉarākaliṉ, cīrkeḻu maṉṉar eṉṟatu, vēḷirum vēntarumākiya irutiṟat tārkkum potu. vēntar eṉṟatu, cēra pāṇṭiyari. keṭāta aḻuntūr eṉṟalumām. neruni āṭiṉi, alar aḻuntūr ārppiṉum peritu eṉṟatu, av valar virintu paravutali uṇarttiyapaṭi.
(u-ṟi) nantiṉ ēṟṟi āral kaḷvaṉākat tuṇiyoṭu puṇarum eṉṟatu, nī alar kūṟuvōr aṟiyumpaṭi niṉakkut tuṇiyākiya parattiyoṭu kūṭi viḷiyāṭiṉi eṉṟapaṭi.
(mē-ḷ) `āṟṟaloṭu puṇarnta'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ēṟṟi eṉa varutaṟku, `piṇarmōṭṭu nantiṉ pēḻvāy ēṟṟi' eṉpataṉi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar, pērā.
247. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷatu vēṟupāṭu kaṇṭu vēṟupaṭṭa tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		maṇṇā muttam oḻukkiya vaṉamuli
		naṉmāṇ ākam pulampat tuṟantōr
		aruḷilar vāḻi tōḻi poruḷpurintu
		iruṅkiḷi eṇkiṉ aḻalvāy ēṟṟi
	5.	karuṅkōṭṭu iruppi veṇpū muṉiyiṉ
		peruñcem puṟṟiṉ iruntali yiṭakkum
		ariya kāṉam eṉṉār pakipaṭa
		muṉipāḻ paṭṭa āṅkaṇ nāṭpārttuk
		kolival yāṉi curaṅkaṭi koḷḷum
	10.	ūṟupaṭu kavaliya āṟupala nīntip
		paṭumuṭi naciiya paṟineṭuṅ kaḻuttiṟ
		pāṟukiḷi cēkkuñ cēṭcimik
		kōṭuyar piṟaṅkal maliyiṟan tōrē.
- 1maturi marutaṅkiḻār makaṉār peruṅkaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 3. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
3-13. poruḷ purintu - poruḷi virumpi, iru kiḷi eṇkiṉ aḻal vāy ēṟṟi - periya cuṟṟattiṉiyuṭiya veppam poruntiya vāyiṉiyuṭiya āṇ karaṭi, karu kōṭṭu iruppi veṇ pū muṉiyiṉ - kariya kiḷikaḷiyuṭiya iruppi marattiṉ veḷḷiya pūviṉi veṟuttāl, peru cem puṟṟiṉ iru tali iṭakkum - periya cemmaṇ puṟṟiṉatu periya ucciyiṉip peyarttiṭum, ariya kāṉam eṉṉār - cellutaṟku ariya kāṉam eṉṟu niṉiyārāy, pakipaṭa muṉi pāḻpaṭṭa  āṅkaṇ - māṟukoṇṭu pōrceytalāl cīṟūr pāḻākiya avviṭattē, āḷ pārttu kolival yāṉi curam kaṭi koḷḷum - āṭkaḷiṉ varukiyi etirnōkkik kollutal valla yāṉi curaneṟiyik kāval koṇṭiruttaliṉ, ūṟupaṭu kavaliya āṟu pala nīnti - iṭiyūṟu mikka kavarttaṉavākiya pala neṟikaḷiyum kaṭantu, paṭumuṭi naciiya paṟi neṭu kaḻuttiṉ - mikka pulāl nāṟṟattiṉi virumpiccellum paṟattaliyuṭiya nīṇṭa kaḻuttiṉiyuṭiya, pāṟu kiḷi cēkkum - paruntu marakkiḷikaḷiṟ ṟaṅkiyirukkum, cēṇ cimi kōṭu uyar piṟaṅkal mali iṟantōr - nīṇṭa ucciyiṉiyum cikaram uyarnta pakka malikaḷiyum uṭiya maliyik kaṭantu ceṉṟōr ākiya nam talivar;
1-3. maṇṇā muttam oḻukkiya vaṉamuli - kaḻuvāta muttamāya kaṇṇīr oḻukaviṭappeṟṟa aḻakiya muliyiṉiyuṭiya, nal māṇ ākam pulampat tuṟantōr - nalla māṇpuṟṟa mārpu taṉittu varuntat tuṟantu ceṉṟōr ākaliṉ, aruḷ ilar - aruḷ aṟṟavarē yāvar; eṉ ceyvām.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! poruḷ purintu, ariya kāṉam eṉṉār, āṟu pala nīnti, mali yiṟantōr ākiya nam talivar ākam pulampat tuṟantōrākaliṉ aruḷ ilar.
(vi-ri) maṇṇutal - kaḻuvutal. muttam kaḻuvappaṭuvatākaliṉ, maṇṇā muttam oḻukkiya vaṉamuli eṉpuḻi, maṇṇā muttam eṉṟatu kaṇṇīrt tuḷikaḷi; itu veḷippaṭi eṉappaṭum. paki paṭa - māṟupaṭṭup pōrceytalāl eṉṟapaṭi. muṉi - vēṭṭuvar ūr. cēṭcimiyi yuṭiya maliyeṉavum, piṟaṅkal mali yeṉavum kūṭṭuka.
248. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟic ciṟippuṟamākat talimakaṭkuc colluvāḷāyt 
talimakaṉ kēṭpat tōḻi colliyatu.)
		nakinī kēḷāy tōḻi alkal
		vayanāy eṟintu vaṉpaṟaḻ taḻīi
		iḷiyar eytutaṉ 1maṭakkik kiḷiyoṭu
		nāṉmulip piṇaval coliyak kāṉoḻintu
	5.	arumpuḻi muṭukkar āṭkuṟittu niṉṟa
		taṟukaṭ paṉṟi nōkkik kāṉavaṉ
		kuṟukiṉaṉ toṭutta kūrvāyp pakaḻi
		maṭicelaṉ muṉpiltaṉ paṭicelac cellātu
		aruvaḻi vilakkumem peruviṟal pōṉmeṉa
	10.	eyyātu peyaruṅ kuṉṟa nāṭaṉ
		ceṟiyaril tuṭakkaliṟ parīip puriyaviḻntu
		ēntukuvavu moympiṟ pūccōr māli
		ēṟṟamil kayiṟṟiṉ eḻilvantu tuyalvara
		ilvantu niṉṟōṟ kaṇṭaṉaḷ aṉṉi
	15.	vallē yeṉmukam nōkki
		nalli maṉṉeṉa nakūuppeyarn tōḷē.                   - kapilar.
(co-ḷ) 1. tōḻi-, alkal naki nī kēḷāy - (nēṟṟu) iravu nikaḻntu nakikkiṭaṉāya ceytiyoṉṟik kēṭpāyāka;
2-10. vaya nāy eṟintu - valiya vēṭṭamāṭum nāyik kaṭintu vilakki, iḷiyar eytutal maṭakki - vēṭṭuvar neruṅkutalit taṭuttu, vaṉpaṟaḻ taḻīi - valiyaminta kuṭṭikaḷi aṇittak koṇṭu, kiḷiyoṭu nāṉmulip piṇaval coliya - toṅkum muliyiṉiyuṭiya peṇpaṉṟi kiḷiyuṭaṉ peyara, kāṉ oḻintu - kāṭṭiṉiṉṟum veḷivantu, arumpuḻi muṭukkar - ariya vāyilāya muṭukkilē, āḷ kuṟittu niṉṟa - vēṭṭam ceyyum āṭ kaḷi etirnōkki niṉṟa, taṟukaṇ paṉṟi - añcāmiyiyuṭiya paṉṟiyiṉi, kāṉavaṉ - vēṭṭuvaṉ, kuṟukiṉaṉ nōkki - aṇimi yiṟ ceṉṟu nōkki, toṭutta kūrvāy pakaḻi - taṉ villiṟkōtta kūriya muṉiyiyuṭiya ampiṉi, maṭi celal muṉpiṉ taṉ paṭi cela - maṭuttal aminta valiyiyuṭiya taṉ paṭi iriyavum, cellātu - tāṉ peyarātu niṉṟu, aru vaḻi vilakkum - pakivar varum ariya vaḻiyil avarit taṭuttu niṟkum, em peru viṟal pōṉm eṉa - emmuṭiya periya vallāḷaṉip pōlum itu veṉa (viyantu), eyyātu peyarum kuṉṟanāṭaṉ - ap paṉṟiyiṉmēl eyyātu mīḷum iṭamāya mali poruntiya nāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya nam talivaṉ ākiya;
11-14. ceṟi aril tuṭakkaliṉ - neruṅkiya tūṟukaḷ paṟṟiyiḻutta lāl, parīi puri aviḻntu - aṟupaṭṭup purikaḷ aviḻntu vīḻtaliṉ, ēntu kuvavu moympil - uyarntu tiraṇṭa tōḷi ṭattē, pū cōr māli - pūkkaḷ utirappeṟum māliyāṉatu, ēṟṟu imil kayiṟṟiṉ eḻil vantu tuyalvara - erutiṉ timiliṉkaṭ kiṭakkum kayiṟṟippōṉṟu aḻaku peṟa acintiṭa, il vantu niṉṟōṉ- nam illiṉkaṇ vantu niṉṟavaṉi;
14-16. aṉṉi kaṇṭaṉaḷ - nam aṉṉi kaṇṭaṉaḷāki, vallē eṉ mukam nōkki - viriya eṉ mukattiṉippārttu, nalli maṉ eṉa - nī mikavum nallavaḷ eṉṟu, nakūu peyarntōḷ - nakā niṉṟu puṟam pōyiṉaḷ, eṉceyvām.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! alkal naki nī kēḷāy; kāṉavaṉ taṟukaṭ paṉṟiyi nōkki em peruviṟal pōṉmeṉap pakaḻi eyyātu peyarum kuṉṟa nāṭaṉ il vantu niṉṟōṉik kaṇṭaṉaḷāki, aṉṉi eṉmukam nōkki nalli maṉṉeṉa nakūup peyarntōḷ.
(vi-ri) vayanāy eṟintu iḷiyar eytutali maṭakkik kāṉ oḻintu āṭkuṟittu niṉṟa paṉṟi eṉka. paṉṟiyi nōkkiya kāṉavaṉ ataṉatu taṟukaṇmiyi viyantu, tāṉ vilnāṇiṟ ṟoṭutta ampiṉi ataṉmītu eyyātu avviṭattu niṉṟu peyar vāṉ eṉka. taṉ paṭi celac cellātu aruvaḻi vilakkum eṉṟatu, `varuvicip puṉalik kaṟciṟipōla, oruvaṉ tāṅkiya perumi'1 eṉṉum vañcit tiṇiyiṉ tuṟipporuḷ amintatu. moympiṟku ēṟṟiṉ imilum, pūccōr mālikkuk kayiṟum uvamiyām. il vantu niṉṟōṉ eṉpataṉāl iravukkuṟi vantu niṉṟāṉ eṉpatu peṟṟām. aṉṉi niṉṟōṉik kaṇṭu eṉ mukam nōkki nallimaṉ eṉak kūṟi nakkuc ceṉṟāḷ eṉṟatu, avaṉ varukikku yāṉ vāyilātal karutit tīṅkuṭiyi yeṉa iḻittuk kūṟi vekuṇṭu ceṉṟāḷ eṉṟapaṭiyām. nallimaṉ eṉṟatu kuṟippu moḻi. nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum pērāciriyarum ic ceyyuḷit talivi kūṟṟākak koṇṭaṉar.
(u- ṟi) paṉṟiyāṉatu taṉatu piṇaval mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku uṇṭāya ūṟupāṭṭi nīkkik kāttu niṉṟatu eṉpatu, talivaṉ iṟ ceṟittalāl talivikku uṇṭākum ētatti nīkkik kākka vēṇṭum eṉṟapaṭi.
(mē-ḷ) `variviṭi vitta kālattu varuntiṉum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattē, talivi kūṟṟiṟku ic ceyyuḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, itu vantōṉ ceviliyi etirntuḻik kūṟiyatu eṉṟaṉar, nac.
`eḷḷal iḷami'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattē `naki nī kēḷāy tōḻi' eṉpatu taṉ pētimi poruḷāka naki piṟantatu; eṉṉi? tāṉ ceyta tavaṟṟiṟkut tāy taṉṉi vekuṇṭatu taṉakku nakiyākak koṇṭamiyiṉ eṉṟum, `nalli maṉṉeṉa nakūup peyarntōḷē; eṉpatu piṟar eḷḷiyatu poruḷākat taṉkaṇ naki piṟantatu; eṉṉi? taṉ makaḷ taṉṉi matiyātu ikaḻntāḷeṉa nakkavāṟu; itu vekuḷip poruḷāka nakka taṉṟō veṉiṉ, atu vīrarkkē urittākalvēṇṭum, ivaḷ avaḷi vekuṇṭu taṇṭam ceyvāḷ allaḷ ataṟkē uvappiṉ allatu eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, pērā.
249. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷatu vēṟupāṭu 
kaṇṭu āṟṟāḷāya tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		amma vāḻi tōḻi paṉṉāḷ
		ivvūr ampal evaṉō vaḷvār
		vicipiṇit tiyātta arikōl teṇkiṇi
		iṉkural akavunar irappiṉ nāṭoṟum
	5.	poṉkōṭṭuc ceṟittup polantār pūṭṭic
		cāntam putitta 3ēntutuḷaṅ keḻilimil
		ēṟumun tuṟuttuc cālpataṅ kuvii
		neṭuntēr kaḷiṟṟoṭu curakkuṅ koṭumpūṭ
		palvēl mucuṇṭi vēmpi yaṉṉaveṉ
	10.	nalleḻil iḷanalan toliyiṉum nalkār
		palpūṅ kāṉattu alkuniḻal aciit
		tōkit tūvit toṭittār maḻavar
		nākā vīḻttut tiṟṟi tiṉṟa
		pulavukkaḷan tuḻiiya tukaḷvāyk kōṭi
	15.	nīḷvaric cilampiṉ irivēṭ ṭeḻunta
		vāḷvari vayappuli tīṇṭiya viḷicettu
		vēṟuvēṟu kavaliya āṟuparin talaṟi
		uḻimāṉ iṉaniri yōṭuṅ
		kaḻimāy piṟaṅkaṉ maliyiṟan tōrē.
- nakkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, amma - nāṉ kūṟuvataṉik kēṭpāyāka;
11-14. tōki  tūvi toṭi tār maḻavar - mayiliṉ ciṟakāṟ ṟoṭuttaliyuṭiya māliyi aṇinta maḻavar, palpū kāṉattu alku niḻal acii - pala pūkkaḷiyuṭiya kāṭṭil curuṅkiya niḻalil taṅki, nāku ā vīḻttu - iḷiya pacuviṉik koṉṟu, tiṟṟi tiṉṟa - ataṉ ūṉit tiṉṟa iṭamāya, pulavu kaḷam tuḻiiya - pulāl vīcum iṭattiṉit tuḻāviya, tukaḷvāyk kōṭi - puḻutiyit taṉṉiṭattē koṇṭa mēl kāṟṟiṉ oliyiṉi;
15-19. uḻi māṉ iṉam niri - āṇmāṉuṭaṉ kūṭiya kūṭṭamāya māṉiṉam, nīḷ varic cilampiṉ iri vēṭṭu eḻunta -nīṇṭa mūṅkiliyuṭiya pakka maliyil iriyiṉik koḷḷu
taṟku virumpi eḻunta, vāḷ vari vaya puli tīṇṭiya viḷi cettu - oḷi poruntiya varikaḷiyuṭiya valiya puli (māṉip) paṟṟiyatāl eḻum oliyākak karuti, vēṟu vēṟu kavaliya āṟu parintu alaṟi ōṭum - vēṟu vēṟāya kavartta neṟikaḷil varunti alaṟi ōṭāniṟkum, kaḻi māy piṟaṅkal mali iṟantōr - mūṅkilāl maṟiyum uyarnta tōṟṟamuṭiya maliyik kaṭantu ceṉṟa nam talivar;
2-10. vaḷ vār vici piṇittu yātta - vaḷam poruntiya vāriṉāl iṟukap piṇittuk kaṭṭiya, arikōl teṇkiṇi iṉkural akavunar - iḻuttu aṭikkum kōliṉiyuṭiya teḷinta oliyiṉa tākiya kiṇip paṟiyiṉ iṉiya kurali eḻuppip pāṭum pāṇar, irappiṉ - irantu vēṇṭiṉ, nāṭoṟum - nāḷtōṟum, poṉ kōṭṭuc ceṟittu - poṟ pūṇiṉik kompil neruṅkac cērttu, polam tār pūṭṭi - poṉṉari māliyiṉik kaḻuttil aṇivittu, cāntam putitta ēntu tuḷaṅku eḻil imil - cantaṉam pūciya uyarntu aciyum aḻakiya timiliṉiyuṭiya, ēṟu muntuṟuttu - ēṟṟiṉi muṉṉar niṟutti, cālpatam kuvii - mikka uṇaviṉik kuvittu, neṭu tēr kaḷiṟṟoṭu curakkum - nīṇṭa tēriṉi yāṉi
yuṭaṉ cēra aḷikkum, koṭum pūṇ palvēl mucuṇṭi - vaḷinta pūṇiyum pala vēṟpaṭiyiyumuṭiya mucuṇṭi eṉpāṉatu, vēmpi aṉṉa - vēmpi eṉum ūr pōṉṟa, eṉ nal eḻil iḷanalam toliyiṉum - eṉatu nalla aḻakiya iḷamic cevvi tolinta vaḻiyum, nalkār - vantu aruḷ ceykiṉṟilar; (aṅṅaṉamākavum);
1-2. palnāḷ i ūr ampal evaṉō - pala nāḷāka ivvūrār ceyyum ampal eṉṉiyō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! mali yiṟantōr, mucuṇṭi eṉpāṉatu vēmpi aṉṉa eṉ nalam toliyiṉum nalkār; (aṅṅaṉamākavum) paṉṉāḷ ivvūr ampal evaṉō?
(vi-ri) arikōl - aritteḻum ōciyic ceyyum kōl. poṉ - poṉṉāṟ ceyta kuppikku ākupeyar. cāl patam kuvii ēṟu muntuṟuttu neṭuntēr kaḷiṟṟoṭu curakkum eṉa māṟuka. kōṭi - mēl kāṟṟu; īṇṭu ataṉ olikku āyiṟṟu. vayappuli tīṇṭiya - vayappuli māṉit tīṇṭiya eṉa oru col varuvitturikka. tīṇṭiya eṉṉum peyareccam kāriyap peyar koṇṭatu.
(u-ṟi) kōṭiyāṉatu pulāl nāṟum kaḷattit tuḻāvip puḻutiyit taṉṉiṭattuk koṇṭateṉpatu, ampaṟ peṇṭir piṟar paḻiyāyavaṟṟiyē ārāyntu taṅkaṇ koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟapaṭi. kōṭiyiṉ oliyi vayappuli māṉit tīṇṭiya oliyākak karuti māṉiṉam añcumeṉṟatu, talivaṉālāya meyvēṟupāṭṭit teyvattāl āyateṉṟa aṉṉi añcukiṉṟāḷ eṉṟapaṭi.
250. neytal
(talimakaṟkuk kuṟinērnta tōḻi talimakaṭkuk kuṟinayappak kūṟiyatu.)
		evaṉkol vāḻi tōḻi mayaṅkupicir
		malkutiri yuḻanta olkunilip puṉṉi
		vaṇṭimir iṇara nuṇṭātu varippa
		maṇaṅkamaḻ iḷamaṇal ekkark kāṇvarak
	5.	kaṇaṅkoḷ āyamoṭu puṇarntuviḷi yāṭak
		koṭuñci neṭuntēr iḷiyaroṭu nīkkit
		tāraṉ kaṇṇiyaṉ cēravan toruvaṉ
		varimaṉi pukaḻnta kiḷaviyaṉ yāvatum
		maṟumoḻi peṟāaṉ peyarntaṉaṉ ataṟkoṇṭu
	10.	arumpaṭar evvamoṭu peruntōḷ cāay
		avvalip paratavar kāṉalañ ciṟukuṭi
		vevvāyp peṇṭir kavviyil kalaṅki
		iṟivaḷi nekiḻnta nammoṭu
		tuṟiyun tuñcātu kaṅku lāṉē.
- cellūrkiḻār makaṉār perumpūtaṅkoṟṟaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-5. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, mayaṅku picir malku tiri uḻanta - neruṅkiya tuḷikaḷ mikka alikaḷāl varuntiya, olku nili puṉṉi - aciyum niliyiṉiyuṭiya puṉṉi marattiṉ, vaṇṭu imir iṇara nuṇtātu varippa - vaṇṭukaḷ olikkiṉṟa pūṅkottuk
kaḷiṉ nuṇṇiya pūmpoṭikaḷ utirntu aḻaku ceyya, maṇam kamaḻ iḷa maṇal ekkar - maṇam nāṟum metteṉṟa maṇal mēṭṭil, kāṇvara kaṇam koḷ āyamoṭu puṇarntu viḷiyāṭa - yām aḻaku poruntak kūṭṭam koṇṭa āyattāroṭu kalantu viḷiyāṭāniṟka (appoḻutu);
6-9. koṭuñci neṭuntēr iḷiyaroṭu nīkki - moṭṭiṉi yuṭiya nīṇṭa tēriṉit taṉ ēvalāḷaroṭu nīkki niṟutti, tāraṉ kaṇṇiyaṉ - tāriṉiyum kaṇṇiyiyum uṭiyaṉ āki, cēravantu - aṇuka vantu, oruvaṉ - oru talivaṉ, vari maṉi pukaḻnta kiḷaviyaṉ - yām iyaṟṟiya vaṇṭal maṉiyip pārāṭṭiya colliyuṭiyaṉāki, yāvatum maṟumoḻi peṟāaṉ peyarntaṉaṉ - yātoṉṟum maṟumoḻi peṟātu mīṇṭu ceṉṟaṉaṉ;
9-14. ataṟkoṇṭu - atu mutal, arum paṭar evvamoṭu - ariya niṉivālāya tuṉpattāl, perum tōḷ cāay - periya tōḷ meliya, a vali paratavar kāṉal am ciṟukuṭi - aḻakiya valikaḷiyuṭiya paratavaratu kaṭaṟkariyiluḷḷa aḻakiya ciṟṟūriluḷḷa, vevvāyp peṇṭir kavviyil kalaṅki - koṭiya vāyiṉiyuṭiya peṇṭir tūṟṟum alarāl uḷḷam kalaṅka, iṟi vaḷi nekiḻnta nammoṭu - muṉkiyiṭattu vaḷi nekiḻappeṟṟa nammuṭaṉ, kaṅkulāṉ - irāppoḻutilē, tuṟiyum tuñcātu - kaṭaṟṟuṟiyum tuñcu niṉṟilatu;
1. evaṉ kol - itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉiyō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! nām iḷa maṇal ekkarilē āyamoṭu puṇarntu viḷiyāṭa, oruvaṉ tēriṉi iḷiyaroṭu nīkki aṇuka vantu, varimaṉi pukaḻnta kiḷaviyaṉāy maṟumoḻi peṟāaṉ peyarntaṉaṉ; ataṟkoṇṭu peruntōḷ cāay, kavviyiṟ kalaṅki, vaḷi nekiḻnta nammoṭu kaṅkulāṉ tuṟiyum tuñcātu; (iḵtu) evaṉ kol?
(vi-ri) maṇam - puṉṉip pūviṉ maṇam. viḷiyāṭa eṉṉum eccam, kālattiyum iṭattiyum kuṟittuniṉṟatu. tār, kaṇṇi eṉpavaṟṟiṉ poruḷik kaṭavuḷvāḻttuc ceyyuḷuriyiṟ kāṇka. `varimaṉi pukaḻnta kiḷaviyaṉ yāvatum, maṟumoḻi peṟāaṉ peyarntaṉaṉ' eṉṟatu, nampāluḷḷa aṉpumikutiyāl nam vāyc collikkēṭka virumpi avaṉ nam vaṇṭal maṉiyip pukaḻntāṉākavum, nām maṟumoḻi yoṉṟuṅ kūṟātu vaṉkaṇmiyuṭiyēm āyiṉamiyiṉ, vāḷā peyarntu ceṉṟaṉaṉ eṉṟapaṭi. ataṟkoṇṭu evvamoṭu tōḷ cāayk kavviyiṟ kalaṅki iṟivaḷi nekiḻnta nammoṭu eṉṟamiyāl, nām uṟṟa nōykkuk kāraṇam, nam uḷḷam avaṉ kaṇṇatāka nām avaṉip pirintiruttalēyām eṉpatum, nammoṭu kaṅkulāṉē tuṟiyum tuñcātu eṉṟamiyāl, avaṉip pirinta varuttattāl nām iravilē tuyiliṉṟiyē varuntukiṉṟōm eṉpatum kūṟiṉāḷām. tuṟiyum tuñcātu eṉṟatu, iravilē tuyiliṉṟiyē viḻittirukkum tamakku akkaṭaloli iṭiyaṟātu tōṉṟi mika varuttutaliṉ, nammip pōl ikkaṭalum tuñcu kiṉṟilatē; iḵteṉṉi eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷām. eṉavē, nām uṟṟa nōyum vevvāyp peṇṭir kavviyum oḻiya vēṇṭiṉ, ap peruntakiyiṉ kuṟiyiṉi nayantu ēṟṟuk kōṭal vēṇṭum eṉat tōḻi kuṟinayappak kūṟiṉāḷām eṉka. cāay, kalaṅki eṉṉum eccaṅkaḷic cāaya, kalaṅka eṉat tirikka; tiriyātu tōḷ cāay, uḷḷaṅ kalaṅki, vaḷi nekiḻnta nammoṭu eṉac ciṉikaḷiṉ viṉiyi mutaloṭu cārtti yurittalumām.
251. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷatu 
vēṟupāṭu kaṇṭu tōḻi colliyatu.)
		tūtum ceṉṟaṉa tōḷuñ ceṟṟum
		ōti yoṇṇutal pacaliyum māyum
		vīṅkiḻi nekiḻac cāayc cellaloṭu
		nāmpaṭar kūrum aruntuyar kēṭpiṉ
	5.	1nantaṉ veṟukki yeytiṉum maṟṟavaṇ
		taṅkalar vāḻi tōḻi velkoṭit
		tuṇikā laṉṉa puṉitērk kōcar
		toṉmū tālat tarumpaṇip potiyil
		iṉṉici muracaṅ kaṭippikut tiraṅkat
	10.	temmuṉi cititta ñāṉṟi mōkūr
		paṇiyā miyiṟ pakitali vanta
		mākeḻu tāṉi vampa mōriyar
		puṉitēr nēmi yuruḷiya kuṟitta
		ilaṅkuveḷ ḷaruviya 2aṟivā yumpar
	15.	mācil veṇkōṭṭu aṇṇal yāṉi
		vāyuḷ tappiya aruṅkēḻ vayappuli
		mānilam neḷiyak 1kuttip pukaloṭu
		kāppila vikun tēkkamal cōli
		nirampā nīḷiṭip pōki
	20.	arampōḻ avvaḷi nilinekiḻt tōrē.        
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 6. tōḻi-, vāḻi-,
1. tūtum ceṉṟaṉa - nām talivarpāṟ celuttiya tūtarkaḷum ceṉṟuḷḷārkaḷ;
3-4. vīṅku iḻi nekiḻac cāay - ceṟinta aṇikaḷ nekiḻntu viḻa melintu, cellaloṭu nām paṭarkūrum aruntuyar kēṭpiṉ - nām varuttattuṭaṉ niṉivu mikum ariya tuyariṉi attūtuvar kūṟak kēṭpiṉ;
6-14. velkoṭi tuṉi kāl aṉṉa puṉi tērk kōcar - vellum koṭiyiṉiyuṭiya viriyum kāṟṟiyotta aṇi ceyappeṟṟa tēriṉiyuṭiya kōcar eṉpār, tol mutu ālattu arumpaṇip potiyil - mikka toṉmi vāynta ālamarattiṉ ariya kiḷikaḷi
yuṭiya maṉṟattē, iṉ ici muracam kaṭippu ikuttu iraṅka - iṉiya ōciyiyuṭiya muracam kuṟuntaṭiyāl aṭikkap peṟṟu olikka, tevmuṉi cititta ñāṉṟi - pakivaratu pōr muṉiyi aḻittakālattē, mōkūr paṇiyāmiyiṉ - mōkūr eṉpāṉ paṇintu vārāmiyiṉ, paki talivanta mā keḻu tāṉi vampa mōriyar - avaṉpāṟ paki ēṟaṭṭuk koṇṭavarākiya kutirikaḷ poruntiya cēṉiyiṉi yuṭiya putiya mōriyar eṉpār, puṉi tēr nēmi uruḷiya kuṟitta - puṉiyappeṟṟa tēr uruḷi taṭiyiṉṟic cellutaṟporuṭṭu uṭittu vaḻiyākkiya, ilaṅku veḷ aruviya aṟivāy umpar - viḷaṅkum veḷḷiya aruvikaḷiyuṭiya malineṟikku appāṟpaṭṭa;
15-19. mācu il veṇkōṭṭu aṇṇal yāṉi - kuṟṟamaṟṟa veḷḷiya kompiṉiyuṭiya perumi vāynta yāṉiyāṉatu, vāyuḷ tappiya aruṅkēḻ vaya puli - taṉ vāyiṉiṉṟum tappiya ariya niṟattiyuṭiya valiya puliyi, mānilam neḷiyak kutti - periya nilam kuḻiyak kuttikkoṉṟu, pukaloṭu kāppila vikum tēkku amal cōli - cerukkuṭaṉ pātukāval iṉṟit taṅki yirukkum tēkku maraṅkaḷ niṟinta kāṭākiya, nirampā nīḷ iṭippōki - toliyāta nīṇṭa iṭattilē ceṉṟu;
20. aram pōḻ avvaḷi nili nekiḻttōr - arattāl aṟuttu iyaṟṟappaṭṭa aḻakiya vaḷiyiṉi niliyiṉiṉṟum nekiḻac ceytōrākiya nam talivar;
5-6. nantaṉ veṟukki eytiṉum - nantaṉ eṉpāṉ tokuttu vitta celvattiyē eytuvatāyiṉum, maṟṟu avaṇ taṅkalar - iṉi aṅkut taṅkiyirār (virintu vantuṟuvār);
1-2. tōḷum  ceṟṟum - iṉi nām tōḷum vaḷi ceṟiyap peṟuvēm, ōti oḷ nutal pacaliyum māyum - nam kūntal cūḻnta oḷi taṅkiya neṟṟiyiṉkaṇ uḷḷa pacaliyum maṟintoḻiyum.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! tūtum ceṉṟaṉa, nīḷiṭip pōki nilinekiḻttōrākiya nam talivar, nāmpaṭar kūrum aruntuyar kēṭpiṉ nantaṉ veṟukki eytiṉum avaṇ taṅkalar; ataṉāl nām tōḷum ceṟṟum, nannutaṟ pacaliyum māyum.
(vi-ri) ceṟṟum - ceṟiyappeṟum eṉṟapaṭi; vaḷi ceṟiyap peṟum eṉa viritturikka. cellal - uṭaṟṟuṉpamum, paṭar kūrtal uḷattuṉpamum ām. pāṭalipuratti yāṇṭa nanta aracarkaḷil oruvaṉ makāpatumam eṉum pēreṇ aḷavu nitiyam paṭittu ataṉāl makāpatuma nantaṉ eṉap peyar ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉā ṉeṉṟu vaṭa nūlkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭutaliṉ, avaṉatu celvattik kuṟittu, `nantaṉ veṟukki eytiṉum' eṉṟāḷ: nantaṉ veṟukki pōl vatōr celvatti eytiṉum eṉṟalumām. mōkūr - mōkūrkku maṉṉaṉākiya paḻiyaṉ; ākupeyar. `mōkūr, valampaṭu kuḻūunili yatira maṇṭi'1 eṉpuḻiyum ākupeyarātal kāṇka. kōcar eṉpār paḻiyaṉuṭiya mōkūril vañciṉam kūṟip porutaṉar eṉpatu, `paḻiyaṉ mōkūr aviyakam viḷaṅka, nāṉmoḻik kōcar tōṉṟi yaṉṉa'2 eṉpataṉ uriyāl aṟiyap paṭum. mōkūr maṉṉaṉ kōcarkkup paṇiyāmiyiṉālē kōcarkkut tuṇiyāy vanta mōriyar eṉka. mōriyar tērnēmi yuruḷumpaṭi maliyiyaṟuttu vaḻi ceytamiyi `āynalam tolinta mēṉiyum'3 eṉṉum akappāṭṭiṉ uriyiṟ kāṇka. vāyuḷ tappiya - vāyiṉiṉṟum tappiya; urupu mayakkam.
252. kuṟiñci
(talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat tōḻikkuc 
colluvāḷāyt talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		iṭampaṭupu aṟiyā valampaṭu vēṭṭattu
		vāḷvari naṭuṅkap pukalvantu āḷi
		uyar nutal yāṉip pukarmukat toṟṟi
		veṇkōṭu puykkun taṇkamaḻ cōlip
	5.	peruvari yaṭukkattu oruvēl ēntik
		taṉiyaṉ varutal avaṉum añcāṉ
		paṉivār kaṇṇēṉ āki nōyaṭa
		emiyēṉ iruttali yāṉum āṟṟēṉ
		yāṅkuc ceyvāṅkol tōḻi īṅkit
	10.	tuyyaviḻ paṉimalar utira vīcit
		1toḻiṉmaḻi poḻinta pāṉāṭ kaṅkul
		eṟitirit tivali tūuñ ciṟukōṭṭup
		peruṅkuḷaṅ kāvalaṉ pōla
		aruṅkaṭi aṉṉiyun tuyiṉmaṟan taṉaḷē.     - 2nakkaṇṇiyār.
(co-ḷ) 9. tōḻi-,
1-5. iṭam paṭupu aṟiyā valampaṭu vēṭṭattu - tāṉ vīḻttum vilaṅkukaḷ iṭatu pakkattē vīḻtali aṟiyāta veṟṟi poruntiya vēṭṭattiṉuṭiya, vāḷ vari naṭuṅka pukalvantu - puli naṭuṅkiṭa viruppaṅkoṇṭu (pāyntu), āḷi - āḷiyāṉatu, uyar nutal yāṉi pukar mukattu oṟṟi - uyarnta neṟṟiyiṉiyuṭiya yāṉiyiṉ puḷḷi poruntiya mukattit tākki, veṇkōṭu puykkum - ataṉ veḷḷiya tantattiṉip paṟikkum (iṭamāya), taṇ kamaḻ cōli peruvari aṭukkattu - taṇṇeṉak kamaḻum cōliyiṉi yuṭiya periya maliyiṉ cāraliṉkaṇ;
5-6. oru vēl ēnti taṉiyaṉ varutal avaṉum añcāṉ - vēl oṉṟiṉiyē koṇṭu taṉiyaṉāki varutalit talivaṉum añcu kiṉṟilaṉ;
7-8. paṉi vār kaṇṇēṉ āki nōy aṭa - nīr oḻukum kaṇṇiṉēṉ ākip piritaṟṟuṉpam varutta, emiyēṉ iruttali yāṉum āṟṟēṉ - taṉittu iruttali yāṉum āṟṟukilēṉ;
9-14. aru kaṭi aṉṉiyum - kaṭiya kāvalkoṇṭa nam aṉṉiyum, īṅki tuy aviḻ paṉi malar utira - iṇṭiyiṉ ārkku virinta kuḷirnta malar utirumāṟu, vīci toḻil maḻi poḻinta pāṉāḷ kaṅkul - peytaṟ ṟoḻiliyuṭiya mēkam nīriṉi vīcic corinta naṭunāḷ iravil, eṟi tiritivali tūum - vīcum alikaḷiṉ tuḷikaḷ parakkum, ciṟu kōṭṭup peruṅ kuḷam kāvalaṉ pōla - ciṟiya kariyiṉiyuṭiya periya kuḷattik kāttal ceypavaṉpōlak (kāval ceytu), tuyil maṟantaṉaḷ - tūṅkutali maṟantuviṭṭaṉaḷ; yāṅkuc ceyvām - yām eṉ ceyvām.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! peruvari yaṭukkattu avaṉum taṉiyaṉ varutal añcāṉ; yāṉum nōyaṭa emiyēṉ iruttali āṟṟēṉ; aruṅkaṭi aṉṉiyum peruṅkuḷam kāvalaṉ pōlat tuyil maṟantaṉaḷ; yāṅkuc ceyvām.
(vi-ri) iṭampaṭupu aṟiyā eṉṟamiyāl, puli eppoḻutum ēṉi vilaṅkukaḷi valappakkattē vīḻtti uṇṇum iyalpiṉatu eṉpatu peṟṟām. muṉṉar, puliyiṉ ciṟappuk kūṟiṉamiyāl, vāḷ variyum eṉṉum ciṟappummi tokkateṉak kōṭalumām. pukal varal - virumputal. āḷi - yāḷi. ciṅkam puykkum aṭukkam eṉavum cōli aṭukkam eṉavum kūṭṭuka. puykkum eṉṉum peyareccam iṭappeyar koṇṭatu: emiyēṉ - tamiyēṉ. taṉiyaṉ varutal avaṉum añcāṉ, emiyēṉ iruttali yāṉum āṟṟēṉ eṉṟamiyāl, yāṉ avaṉatu kūṭṭattiyē virumputaluṭiyēṉ; eṉiṉum ētam mikka variyaṭukkattu avaṉ taṉiyaṉāy varutali añcukiṉṟēṉ eṉṟāḷām. toḻil maḻi - uḻutoḻiṟku utavum maḻi eṉṟalumām. `maḻitoḻi lutava'1 eṉpuḻi nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ivviru poruḷum koṇṭamiyuṅ kāṇka. teḻi maḻi eṉṉum pāṭattiṟku - kaṉikkiṉṟa maḻi yeṉka. malar utira vīcip poḻinta eṉṟamiyāl, perumpeyal eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. naḷḷiravil maḻi poḻiya, ataṉāl nīr nirampiya ali mōtā niṟka, ataṉ tuḷikaḷ kariyiṉ mēliṭa meṅkum parattaliṉ melinta kariyiṉiyuṭiya apperiya kuḷattik kāppavaṉ, ataṉ kari uṭiyātapaṭi tuyileṉpatu ciṟitumiṉṟi ataṉic cūḻvantu kāppaṉ ākaliṉ, avvāṟu naḷḷiravilum tuyilātu taṉṉip palkālum nōkkutaluṭiya aṉṉikkuk kuḷaṅ kāvalaṉi uvami kūṟiṉāḷ. kāvalaṉ tuyiliṉṟik kuḷattik kāttalpōla, aṉṉiyum tuyiliṉṟi eṉṉik kākkiṉṟaṉaḷ eṉat toḻil uvamam koḷka. eṟi tirit tivali tūum ciṟukōṭṭup peruṅkuḷam eṉṟamiyāl, taliviyāṉavaḷ kāmaveḷḷam mikutalāl, taṉatu nāṇ nuṇuki niṉṟa niliyik kuṟittāḷām. `mikupeyal, uppucciṟi nillā veḷḷam pōla, nāṇu vari nillāk kāmam naṇṇi'1 eṉpatu īṇṭu aṟiyaṟpālatu. avaṉum yāṉum, aṉṉiyum eṉṉum ummikaḷ kaṭṭuric cuvipaṭa niṉṟaṉa; eṇṇummiyumām. kol - aci.
253. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaṭkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		vikal tōṟum pacali pāyaveṉ
		meyyum perumpiṟi tākiṉṟu oyyeṉa
		aṉṉiyum amarā mukattiṉaḷ alarē
		vāṭāp pūviṟ koṅkar ōṭṭi
	5.	nāṭupala tanta pacumpūṇ pāṇṭiyaṉ
		poṉmali 1neṭunakark kūṭal āṭiya
		iṉṉici yārppiṉum peritē īṅkiyāṉ
		cilanāḷ uyyaleṉ pōṉmeṉap palaniṉintu
		āḻal vāḻi tōḻi vaṭāatu
	10.	āriruḷ naṭunāḷ 2ērā oyyap
		pakimuṉi yaṟuttup palliṉañ cāayk
		kaṇañcāl kōvalar neṭuviḷip payiraṟintu
		iṉantalit tarūun tuḷaṅkimil nallēṟṟut
		taḻūuppiṇar eruttan tāḻap pūṭṭiya
	15.	antūm pakalamik kamañcelap peyta
		tuṟukāḻ valciyar toḻuvaṟi vuvik
		kaṉṟuṭip peruniri maṉṟuniṟi tarūum
		nērā vaṉtōḷ vaṭukar perumakaṉ
		pērici erumi naṉṉāṭ ṭuḷḷati
	20.	ayiriyāṟu iṟantaṉa rāyiṉum mayariṟantu
		uḷḷupa tilla tāmē paṇittōṭ
		kurumpi meṉmuli arumpiya cuṇaṅkiṉ
		nucuppaḻit tolivarun tāḻiruṅ kūntal
		māka vicumpil tilakamoṭu patitta
	25.	tiṅkaḷ aṉṉaniṉ tirumukattu
		oṇcūṭṭu avirkuḻi malinta nōkkē.
- nakkīrar.
(co-ḷ) 1-9. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, vikal tōṟum pacali pāya - nāṭōṟum pacaliyāṉatu paṭartaliṉ, eṉ meyyum oy eṉap perum piṟitu ākiṉṟu - eṉ uṭampum viriya iṟappiṉi eyti varukiṉṟatu, aṉṉiyum amarā mukattiṉaḷ - aṉṉiyum veṟutta mukattiṉaḷ ākiṉṟāḷ, alarē - ūrkkaṇ eḻum alartāṉ, vāṭāp pūviṉ koṅkar ōṭṭi - poṟpūviṉi aṇinta koṅkari akaṟṟi, nāṭu pala tanta pacum pūṇ pāṇṭiyaṉ- avar tam nāṭu palavaṟṟiyum  kippaṟṟiya pacumpūṇ pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpāṉ, poṉ mali neṭu nakark kūṭal - poṉmikka periya nakaramākiya kūṭaṟkaṇ, āṭiya iṉ ici ārppiṉum peritu - avveṟṟik kaḷippiṉāl āṭiya iṉiya oliyiyuṭiya āravārat tiṉum mikkatākiṉṟatu, īṅku yāṉ cilanāḷ uyyaleṉ pōlum eṉa - ivviṭattēyāṉ iṉic cilanāḷum uyir tarittirēṉ pōlum eṉṟu, pala niṉintu āḻal - palavum eṇṇit tuyaril aḻuntātē;
9-11. vaṭāatu - vaṭakkiṉkaṇṇatākiya, ār iruḷ naṭu nāḷ- iyaṅkutaṟkariya naḷḷiravil, ēr ā oyya - aḻakiya pacukkaḷik kavarntu varutaṟku, paki muṉi aṟuttu - pakip pulattik keṭuttu, pal iṉam cāay - pala iṉaṅkaḷiyum ōṭṭiyum,
12-20. neṭu viḷi payir aṟintu - taṅkaḷ neṭiya kūppīṭ ṭoliyi aṟintu, iṉam talit tarum - pacuviṉattit tammiṭattu aṇittuk koṭuvarum, tuḷaṅku imil nal ēṟṟu - aciyum mucuppiṉiyuṭiya nalla ēṟukaḷiṉ, taḻūup piṇar eruttam - caruccari koṇṭa kaḻuttil, tāḻap pūṭṭiya - toṅkap pūṭṭiyuḷḷa, am tūmpu akal ami kamam cela peyta - aḻakiya uṭṭoḷiyiṉi yuṭiya akaṉṟa mūṅkiṟ kuḻāyil niṟivu eytumāṟu peyta, tuṟu kāḻ valciyar - ceṟivumikka uṇaviṉarākiya, kaṇam cāl kōvalar- kūṭṭam mikka kōvalaratu, toḻu aṟi vuvi - toḻuvākiya aṟiyiṉik kavarntum, kaṉṟu uṭi peruniri maṉṟu niṟi tarūum - kaṉṟukaḷiyuṭiya āṉiriyi maṉṟukaḷ niṟiyumāṟu koṇarum, nērā vaṉtōḷ vaṭukar perumakaṉ - oppillāta valiya tōḷiṉiyuṭiya vaṭukar talivaṉākiya, pēr ici erumi nal nāṭṭu uḷḷati - periya pukaḻiṉiyuṭiya erumi eṉpāṉatu nalla nāṭṭiṉkaṇ uḷḷatākiya, ayiriyāṟu iṟantaṉar āyiṉum - ayiri eṉṉum yāṟṟiṉik kaṭantu (nam talivar) ceṉṟaṉar āyiṉum;
21-26. paṇi tōḷ - mūṅkilpōṉṟa tōḷiṉiyum, kurumpi mel  muli - kurumpi pōṉṟa melliya muliyiṉiyum, arumpiya cuṇaṅkiṉ - arumpiya cuṇaṅkiṉiyum, nucuppu aḻittu olivarum tāḻ irum kūntal - iṭiyiṉi varuttit taḻitta tāḻnta kariya kūntaliyum uṭiya, niṉ - niṉatu, māka vicumpil tilakamoṭu patitta tiṅkaḷ aṉṉa tirumukattu - mākamākiya vāṉil uḷḷa iṭiyē tilakam patitta tiṅkaḷ pōṉṟa aḻakiya mukatti
luḷḷa, oṇcūṭṭu avir kuḻi malinta nōkku - oḷḷiya mattaka maṇi yaminta  viḷaṅkukiṉṟa kātaṇiyākiya kuḻiyoṭu poruta pārviyiṉi;
20-21. tām mayar iṟantu uḷḷupa - avar maṟaviyaṟṟu eṉṟum  niṉiyāniṟpar.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! meyyum perum piṟitu ākiṉṟu; aṉṉiyum amarā mukattiṉaḷ; alar pacumpūṇ pāṇṭiyaṉ kūṭal āṭiya ārppiṉum peritu; yāṉ uyyaleṉ pōlum eṉa niṉintu āḻal; (nam talivar) vaṭāatu erumi naṉṉāṭṭu uḷḷatākiya ayiriyāṟu iṟantaṉarāyiṉum, niṉ tirumukattu avirkuḻi malinta nōkkut tām mayar iṟantu uḷḷuvar.
cāy, vuvip peruniri tarūum erumi eṉka.
(vi-ri) vāṭāppū - poṉṉāṉiyaṉṟa pūviṟku veḷip paṭi. pūveṉṟatu veṭci mutaliya pōrppūviṉi. āṭiya - veṟṟik kaḷippāl āṭiya eṉka. āṭiya ārppu - āṭiyakāli eḻunta ārppu. iṉ ici -iṉiya iyaṅkaḷiṉ ōciyumām. pōṉm - pōlum; oppil pōli. vaṭāatu naṉṉāṭu eṉak kūṭṭuka. paki muṉi eṉṟatu, karantiyāratu pōr muṉi. paliṉam eṉpuḻip paṉmi, mikutiyi uṇarttiṟṟu. taḻūup piṇar - piṇar taḻūu eṉa māṟuka; taḻuvutal; poruntutal. valciyarākiya kōvalaratu toḻu aṟi eṉṟiyikka. kōvalar eruttiṉ kaḻuttil mūṅkiṟkuḻāyil uṇaviṉip peytu pūṭṭiyiruppar eṉpatu `kōvalar, maḻaviṭip pūṭṭiya kuḻāayt tīmpuḷi, ceviyaṭi tīrat tēkkilip pakukkum, pulli naṉṉāṭu'1 eṉap piṟāṇṭum kūṟappaṭṭatu. niṟi - niṟiya. uḷḷati - ai pakutipporuḷ vikuti. māka vicumpu - irupeyaroṭṭu. tilakamoṭu patitta tiṅkaḷ aṉṉa eṉṟatu, ilporuḷ uvami. tilakamoṭu - vēṟṟumi mayakkam. cūṭu - kuḻiyiṟ patitta mattaka maṇi.
254. mulli
(viṉimuṟṟi vanteytiya talimakaṉ tērppākaṟkuc colliyatu.)
		narivirā vuṟṟa naṟumeṉ kūntaṟ
		cemmutu ceviliyar 2palapā rāṭṭap
		polaṉcey kiṇkiṇi nalampeṟu cēvaṭi
		maṇaṉmali muṟṟattu nilamvaṭuk koḷāa
	5.	maṉiyuṟi puṟaviṉ ceṅkāl cēval
		tuṇiyoṭu kuṟumpaṟi payiṟṟi mēṟcela
		viḷiyāṭu āyat tiḷiyōrk kāṇṭoṟum
		namvayiṉ niṉiyum naṉṉuta larivi
		pulampoṭu vatiyum kalaṅkañar akala
	10.	vēntuṟu toḻiloṭu vēṟupulattu alki
		vantuviṉi muṭittaṉa māyiṉ nīyum
		paṇinili muṉiiya viṉinavil puravi
		iḻiyaṇi neṭuntēr āḻi yuṟuppa
		nuṇkoṭi miṉṉiṟ pimpayir tumiyat
	15.	taḷavam 1mulliyoṭu taliit taṇṇeṉa
		veṟikamaḻ koṇṭa vītati puṟaviṉ
		neṭiyiṭi piṉpaṭak kaṭavumati eṉṟiyāṉ
		colliya 2aḷavi nīṭātu valleṉat
		tārmaṇi māvaṟi vuṟāa
	20.	ūrnaṇit tantaṉi uvakiyām peṟavē.
- 3maturi aṟuvi vāṇikaṉ iḷavēṭṭaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-9. (pāka!), nari virāvuṟṟa naṟumel kūntal - narimayir kalappuṟṟa naṟiya melliya kūntaliyuṭiya, cemmutu ceviliyar - cevviya mutiya cevilit tāyar, pala pārāṭṭa - taṉṉip palavāṟu pārāṭṭik kūṟa, polaṉ cey kiṇkiṇi nalampeṟu cē aṭi - poṉṉāṟ ceyyappeṟṟa cataṅkiyi aṇinta aḻakiya aṭiyāl, maṇal mali muṟṟattu nilam vaṭuk koḷāa - maṇal paranta muṟṟattē nilam vaṭukkoḷḷumāṟu ceytu, maṉi uṟi puṟaviṉ ceṅkāl cēval - maṉiyiṟ ṟaṅki yuṟiyum civanta kāliṉiyuṭiya puṟaviṉ cēval, tuṇiyoṭu kuṟum paṟi payiṟṟi - taṉ peṭiyoṭu kuṟukak kuṟukap paṟattalic ceytu, mēlcela - piṉpu mēlē eḻuntiṭa, viḷiyāṭu āyattu iḷiyōrk kāṇtoṟum - viḷiyāṭum taṉ tōḻiyarāya iḷiyarik kāṇuntoṟum, namvayiṉ niṉiyum - nampāl niṉivu koḷḷum, nal nutal arivi - aḻakiya neṟṟiyiṉi yuṭiya nam talivi, pulampoṭu vatiyum kalaṅku añar akala - taṉimiyoṭu taṅkiyirukkum mikka tuṉpam nīṅkumāṟu;
10-11. vēntu uṟu toḻiloṭu - vēntaṟku uṟṟuḻi utavum viṉiyiṉāl, vēṟu pulattuvantu alki - iv vēṟṟu nāṭṭiṉ kaṇ vantu taṅki, viṉimuṭittaṉam āyiṉ - mēṟkoṇṭa viṉiyiṉi muṭittuḷōm ākaliṉ;
11-18. nīyum, paṇi nili muṉiiya viṉinavil puravi - pantiyil niṟṟali veṟutta pōrttoḻiliṟ payiṉṟa kutiri pūṭṭiya, iḻi aṇi neṭu tēr āḻi - muttāram mutaliyaṉa aṇinta nīṇṭa tēr uruḷi, nuṇ koṭi miṉṉil uṟuppa - nuṇṇiya miṉṉuk koṭi pōṉṟu celuttap peṟutaliṉ, pim payir tumiya - vaḻiyiluḷḷa paciya payirkaḷ aṟupaṭṭu vilaka, taḷavam mulliyoṭu talii - cemmulli veṇmulliyuṭaṉ kūṭi, taṇ eṉa veṟi kamaḻkoṇṭa vī tati puṟaviṉ - taṇṇeṉṟu naṟumaṇam kamaḻum malar ceṟinta kāṭṭiṉatu, neṭiiṭi piṉpaṭak kaṭavumati eṉṟu - nīṇṭavaḻi piṉṉuṟac celuttuvāy eṉṟu, yāṉ colliya aḷavi - yāṉ kūṟiya māttiriyāṉē;
18-20. nīṭātu - tāḻkkātu, uvaki yām peṟa - yām makiḻvu peṟum paricu, tār maṇi mā aṟivuṟāa - maṇimāli yaṇinta kutiriyiṉ uḷḷam aṟintu, val eṉa - viriya, ūr naṇi tantaṉi - ūrkku aṇittākat tēriṉik koṇṭu vantaṉi; (vāḻiyar.)
(muṭipu) pāka! vantu viṉi muṭittaṉamāyiṉ, arivi pulam poṭu vatiyum kalaṅkañar akala, neṭuntēriṉip puṟaviṉ neṭiyiṭi piṉpaṭak kaṭavumati eṉṟu yāṉ colliya aḷavi yām uvaki peṟa valleṉa ūr naṇittantaṉi (vāḻiyar.)
(vi-ri) kiṇkiṇi nalampeṟu cēvaṭi - kiṇkiṇiyāṉatu aḻaku peṟutaṟku ētuvākiya cēvaṭi eṉṟumām `kuḻikku viḷakkākiya oṇṇutal poṉṉiṉ, iḻikku viḷakkākiya........ makaḷir'1 eṉpatu kāṇka. vaṭukkoḷāa - vaṭukkoḷḷa eṉpatu tirintu niṉṟatu; vaṭucceytu eṉṟumām. pārāṭṭa viḷiyāṭum, nilamvaṭukkoḷa viḷiyāṭum, mēṟcela viḷiyāṭum eṉat taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. maṇal muṟṟattē iḷiyōr ōṭi viḷiyāṭu taliṉ aṅkuḷḷa cēvalum peṭiyum mēleḻuntu celva vāyiṉa. aṅṅaṉam. puṟākkaḷ cēvalum peṭiyumāka mēṟcella, iḷam paruvamuṭiya makārum makaḷirum viḷiyāṭum makiḻccimikka viḷiyāṭṭu, kūṭiyiruppavarkkē iṉpam  viḷikkumākaliṉ, nām talivaruṭaṉ kūṭi ivviṉpiṉi nukarap peṟṟilēmeṉṟu talivi nammi niṉintu varuntuvaḷ eṉak karuti, av varuttam nīṅka nī tēriṉi virintu kaṭavuti eṉṟu talivaṉ pākaṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka. viṉi navil puravi - kuṟippaṟintu cellum toḻil payiṉṟa puravi eṉṟumām. paṇi nili muṉiiya eṉṟataṉāl, atuvum virintu cēṟaṟkaṇ maṉaveḻucciyuṭiyatātal kūṟiya vāṟāyiṟṟu. uṟuppa - ūṭaṟuttuc cella. neṭiya iṭi eṉpatu neṭiyiṭi eṉa vikāramāyiṟṟu. mā - kutiriyiṉ uḷḷam. aṟivuṟāa - aṟintu tantaṉiyākaliṉ, vāḻi eṉa viritturikka.
255. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷ āṟṟāmi mītūrat tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		ulakukiḷarn taṉṉa urukeḻu vaṅkam
		pulavuttirip peruṅkaṭal nīriṭip pōḻa
		iravum elliyum aciviṉ ṟāki
		viricelal iyaṟki vaṅkū ḻāṭṭak
	5.	kōṭuyar tiṇimaṇal akaṉtuṟi nīkāṉ
		māṭa voḷḷeri maruṅkaṟin toyya
		āḷviṉip pirinta kātalar nāḷpala
		kaḻiyā miyē aḻipaṭa rakala
		varuvar maṉṉāl tōḻi taṇpaṇip
	10.	porupuṉal vippiṉ nammū rāṅkaṇ
		karuviḷi muraṇiya taṇputaṟ pakaṉṟip
		peruvaḷa malara alli tīṇṭip
		palavukkāyp puṟatta pacumpaḻap pākal
		kūtaḷa mūtilik koṭinirit tūṅka
	15.	aṟaṉiṉ ṟalikkum āṉā vāṭi
		kaṭimaṉi māṭattuk kaṅkul vīcit
		tiruntiḻi nekiḻntu peruṅkaviṉ cāya
		nirivaḷi yūrun tōḷeṉa
		uriyoṭu cellum aṉpiṉarp peṟiṉē.
- maturi marutaṉiḷanākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 9. tōḻi-,
1-7. ulaku kiḷarntaṉṉa - ulaku puṭipeyarntā lotta, urukeḻu vaṅkam - accam tōṉṟum nāvāy, pulavu tiri peruṅ kaṭal nīr iṭippōḻa - pulāl vīcum alikaḷiyuṭiya periya kaṭaliṉ nīri iṭiyē piḷantu cella, iravum elliyum acivu iṉṟu āki -iravum pakalum ōriṭattum taṅkutal iṉṟi, viri celal iyaṟki vaṅkūḻ āṭṭa - virintu cellum iyaṟkiyiṉatāya kāṟṟu acittuc celutta, nīkāṉ - nāvāy ōṭṭuvāṉ, kōṭu uyar tiṇi maṇal akaṉ tuṟi - kari uyarnta ceṟinta maṇaliyuṭiya akaṉṟa tuṟikkaṇ, māṭa oḷ eri maruṅku aṟintu oyya - māṭattiṉ mītuḷḷa oḷḷiya viḷakkiṉāl iṭam aṟintu celutta, āḷviṉip pirinta kātalar - poruḷīṭṭum muyaṟcipaṟṟi nammip pirintu ceṉṟa nam talivar;
9-19. taṇ paṇip poru puṉal vippiṉ nam ūr āṅkaṇ- kuḷirnta maruta nilamāya nīrmōtum nāṭṭiluḷḷa namatu ūriṭattē, karuviḷi muraṇiya taṇ putal pakaṉṟi - karuviḷiyiṉ pūviṉoṭu māṟupaṭṭa kuḷirnta pakaṉṟic ceṭiyiṉ, peruvaḷam malara alli tīṇṭi - mikka ceḻumiyuṭiya malarkaḷiṉ akavitaḻi acittu, palavuk kāyppuṟatta pacum paḻap pākal - palākkāy pōlum puṟattiṉiyuṭiya aḻakiya paḻattiṉiyuṭiya pākaṟkoṭikaḷ, kūtaḷa mutuilik koṭi niri tūṅka - kūtāḷiyiṉ mutiya ilikaḷiyuṭiya koṭikaḷiṉ kūṭṭattiṟkiṭantu aciyumpaṭi ceytu, aṟaṉ iṉṟu alikkum āṉāvāṭi - nammi aṟamillātu varuttum nīṅkāta vāṭi, kaṭi maṉi māṭattuk kaṅkul vīci - kāval poruntiya māṭamāya maṉiyil iravellām vīcaliṉ, tiruntu iḻi nekiḻntu peruṅkaviṉ cāya - tiruntiya aṇikaḷ cōrap periya aḻaku keṭa, niri vaḷi ūrum tōḷ eṉa - tōḷāṉatu variciyākavuḷḷa vaḷikaḷ nekiḻntu viḻappeṟum eṉṉum, uriyoṭu cellum aṉpiṉar peṟiṉ - uri koṇṭu tūtu cellum aṉpuṭiyāri nāmpeṟuvōmāyiṉ;
7-9. aḻipaṭar akala - namatu mikka tuṉpam nīṅka, nāḷ pala kaḻiyāmi - palanāḷ tāḻkkātu, varuvar - virintu vantuṟuvar, maṉ - aṅṅaṉam ceṉṟurippār ilarē; (eṉ ceyvām.)
(muṭipu) tōḻi! āḷviṉip pirinta naṅ kātalar, vāṭi, kaṭimaṉi māṭattuk kaṅkul vīca, iḻi nekiḻntu kaviṉ cāya, tōḷ vaḷiyūrum eṉa uriyoṭu cellum aṉpiṉarp peṟiṉ, nāḷ pala kaḻiyāmiyē, aḻipaṭar akala varuvar maṉ.
(vi-ri) ulaku kiḷarntaṉṉa vaṅkam eṉpatu vaṅkattiṉ perumi kūṟiyapaṭi; ulaku puṭi peyarvatu pōlum puṭi peyarcciyiyuṭiya vaṅkam eṉka. vaṅkūḻ - kāṟṟu. māṭa oḷḷeri. kalaṅkaḷ tuṟiyaṟintu varutaṟ poruṭṭu uyariya māṭattiṉmītu amikkappeṟṟa oḷḷiya viḷakku. itu kalaṅkari viḷakkam eṉavumpaṭum. vaṅkam nīkāṉ oyya āḷviṉip pirinta kātalar eṉṟamiyāṉ, poruḷīṭṭutaṟkuk kalattiṟ pirinta talivaṉ eṉpatāyiṟṟu. pakaṉṟiyiṉ malar veṇṇiṟamuṭiya tākaliṉ karuviḷi muraṇiya pakaṉṟi malar eṉappaṭṭatu. pakaṉṟi - civati. tūṅka - tūṅkac ceytu eṉa viritturikka. vīci, vīca eṉpataṉ tiripu. vīca eṉṉum pāṭamē koḷḷutalum ām. eṉa uriyoṭu - eṉavurikkum uriyoṭu eṉa viritturikka. uriyoṭu cellum aṉpiṉar eṉpatu, ceṉṟu urikkum aṉpiṉar eṉṟapaṭi. cellum aṉpiṉarp peṟiṉē eṉṟatu tōḻi aṅṅaṉam ceytilaḷ eṉpataṉik kuṟippiṟ kūṟiyavāṟām.
(mē-ḷ) `munnīr vaḻakkam makaṭūuvō ṭilli'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ic ceyyuḷ kalattiṟ pirivu talimakaḷi yoḻiyap pirintamikku utāraṇameṉṟaṉar, iḷam.
`iruvakip pirivum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, icceyyuḷ tūtu viṭuvatu kāraṇamāka urittatu eṉavum, im maṇimiṭi pavaḷattup piṉpaṉi vantavāṟum naṇpakal kūṟāmiyum avar kuṟitta kālam ituveṉpatu tōṉṟiyavāṟum kāṇka eṉavum, `ēvaṉ marapiṉ'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ip pāṭṭuḷ vaṇikaṉ talivaṉākavum koḷḷak kiṭattaliṉ, taliviyum avvaruṇat taliviyāmeṉ ṟuṇarka eṉavum kūṟiṉar, nac.
256. marutam
(tōḻi talimakaṟku vāyiṉ maṟuttatu.)
		piṇaṅkaril vaḷḷi nīṭilip potumpiṉ
		maṭituyiṉ muṉiiya vaḷḷukir yāmi
		noṭiviṭu kalliṟ pōki akaṉṟuṟip
		pakuvāy niṟiya nuṅkiṟ kaḷḷiṉ
	5.	3ukuvā raruntu makiḻpiyaṅku naṭiyoṭu
		tīmperum paḻaṉa muḻakki ayalatu
		āmpaṉ mellaṭi yoṭuṅkum ūra
		4poyyāl aṟiveṉiṉ māya matuvē
		kiyakap paṭṭami yaṟiyāy neruni
	10.	miyeḻil uṇkaṇ maṭantiyoṭu viyi
		ērtaru putuppuṉal uritiṉi nukarntu
		paratti yāyaṅ karappavum ollātu
		kavviyā kiṉṟāṟ peritē kāṇṭakat
		tolpukaḻ niṟinta palpūṅ kaḻaṉik
	15.	karumpamal paṭappip perumpeyark kaḷḷūrt
		tirunutaṟ kuṟumaka ḷaṇinalam vavviya
		aṟaṉi lāḷaṉ aṟiyē ṉeṉṟa
		tiṟaṉil veñcū ḷaṟikari kaṭāay
		muṟiyār peruṅkiḷi ceṟiyap paṟṟi
	20.	nīṟutalip peyta ñāṉṟi
		vīṟucā laviyattu ārppiṉum peritē.
- maturit tamiḻkkūttaṉār kaṭuvaṉ maḷḷaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-7. piṇaṅku aril vaḷḷi nīṭu ili potum pil - piṉṉiya tūṟukaḷiyuṭiya vaḷḷikkoṭiyiṉ nīṇṭa ilic ceṟivil, maṭituyil muṉiiya vaḷ ukir yāmi - maṭintu kiṭakkum tuyili veṟutta valiya nakattiṉiyuṭiya yāmi, noṭi viṭu kallil pōki - olittal ceykiṉṟa paralkaḷiṉ vaḻiyē ceṉṟu, akaṉ tuṟi - akaṉṟa nīrttuṟikkaṇṇē, pakuvāy niṟiya - piḷanta vāy niṟintiṭa, nuṅkiṉ kaḷḷiṉ ukuvār aruntu makiḻpu iyaṅku naṭiyoṭu - paṉaṅkaḷḷiṉ aruntuṅkāl cintiya oḻukkiṉi uṇṭa kaḷippuṭaṉ cellum naṭiyoṭu, tīmperum paḻaṉam uḻakki - iṉiya periya vayalkaḷik kalakki, ayalatu āmpal mel aṭi oṭuṅkum ūra - ataṉ ayalatākiya āmpaliṉ melliya ilikaṭkuḷḷē oṭuṅkik kiṭakkum ūraṉē!
8-13. poyyāl - nī poy kūṟātē, niṉ māyam aṟiveṉ - niṉ vañcaṉiyi aṟivēṉ, atu kiyakappaṭṭami aṟiyāy - niṉ parattami veḷippaṭṭamiyi nī aṟintili, neruni - nēṟṟu, mi uṇ eḻil kaṇ maṭantiyoṭu - miyuṇṭa aḻakiya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya parattiyuṭaṉ, viyi ērtaru putuppuṉal - viyiyiṉ aḻaku poruntiya putiya puṉalil, uritiṉil nukarntu - urimiyuṭaṉ iṉpam nukarntiṭa, paratti āyam karappavum - ataṉip parattiyar kūṭṭam ūrār aṟiyāmal maṟikkavum, ollātu kavvi ākiṉṟu peritu - maṟikka muṭiyāmal mikavum alar eḻukiṉṟatu; (av valartāṉ)
13-15. kāṇtaka tol pukaḻ niṟinta - aḻaku viḷaṅka paḻimi yāṉa pukaḻ mikka, pal pū kaḻaṉi - palavakiyāṉa pūkkaḷ niṟinta vayalkaḷiyum, karumpu amal paṭappi - karumpu mikka tōṭṭaṅ kaḷiyumuṭiya, perum peyark kaḷḷūr - mikka ciṟappu vāynta kaḷḷūr eṉṉum ūriṉkaṇ;
16-21. tiru nutal kuṟumakaḷ aṇinalam vavviya - aḻakiya neṟṟiyiṉiyuṭiya iḷiyāḷ oruttiyiṉ aḻakiya nalattiṉik kavarntuṇṭa, aṟaṉ ilāḷaṉ aṟiyēṉ eṉṟa tiṟaṉ il vemcūḷ -aṟa neṟi nillā ṉoruvaṉ piṉpu tāṉ avaḷi aṟiyēṉ eṉṟu kūṟiya nītiyillāta koṭiya cūḷiṉi, aṟikari kaṭāay - avartam kūṭṭam uṇarnta cāṉṟāvāri viṉavi uṇarntu, (avaṉi), muṟi ār peruṅ kiḷi ceṟiyap paṟṟi - (aviyattār) taḷirkaḷ poruntiya periya marattiṉ kiḷiyil iṟukappiṇittu, nīṟu talip peyta ñāṉṟi - nīṟṟiṉi avaṉ taliyiṟ peyta kālattē, vīṟucāl aviyattu ārppiṉum peritu - ciṟappu mikka aviyiṉkaṇ eḻunta āravārattiṉum peritāyiṟṟu.
(muṭipu) ūra! poyyāl! aṟivaṉ niṉ māyam! atuvē kiyakappaṭṭami aṟiyāy! neruni maṭantiyoṭu viyip puṉal uritiṉil nukarntu; paratti āyam karappavum ollātu kavviyākiṉṟu; atu kaḷḷūr aṟaṉilāḷaṉi, veñcūḷ aṟikari kaṭāay, kiḷipaṟṟi; nīṟutalip peyta ñāṉṟi aviyattu ārppiṉum peritu.
(vi-ri) noṭi viṭutal - veṭittal; noṭiviṭu kalliṟ pōki eṉpataṟkuc ciṟārkaḷ nīrilē tattic cellumāṟu viṭukiṉṟa vaṭṭakkalpōlac ceṉṟu eṉṟalumām. nuṅkiṟkaḷ - paṉaṅkaḷ - ukuvār - kaḷ citaṟuṇṭu oḻukuvatu, `mēṟṟuṟik koḷīiya kaḻāliṟ kīḻttuṟi, ukuvār aruntap pakuvā yāmi'1 eṉap piṉ varuvatuṅ kāṇka. niṟiya aruntum eṉak kūṭṭuka. aruntum eṉṉum peyareccam kāraṇapporuṭṭu. kiyakap paṭṭami eṉṟatu teḷiya aṟiyappaṭṭamiyi uṇarttiṟṟu. kuṟumakaḷ aṇinalam vavviya aṟaṉilāḷaṉ aṟiyēṉ eṉṟa tiṟaṉil veñcūḷ eṉṟamiyāl tīviṉiyāḷaṉ oruvaṉ ōr iḷamakaḷiṉ nalatti nukarntu piṉ avaḷik kiviṭuttu, avaḷatu muṟiyīṭṭiṉik kēṭṭu avaṉi ucāviya cāṉṟōr muṉ, yāṉ avaḷi aṟintilēṉ eṉap poyc cūḷ kūṟiṉāṉ eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. veñ cūḷ aṟikari kaṭāayc ceṟiyap paṟṟi, nīṟu talip peyta eṉṟamiyāl, cāṉṟōrkaḷ kariyāvār kūṟṟiṉālē uṇmi teḷintu avaṉi avvāṟu oṟuttaṉar eṉpatāyiṟṟu. mūṉṟu kavarāya kiḷikaḷiṉ naṭuvē vittup piṇittalum nīṟutalip peytalum aññāṉṟu kuṟṟattiṟku vitikkum taṇṭaṅkaḷām eṉka.
(u-ṟi) vaḷḷi nīṭilip potumpil tuyilmuṉiiya yāmi, kaḷḷiṉ ukuvār aruntu makiḻpiyaṅku naṭiyoṭu paḻaṉam uḻakki, ayala āmpal mellaṭi oṭuṅkum ūra eṉṟatu, niṉ maṉiyiṭattut tuyiṟali veṟuttuc ceṉṟu, parattiyar cēriyil avar iṉpam nukarnta perumitattōṭu, iṟparattipāṟ ceṉṟu taṅkuvi eṉṟavāṟām.
257. pāli
(uṭaṉ pōkāniṉṟa talimakaṭkut talimakaṉ colliyatu.)
		vēṉiṟ pātirik kūṉi māmalar
		naṟivāy vāṭal nāṟum nāḷcuram
		ariyār cilampiṉ cīṟaṭi civappa
		emmo ṭorāṟu paṭīiyar yāḻaniṉ
	5.	pommal ōti potuḷa vāri
		arumpaṟa malarnta āypū marāattuc
		curumpucūḻ alari tii vēyntaniṉ
		tēmpāy kūntal kuṟumpala mocikkum
		vaṇṭukaṭin tōmpal tēṟṟāy aṇikoḷa
	10.	nuṇkōl elvaḷi teḷirkkum muṉki
		melliṟip paṇittōḷ viḷaṅka vīci
		valluvi maṉṉāl naṭiyē kaḷvar
		pakimiku kavalic ceṉṉeṟi kāṇmār
		micimarañ cērttiya kavimuṟi yāattu
	15.	nārari maruṅkiṉ nīrvarap poḷittuk
		kaḷiṟucuvit tiṭṭa kōtuṭit tataral
		kallā umaṇarkkut tīmūṭ ṭākum
		tuṉpuṟu takuna āṅkaṇ puṉkōṭṭu
		arilivar puṟṟattu alkiri nacii
	20.	veḷḷarā miḷira vāṅkum
		piḷḷi eṇkiṉ malivayi ṉāṉē.
- uṟiyūr maruttuvaṉ tāmōtaraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 12-21. (kuṟumakaḷ) kaḷvar paki miku kavali celneṟi kāṇmār - āṟali kaḷvarkaḷuṭiya paki mikka kavartta neṟikaḷil vampalar tām cellutaṟkuriya neṟiyituveṉak kāṇum poruṭṭu, micimaram cērttiya kavi muṟi yāattu - mēliṭattē ēṇi cārttappeṟṟa kavartta taḷirkaḷiyuṭiya yā marattiṉ, nār ari maruṅkiṉ nīr vara poḷittu - nāriṉiyuṭiya aṭimarattil nīr varumpaṭi urittu, kaḷiṟu cuvittiṭṭa kōtu uṭi tataral - kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi cuvittup pōkaṭṭa cakkiyākiya cuḷḷikaḷ, kallā umaṇarkku tīmūṭṭu ākum - kallāta uppu vāṇikarkkut tīmūṭṭut turumpu ākum, tuṉpu uṟu takuna āṅkaṇ - tuṉpam uṟattakuvaṉa ākiya avviṭaṅkaḷil, puṉ kōṭṭu aril ivar puṟṟattu- pulliya puṭikaḷiyuṭiya ciṟu tūṟukaḷ paṭarnta puṟṟiṉkaṇ, alku iri nacii - irāppoḻutil uṇṇum iriyiṉi virumpi, veḷarā miḷira vāṅkum - veḷḷiya pāmpukaḷ piṟaḻumpaṭi kiyāl vavvum, piḷḷi eṇkiṉ malivayiṉ āṉa - kuṭṭik karaṭikaḷiyuṭiya maliyiṉiṭattē;
1-12. pātiri naṟivāy kūṉi māmalar vāṭal nāṟum - pātiriyiṉ tēṉ poruntiya vaḷiviyuṭiya ciṟanta pūkkaḷiṉ vāṭal nāṟukiṉṟa, vēṉil nāḷ - vēṉiṟkālattiṉ pakaṟpoḻutil, curam - curattiṉ kaṇṇē, ari ār cilampiṉ ciṟu aṭi civappa - paraṟkal poruntiya cilampiṉi aṇinta niṉ ciṟiya aṭikaḷ civakka, emmoṭu ōr āṟu paṭīiyar - emmuṭaṉ oru neṟiyil porunti varaṟku, pommal ōti potuḷa vāri- polivuṟṟa kūntali neruṅka vāri, arumpu aṟa malarnta āy pū marāattuc curumpu cūḻ alari - arumpu illiyāka malarnta aḻaki pūkkaḷiyuṭiya veṇkaṭampiṉ vaṇṭukaḷ cūḻnta malarkaḷi, tii vēynta - toṭuttuc cūṭiya, niṉ tēm pāy kūntal kuṟum pala mocikkum vaṇṭu - niṉatu tēṉ poruntiya kūntalil kuṟiyaṉavāyp palavāka moykkum vaṇṭukaḷi, kaṭintu ōmpal tēṟṟāy - kaṭintu pātukāttaliyum aṟiyāyāy, aṇikoḷa - aḻaku porunta, nuṇkōl el vaḷi teḷirkkum muṉki - nuṇṇiya tiraṇṭa oḷi poruntiyavaḷi olikkum muṉ kiyiyuṭiya, mel iṟi paṇitōḷ viḷaṅka vīci - melliya cantiṉiyuṭiya mūṅkil pōlum tōḷkaḷi viḷakkamuṟa vīci, naṭi valluvi maṉ - peritum naṭattal vaṉmiyuṭiyi ākiṉṟāy.
(muṭipu) (kuṟumakaḷ!) vēṉil nāḷ curam cīṟaṭi civappa emmoṭu ōrāṟu paṭīiyar, ōti potuḷa vāri alari tii vēynta niṉ kūntal mocikkum vaṇṭu kaṭintu ōmpal tēṟṟāy; paṇittōḷ viḷaṅka vīci naṭivalluvi maṉ.
(vi-ri) koṭumi mikka maliyiṉ kaṇṇatākiya curattilē vemmi mikka vēṉil nāḷil emmuṭaṉ orupeṟṟiyē cellutalē virumpic cīṟaṭi civappa vaṇṭu kaṭintōmputal tēṟṟāy; paṇit tōḷ viḷaṅka vīci naṭattal vaṉmiyi yuṭiyiyāyiṉāy; kuṟumakaḷē! niṉ aṉpiruntavāṟu eṉṉē! eṉṟu talivaṉ taliviyiṉ naṭiyi viyantu pārāṭṭiṉāṉ eṉka. kūntali vāri malarkaḷit toṭuttuc cūṭiṉāḷ eṉpatu, talivaṉuṭaṉ cēṟaṟkaṇ talivikkuḷḷa ārva mikutiyip pulappaṭuttiyapaṭiyām. ōmpal - taṉṉik kāttalum vaṇṭu mīṭṭum vārāmaṟ kāttalumām. micimaram - marattiṉ mēṟ pakuti. kōṭu - puṟṟiṉ puṭippu.
(mē-ḷ) `koṇṭu talik kaḻiyiṉum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷ koṇṭutalik kaḻitaṟkaṇ talivaṉ, naṭiyi viyantatu eṉṟār, nac.
258. kuṟiñci
(alla 2kuṟippaṭṭup patippeyarnta talimakaṉ taṉṉeñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		naṉṉaṉ utiyaṉ aruṅkaṭip pāḻit
		toṉmutir vēḷir ōmpiṉar vitta
		poṉṉiṉum aruminaṉ kaṟintum aṉṉōḷ
