(neñcē!) poṉ aṭarntaṉṉa - poṟ pūkkaḷ neruṅki yiruntāṟpōṉṟa, oḷ iṇar cerunti pal malar vēynta - oḷiporuntiya kottukkaḷiyuṭiya ceruntiyiṉ pala pūkkaḷic cūṭiya, nalam peṟu kōtiyaḷ - aḻaku poruntiya kūntaliyuṭi yāḷāki, tiṇi maṇal aṭikari - ceṟinta maṇaliyuṭiya kaṭaṟkariyil, alavaṉ āṭṭi - ñeṇṭiṉi ōṭṭi viḷiyāṭi, aciyiṉaḷ irunta - iḷippāṟi yirunta, āytoṭi kuṟumakaḷ - āynta toṭiyiṉi aṇinta talivi, nalam cāl viḻu poruḷ kalam niṟi koṭuppiṉum, naṉmi mikka ciṟanta poruḷum aṇikalamum niṟiyak koṭuppiṉum - peṟal aruṅ kuriyaḷ āyiṉ - peṟutaṟku ariyaḷ āṉāl;
11-14. viri tiri kaṇ tiraḷ muttam koṇṭu - virinta kaṭalil tiraṇṭa vaṭiviṉiyuṭiya muttukkaḷikkoṇṭu, tēṉ imir akaṉkari ñāṅkar pakukkum - vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum akaṉṟa kariyiṭattē avikaḷip pakuttuk koḷḷum, kāṉal am peruntuṟi paratavaṉ - cōliyiṉiyuṭiya aḻakiya periya tuṟiyiṉiyuṭiya neytaṟ ṟalivaṉākiya avaḷ tanti, namakku-,
6-11. nām uṟi tēem marūu peyarntu - nām taṅkum in nāṭṭil payiṉṟiruttaliṉ nīṅki, avaṉoṭu - avaṉuṭaṉ kūṭi, iru nīr cērppiṉ uppu uṭaṉ uḻantum - periya kaṭaṟkariyiṉi yaṭutta uppuc ceṟuvil uppup potiyuṭaṉ tirintu varuntiyum, peru nīr kuṭṭam puṇiyoṭu  pukkum - periya kaṭaliṉ āḻattil puṇiyiṉ kaṭ ceṉṟum, paṭuttaṉam paṇintaṉam aṭuttaṉam iruppiṉ - avaṉ vayamākiyum paṇintum cārntum iruppiṉ, aṟam terintu - aṟamātali uṇarntu, tarukuvaṉ kol - avaḷit taruvāṉō?
(muṭipu) neñcē! viḻupporuḷ kalam niṟi koṭup piṉum peṟalaruṅ kuriyaḷ āyiṉ, nām peyarntu avaṉoṭu uṭaṉ uḻantum, puṇiyoṭu pukkum paṭuttaṉam paṇintaṉam aṭut taṉam iruppil, paratavaṉ aṟanterintu namakku (avaḷit) tarukuvaṉ kollō?
(vi-ri) poṉ aṭarntaṉṉa - poṉ takaṭāyiṉāṟpōṉṟa (ceruntimalar) eṉṟurittalumām. kaṭaṟkari yiṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa ciṟu ñeṇṭukaḷ makkaḷik kāṇpuḻi virintu ōṭi aḷiyiṟ pukutal ciṟanta tōr kāṭciyāka irukkumākaliṉ, ciṟuvar ciṟumiyar avaṟṟi aṅkum iṅkum alittu viḷiyāṭutal iṟṟikkum kaṇkūṭu. viḻupporuḷ - poṉ. kalam - aṇikalam. niṟi - niṟiya. maruvutal - payiṉṟu vatital. oppuṭaṉ uḻutum eṉṟu pāṭaṅ kāṇappaṭiṉum, ivvāciriyarē, 1`iruṅkaḻic ceṟuviṉ uḻāatu ceyta, veṇkal uppiṉ koḷḷi cāṟṟi' eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttaliṉ, īṇṭu uḻutum eṉṟa pāṭam poruntāmi yuṇarka. uppuṭaṉ uḻattalāvatu uppu viṟṟut tirintu varuntutal. paṭuttal - akap paṭuttal; avaṉ vayam ākkutal. aṭuttal - cārntiruttal. tarukuvaṉ kollō eṉṟamiyāl. taruvāṉāyiṉ avvāṟu ceytum avaḷip peṟakkaṭavēm eṉṟu talimakaṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ic ceyyuḷit talivaṉ poruḷvayiṟ piriyak karutiyatākak koṇṭu urippar.
(mē-ḷ) `veḷippaṭitāṉē'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, ic ceyyuḷik kāṭṭi, itaṉuḷ poruḷ koṭuttāṟ peṟal ariyaḷāyiṉ, taṉṉi vaḻipaṭṭāl tanti taruvaṉō? atu namakku aritākaliṉ, iṉṉum poruḷ nām mikat tēṭi vantu varitum eṉap poruḷ vayiṟ piriyak karutiyavāṟu kāṇka eṉpar, nac.
281. pāli
(talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaḷatu vēṟupāṭu 
kaṇṭu āṟṟāḷākiya tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		ceyvatu terinticiṉ tōḻi alkalum
		2akaluḷ āṅkaṇ accaṟak kūṟiya
		coṟpaḻu tākum eṉṟum añcātu
		olkiyal maṭamayil oḻitta pīli
	5.	vāṉpōḻ valvil cuṟṟi nōṉcili
		avvār viḷimpiṟku aminta novviyal
		kaṉikura licikkum viricelaṟ kaṭuṅkaṇi
		muraṇmiku vaṭukar muṉṉuṟa mōriyar
		teṉṟici mātiram muṉṉiya varaviṟku
	10.	viṇṇuṟa vōṅkiya paṉiyiruṅ kuṉṟattu
		oṇkatirt tikiri yuruḷiya kuṟitta
		aṟiyiṟan tavarō ceṉṟaṉar
		paṟiyaṟin taṉṉa alarnamak koḻittē.
- māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-3. tōḻi-, alkalum akaluḷ āṅkaṇ - nāṭōṟum ūriṉkaṇṇē yiruntu, accu aṟak kūṟiya col - namakku accam iṉṟākak kūṟiya col, paḻutu ākum eṉṟum añcātu - paḻutu paṭum eṉavum añcāmal;
4-12. olku iyal maṭa mayil oḻitta pīli - nuṭaṅkum taṉmi vāynta iḷami poruntiya mayil kaḻitta tōkiyi, vāṉ pōḻ valvil cuṟṟi - nīṇṭa vāriṉāl valiya villil vittuk kaṭṭi, nōṉ cili - anta valiya villiṉ, av vār viḷimpiṟku aminta - aḻakiya neṭiya nāṇiṉ viḷimpiṟkup poruntiya, novvu iyalkaṉi kural icikkum viri celal kaṭu kaṇi - virivuttaṉmiyuṭiya mikka oli olikkum virintacelavu poruntiya kaṭiya ampukaḷiyuṭiya, muraṇ miku vaṭukar muṉ uṟa - māṟupāṭu mikka vaṭukar tamakku muṉṉē tuṇiyāki vara, mōriyar teṉ tici mātiram muṉṉiya varaviṟku - mōriyar eṉpār teṉṟici nāṭukaḷip paṟṟa eṇṇip pōnta varukikku, viṇ uṟa ōṅkiya paṉi iru kuṉṟattu - vāṉ aḷāva uyarnta paṉiyuṭiya periya maliyiṉi, oṇ katir tikiri uruḷiya - tamatu oḷḷiya katirkaḷiyuṭiya āḻi taṭiyiṉṟic cella, kuṟitta - pōḻntu vaḻiyākkiya, aṟi iṟantu - pāṟikaḷik kaṭantu;
12-13. paṟi aṟintaṉṉa alar namakku oḻittu - paṟi aṟintālotta alariṉi nampāl viṭuttu, avarō ceṉṟaṉar - nam talivar pōyviṭṭār;
1. ceyvatu terinticiṉ - nām iṉic ceyaṟpālataṉi ārāyntu kāṇpāyāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi, accaṟak kūṟiyacoṟ paḻutāku meṉṟum añcātu, avar aṟiyiṟantu alar namakku oḻittuc ceṉṟaṉar; ceyvatu terinticiṉ.
(vi-ri) akaluḷ - akaṉṟa iṭattiyuṭiya ūr; `akaluḷāṅ kaṇ aṟiyunar viṉāyum'1 eṉpuḻi nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urittami kāṇka. accam - accu eṉak kuṟintu niṉṟatu. kūṟiya collāvatu - niṉṉiṟ piriyēṉ eṉṟu palakālum teruṭṭiya col. añcātu ceṉṟaṉar eṉṟu iyiyum, novvu iyal kaṉi kural - kaṇi virintu cellutalāl eḻum ōci. paṟi aṟintaṉṉa alar - paṟiyaṟintāṟ pōla yāṇṭum parantu veḷippaṭum alar. alar namakku oḻittu eṉṟatu, alarākiya tuṉpam oṉṟiyē namakku uriyatākkic ceṉṟaṉar eṉṟapaṭi.
282. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟikkaṇ talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟamākat tōḻi talimakaṭkuc colluvāḷāyc colliyatu. talimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ 
tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatumām.)
		perumalic cilampiṉ vēṭṭam pōkiya
		ceṟimaṭi yampiṉ valviṟ kāṉavaṉ
		porututoli yāṉi veṇkōṭu koṇṭu
		nīrtikaḻ cilampiṉ naṉpoṉ akaḻvōṉ
	5.	kaṇporutu imikkun tiṇmaṇi 2kiḷarppa
		vinnuti vāṉmarup poṭiya ukka
		teṇṇīr āli kaṭukkum muttamoṭu
		mūvēṟu tāramum 1oruṅkuṭaṉ koṇṭu
		cāntam poṟimara māka naṟinār
	10.	vēṅkik kaṇṇiyaṉ iḻitaru nāṭaṟku
		iṉtīm palaviṉ ērkeḻu celvattu
		entiyum etirntaṉaṉ koṭiyē alarvāy
		ampal ūrum avaṉoṭu moḻiyuñ
		cāyiṟit tiraṇṭa tōḷpā rāṭṭi
	15.	yāyu mavaṉē yeṉṉum yāmum
		vallē varuka varinta nāḷeṉa
		nalliṟi melviral kūppi
		illuṟi 2 kaṭavuṭku okkutum paliyē.
- tol kapilar.
(co-ḷ) 1-10. tōḻi! perumalic cilampiṉ vēṭṭam pōkiya - periya maliyiṉ pakkattē vēṭṭikkuc ceṉṟa, ceṟimaṭi ampiṉ val vil kāṉavaṉ - mūṭṭuvāy ceṟinta ampiṉiyum valiya villiṉiyumuṭiya vēṭṭuvaṉ, porutu toli yāṉi veṇ
kōṭu koṇṭu - taṉṉuṭaṉ pōr ceytupaṭṭa yāṉiyiṉ veḷḷiya kompiṉik koṇṭu, nīr tikaḻ cilampiṉ - nīr viḷaṅkum maliyil, naṉ poṉ akaḻvōṉ - ciṟanta poṉṉi akaḻntu eṭuppōṉ, kaṇ porutu imikkum tiṇ maṇi kiḷarppa - kaṇṇiṉip porutu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa tiṇṇiya maṇikaḷ poṉṉuṭaṉ mīppōntu viḷaṅka avaṟṟi, vi nuti vāl maruppu oṭiya ukka - kūriya muṉiyi yuṭiya veḷḷiya yāṉik kompu oṭitalāl utirnta, teḷ nīr āli kaṭukkum muttamoṭu - teḷinta nīrmiyiyuṭiya ālaṅ kaṭṭiyi yokkum muttukkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭṭi, mū vēṟu tāramum - vevvēṟākiya ammūṉṟu paṇṭaṅkaḷiyum, cāntam poṟi maram āka oruṅku uṭaṉ koṇṭu - cantaṉa maram kāvu maramāka avaṟṟi oru cērak kāvik koṇṭu, naṟi nār vēṅkik kaṇṇiyaṉ - naṟikkoṭiyāya nāriṉāl vēṅkimalarit toṭutta kaṇṇiyi yuṭiyaṉāy, iḻitarum nāṭaṟku - am maliyiṉiṉṟum iṟaṅkivarum nāṭṭiyuṭiya nam talivaṉukku;
11-18. iṉ tīm palaviṉ ēr keḻu celvattu entiyum - mikka iṉimiyuṭiya palāmaraṅkaḷiyuṭiya aḻaku mikka celvatti yuṭiya nam tantiyum, koṭi etirntaṉaṉ - niṉṉik koṭuttali ēṟṟuk koṇṭāṉ, alarvāy ampal ūrum - paḻimoḻiyiṉic cilarum palarum aṟiyak kūṟum vāyiṉiyuṭiya ūriluḷḷārum, avaṉoṭu moḻiyum - avaṉoṭu niṉṉic cērntē kūṟuvar, yāyum - nam tāyum, cāy iṟi tiraṇṭa tōḷ pārāṭṭi - vaḷinta cantiṉiyuṭiya tiraṇṭa niṉ tōḷkaḷip pārāṭṭi, avaṉē eṉṉum - niṉakku uriyaṉ avaṉē eṉṟu kūṟuvaḷ, yāmum - nāmum, varinta nāḷ vallē varuka eṉa - nam maṇattiṟku varinta nāḷ virintu varuvatāka eṉṟu, nal iṟi melviral kūppi - nalla iṟiyiṉiyuṭiya melliya viralkaḷik kuvittu, illuṟi kaṭavuṭku - maṉiyuṟi teyvattiṟku, pali ōkkutum - pali celuttuvōmāka.
(muṭipu) kāṉavaṉ veṇkōṭu koṇṭu poṉ akaḻvōṉ mūvēṟu tāramum oruṅkuṭaṉ koṇṭu iḻitarum nāṭaṟku, entiyum koṭi etirntaṉaṉ; alarvāy ampalūrum avaṉoṭu moḻiyum; yāyum avaṉē eṉṉum; yāmum nāḷ vallē varuka eṉa illuṟi kaṭavuṭkup pali ōkkutum.
(vi-ri) nīr tikaḻ cilampu - nīr veḷippaṭṭu viḷaṅkum mali; mārikkaṇ uṇṭa nīriṉik kōṭikkaṇ umiḻum maliyeṉka. kaṇ porutu - kaṇṇoḷiyuṭaṉ māṟupaṭṭu; kaṇ vaḻukkuṟa viḷaṅkum eṉṟapaṭi. poṉṉuṭaṉ maṇi kiḷarppa eṉka. āli - nīrttiraḷ eṉṉum poruḷatu; ālam - nīr. teṇṇīr - teḷinta nīr eṉṟē koṇṭu āli peyar māttiriyāy niṉṟatu eṉṟalumām. avaṟṟi muttamoṭu kūṭṭi eṉac cila col varuvitturikka. oruṅkuṭaṉ cāṟṟi eṉṉum pāṭattiṟku oruṅkē vili kūṟi eṉap poruḷ urikka. vēṭṭam pōkiya kāṉavaṉ, yāṉikkōṭum poṉṉum muttum cantaṉamum vēṅki malarum peṟṟu varuvaṉ eṉṟatu, am maliyiṉ ciṟappukkūṟiyapaṭi. attakiya nāṭṭiṉi yuṭiyavaṉ nam talivaṉ eṉṟamiyāl, avaṉ arum poruḷ palavaṟṟi vari poruḷākak koṇṭu vantuḷāṉ eṉṟa vāṟāyiṟṟu. enti niṉṉi avaṉukkuk koṭuttal orutali eṉpāḷ `etirntaṉaṉ koṭi' yeṉat tuṇivu paṟṟik kūṟiṉāḷ. etirntaṉaṉ koṭi eṉpataṟku niṉ poruṭṭut talivaṉ koṇṭuvanta poruṭkoṭiyi ēṟṟuk koṇṭaṉaṉ eṉṟalumām. enti koṭi etirntamiyālum, yāyum avaṉē uriyaṉ eṉṟatālum, ūrum avaṉoṭu cārttiyē moḻi talālum nī talivaṉukkē uriyiyātalāl orutali; eṉiṉum maṇanāḷ virintu varuka eṉṟu kaṭavuṭkup pali ōkkutum eṉat tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka. talivaṉ kēṭṭu virintu variya varuvāṉātal itaṉ payaṉ. `yāyum avaṉē' eṉpatu tāy teyvattip parāvuṅ kuṟippiṉateṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttu. icceyyuḷ talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyateṉak koḷḷiṉ, niṉ eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷil nam eṉa urittuk koḷka. ākkutum pali eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ pali amippōm eṉṟu urikka. `cērppaṉi, yāṉuṅ kātaleṉ yāyunaṉi veyyaḷ entiyuṅ koṭīiyarvēṇṭum, ampa lūrum avaṉoṭu moḻimē'1 eṉpatu icceyyuṭ karuttoṭu peritum ottiruttal aṟintu iṉpuṟaṟpālatu.
(mē-ḷ) `kaḷavalarāyiṉum'2 eṉṉum cūttirattu, teyvam vāḻttalum eṉṉum pakutikkaṇ icceyyuḷik kāṭṭi, itaṉuḷ `tōḷ pārāṭṭi, yāyum avaṉē yeṉṉum' eṉṟu yāy teyvam parāyiṉāḷ eṉpatupaṭak kūṟi, yām atteyvattiṟkup pali koṭuttum eṉṟavāṟu kāṇka eṉṟaṉar, nac.
283. pāli
(uṭaṉpōkku valitta tōḻi talimakaṟkuc colliyatu.)
		naṉṉeṭuṅ katuppoṭu peruntōḷ 3nīviya
		niṉivaṇ oḻital añciya eṉṉiṉuñ
		celavutalik koṇṭa peruvitup puṟuvi
		palkavar maruppiṉ mutumāṉ pōkkic
	5.	cilluṇāt tanta cīṟūrp peṇṭir
		tirivayiṉ teviṭṭuñ cēṭpulak kuṭiñip
		pital meṉkural aituvan ticittoṟum
		pōkunar pulampu māṟē 4ēkutaṟku
		ariya ākumeṉ ṉāmik karimaraṅ
	10.	kaṇṇaki iḷaṅkuḻi kālmutal kaviṉil
		vicumpuṭaṉ iruṇṭu vemmi nīṅkap
		pacuṅkaṇ vāṉam pāytaḷi poḻinteṉap
		puṉṉukum peṭutta naṉṉeṭuṅ kāṉattu
		ūṭṭu pañcip picirparan taṉṉa
	15.	vaṇṇam mūtāy taṇṇilam 5 varippa
		iṉiya ākuka taṇintē
		iṉṉā nīppiṉ niṉṉoṭu celaṟkē.
- maturi marutaṉiḷanākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-3. taliva! nal neṭuṅ katuppoṭu peruntōḷ nīviya - taliviyiṉ nalla nīṇṭa kūntaloṭu periya tōḷiṉit taṭaviya, niṉ ivaṇ oḻital añciya eṉṉiṉum - niṉṉi oḻintu iṅkut taṅkiyiruttali añciyuḷa eṉṉik kāṭṭilum, celavu talik koṇṭa - uṭaṉpōkkiṉi mēṟkoṇṭa, peru vituppu uṟuvi - periya viriviṉi yuṟṟirukkum talivi.
17. iṉṉā nīppiṉ niṉṉoṭu celaṟku - tuṉpattit tarum piriviṉkaṇ niṉṉuṭaṉ pōtaṟku;
4-9. pal kavar maruppiṉ mutumāṉ pōkki - pala kiḷikaḷ poruntiya kompiṉiyuṭiya mutiya māṉkaḷit turantu, cil uṇā tanta - ciṟiya uṇavup poruḷik koṇṭuvanta, cīṟūr peṇṭir tirivayiṉ - ciṟiya ūrkaḷiluḷḷa peṇkaḷ tiriyu miṭattē, teviṭṭum cēṭpulak kuṭiñi - cēymiyiṭattē iruntu olikkum pērāntiyiṉ, pital meṉ kural aitu vantu icit toṟum - varuttam tarum melliya kural meṉmiyāka vantu olikkun tōṟum, pōkunar pulampum āṟu - vaḻip pōvār kēṭṭu varuntum cura neṟikaḷ, ēkutaṟku ariya ākum eṉṉāmi - cellaṟku ariyaṉa ākum eṉṉātapaṭi;
9-16. kari maram kaṇ aki iḷam kuḻi kāl mutal kaviṉi - karinta maraṅkaḷ tammiṭattē kiḷikkappeṟum iḷiya taḷirkaḷ aṭi mutaṟ kiḷittu aḻaku peṟavum, vemmi nīṅka - veppam oḻiyavum, vicumpu uṭaṉ iruṇṭu - vāṉil oruṅkē iruṭciyuṟṟu. pacuṅ kaṇ vāṉam pāy taḷi poḻinteṉa - pacumiyit taṉpāṟ koṇṭa mēkam paranta tuḷiyiṉic corintatāka, pul nukumpu eṭutta nalneṭuṅ kāṉattu - puṟkaḷ kuruttiṉi viṭṭa nalla nīṇṭa kāṭṭil, ūṭṭu pañcip picir parantaṉṉa - cenniṟam ūṭṭiya pañciṉ pitir paraviyatu pōṉṟa, vaṇṇam mūtāy taṇ nilam varippa - cenniṟamuṭiya tampalap pūccikaḷ kuḷirnta nilattē aḻakuṟutta; taṇintu iṉiya ākuka - veppam taṇintu iṉimiyuṭaṉ ākuka.
(muṭipu) taliva! peruvituppuṟuvi niṉṉoṭu celaṟku, āṟukaḷ ēkutaṟku ariya ākum eṉṉāmi, vāṉam taḷi poḻin teṉa, naṉṉeṭuṅ kāṉattu mūtāy varippa, taṇintu iṉiya ākuka!
(vi-ri) añciya - añci uṭaṉpōkku valitta eṉṟapaṭi. katuppum tōḷum nīvit taliyaḷi ceyta pēraṉpuṭiya niṉṉip pirintu talivi āṟṟiyiruttal ariteṉa añci yāṉ uṭaṉpōkku valittēṉāka, talivi eṉṉiṉum muntuṟa uṭaṉpōkkiṟku viri kiṉṟāḷ eṉpāḷ, `añciya eṉṉiṉum, celavutalik koṇṭa peruvituppuṟuvi' eṉṟāḷ. vituppuṟutal - ārvamuṭaṉ virital. cil uṇā - māṉ uṇṭu eñciya ciṟiya uṇavu; tiṉi mutaliyaṉa. cēṭpulattiṉiṉṟu olittalāl kuṭiñiyiṉ kural meṉkural eṉappaṭṭatu. kaviṉi - kaviṉa eṉat tirikka. pul - puṟakkāḻaṉavākiya paṉi mutaliyaṉavumām. maḻi peyta piṉṟi nilattilē parantukiṭakkum tampalap pūccikaḷ nilattiṉik kōlam ceytāṟpōl iṉiya kāṭciyip payakkumākaliṉ `vaṇṇam mūtāy taṇṇi lam varippa iṉiya vākuka' eṉṟāḷ. uṭaṉpōkkil talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ cel liṉum tōḻi āyattār uḷḷiṭṭāriyum payiṉṟa iṭattiyum viṭuttuc cēṟaliṉ ataṉiyum pāliyākavē koṇṭu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam ceytaṉar cāṉṟōr. uṭaṉpōkkāṉatu taṉakkum tāy mutalāyiṉārkkum iṉṉāmi viḷittaliṉ ataṉi - iṉṉā nīppu eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka.
284. mulli
(viṉimuṟṟiya talimakaṉ tērppākaṟkuc colliyatu.
 taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatumām.)
		ciṟiyili nellik kāykaṇ ṭaṉṉa
		kuṟuviḻik kaṇṇa kūralaṅ kuṟumuyal
		muṭanti varakiṉ vīṅkupīḷ aruntupu
		kuṭantiyañ ceviya kōṭpavar oṭuṅki
	5.	iṉṟuyi leḻuntu tuṇiyoṭu pōki
		muṉṟil ciṟuniṟi nīrkaṇ ṭuṇṇum
		puṉpulan taḻīiya poṟimutaṟ ciṟukuṭit
		tiṉikkaḷ ḷuṇṭa teṟikōl maṟavar
		vicitta villar vēṭṭam pōki
	10.	mullip paṭappip pulvāy keṇṭuṅ
		kāmar puṟavi ṉatuvē kāmam
		nammiṉun tāṉṟali mayaṅkiya
		ammā arivi uṟiviṉ ūrē.
- iṭikkāṭaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 11-13. kāmam nammiṉum talimayaṅkiya - kāmattāl nammik kāṭṭiṉum mikka mayakkamuṟṟa, am mā arivi - aḻakiya māmi niṟamuṭiya nam talivi, uṟivu iṉ ūr - uṟitaluṟum iṉiya ūrāṉatu;
1-11. ciṟi ili nellik kāy kaṇṭaṉṉa - ciṟiya ilikaḷi yuṭiya nelliyiṉ kāyik kaṇṭālotta, kuṟuviḻik kaṇṇa - kuṟiya viḻiporuntiya kaṇkaḷiyum, kūral kuṟu muyal - kūriya mayiriṉiyumuṭiya kaṟiya muyalkaḷ, muṭanti varakiṉ vīṅku pīḷ aruntupu - vaḷintu kiṭakkum varakiṉatu parutta kuruttiṉit tiṉṟa, kuṭanti am ceviya - vaḷinta aḻakiya ceviyiṉavāki, kōḷ pavar oṭuṅki - kāykaḷik koṇṭa koṭikaḷuṭ pukuntu kiṭantu uṟaṅki, iṉ tuyil eḻuntu - iṉiya tuyiliṉiṉṟum eḻuntu, tuṇiyoṭu pōki - tam tuṇiyoṭu pōki, muṉṟil ciṟu niṟi nīr kaṇṭu uṇṇum - vīṭṭu muṟṟattēyuḷḷa ciṟiya cālkaḷiluḷḷa nīrikkaṇṭu parukum, puṉ pulam taḻīiya - mulli nilattu ūrkaḷ cūḻnta, poṟi mutal ciṟukuṭi - kuṉṟiṉ aṭikaḷiluḷḷa ciṟiya ūr kaḷiṭattē, tiṉik kaḷ uṇṭa teṟi kōl maṟavar - tiṉiyiṉā lākkiya kaḷḷiṉiyuṇṭa nāṇiṉit teṟittu viṭukkum ampiṉi yuṭiya maṟavarkaḷ, vicitta villar - vēkamāka iḻuttu nāṇ pūṭṭiya villiṉarāy, vēṭṭam pōki - vēṭṭiyāṭi, mullip paṭappi pulvāy keṇṭum - mullinilat tōṭṭattē māṉi aṟuttu uṇṇum, kāmar puṟaviṉatu - aḻakiya kāṭṭiṉkaṇṇatu.
(muṭipu) kāmam nammiṉum talimayaṅkiya arivi ūr kāmar puṟaviṉatu; kuṟumuyal varakiṉ pīḷ aruntupu, pavar oṭuṅki, tuyileḻuntu, tuṇiyoṭu pōki, muṉṟil niṟiyiṉ nīr kaṇṭu uṇṇum puṉpulam eṉavum, ciṟu kuṭi maṟavar vēṭṭam pōkip pulvāy keṇṭum puṟavu eṉavum iyiyum.
(vi-ri) kaṇṇavākiya kuṟumuyal, kūraliyuṭiya kuṟumuyal eṉka. kūral - mayir. niṟi - nīrccāl. puṉpulam - mulliniṟam. muṉṟil niṟi nīr kaṇṭu uṇṇum puṉpulam eṉṟamiyāl, mulli nilattu ūrkaḷ eṉap poruḷurikkappaṭṭatu. maruta nilattu nellāṟ, kaḷ amittal pōlak kuṟiñci nilattut tiṉiyāl kaḷ amittu uṇparākaliṉ, tiṉikkaḷ uṇṭa maṟavar eṉṟāṉ. puṟaviṉatu ākaliṉ nam tēriṉi āṇṭu virintu celuttuka eṉat talimakaṉ pākaṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka. neñciṟkuk kūṟiyatāyiṉ, taliviyirukkum ūr puṟaviṉatu; nām iṉṉum īṇṭuḷōm! eṉṟāṉ eṉka.
285. pāli
(1uṭaṉpōk kuṭaṉpaṭuvitta tōḻi, talimakaṭkuc colliyatu.)
		oḻiyac ceṉmār celpa eṉṟunām
		aḻipaṭar uḻakkum avala neñcattu
		evvam ikantucēṇ akala viyeyiṟṟu
		ūṉacip piṇaviṉ uṟupaci kaḷiiyar
	5.	kāṭutēr maṭappiṇi yalaṟak kaliyiṉ
		ōṭukuṟaṅku aṟutta cennā yēṟṟi
		veyilpulan tiḷikkum vemmiya payilvari
		irumpuli vēṅkik karuntōl aṉṉa
		kalleṭut teṟinta palkiḻi uṭukki
	10.	ulaṟukuṭi vampalar uyarmaram ēṟi
		ēṟuvēṭ ṭeḻunta iṉantīr eruvi
		āṭucevi nōkkum attam paṇittōḷ
		kuvaḷi yuṇkaṇ ivaḷum nammoṭu
		varūum eṉṟaṉarē kātalar
	15.	vārāy tōḻi muyaṅkukam palavē.
- kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattuk kārikkaṇṇaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 15. tōḻi-,
14. kātalar, - nam kātalar;
1-3. oḻiyac ceṉmār celpa eṉṟu - nām pirintirukkap poruḷīṭṭac collutaṟkuc celvār eṉṟu, nām aḻipaṭar uḻakkum - nām mikka tuṉpattāl eppoḻutum varuntum, avalam neñcattu - āṟṟāmiyuṭiya maṉattiṉ, evvam ikantu cēṇ akala - tuṉpam nīṅkic cēymikkaṇ oḻiyumāṟu;
3-14. vi eyiṟṟu ūṉ nacip piṇaviṉ uṟupaci kaḷiiyar - kūriya palliṉiyuṭiya ūṉi virumpum peṇnāyiṉ mikka paciyiṉip pōkkutaṟku, kāṭu tēr maṭap piṇi alaṟa - taṉ kaliyiṉik kāṭṭil tēṭum iḷiya peṇmāṉ kataṟa, kaliyiṉ ōṭu kuṟaṅku aṟutta cennāy ēṟṟi - āṇmāṉiṉ ōṭit tappaṟ kuriya toṭiyiṉic cititta cennāyiṉ āṇ, veyil pulantu iḷikkum - veyyili veṟuttu meliyum, vemmiya - veppam poruntiyaṉavum, payil vari irumpuli vēṅki - varikaḷ poruntiya periya vēṅkippuliyiṉ, karun tōl aṉṉa - viḷaṅkum tōliṉip pōṉṟa, kal eṭuttu eṟinta - āṟalippōr kalli eṭuttu eṟitaliṉ, pal kiḻi uṭukki ulaṟu kuṭi vampalar - pala kiḻivu kaḷik koṇṭa uṭiyiṉiyum ulaṟiya kuṭiyiṉiyumuṭiya putiyarāya vaḻiccelvār, uyar maram ēṟi - uyarnta marattil ēṟi, ēṟuvēṭṭu eḻunta - uṇavi eṟṟutali virumpi eḻunta, iṉam tīr eruvi - iṉattiṟ pirintu vanta paruntu, āṭu cevi nōkkum attam - vāṉil aciyum cevviyi nōkkiyiruppaṉavum ākiya kāṭṭu neṟikaḷil, paṇittōḷ - perutta tōḷiṉiyum, kuvaḷi uṇkaṇ ivaḷum - kuvaḷimalarip pōṉṟa miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷiyumuṭiya ivaḷum, nammoṭu varūum eṉṟaṉar - nammuṭaṉ vārāniṟpaḷ eṉṟu kūṟiṉar;
15. vārāy muyaṅkukam palavē - ākiyāl nām paṉmuṟi muyaṅkutaṟku varuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! kātalar nām aḻipaṭar uḻakkum neñcattu evvam ikantu cēṇ akala, attam ivaḷum nammoṭu  varūum eṉṟaṉar; ākiyāl muyaṅkukam pala; vārāy.
(vi-ri) akala eṉṉum eccam eṉṟaṉar eṉpataṉ mutaṉili koṇṭu muṭiyum. kāṭu tēr piṇi - kāṭṭilē āṇmāṉ irukkumiṭatti ārāyntu tiriyum piṇi. ōṭu kuṟaṅku - ōṭutaṟku ētuvākiya kuṟaṅku. cennāy ēṟṟi veyil pulantu iḷikkum vemmiya eṉṟatu, kāṭṭiṉ vemmi mikuti kūṟiyapaṭi. vemmiya attam eṉavum, vampalar maram ēṟi eruvi āṭu cevi nōkkum attam eṉavum iyiyum. ēṟu - eṟital; `kūrukirp paruntiṉ ēṟukuṟittorīi'1 eṉṟataṉālum iḵtu ip poruṭṭātal aṟiyappaṭum. cevvi; cevi eṉṟāyatu. uṇavu kuṟittu eḻunta paruntu vicumpilē iyaṅkum ticipaṟṟi, kāṭṭiṉ iyalpu aṟiyappaṭumākaliṉ, vampalar ataṉi nōkkuvārāyiṉar eṉka.
286. marutam
(varin teytuvaleṉṟu nīṅkun talimakaṉ talimakaḷi āṟṟuvittuk koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟu tōḻiyik kippaṟṟiyatu. taṉṉit toṭṭuc cūḷuṟuvāṉākak karutit tōḻi colliyatu.)
		veḷḷi viḻuttoṭi meṉkarup pulakki
		vaḷḷi nuṇṇiṭi vayiṉvayiṉ nuṭaṅka
		mīṉciṉi yaṉṉa veṇmaṇaṟ kuviik
		kāñci nīḻal tamarvaḷam pāṭi
	5.	ūrkkuṟu makaḷir kuṟuvaḻi viṟanta
		iṟāal aruntiya ciṟuciral marutiṉ
		tāḻciṉi yuṟaṅkun taṇṭuṟi ūra
		viḻiyā vuḷḷam viḻiyum āyiṉum
		eṉṟuṅ, kēṭṭavi tōṭṭi yāka mīṭṭāṅku
	10.	aṟaṉum poruḷum vaḻāmi nāṭit
		taṟṟaka vuṭimi nōkki maṟṟataṉ
		piṉṉā kummē muṉṉiyatu muṭittal
		aṉiya periyō roḻukka mataṉāl
		ariya periyōrt teriyuṅ kāli
	15.	nummō raṉṉōr māṭṭum iṉṉa
		poyyoṭu miṭintavi tōṉṟiṉ
		meyyāṇ ṭuḷatōviv vulakat tāṉē.
- ōram pōkiyār.
(co-ḷ) 1-7. veḷḷi viḻu toṭi - veḷḷiyiṉālākiya ciṟanta pūṇiyuṭiya, mel karuppu ulakki - melliya karum pākiya ulakkiyāl, vaḷḷi nuṇ iṭi - koṭipōṉṟa nuṇṇiya iṭi, vayiṉ vayiṉ nuṭaṅka - aṅkum iṅkum aciya, mīṉ ciṉi aṉṉa veṇ maṇal kuvii - mīṉiṉ muṭṭi pōṉṟa veḷḷiya maṇalik kuvittu, kāñci nīḻal - kāñci marattiṉ niḻalil, tamar vaḷam pāṭi - tam kuṭiyiṉar perumikaḷip pāṭi, ūrkkuṟu makaḷir kuṟuvaḻi - ūriluḷḷa iḷiya peṇkaḷ kuṟṟuṅkāl, viṟanta iṟāal aruntiya ciṟu ciral - ceṟinta iṟāl mīṉit tiṉṟa ciṟiya ciraṟpaṟavi, marutiṉ tāḻciṉi uṟaṅkum taṇ tuṟi ūra - marutamarattiṉ tāḻnta kiḷiyiṟ ceṉṟu tuyilum kuḷirnta tuṟiyiyuṭiya ūraṉē!
8-13. eṉṟum viḻiyā uḷḷam viḻiyum āyiṉum - aṟaṉallavaṟṟi eṉṟum virumpā uḷḷam orōvaḻi mayaṅki virumpumāyiṉum, kēṭṭavi tōṭṭiyāka - kēṭṭa kēḷvikaḷ aṅkuca māka, mīṭṭu - uḷḷa māya yāṉiyi mīṭṭu, aṟaṉum poruḷum vaḻāmi nāṭi - illaṟamum poruḷum vaḻuvā vakiyi āyntu, taṉ takavuṭimi nōkki maṟṟu ataṉpiṉ - taṉatu takutiyuṭimiyi  uṇarntu ataṉ piṉṉarē, muṉṉiyatu muṭittal ākum - tāṉ karutiyati muṭittal uḷatākum, periyōr oḻukkam - periyōr oḻukkaṅkaḷ, aṉiya - at taṉmiyavām;
13-17. ataṉāl-, ariya periyōrt teriyuṅkāli- ariyaṉa ceyyum periyōr ceyalkaḷi ārāyumiṭattu, num ōraṉṉōr māṭṭum - nummi yotta periyōriṭattum, iṉṉa poy yoṭu miṭintavi tōṉṟiṉ - ittakiyaṉa poyyoṭu kūṭiya kūṟṟukkaḷ tōṉṟiṉ, ivvulakattāṉ - ivvulakiṉkaṇ, mey yāṇṭu uḷatō - mey eṅku uḷatākum?
(muṭipu) taṇ tuṟi ūra! viḻiyā vuḷḷam viḻiyum āyiṉum, mīṭṭu, nāṭi, nōkki, ataṉ piṉṉākum muṉṉiyatu muṭittal; periyōr oḻukkam aṉiya; ataṉāl teriyuṅ kāli nummōraṉṉōr māṭṭum poyyoṭu miṭintavi tōṉṟiṉ ivvulakattāṉ mey yāṇṭuḷatō?
(vi-ri) kuṟumakaḷir kāñci niḻalil veṇmaṇaṟ kuvii karuppu ulakkiyāl iṭinuṭaṅkat tamar vaḷampāṭik kuṟuvaḻi eṉka. tamar eṉṟatu, tam tiṇi mutalvarkaḷiyum aracar mutalā yiṉāriyum kuṟikkum. karumpulakkiyāl kuṟuvar eṉṟatu, maruta nilattiṉ vaḷamikuti kūṟiyapaṭi; tīṅkarumpu nallulakki yākac ceḻumuttam, pūṅkāñci nīḻal avippār pukār makaḷir, āḻik koṭit tiṇṭērc cempiyaṉ vampalartārp, pāḻit taṭavarit tōṭ pāṭalē pāṭal, pāvimār ārikkum pāṭalē pāṭal'1 eṉṉum vaḷḷip pāṭṭu īṇṭu aṟintu iṉpuṟaṟpālatu. aruntiya ciṟuciral eṉṟataṉāl varāal eṉṉum pāṭam poruntāmi aṟika. pāṭṭiṉik kēṭṭu uṟaṅkum eṉka. viḻiyā uḷḷam eṉṟatu, iyalpilē naṉṟallataṉi virumpāta uḷḷam eṉa uḷḷattiṉ tūymi kūṟiyapaṭi. kēṭṭavi tōṭṭiyāka eṉṟami yāl, uḷḷamākiya yāṉiyi eṉka. iḵtu ēkatēca uruvakam. uḷḷam virumpiya vaḻi ataṉic cellaviṭātu, virumpiya poruḷiṉ nalam tīṅkiṉi ārāyntu, naṉmiyāyiṉa vaḻic celuttavēṇṭu meṉpatu, `ceṉṟa viṭattāṟ celaviṭā tītorīi, naṉṟiṉpāl uyppatu aṟivu'1 eṉṉum vāyuṟi vāḻttāl aṟiyap paṭum. aṅṅaṉam uḷḷatti mīṭṭu nalvaḻippaṭuttaṟkuc cāṉṟōrpāl tām kēṭṭa poruṇ moḻikaḷ peritum tuṇiyāvaṉa ākaliṉ, kēṭṭavi tōṭṭiyākak mīṭṭu eṉṟār. talivaṉ taṉṉik kippaṟṟiya ataṉic cūḷuṟavākak ` karutiya tōḻi, ulakattup periyōr oḻukkam attaṉmiya ākaliṉ, nummāṭṭup poy tōṉṟu māṟilli eṉpatupaṭa avaṉukkuk kūṟiṉāḷ eṉpatu ic ceyyuḷiṉ karuttākum. āciriyar nakkīraṉārum ic ceyyuḷiṉkaruttu ituvēyākak  koṇṭuḷḷār. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, muṉṉiyatu  eṉpataṟkup puṟattoḻukkam eṉṟum, ariya periyōrt teriyuṅkāli eṉpa taṟkup periyōr oḻukkatti ārāyu miṭattu avi ariyavāy iruntaṉa eṉṟum poruḷ koṇṭu, talimakaṉ muṉpu cūḷ tampiṉāṉ eṉat tōḻi pulantu kūṟu kiṉṟāḷākak karuttuk koṇṭaṉar.
(mē-ḷ) `kuṟiyeṉap paṭuvatu.... moḻipa'2 eṉṉum cūttirattuc `ceṟippaṟivuṟukkappaṭṭa talimakaṉ teruḷ vāṉāyiṉ innāḷ varival, attuṇiyum ivaḷi āṟṟuvittuk koṇṭiru; niṉakku aṭikkala meṉak kippaṟṟum; kippaṟṟiyataṉit tōḻi cūḷuṟavākak karutiṉāḷ; ulakattuc cūḷuṟuvār pacuviyum pārppāriyum peṇṭiriyum toṭṭuc cūḷuṟuvarākalāṉ; cūḷuṟa vāṟṟār eṉpatu niṉintu, nī varival eṉṟatē amiyātō? cūḷuṟal vēṇṭumō? poyttalum vāyttalum uṭiyāraṉṟō cūḷuṟuvar? meyyallatu collātārkkuc cūḷuṟavu vēṇṭumō? niṉ kaṇṇum poyyuṇṭāyiṉ meyyeṉpatu ivvulakattu nilipeṟṟavaḻi illiyākātē? eṉṉum;' eṉṟu urittu ataṟku utāraṇamāka icceyyuḷik kāṭṭiṉar, nakkīrar.
`kūtir vēṉil eṉṟiru pācaṟi'3 eṉṉum cūttirattu, kaṭṭami oḻukkattuk kaṇṇumiyāṉum eṉṉum pakutikku, ic ceyyuḷil `viḻiyā vuḷḷam viḻiyu māyiṉum...... ariya periyōrt teriyuṅ kāli' eṉṉum pakutiyi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, itu  tokuttuk kūṟiyatu eṉṟum, `peṟaṟkarum perumporuḷ muṭinta piṉ vanta'1 eṉṉam cūttirattu, periyōr oḻukkam peritu eṉak kiḷantu peṟutali yillāp piḻippiṉum eṉṟa pakutiyil icceyyuḷik kāṭṭi, itaṉuḷ aṟaṉ eṉṟatu, illaṟatti; taṟṟakavuṭimi nōkki eṉṟatu, taṉṉāl avvaṟaṉum poruḷum takutippā ṭuṭiyavām taṉmi nōkki eṉṟavāṟām; muṉṉiyatu eṉṟatu, puṟat toḻukkatti; periyōr oḻukkam aṉiya eṉṟatu, periyōr oḻukkam periya eṉṟavāṟu, eṉṟu urittu, itu muṉṉar nikaḻnta poyccūḷ paṟṟi, nummaṉōr māṭṭum iṉṉa poyccūḷ piṟakkumāyiṉ ivvulakattu meykcūḷ iṉi iṉṟām; ataṉāl periyōrit tamatu oḻukkattit tēruṅ kāli ariyavāyiruntaṉaveṉat talivaṉi nōkkit tōḻi kūṟaliṉ, avaṉi vaḻipāṭu tappiṉāḷāyiṟṟu. uḷḷuṟi yuvamam itaṟku ēṟkumāṟu uṇarka eṉṟum kūṟiṉār, nac.
287. pāli
(pirintu pōkāniṉṟa talimakaṉ iṭiccurattu niṉṟu taṉṉeñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		toṭiyaṇi muṉkit tokuviral kuviip
		paṭiva neñcamoṭu pakaltuṇi yāka
		nōṅkol aḷiyaḷ tāṉē tūṅkunili
		mariyēṟu coṟinta māttāḷ kantiṉ
	5.	curiyivar potiyil aṅkuṭic cīṟūr
		nāṭpali maṟanta narikkaṇ iṭṭikip
		purici mūḻkiya poriyari ālattu
		orutaṉi neṭuvīḻ utitta kōṭi
		tuṇippuṟā irikkun tūmaḻi naṉantalik
	10.	kaṇikkāl ampiṇi ēṟumpuṟam nakka
		olkunili yāattu ōṅkuciṉi payanta
		alkuṟu variniḻal aciyiṉam nōkka
		arampuvantu alikkum māli
		nirampā nīḷiṭi varuntutum yāmē.
- kuṭavāyiṟ kīrattaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 3-9. mari ēṟu coṟinta - āṇ mari urāyntami yāl, tūṅku nili - aciyum niliyiṉip poruntiya, mā tāḷ kantiṉ - kariya aṭi poruntiya kantiṉiyuṭiya, curi ivar potiyil - curikkoṭi paṭarnta ampalattiṉkaṇṇē, am kuṭi cīṟūr - aḻakiya kuṭiyiruppiṉiyuṭiya cīṟūriluḷḷār, nāṭ pali maṟanta - viṭiyaṟkālattē yiṭum paliyi maṟantamiyāl, narikkaṇ- veḷitākiya iṭattiṉiyuṭiya, iṭṭiki - palipīṭatti yaṭuttu, purici mūḻkiya pori ari ālattu - matilpōṟ cūḻnta porikaḷ poruntiya aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya ālamarattiṉ, oru taṉi neṭu vīḻ utitta kōṭi - oṉṟāyt taṉitta nīṇṭa viḻutiṉi mōtiya kōṭik kāṟṟu, tuṇip puṟā irikkum - tuṇiyuṭaṉ mēviyirunta puṟākkaḷi ōṭṭum iṭamākiya, tū maḻi naṉantali - maḻi aṟṟa akaṉṟa iṭattē;
10-14. kaṇik kāl am piṇi ēṟu puṟam nakka - tiraṇṭa kāliṉiyuṭiya aḻakiya piṇiyiṉi ataṉ kali mutukiṭattē nakka, olku nili yāttu ōṅku ciṉi payanta - aciyum niliyiṉiyuṭiya yāmarattiṉ uyarnta kiḷikaḷālākiya, alkuṟu vari niḻal aciyiṉam nōkka - curuṅkiya varivariyāya niḻalil taṅkiṉēmāy nōkka, arampu vantu alikkum māli- kuṟumpāka vantu varuttum māliyil, nirampā nīḷiṭi yām varuntutum - intat toliyāta nīṇṭa neṟiyil yām taṉittu varuntukiṉṟēm; (nam talivi,)
1-3. toṭi aṇi muṉkit tokuviral kuvii - vaḷiyiṉi aṇinta muṉkiyil tokka viralik kuvittu, paṭiva neñcamoṭu - nammik kāṇalām viratam koṇṭa neñcat tuṭaṉ, pakal tuṇiyāka nōmkol - pakal nēramē taṉakkut tuṇiyāka varuntiyiruppaḷō, aḷiyaḷ - iraṅkattakkāḷ.
(muṭipu) yām yāttu vari niḻal aciyiṉam nōkka, arampu vantalikkum māli nirampā nīḷiṭi varuntutum; nam kātali paṭiva neñcamoṭu pakal tuṇiyāka nōṅkol? aḷiyaḷ.
(vi-ri) toku viral kuvii eṉṟatu, makaḷirkku varuttam mikka vaḻi nikaḻvatoru nikaḻcci. paṭivam - nōṉpu. vēṟu tuṇiyiṉṟi yeṉpāṉ, pakal tuṇiyāka eṉṟāṉ. tāṉ virintu ceṉṟu avaḷatu varuttattip pōkkamuṭiyāmi karuti aḷiyaḷ tāṉē eṉṟāṉ. kantu - potiyiliṉkaṇ teyvam uṟiyum taṟi; ataṉ aṭi karitāyiruttaliṉ māttāṭkantu eṉappaṭṭatu. aṅkuṭic cīṟūr - muṉpu aḻakiṉ kuṭiyiruppiṉatā yirunta cīṟūr eṉka. cīṟūr kuṭi pōkappaṭṭamiyiṉ, nāṭkālattē pali koṭuppāriṉṟip potiyil pāḻpaṭṭatu eṉpāṉ, mariyēṟu coṟinta tūṅku nilikkantu eṉavum, curi ivar potiyil eṉavum kūṟiṉāṉ. iṭṭiki - ceṅkal; īṇṭup palipīṭam; ataṉkaṇ paliyiṭap peṟāmiyiṉ narikkaṇ iṭṭiki eṉap paṭṭatu. `kalikeḻu kaṭavuḷ kantaṅki viṭap, palikaṇ māṟiya pāḻpaṭu potiyil'1 `kaṭavuḷ pōkiya karuntāṭ kantam'2 eṉpaṉa vaṟṟālum potiyilil kantiṉkaṇṇē teyvam uṟitalum, paliyiṭuvāriṉṟi atu pāḻpaṭutalum aṟiyappaṭum.  tūmaḻi - maḻi tū eṉa māṟuka; tūvutal - oḻital; `karumañ ceyavoruvaṉ kitūvēṉ'3 eṉpuḻit tūvutal oḻital eṉṉum poruṭṭātal kāṇka. piṇiyiṉ veppattit taṇittaṟkāka ēṟu ataṉ puṟattiṉi nakkum eṉka. nōkka alikkum māli eṉka. arampu- kuṟumpu. iru pēraracarkku iṭiyē ciṟitu poḻutu pukuntu kuṭikaḷi varuttic cellum kuṟunila maṉṉar pōlap pakalukkum iravukkum naṭuvē pukuntu ciṟitu poḻutu niṉṟu taṇantāriṭit tuṉpuṟuttic celvatākaliṉ, māliyi arampu eṉṟāṉ. `puṉkaṇ mālik kuṟumpeṟintu ōṭṭi'4 eṉṟār iḷaṅkō vaṭikaḷum. nirampā nīḷiṭi - cellat toliyāta neṭiya vaḻi.
288. kuṟiñci
(pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ tōḻi ceṟippaṟivuṟīi varivu kaṭāyatu.)
		ceṉmati ciṟakkaniṉ uḷḷam niṉmali
		āram nīviya ampakaṭṭu mārpiṉi
		cāral vēṅkip paṭuciṉip putuppū
		murukumuraṇ koḷḷum uruvak kaṇṇiyi
	5.	eritiṉ kolli yiṟiñciya ēṉal
		evvaṅ kūṟiya vikalum varuvōy
		kaṉimutir aṭukkattem taṉimi kāṇṭaliṉ
		eṇmi ceytaṉi yākuvi naṇṇik
		koṭiyōr kuṟukum neṭiyiruṅ kuṉṟattu
	10.	iṭṭāṟu iraṅkum viṭṭoḷir aruvi
		aruvari yiḻitarum veruvaru paṭāark
		kayantali manti uyaṅkupaci kaḷiiyar
		pārppiṉ tanti paḻaccuḷi toṭiṉum
		naṉinōy ēykkum paṉikūr aṭukkattu
	15.	makaḷir māṅkāṭṭu aṟṟē tukaḷaṟak
		5kontō ṭutirtta katuppiṉ
		antīṅ kiḷavi tanti kāppē.
-6viṟṟūṟṟu mūteyiṉaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-6. (taliva!) niṉ mali āram nīviya am pakaṭṭu mārpiṉi - niṉatu maliyiṉ cantaṉam pūcappeṟṟa aḻakiya viḷakkam poruntiya mārpiṉiyuṭiyiyāy, cāral vēṅki paṭuciṉi putu pū - pakkamaliyiluḷḷa vēṅkimarattiṉ mikka kiḷikaḷiluḷḷa putiya pūkkaḷālāya, muruku muraṇkoḷḷum uruvak kaṇṇiyi - piṟavaṟṟiṉ maṇattuṭaṉ māṟupaṭum aḻakiya kaṇṇiyiyuṭiyiyāy, eritiṉ kolli iṟiñciya ēṉal - tīyāṉatu eritta kolliyiṉkaṇ viḷintu katir vaḷinta tiṉippuṉattē, evvam kūṟiya vikalum varuvōy - niṉatu tuṉpattik kūṟutaṟku nāṭōṟum varuvōy!
8-15. koṭiyōr naṇṇi kuṟukum neṭi iru kuṉṟattu - āṟalippōr porunti neruṅkiyirukkum nīṇṭa periya kuṉṟiṉ, iṭṭu āṟu iraṅkum viṭṭu oḷir aruvi - kuṟukiya neṟiyil olikkum iṭiyiṭṭu viḷaṅkum aruviyāṉatu, aruvari iḻitarum veruvarupaṭāar - ariya maliyiṉiṉṟu vantu viḻum iṭamākiya accantōṉṟum ciṟu tūṟukaḷilirukkum, kayantali manti uyaṅku paci kaḷiiyar - melliya taliyiṉiyuṭiya mantiyiṉ varuttum paciyiṉi nīkkumāṟu, pārppiṉ tanti - ataṉ kuṭṭiyiṉatu tantiyāya kaṭuvaṉ, paḻaccuḷi toṭiṉum - palāp
paḻattiṉ cuḷiyiṉit tōṇṭiṉum, naṉi nōy ēykkum paṉi kūr aṭukkattu - mikka tuṉpam vantu poruntum naṭukkam mikka (teyvamuṭiya) pakkamaliyil uḷḷa, makaḷir māṅkāṭṭu aṟṟē- kaṉṉiyar uṟiyum māṅkāṭu eṉṉum ūriṉ kāvali ottatu;
15-17. tukaḷ aṟa - kuṟṟamaṟa, kontōṭu utirtta - pūṅkottuk kaḷi utirttu aṇinta, katuppiṉ kūntaliyum, am tīm kiḷavi tanti kāppu - aḻakiya iṉiya coṟkaḷiyumuṭiya ivaḷ tantiyiṉ kāvaliṭam;
7-8. (aṅṅaṉamākavum), kaṉi mutir aṭukkattu em taṉimi kāṇṭaliṉ - kaṉikaḷ mutirntuḷa intap pakkamaliyil nāṅkaḷ taṉimiyuṟṟiruttalik kāṇutaliṉ, eṇmi ceytaṉi ākuvi -nī eṅkaḷ arumiyuṇarātu eḷimi ceytaṉi ākiṉṟāy;
1. niṉ uḷḷam ciṟakka - niṉ eṇṇam ciṟappuṟuvatāka; ceṉmati - ceṉṟu varuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) taliva! mārpiṉi, kaṇṇiyi, ēṉal evvaṅkūṟiya vikalum varuvōy! antīṅ kiḷavi tanti kāppu, paṉikūr aṭukkattu makaḷir māṅkāṭṭu aṟṟu; (aṅṅaṉamākavum) em taṉimi kāṇṭaliṉ eṇmi ceytaṉi ākuvi; ceṉmati; ciṟakka niṉ uḷḷam.
(vi-ri) tōḻi, ceṟippaṟivuṟutti, talivaṉip pakaṟkuṟi vilakkukiṉṟāḷākaliṉ, ceṉmati eṉṟum, talivi iṟceṟippu uṟṟamiyāl, iṉi eytutaṟku ariyaḷ eṉa maṉam māḻkiyirātu muyaṉṟu varintu koḷḷuti eṉpāḷ, ciṟakka niṉ uḷḷam eṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ. niṉ mali āram nīviya eṉṟataṉāl, talimakaṉ ammali nāṭṭiṟkut talivaṉ ātal peṟṟām. putup pūvālākiya kaṇṇiyiyuṭiyi eṉṟu iyiyum. muruku muraṇ koḷḷal, piṟa pūkkaḷiṉ maṇattuṭaṉ māṟupaṭutal; murukaṉuṭaṉ māṟupaṭat takka kaṇṇiyi yuṭiyiyāy eṉṟalumām. tīyiṉāl kāṭṭiṉi yerittut tiruttiya kolliyākaliṉ, eritiṉ kolli eṉappaṭṭatu. evvaṅ kūṟutal - keṭuti viṉātal mutaliyaṉavum ām. aṭukkattu em taṉimi kāṇṭaliṉ eṉṟamiyāl piṟavaḻit taṉittuk kāṇṭal aritu eṉpatāyiṟṟu. neṭiya eṉpatu neṭi eṉa vikāramāyiṟṟu. kuṉṟattu iraṅkum aruvi iḻitarum paṭāar eṉka. iḻitarum eṉṉum peyareccam iṭappeyar koṇṭatu. paṭāar - ciṟutūṟu; kuṟuṅkāṭu eṉalumām. kuṭṭiyi īṉṟa manti eṉpatu tōṉṟa āṇ kuraṅku pārppiṉ tanti eṉappaṭṭatu. īṉṟa mantiyiṉ aḻi paciyip pōkkutaṟku ataṉ kaṇavaṉākiya kaṭuvaṉ, tamakku iyalpil uṇavāyuḷḷa paḻaccuḷiyit tōṇṭi eṭuttal kuṟṟamākā teṉpatu tōṉṟa, `manti uyaṅku paci kaḷiiyar....... tanti paḻaccuḷi toṭiṉum' eṉappaṭṭatu. takkaṉa takātaṉa aṟintu oḻukaṟpālārāya makkaḷuṭ paṭātu vilaṅkiṉattiṟpaṭṭa kuraṅku, iṉṟiyamiyāk kaṭappāṭupaṟṟip paḻaccuḷi toṭṭa tāyiṉ, āṇṭuṟiyum teyvattāl ataṟkum tuṉpam uṇṭākum eṉa avviṭattiṉ iyalpu kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. ēykkum - ēyum eṉṟapaṭi. eṉavē, makaḷirē yuṟiyum māṅkāṭu eṉum pati, teyvattiṉ kāppu mikkuṭiyateṉpatu pōtarum. kontōṭu kontukaḷi; vēṟṟumi mayakkam.
289. pāli
(pirintu pōkāniṉṟa talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		ciliyē ṟaṭṭa kaṇivīḻ vampalar
		uyarpatuk kivarnta tatarkoṭi atiral
		1neṭunili naṭukal nāṭpalik kūṭṭuñ
		curaṉiṭi vilaṅkiya maraṉōṅ kiyaviṉ
	5.	vantuviṉi valitta namvayiṉ eṉṟum
		terumaral uḷḷamoṭu varuntal āṉātu
		nekiḻā meṉpiṇi vīṅkiya kiciṟitu
		aviḻiṉum uyavum āymaṭat takuvi
		cēṇuṟi pulampiṉ nāṇmuṟi yiḻitta
	10.	tiṇcuvar nōkki niṉintu kaṇpaṉi
		nekiḻnūl muttiṉ mukiḻmulit teṟippa
		miyaṟa virinta paṭiyami cēkki
		aimeṉ tūvi aṇicēr pacii
		miyal koṇṭa mataṉaḻi yirukkiyaḷ
	15.	pakuvāyp palli paṭutoṟum paravi
		nalla kūṟeṉa naṭuṅkip
		pulleṉ māliyoṭu poruṅkol tāṉē.
- eyiṉanti makaṉ iḷaṅkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-6. cili ēṟaṭṭa kaṇi vīḻ vampalar uyar patukku - villiṟ kōtta kaṇiyāl vīḻntiṟanta putiyarkaḷiṉ uṭali mūṭiya uyarnta kaṟkuviyalkaḷil, ivarnta - ēṟippaṭarnta, tatarkoṭi atiral - neruṅkiya koṭiyākiya kāṭṭu mallikiyiṉ pūkkaḷ, neṭu nili naṭukal nāḷ pali kūṭṭum - uyarnta niliyiṉiyuṭiya naṭukalliṉ kālippalikkuk kūṭṭappeṟum, curaṉiṭi vilaṅkiya maraṉ ōṅku iyaviṉ - pālinilattē kuṟukkē cellum maraṅkaḷ uyarntuḷa neṟiyil, vantu viṉivalitta namvayiṉ - vantu poruḷīṭṭum toḻilil uṟutikoṇṭuḷa nammiṭattē, eṉṟum terumaral uḷḷa moṭu varuntal āṉātu - eppoḻutum niṉintu cuḻalum uḷḷattāl varuntutal amiyātu;
7-8. meṉpiṇi vīṅkiya ki nekiḻā ciṟitu aviḻiṉum - met teṉṟa piṇippu mikka ki nekiḻntu ciṟitu vilakiṉum, uyavum āy maṭa takuvi - varuntum aḻakiya maṭappamuṭiya nam talivi;
9-14. cēṇ uṟi pulampiṉ nāḷ muṟi iḻitta - nām cēymik kaṇ uṟiyum taṉimiyāl nāṭōṟum muṟi muṟiyākak kōṭiṭṭu vanta, tiṇ cuvar nōkki niṉintu - tiṇṇiya cuvariṉip pārttu uṭaṉuṟiyap peṟāmiyi niṉintu, kaṇpaṉi nekiḻ nūl muttiṉ mukiḻmuli teṟippa - kaṇṇīr nūlaṟṟu utirum muttukkaḷip pōla mukiḻtta mulimītu teṟittu viḻa, mi aṟa virinta paṭi ami cēkki - kuṟṟamaṟa akaṉṟa paṭuttal aminta paṭukkiyil, ai meṉ tūvi aṇi cērpu acii - aḻakiya melliya aṉṉattiṉ ciṟakālāya aṇiyiṉic cērntu taṅki, miyal koṇṭa mataṉ aḻi irukkiyaḷ - mayakkam koṇṭamiyiṉ valiyaṟṟa irukki yaḷāki;
15-17. paku vāyp palli paṭutoṟum paravi - piḷanta vāyiṉiyuṭiya palli olikkuntoṟum teyvattit toḻutu, nalla kūṟu eṉa - nalla moḻiyiṉik kūṟuvāyāka eṉa ataṉi vēṇṭi, naṭuṅki - maṉam naṭuṅki, pul eṉ māliyoṭu porum kol - polivaṟṟa mālikkālattoṭu māṟupaṭuvaḷō?
(muṭipu) nekiḻā meṉ piṇi vīṅkiya ki ciṟitu aviḻiṉum uyavum āy maṭat takuvi, maraṉōṅku iyaviṉ vantu viṉi valitta nam vayiṉ eṉṟum varuntal āṉātu, cēṇ uṟi pulampiṉ, nāṇ muṟi iḻitta cuvar nōkki niṉintu, kaṇpaṉi teṟippa, cēkki aṇi cērpu acii, mataṉ aḻi irukkiyaḷ, palli paṭutoṟum paravi, naṭuṅki, māliyoṭu poruṅkol?
(vi-ri) patukku - patukki, vaḻic celvār atiralip palikkuk kūṭṭum eṉka; paliyākak kūṭṭum eṉṟalumām. āymaṭat takuvi varuntal āṉātu iḻitta cuvar nōkki niṉintu eṉṟu iyiyum. ki ciṟitu aviḻiṉum uyavum āy maṭat takuvi eṉṟatu avaḷatu iḷami kūṟiyapaṭi. iḻitta - kōṭiṭṭa, nōkki eṉṟatu, ceṉṟa nāṭkaḷiyum cellakkaṭava nāṭkaḷiyum eṇṇi yaṟintu eṉṉum kāriyam tōṉṟa niṉṟatu. cēkki aṇicērpu acii mataṉ aḻi irukkiyaḷ eṉṟatu, talivi tuyiliṉṟi varuntutali uṇarttiya paṭi. paṭutal - olittal. nalla eṉṟatu, talivaṉ varavu eṉṟapaṭi. kūṟu eṉa - kūṟu eṉṟu kūṟi, taṇantārkku mālip poḻutu pallāṟṟāṉum tuṉpam viḷippatākalāṉ, ataṉoṭu naṭuṅkip poruṅkol eṉṟāṉ. māliyoṭu māṟupaṭutali māliyō alli maṇantār uyiruṇṇum - vēlinī vāḻi poḻutu'1` eṉpataṉāl aṟika.
290. neytal
(iravuk kuṟikkaṇ talimakaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat 
tōḻikkuc colluvāḷāyt talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		kuṭumik kokkiṉ piṅkāl pēṭi
		iruñcēṟṟu aḷḷal nāṭpulam pōkiya
		koḻumīṉ valcip puṉtalic ciṟāar
		nuṇñāṇ avvalic cēval paṭṭeṉa
	5.	alkuṟu poḻutiṉ melkiri miciyātu
		pitaṟ piḷḷi taḻīi oyyeṉa
		aṅkaṭ peṇṇi yaṉpuṟa naraluñ
		ciṟupaḵ ṟolkuṭip perunīrc cērppaṉ
		kaḻicēr puṉṉi yaḻipūṅ kāṉal
	10.	taṇavā neñcamoṭu tamiyaṉ vantunam
		maṇavā muṉṉum evaṉō tōḻi
		veṇkōṭ ṭiyāṉi viṟaṟpōrk kuṭṭuvaṉ
		teṇṭirip parappiṟ ṟoṇṭi muṉṟuṟic
		curumpuṇa malarnta peruntaṇ neytal
	15.	1maṇiyēr māṇalam orīip
		poṉṉēr vaṇṇaṅ koṇṭaveṉ kaṇṇē.
- nakkīrar.
(co-ḷ) 11. tōḻi-,
1-8. iru cēṟṟu aḷḷal - kariya kuḻampākiya cēṟṟiṉi yuṭiya, pulam - ceyyiṉkaṇ, nāḷpōkiya - kālivēṭṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟa, koḻumīṉ valcip pultali ciṟāar - koḻumīṉākiya uṇaviṉiyuṭiya puṟkeṉṟa taliyiṉiyuṭiya ciṟuvar (iṭṭa), nuṇ ñāṇ a vali cēval paṭṭeṉa - nuṇṇiya kayiṟṟālāya aḻakiya valiyil cēval akappaṭṭatāka, kuṭumik kokkiṉ piṅkāl pēṭi - cūṭṭiṉiyuṭiya ak kokkiṉ paciya kāliṉiyuṭiya pēṭi, alkuṟu poḻutiṉ melku iri miciyātu - taṉittut taṅkiyirukkum kālattē mellum iriyiṉit tiṉṉātu, pital piḷḷi  taḻīi - tuṉpuṟum taṉ kuñciṉit taḻuvi, oy eṉa am kaṇ peṇṇi aṉpu uṟa naralum - aḻakiya iṭattiyuṭiya paṉimarattiṉkaṇ virivāka aṉpu tōṉṟa olittirukkum, ciṟu pal tolkuṭi perunīrc cērppaṉ - ciṟiya palavāya paḻiya kuṭikaḷik koṇṭa kaṭaṟkariyiyuṭiya nam talivaṉ;
9-11. kaḻi cēr puṉṉi aḻi pū kāṉal - kaḻiyic cārnta puṉṉiyiṉ mikka pūkkaḷiyuṭiya cōliyil, taṇavā neñca moṭu tamiyaṉ vantu - aṉpu nīṅkāta neñcamuṭaṉ taṉiyaṉāki vantu, nam maṇavā muṉṉum - nammik kalattaṟku muṉṉum;
11-16. eṉkaṇ - eṉ kaṇkaḷ, veṇkōṭṭu yāṉi viṟal pōrk kuṭṭuvaṉ - veḷḷiya tantattiṉiyuṭiya yāṉiyiyum pōr veṟṟiyiṉiyumuṭiya kuṭṭuvaṉatu, teṇtirip parappiṉ toṇṭi muṉtuṟi - teḷinta alikaḷ poruntiya parap piṉiyuṭiya toṇṭiyiṉ tuṟimuṟṟattē, curumpuṇa malarnta peru taṇ neytal - vaṇṭu tēṉiyuṇṇa malarnta periya taṇṇiya ney taṟpūviṉ, maṇi ēr māṇnalam orīi - nīlamaṇiyi nikartta māṇpuṟṟa aḻakiya niṟattiṉi nīṅki, poṉ nēr vaṇṇam koṇṭa - poṉ, pōlum niṟattik koṇṭaṉa, evaṉō - iḵteṉṉiyō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! cērppaṉ, kāṉal tamiyaṉ vantu nam maṇavā muṉṉum eṉkaṇ kuṭṭuvaṉ toṇṭi muṉṟuṟi neytal māṇnalam orīi, poṉ nēr vaṇṇam koṇṭa; evaṉ?
(vi-ri) kuṭumi - cūṭu; ucciyiṉ kaṇṇatōr uṟuppu. aḷḷaṟ pulam nāṭpōkiya eṉa māṟuka. pōkiya ciṟāar avvaliccēval paṭṭeṉa eṉṟu iyiyum. alkuṟu poḻutu - taṉittut taṅkiyirukkum poḻutu. melku iri-meṉṟu tiṉṉum iri; `melkupu peyarā'1 eṉpuḻi melkutal ipporuṭṭātal kāṇka. pitaṟ piḷḷi - paṟakkalāṟṟā varuttattiyuṭiya piḷḷi; piṟavaṟṟik kaṇṭu añcum varuttattiyuṭiya piḷḷi eṉṟumām. aṉpu - cēvaliṭattuḷḷa aṉpu. kaṇṭārkku irakkam tōṉṟa olikkum eṉṟalumām. taṇavā neñcam - kātal nīṅkāta neñcam. talivaṉ kāṉaliṭattut taṉiyaṉ vantu iravuk kuṟikkaṇ nammi maṇattal oru tali eṉpāḷ, `maṇavā muṉṉum' eṉṟāḷ. iravuk kuṟikkaṇ eṉṉa iṭiyīṭu nēruṅkol eṉṉum accattāl kaṇkaḷ pacaliyuṟṟaṉa eṉpāḷ, `maṇavā muṉṉum eṉkaṇ poṉ nērvaṇṇam koṇṭa' eṉṟāḷ. itu pacappuṟu paruvaral. maṇavā muṉṉum kaṇkaḷ pacaliyuṟṟaṉa evaṉō eṉṟataṉ payaṉ, talivi nammip pirintu ciṟitum āṟṟakilāḷ ākaliṉ, nām virintu varital vēṇṭumeṉat talivaṉ uṇartalām eṉka.
291. pāli
(poruḷvayiṟ pōkāniṉṟa talimakaṉ iṭiccurattut 
taṉṉeñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		vāṉam peyalvaḷaṅ karappak kāṉam
		ulaṟi iliyila vākap palavuṭaṉ
		ēṟuṭi āyattu iṉampaci teṟuppak
		kayaṉaṟa vaṟanta kōṭiyoṭu nayaṉaṟap
	5.	peruvari nivanta maruṅkil koṭuvarip
		puliyoṭu porutu ciṉañciṟantu valiyōṭu
		uravukkaḷiṟu otuṅkiya maruṅkiṟ parūupparal
		ciṟupaṉ miṉmiṉi kaṭuppa evvāyum
		niṟivaṉa imikkum nirampā nīḷiṭi
	10.	eruvi yiruñciṟi yirīiya viriyiṇart
		tātuṇ tumpi muralici kaṭuppap
		pariyiṉa tuyirkkum ampiṉar veruvara
		uvali cūṭiya taliyar kavali
		ārttuṭa ṉarumporuḷ vavvaliṉ yāvatum
	15.	cāttiṭi vaḻaṅkāc cēṭcimi yatara
		ciṟiyili nellit tīñcuvit tiraḷkāy
		utirvaṉa tāam attam tavirviṉṟu
		puḷḷi ampiṇi uṇīiya uḷḷi
		aṟu 1maruppu oḻitta taliya tōlpoti
	20.	maṟumarup piḷaṅkō ṭatirak kūuñ
		cuṭarteṟa varuntiya aruñcuram iṟantāṅku
		uḷḷiṉi vāḻiya neñcē pōteṉap
		pulaṅkamaḻ nāṟṟat tirumpal kūntal
		nalleḻiṉ maḻikkaṇaṅ kātali
	25.	melliṟip paṇittōḷ viḷaṅkumāṇ kaviṉē.
- pālipāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō.
(co-ḷ) 22-25. neñcē-, vāḻiya-, pōtu eṉa pulam kamaḻ nāṟṟattu - malar eṉap pulamelām kamaḻum nāṟṟattiṉiyuṭiya, irumpal kūntal - kariya palavāya kūntaliyum, nal eḻil maḻikkaṇ - nalla aḻakiya kuḷirnta kaṇṇiṉiyum uṭiya, nam kātali - nam kātaliyiṉ, mel iṟip paṇittōḷ - melliya iṟiyiṉi yuṭiya parutta tōḷiṉ, viḷaṅkum māṇkaviṉ - viḷaṅkum māṇpuṟṟa aḻakiṉi;
1-9. vāṉam peyal vaḷam karappa - mēkam peyalāya vaḷattiṉit tarātoḻiya, kāṉam ulaṟi iliyilavāka - kāṭukaḷ kāyntu ilikaḷillātaṉavāka, ēṟu uṭi āyattu iṉam pala uṭaṉpaci teṟuppa - ēṟukaḷiyuṭiya āyamākiya āṉirikaḷ palavaṟṟi yum oruṅkē paci varutta, kayaṉ aṟa viṟanta kōṭiyoṭu - kuḷaṅkaḷ nīraṟavaṟṟiya kōṭiyāl, nayaṉ aṟa peruvari nivanta maruṅkil - vaḷam oḻiyap periya mali uyarnta pakkattil, koṭu varippuliyoṭu ciṉam ciṟantu porutu - vaḷinta varikaḷiyuṭiya puliyuṭaṉ ciṉam mikkup pōr ceytu, valiyōṭu uravukkaḷiṟu otuṅkiya maruṅkil - cerukkuṭaṉ valiya yāṉi naṭanta iṭaṅkaḷil, parūupparal - periya paraṟkaṟkaḷ, ciṟu pal miṉ miṉi kaṭuppa - ciṟiya palavāya miṉmiṉip pūccikaḷippōla, evvāyum niṟivaṉa imikkum nirampā nīḷiṭi - evviṭattum niṟintu oḷiviṭum cellat toliyāta nīṇṭa iṭattē;
10-15. irum ciṟi eruvi irīiya - periya ciṟakiṉiyuṭiya paruntu añciyōṭa, viri iṇar tātu uṇ tumpi mural ici kaṭuppa - virinta pūṅkottukkaḷil pūntukaḷi uṇṇum vaṇṭukaḷ olikkum iciyippōla, pariyiṉatu uyirkkum ampiṉar - virintu celvatāy olikkum ampiṉiyuṭiyavarum, veruvara uvali cūṭiya taliyar - kaṇṭārkku accam tōṉṟat taḻik kaṇṇi cūṭiya taliyiṉiyuṭiyarum ākiya āṟalip pōr, kavali ārttu uṭaṉ arum poruḷ vavvaliṉ - kavartta neṟikaḷil āravārittu oruṅku ariya poruḷkaḷip paṟittuk kōṭaliṉ, yāvatum cāttu iṭi vaḻaṅkā - vāṇikac cāttar ciṟitum neṟiyiṭiyē iyaṅkutal aṟṟatum;
15-22. cēṇ cimi atara - uyarnta maliyuccikaḷi yaṭutta neṟikaḷiṭattavāya, ciṟi yili nellit tīmcuvit tiraḷkāy - ciṟiya iliyiṉiyuṭiya nelliyiṉ iṉiya cuvi poruntiya tiraṇṭa kāykaḷ, utirvaṉa tāam attam  - utirntu parantu kiṭakkum kāṭṭil, tavirvu iṉṟu puḷḷi am piṇi uṇīiya uḷḷi - tavirtal iṉṟip puḷḷikaḷiyuṭiya aḻakiya piṇimāṉ uṇṇa niṉintu, aṟu maruppu oḻitta taliya - aṟṟu viḻum kompiṉi oḻitta taliyiṟ ṟōṉṟuvaṉavum, tōl poti maṟu maruppu - tōlāṟ potiyap peṟuvaṉavum ākiya piṉṉart tōṉṟum kompukaḷākiya, iḷaṅkōṭu atira - iḷiya kompukaḷ atirntiṭa, kūum - āṇmāṉkaḷ kūvi aḻikkum iṭamāya, cuṭar teṟa varuntiya arum curam iṟantu - ñāyiṟu teṟutalāl varutta muṟṟa ariya cura neṟiyik kaṭantu vantu, uḷḷiṉi - niṉittaṉi.
(muṭipu) peruvari nivanta maruṅkil, kaḷiṟu otuṅkiya maruṅkil, paral imikkum nīḷiṭiyil, ampiṉar, taliyar arum poruḷ vavvaliṉ, cāttiṭi vaḻaṅkāta aruñcuram iṟantu, nam kātaliyiṉ māṇkaviṉi uḷḷiṉi.
(vi-ri) porutu ciṉañ ciṟantu eṉak kiṭantavāṟē urittalumām. vali - īṇṭut tarukku; perumitamumām. `paral miṉmiṉi kaṭuppa imikkum' eṉṟatu, veyiliṉ viḷakka mikuti kūṟiyapaṭi. iruñciṟi eruvi irīiya eṉa māṟuka. pariyiṉatu- virintu celvatāy. uyirttal - olittal. kalimāṉ kūum eṉa eḻuvāy varuvitturikka. muṉ muḷitta kompu kaḻaṉṟu vēṟu kompu tōṉṟiya poḻutu, ataṉit tōl mūṭiyirukkumāta lāṉum, appoḻutu urakka olippiṉ avviḷaṅ kompu naṭuṅkum ātalāṉum, `aṟumarup poḻinta taliya tōl poti, maṟumarup piḷaṅkōṭu atirak kūum' eṉappaṭṭatu. varuntiya curam- āṇṭuḷḷa māvum puḷḷum maraṉum mutalāyiṉavellām varuntiya curam eṉka. koṭiya kāṭṭiṉi ittuṇiyum kaṭantu vantu kātaliyiṉ kaviṉi uḷḷutalāṟ payaṉ eṉṉi eṉpāṉ, `neñcē! aruñcuram iṟantu kātali kaviṉ uḷḷiṉi' eṉṟāṉ.
āṅku aci.
292. kuṟiñci
(veḷi yaccuṟīit talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu.)
		kūṟāy ceyvatu tōḻi vēṟuṇarntu
		aṉṉiyum poruḷukuttu alamarum meṉmuṟic
		ciṟukuḷaku aruntu tāymuli peṟāa
		maṟikolip paṭuttal vēṇṭi veṟipuri
	5.	ētil vēlaṉ kōti tuyalvarat
		tūṅkum āyiṉ atūum nāṇuval
		ilaṅkuvaḷi nekiḻnta cellal pulampaṭarntu
		iraviṉ mēyal marūum yāṉik
		kālval iyakkam oṟṟi naṭunāḷ
	10.	variyiṭik kaḻutiṉ 1vaṉkik kāṉavaṉ
		kaṭuvicik kavaṇiṉ eṟinta ciṟukal
		uṭuvuṟu kaṇiyiṟ pōkic cāral
		vēṅki viriyiṉar citaṟittēṉ citiyūup
		palaviṉ paḻattuḷ taṅkum
	15.	malikeḻu nāṭaṉ maṇavāk kālē.
-kapilar.
(co-ḷ) 1. tōḻi-,
7. ilaṅkuvaḷi nekiḻnta cellal - viḷaṅkum vaḷi nekiḻaṟku ētuvāya tuṉpattiṉik kaṇṭu;
1-2. aṉṉiyum - nam aṉṉiyum, vēṟu uṇarntu - atu piṟi toṉṟāṉāyateṉa uṇarntu, poruḷ ukuttu - poruḷic corintu, alamarum - maṉam cuḻalāniṟkum;
2-6. mel muṟi ciṟu kuḷaku aruntu - melliya taḷirāya ciṟiya taḻiyuṇavit tiṉṉum, tāy muli peṟāa - tāy muliyiṉip peṟā toḻiyum, maṟi kolip paṭuttal vēṇṭi - yāṭṭuk kuṭṭiyiṉip palikkākak kollutali virumpi, veṟipuri - veṟiyāṭalic ceyyum, ētil vēlaṉ - yātum iyipillāta vēlaṉ, kōti tuyalvara - māli taṉ mārpil acintiṭa, tūṅkum āyiṉ - veṟi āṭuvāṉāyiṉ, atūum nāṇuval - ataṉiyum nāṇā niṟpēṉ;
7-15. pulam paṭarntu iravil mēyal marūum yāṉi - vēṟṟuppulam ceṉṟu iravil mēytalip porunti varum yāṉiyiṉ, kālval iyakkam oṟṟi - kāl ūṉṟi naṭakkum valiya naṭiyik kūrttu aṟintu, naṭu nāḷ - naḷḷiravil, variyiṭik kaḻutiṉ vaṉkik kāṉavaṉ - maliyiṭattē paraṇmītuḷḷa valiya kiyiyuṭiya vēṭṭuvaṉ, kaṭu vicik kavaṇiṉ eṟinta ciṟu kal - mikka viciyiṉiyuṭiya kavaṇāl eṟinta ciṟukal, uṭu uṟu kaṇiyil pōki  - iṟakiṉip poruntiya ampeṉa virintu ceṉṟu, cāral vēṅki viri iṇar citaṟi - pakkamaliyiluḷḷa vēṅkimarattiṉ virinta pūṅkottukkaḷic citaṟi, tēṉ citiyūu- tēṉi aḻittu, palaviṉ paḻattuḷ taṅkum - palāp paḻattiṉuḷ taṅkiviṭum, malikeḻu nāṭaṉ maṇavākkāl - mali nāṭṭiyuṭiya nam talivaṉ nammi maṇantu koḷḷāta vaḻi;
1. ceyvatu kūṟāy - nām ceyvataṉik kūṟuvāyāka.
(muṭipu) tōḻi, vaḷi nekiḻnta cellal vēṟu uṇarntu aṉṉiyum poruḷ ukuttu alamarum; veṟipuri ētil vēlaṉ tūṅkumāyiṉ atūum nāṇuval; malikeḻu nāṭaṉ maṇavākkāl, ceyvatu kūṟāy.
(vi-ri) vēṟu uṇaral - talivaṉāl uṇṭākiya cellalip piṟi toṉṟāl āyateṉa uṇartal. poruḷ ukuttu alamarum  eṉṟatu, kaṭṭiṉum kaḻaṅkiṉum kuṟikūṟuvāriṭattum vēlaṉi ṭattum vaṟitē poruḷic corintum ataṉāṉum tīrāmiyāl maṉaṅ kalaṅkum eṉṟapaṭi. kuḷakaruntu maṟi, tāymuli peṟāa maṟiyeṉat taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka; eṉṟatu mikka iḷamiyuṭiyatōr maṟiyeṉa iraṅkik kūṟiyapaṭiyām. vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭaliṉpayaṉ maṟiyik kōṟalaṉṟip piṟitiṉṟeṉpāḷ, `maṟikolip paṭuttal vēṇṭit tūṅkum āyiṉ' eṉṟāḷ. atūum nāṇuval eṉṟa ummiyāl, aṉṉi alamarum ataṉiyum nāṇuval eṉṟā ḷākum. `piṟarpaḻiyum tam paḻiyum nāṇuvār nāṇuk - kuṟipati yeṉṉum ulaku'1 eṉpataṉāl, aṉṉiyiṉ payaṉil ceyalum vēlaṉatu tīcceyalum ākiya paḻikaṭkut talivi nāṇuvāḷ āyiṉaḷ eṉka. cellal vēṟu uṇarntu eṉak kūṭṭuka. oṟṟi - maṟiya niṉṟuṇarntu eṉṟalumām. uṭu - nāṇiṟ poruntum ampiṉ aṭiyumām. malikeḻu nāṭaṉ maṇavākkālyātu ceyvatu eṉa añcukiṉṟēṉ eṉpāḷ, ceyvatu kūṟāyeṉat tōḻikkuk kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka. `neṭuvari āciṉip paṇavi yēṟik, kaṭuvicik kavaṇiyiṟ kalki viṭutaliṉ, iṟuvari vēṅkiyiṉ oḷvī citaṟi, āciṉi meṉpaḻam aḷintavi utirāt, tēṉcey iṟāal tuḷipaṭap pōki, naṟuvaṭi māviṉ pintuṇar uḻakkik, kuliyuṭi vāḻik koḻumaṭal kiḻiyāp, palaviṉ paḻattuṭ ṭaṅkum, malikeḻu veṟpaṉ'2 eṉa, ivvāciriyar pāṭiya piṟitoru ceyyuḷiṉ pakuti, `variyiṭi..... malikeḻu nāṭaṉ' eṉṉum ippakutiyoṭu ottiruttal aṟintu makiḻvataṟkuriyatu.
(u-ṟi) kāṉavaṉ eṟinta kal vēṅki viriyiṇar citaṟit tēṉ citiyūu palaviṉ paḻattuḷ taṅkum eṉṟatu, veṟiyāṭalāl eḻum alar, talivaṉ kuṟiyiṭattu varutalik keṭuttu, namatu iṉpatti aḻittu, namatu uḷḷattilē taṅkuvatākum eṉṟapaṭi.


293. pāli
(poruḷvayiṟ piriyakkarutiya talimakaṉ kuṟippaṟintu talimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu. talimakaṉāṟ pirivuṇarttappaṭṭa tōḻi
 talimakaṭkuc colliyatūumām.)
		iliyoḻit tulaṟiya puṉtali yulavi
		valivalan taṉiya vākap palavuṭaṉ
		cilampi cūḻnta pulaṅkeṭu vippiṉ
		1tukilāy ceykip pāvirin taṉṉa
	5.	veyilavirpu nuṭaṅkum 2vevveṅ kaḷarik
		kuyiṟkaṇ ṇaṉṉa kurūukkāy muṟṟi
		maṇikkā caṉṉa māṉiṟa iruṅkaṉi
		ukāa meṉciṉi utirvaṉa kaḻiyum
		vēṉil veñcuram tamiyar tāmē
	10.	3celpa eṉpa tōḻi yāmē
		paṇpil kōvalar tāypirittu yātta
		neñcamar kuḻavi pōla nontunontu
		iṉṉā moḻitum eṉpa
		4eṉmayaṅ kiṉarkolnaṅ kāta lōrē.
kāvaṉmullip pūtaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 10 tōḻi-,
14. nam kātalōr - nam kātalar,
1-3. ili oḻittu ulaṟiya puṉ tali ulavi - ilikaḷi utirttu vaṟṟiya polivaṟṟa ucciyiṉiyuṭiya vēla marattil, vali valantaṉiya āka - vali kaṭṭiyaṉa pōla, cilampi pala uṭaṉ cūḻnta - cilampi nūl palavum oruṅkē cūḻnta, pulam keṭu vippiṉ - nilam pāḻpaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya;
4-5. tukil āy ceyki pā virin taṉṉa - āṭi āyntu neyyuṅkāl pāvāṉatu virintatu pōla, veyil avirpu nuṭaṅkum vev veṅ kaḷari - veyyil viḷaṅki aciyum mikka veppat tiyuṭiya kāṭṭiṭattē;
6-10. kuyil kaṇ aṉṉa kurūuk kāymuṟṟi - kuyiliṉ kaṇṇip pōṉṟu viḷaṅkum kāy muṟṟi, maṇikkācu aṉṉa māl niṟa iru kaṉi - aḻakiya poṟkācu pōṉṟa perumi poruntiya niṟattiṉiyuṭiya periya kaṉi, ukāa meṉciṉi utirvaṉa kaḻiyum - ukāviṉ melliya kiḷikaḷiṉiṉṟum utirntu oḻiyum, vēṉil vem curam tamiyartām celpa eṉpa - vēṉilāl veppamikka curattil taṉittu nam kātalar celluvār eṉṟu ūrār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar; maṟṟum avarē;
10-13. paṇpu il kōvalar tāy pirittu yātta - irakkam illāta kōvalar tāyip pirittuk kaṭṭiya, neñcu amar kuḻavi pōla - neñcattu ataṉi virumpiya kaṉṟupōla, nontu nontu iṉṉā moḻitum eṉpa - yām mika nontu iṉṉātavaṟṟi moḻintirukkiṉṟōm eṉṟu nammip puṟaṅ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar;
14. eṉ mayaṅkiṉar kol - avar iṅṅaṉam mayaṅkiyatu eṉṉiyō?
(muṭipu) tōḻi! (ivvūrār), nam kātalōr vēṉil veñcuram tamiyar celpa eṉpa; maṟṟum avar, yām nontu nontu iṉṉā moḻitum eṉpa; eṉ mayaṅkiṉarkol?
(vi-ri) cilampi cūḻnta vippu eṉṟataṉāl maḻiyiṉ miyum, pā virintaṉṉa veyil avirpu nuṭaṅkum eṉṟataṉāl, vemmi mikutiyum kūṟiyapaṭiyām. virintaṉṉa - virintaṉṉavāka  eṉa viṉiyeccappaṭuttuka. kācu īṇṭup poṟkācu. ukā - pālik kaṇṇuḷḷatōr maram; itu ukāy eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. kuḻavi pōla - kuḻavi palakālum varuntik kataṟuvatupōla eṉa viritturittuk koḷka. talivar koṭiya kāṭṭilē taṉittuc cella, avarip pirintu nām varuntutal iyalpākavum ataṉi aṟintu vittum ūrār nammip puṟaṉ urittal eṉṉeṉpāḷ, `eṉ mayaṅkiṉar' eṉṟāḷ.
294. mulli
(paruva varaviṉkaṇ vaṟpuṟukkun tōḻikkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		maṅkul māmaḻi viṇṇatirpu muḻaṅkit
		tuḷḷuppeyal 1kaḻinta piṉṟip pukiyuṟap
		puḷḷinuṇ tuvali pūvakam niṟiyak
		kātalarp pirinta kiyaṟu makaḷir
	5.	nīrvār kaṇṇiṟ karuviḷi malarat
		tuyttalip pūviṉ putalivar īṅki
		neyttōyt taṉṉa nīrnaṉi yantaḷir
		1iruvakir īruḷiṉ īriya tuyalvara
		avarip pimpūp payila akalvayaṟ
	10.	katirvār kāynel kaṭkiṉitu iṟiñcac
		citarciṉi tūṅkum aṟcira arināḷ
		kāyciṉa vēntaṉ pācaṟi nīṭi
		nannōy aṟiyā aṟaṉi lāḷar
		innili kaḷiya varukuvar kolleṉa
	15.	āṉā teṟitarum vāṭiyoṭu
		nōṉēṉ tōḻiyeṉ 2taṉimi yāṉē.
- kaḻārkkīra ṉeyiṟṟiyār.
(co-ḷ) tōḻi-,
1-3. maṅkul mā maḻi - mikavum iruṇṭa mēkamāṉatu, viṇ atirpu muḻaṅki - vāṉil atirntu muḻaṅki, tuḷḷu peyal kaḻinta piṉṟi - kutittalākiya peytalic ceytu kaḻinta piṉpu, puki uṟa puḷḷi nuṇtuvali - pukiyip pōlap puḷḷiyāya nuṇṇiya paṉit tuḷikaḷ, pū akam niṟiya - pūkkaḷiṉ uḷḷē niṟiyavum;
4-5. kātalarp pirinta kiyaṟu makaḷir - tam kātalarip pirintuḷḷa ceyalaṟṟa makaḷiratu; nīrvār kaṇṇil karuviḷi malara- nīr oḻukum kaṇkaḷippōlak kākkaṇam pū malaravum;
6-8. tuy tali pūviṉ putal ivar īṅki - pañcu pōṉṟa taliyiṉi yuṭiya pūviṉiyuṭiyatum putarkaḷiṟ paṭarvatumāṉa īṅkiyatu, neyt tōyttaṉṉa nīr naṉi am taḷir - neyyiṟṟōyt teṭuttāṟpōṉṟa nīrāl naṉinta aḻakiya taḷirkaḷ, iruvakir īruḷiṉ - irupiḷavākiya īral pōla, īriya tuyalvara - īramuṭiyaṉa vāy acintiṭa;
9-10. avari pim pū payila - avariyiṉ aḻakiya pūkkaḷ eṅkum ceṟiya, akal vayal katirvār kāy nel kaṭku iṉitu iṟiñca - akaṉṟa vayaliṭattē katirkaḷ nīṇṭa kāytta nel kaṇṇiṟku iṉimiyuṟat tali vaḷintiṭa;
11. citar ciṉi tūṅkum aṟcira ari nāḷ - vaṇṭukaḷ kiḷikaḷil aciyāniṟkum muṉpaṉikkālattu naḷḷiravil;
12-14. kāy ciṉa vēntaṉ pācaṟi nīṭi - ciṉa mikka aracaṉatu pācaṟikkaṇṇē neṭitu mēvi, nam nōy aṟiyā aṟaṉ ilāḷar- namatu tuṉpiṉi aṟiyāta aṟaṉ illāta talivar, innili kaḷiya varukuvar kol eṉa - namatu ittuyar niliyiṉi nikka varuvarō eṉṟu eṇṇi;
15-16. āṉātu eṟitarum vāṭiyoṭu - amiyātu vīcum vāṭikkāṟṟāl, eṉ taṉimi - eṉatu taṉimiyi, yāṉ nōṉēṉ - yām poṟēṉākiṉṟēṉ.
(muṭipu) tōḻi! maṅkul māmaḻi peyal kaḻinta piṉṟi aṟcira arināḷ vēntaṉ pācaṟi nīṭi, nannōy aṟiyā; aṟaṉilāḷar innili kaḷiya varukuvar kol eṉa, vāṭiyoṭu eṉ taṉimi nōṉēṉ.
(vi-ri) tuḷḷup peyal eṉṟatu, kārkālattu, mikka maḻi eṉṟapaṭi. pukiyuṟa eṉṟamiyāl, tuvali paṉittuvaliyāyiṟṟu. talivi talivaṉip pirinta varuttattāl nīr oḻukum kaṇṇiṉaḷā yiruttaliṉ, avvāṟē kātalarip pirinta ēṉi makaḷirum tuṉpuṟuvar eṉak koṇṭu; paṉinīr vāra malarntirukkum karuviḷi malarukku, makaḷir nīrvār kaṇṇiṉi uvami kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka. īṅki - iṇṭi. neyyiṟ ṟōytteṭuttā lotta neyppum pacumiyum uṭiya iṇṭiyiṉ taḷir iruvakirāka īral pōṉṟirukku mākaliṉ, ataṉi iruvakir īruḷiṉ īriya tuyalvara eṉṟāḷ. īruḷ - īral; `īruṭṭaṭi mūṭi'1 eṉpataṉāl ītu aṟiyappaṭum. vāṭik kāṟṟiṉāl vaṇṭukaḷ ōriṭattil niliyutaliṉṟik kiḷikaḷilē tirintukoṇṭirukkumākaliṉ, citarciṉi tūṅkum aṟcira ari nāḷ eṉṟāḷ. talivar vantuviṭuvar eṉa vaṟpuṟukkum tōḻikkut tām kuṟitta kārpparuvamēyaṉṟik kūtirum muṉpaṉiyum vantum nām eytalākum tuṉpattiṉi aṟintu, varutalillāta aṟaṉil lātavar iṉiyum varuvarō eṉa aiyuṟṟu vāṭiyāl eṉ taṉimiyip poṟukkalāṟṟēṉ ākiṉṟēṉ eṉat talimakaḷ vaṉpuṟi etiraḻintu kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka. varukuvar kol eṉa eṉpatu vārār eṉak karuti eṉṟapaṭi. tāṉ nōṉāmikkup piṟitum ētuk kūṟuvāḷ, `vāṭiyoṭu nōṉēṉ' eṉṟāḷ.
(mē-ḷ) `tiṇi mayakkuṟutalum'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, icceyyuḷik kāṭṭi, im maṇimiṭi pavaḷattu, mulliyuḷ muṉpaṉi vantatu; nilamum karuvum mayaṅkiṟṟu; eṉṟār, nac.
295. pāli
(piriviṭi vēṟupaṭṭa talimakaṭkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		nilanī raṟṟu nīḷcuṉi vaṟappak
		kuṉṟukō ṭakiyak kaṭuṅkatir teṟutaliṉ
		eṉṟūḻ 1nīṭiya vēypaṭu naṉantali
		nilavuniṟa maruppiṟ peruṅki cērtti
	5.	vēṅki veṉṟa veruvaru paṇittōḷ
		ōṅkal yāṉi yuyaṅkimatan tēmpik
		paṉmara oruciṟi piṭiyōṭu vatiyum
		kalluṭi atara kāṉam nīnti
		kaṭaṉīr uppiṉ kaṇañcāl umaṇar
	10.	uyaṅkupaka ṭuyirppa acii murampiṭittu
		akaliṭaṅ kuḻitta akalvāyk kūval
		āṟucel vampalar aciviṭa ūṟum
		puṭiyalaṅ kaḻaṟkāṟ pulli kuṉṟattu
		naṭiyaruṅ kāṉam vilaṅki nōṉcilit
	15.	toṭiyami pakaḻit tuvaṉṟunili vaṭukar
		piḻiyār makiḻar kaliciṟan tārkkum
		moḻipeyar tēem iṟantaṉar āyiṉum
		paḻitīr māṇalan tarukuvar mātō
		mārip pittikattu īritaḻ puriyum
	20.	amkaluḻ koṇṭa ceṅkaṭi maḻikkaṇ
		maṇaṅkamaḻ aimpāl maṭantiniṉ
		aṇaṅkunili peṟṟa taṭameṉ ṟōḷē.                - māmūlaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 19-21. māri pittikattu īritaḻ puriyum - mārik kālattē pūkkum picci malariṉ kuḷirnta itaḻi ōkkum, am kaluḻ koṇṭa cem kaṭi maḻik kaṇ - aḻaku oḻukum civanta kaṭi yiṉi yuṭiya kuḷirnta kaṇṇiṉiyum, maṇam kamaḻ aimpāl - maṇam nāṟum kūntaliyumuṭiya, maṭanti - maṭantiyē;
9. kaṭal nīr uppiṉ kaṇam cāl umaṇar - kaṭal nīrāl viḷinta uppuvāṇikaratu kūṭṭam;
1-8. nilam nīr aṟṟu - nilaṅkaḷ nīr illiyāki, nīḷ cuṉi vaṟappa - āḻnta cuṉikaḷ vaṟaḷavum, kuṉṟu kōṭu akiya - maliyilē marakkiḷikaḷ eriyavum, kaṭu katir teṟutaliṉ - ñāyiṟṟiṉ kaṭiya katir teṟutalāl, eṉṟūḻ nīṭiya vēy paṭu naṉan tali - vemmi mikka mūṅkilkaḷ teṟikkum akaṉṟa iṭamākiya, vēṅki veṉṟa veruvaru paṇi tōḷ - vēṅkippuliyiṉi veṉṟa accam varum parutta kiyiṉiyuṭiya, ōṅkal yāṉi uyaṅki matam tēmpi - mali pōṉṟa yāṉi varunti matam vāṭi, nilavu niṟa maruppiṉ peruṅ ki cērtti - nilāppōlum niṟattiṉi yuṭiya kompilē periya kiyi aṇittuk koṇṭu, pal mara oru ciṟi piṭiyoṭu vatiyum - pala maraṅkaḷi yuṭiya oru pakkattē taṉ piṭiyōṭu taṅkik kiṭakkum, kal uṭi atara kāṉam nīnti - kaṟkaḷ poruntiya neṟikaḷiyuṭiya kāṭṭiṉik kaṭantu;
10-12. uyaṅku pakaṭu uyirppa acii - uppuppoṟiyiṉi īrttu varuntiya erutukaḷ iḷippāṟat taṅki, murampu iṭittu akal iṭam kuḻitta akalvāyk kūval - vaṉṉilatti iṭittu virinta iṭattilē tōṇṭiṉa akaṉṟa vāyiṉiyuṭiya kiṇaṟu, āṟu cel vampalar aciviṭa ūṟum - anneṟiyē cellum putiyarkaḷ taḷarcci nīṅka ūṟum iṭamākiya;
13-17. puṭiyal kaḻal kāl pulli kuṉṟattu - olikkiṉṟa vīrak kaḻalaṇinta kāliyuṭiya pulli eṉpāṉatu vēṅkaṭamaliyic cārnta, naṭi aru kāṉam vilaṅki - cellaṟku ariya kāṭṭiṉik kaṭantu, nōṉ cili toṭi ami pakaḻi tuvaṉṟa nili vaṭukar - valiya villil toṭuttal aminta ampukaḷiṉ ceṟiviṉi yuṭiya vaṭukar, piḻi ār makiḻar - kaḷḷiṉiyuṇṭa makiḻcci yuṭiyarāy, kali ciṟantu ārkkum - cerukku mikku āravārikkum, moḻi peyar tēem iṟantaṉar āyiṉum - vēṟṟumoḻi vaḻaṅkum tēyattikkaṭantu ceṉṟuḷārāyiṉum;
21-22. niṉ aṇaṅku nilipeṟṟa taṭameṉ tōḷ - niṉatu aḻaku nilipeṟṟa periya melliya tōḷiṉ;
18. paḻitīr māṇ nalam tarukuvar - kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟanta nalattiṉi vantu tantaruḷvar.
(muṭipu) maṭanti! (nam talivar) pullikuṉṟattuk kāṉam vilaṅki, vaṭukar kaliciṟantārkkum moḻi peyar tēem iṟantaṉar āyiṉum, niṉtōḷ māṇ nalam tarukuvar.
umaṇar kāṉam nīntip pakaṭu uyirppa aciik kuḻitta kūval vampalar aciviṭa ūṟum pullikuṉṟam eṉka.
(vi-ri) eṉṟūḻ nīṭiya kāṉam eṉavum, naṉantalik kāṉam eṉavum, yāṉi piṭiyoṭu vatiyum kāṉam eṉavum taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. yāṉi maruppiṟ ki cērtti vatiyum eṉka. ōṅkal - uyarcciyumām. aci - acivu; nīr vēṭkiyāl ām taḷarcci. talivar virintu vantu niṉ tuṉpattip pōkki iṉpam taruvar eṉpāḷ `niṉ tōḷ māṇalam tarukuvar' eṉṟāḷ.
296. marutam
(vāyilvēṇṭic ceṉṟa talimakaṟku vāyiṉmaṟukkun tōḻi colliyatu.)
		kōti yiṇara kuṟuṅkāṟ kāñcip
		pōtaviḻ naṟuntā taṇinta kūntal
		arimatar maḻikkaṇ māa yōḷoṭu
		neruniyuṅ kamaḻpoḻil 1tuñci iṉṟum
	5.	perunīr viyi avaḷoṭu āṭip
		pularā mārpiṉi vantuniṉṟu emvayiṉ
		karattal kūṭumō maṟṟē parappiṟ
		paṉmīṉ koḷpavar mukanta ippi
		nārari naṟaviṉ makiḻnoṭik kūṭṭum
	10.	pēricik koṟkip porunaṉ veṉvēl
		kaṭumpakaṭṭu yāṉi neṭuntērc ceḻiyaṉ
		malipuri neṭunakark 2kūṭal āṭiya
		malitaru kampali pōla
		alarā kiṉṟatu palarvāyp paṭṭē.
- maturip pērālavāyār.
(co-ḷ) taliva!,
1-7. kōti iṇara kuṟuṅkāl kāñci - māli pōṉṟa kottukkaḷiyuṭiya kuṟiya aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya kāñcimarattiṉ, pōtu aviḻ naṟum tātu aṇinta kūntal - malar virinta naṟiya poṭiyiṉi aṇinta kūntaliyum, arimatar maḻik kaṇ - cevvari paranta matartta kuḷirnta kaṇṇiṉiyum uṭiya, mā ayōḷoṭu - māmi niṟattiṉiyuṭiya parattiyuṭaṉ, neruniyum kamaḻ poḻil tuñci - nēṟṟum maṇam kamaḻum poḻilil tuyiṉṟu, iṉṟum avaḷoṭu perunīr viyi āṭi- iṉṟum avaḷuṭaṉ viyi nīrpperukkil viḷiyāṭi, pularā mārpiṉi - īram pularāta mārpiṉiyuṭiyiyāy, em vayiṉ vantu niṉṟu - empāl vantu niṉṟu, karattal kūṭumō - emakku maṟittal iyalumō?, maṟṟu - iyalātaṉṟē, (acceyal)
7-14. parappil - kaṭal nīrp parappil, pal mīṉ koḷpavar - pala mīṉkaḷiyum piṭippōr, mukanta ippi - avaṟṟuṭaṉ cērak koṇṭa cippikaḷi, nār ari naṟaviṉ makiḻ noṭik kūṭṭum - paṉṉāṭiyāl arikkappeṟum makiḻcci tarum kaḷḷiṉ vili yākac cērkkum, pēr icik koṟkip porunaṉ - periya pukaḻiṉiyuṭiya koṟkikkut talivaṉākiya, veṉ vēl- veṉṟiyit tarum vēliyum, kaṭu pakaṭṭu yāṉi neṭuntērc ceḻiyaṉ - kaṭiya periya yāṉiyiṉiyum neṭiya tēriṉiyumuṭiya pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñ ceḻiyaṉ, malipuri neṭunakar kūṭal - maliyi yotta nīṇṭa kōyiliyuṭiya maturikkaṇ, āṭiya - veṟṟik kaḷippāl āṭiyataṉāl eḻunta, malitaru kampalipōla - niṟinta āravārampōla, palarvāyp paṭṭu alarākiṉṟatu - palar vāyālum kūṟappaṭṭu alarākiṉṟatu.
(muṭipu) taliva! māayōḷoṭu neruniyum poḻil tuñci, iṉṟum viyi avaḷoṭu āṭip pularā mārpiṉi vantu niṉṟu; emvayiṟ karattal kūṭumō? maṟṟu; atu ceḻiyaṉ kūṭal āṭiya kampali pōlap palar vāyppaṭṭu alarākiṉṟatu.
(vi-ri) pularā mārpiṉi eṉpataṉi iraṭṭuṟa moḻita lākak koṇṭu, paratti ceyta kuṟi nīṅkāta mārpiṉiyuṭiyāy eṉavum urittuk koḷka. parattiyiṟ pirintu vantu taliviyi ūṭal nīkkik kūṭa viḻintu tōḻiyi vāyil vēṇṭi niṟpavaṉ, yāṉ parattiyi aṟiyēṉ eṉṟāṉāka, nī neruni avaḷuṭaṉ tuñci avaḷ ceyta kuṟiyōṭum iṉṟu avaḷuṭaṉ nīrāṭip pularāta mārpōṭum vantu niṉṟu, niṉ puṟat toḻukkatti maṟittal yāṅṅaṉam eṉpāḷ, `pularā mārpiṉi vantu niṉṟu emvayiṟ karattal kūṭumō' eṉṟāḷ. maṟṟu, viṉimāṟṟu. ippi - muttukku āku peyarumām.
pāṇṭiyaṉ kūṭalil āṭiyapoḻutu āravāram eḻuntatu, `vāṭāp pūviṟ koṅkarōṭṭi, nāṭupala tanta pacumpūṭ pāṇṭiyaṉ, poṉmali neṭunakark kūṭa lāṭiya, iṉṉici yārppiṉum peritē'1 eṉa muṉṉarum pōntami aṟiyaṟpālatu.
297. pāli
(poruḷvayiṟ pōkāniṉṟa  talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		pāṉāṭ kaṅkulum perumpuṉ māliyum
		āṉā nōyoṭu aḻipaṭark kalaṅki
		namvayiṉ niṉiyum iṭumpi kimmika
		eṉṉi yākumō neñcē namvayiṉ
	5.	iruṅkaviṉ illāp perumpul tāṭik
		2kaṭuṅkaṇ maṟavar pakaḻi māytteṉa
		3maruṅkul nuṇukiya pēemutir naṭukal
		peyarpayam paṭarat 4tōṉṟu kuyileḻuttu
		iyipuṭaṉ nōkkal cellā tacivuṭaṉ
	10.	āṟucel vampalar viṭṭaṉar kaḻiyuñ
		1cūrmuta lirunta ōmiyam puṟaviṉ
		īrmuḷ vēlip pulavunāṟu muṉṟil
		eḻuti yaṉṉa koṭipaṭu verukiṉ
		pūḷi yaṉṉa poṅkumayirp piḷḷi
	15.	maticūḻ mīṉil tāyvaḻip paṭūuñ
		ciṟukuṭi maṟavar cēkkōḷ taṇṇumikku
		eruvic cēval iruñciṟi peyarkkum
		veruvaru kāṉam nammoṭu
		varuval eṉṟōḷ makiḻmaṭa nōkkē.
- maturi maruta ṉiḷanākaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 4. neñcē-,
5-11. irum kaviṉ illāp perum pul tāṭi - mikka aḻaku illāta periya polivaṟṟa tāṭiyiṉiyum, kaṭuṅkaṇ maṟavar pakaḻi māytteṉa - vaṉkaṇmiyiyum uṭiya maṟavaratu ampu tēytta
tāka, maruṅkul nuṇukiya pēem mutir naṭukal - pakkam melinta accam mikka naṭu kallil, peyar payam paṭarat tōṉṟu kuyil eḻuttu - peyarum pīṭum viriyat tōṉṟumāṟu poṟitta eḻuttuk
kaḷi, iyipu uṭaṉ nōkkal cellātu - oruṅku cērttu nōkkutal māṭṭārāy. acivuṭaṉ - taḷarcciyuṭaṉ, āṟu cel vampalar viṭṭaṉar kaḻiyum - vaḻic cellum putiyar ataṉi viṭuttu nīṅkum, cūr mutal irunta ōmip puṟaviṉ - teyvam aṭiyilirukkappeṟṟa ōmi maraṅkaḷiyuṭiya kāṭṭiluḷḷa;
12-16. īrmuḷ vēlip pulavu nāṟu muṉṟil - īrkiṉṟa muḷvēli yiṉiyuṭiya pulāl nāṟum muṟṟattē, eḻuti yaṉṉa koṭi paṭu verukiṉ - ōviyattu eḻutiṉāṟ pōṉṟa melintu nīṇṭa pūṉiyiṉ, pūḷi aṉṉa poṅku mayirp piḷḷi - pūḷip pūviṉi yottu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa mayiriṉiyuṭiya kuṭṭikaḷ, maticūḻ mīṉil tāy vaḻip paṭūum - tiṅkaḷic cūḻntuḷḷa viṇmīṉ pōlat tam tāyiṉ piṉ cūḻntirukkum, ciṟu kuṭi maṟavar - cīṟūriluḷḷa maṟavarkaḷatu, cē kōḷ taṇṇumikku - ēṟu kōṭ paṟi yolikku, eruvic cēval iruciṟi peyarkkum - āṇ paruntu taṉatu periya ciṟakiṉi eḻuppip paṟantēkum, veruvaru kāṉam - accam varum kāṭṭiṟku, nammoṭu varuval eṉṟōḷ - nammuṭaṉ varuvēṉ eṉṟu kūṟiya nam taliviyiṉ, makiḻmaṭa nōkku - makiḻcciyit tarum maṭappam vāynta nōkkam;
1-4. pāl nāḷ kaṅkulum perum pul māliyum - pāti nāḷākiya iravilum mikka polivaṟṟa mālikkālattum, āṉā nōyoṭu aḻi paṭark kalaṅki - amiyāta varuttattuṭaṉ mikka tuyarattāṟ kalaṅka, namvayiṉ niṉiyum iṭumpi - nammi niṉitalālākum tuṉpam, ki mika - aḷavu kaṭattaliṉ, namvayiṉ eṉṉi ākumō - nampāl eṉṉa tīṅku nikaḻumō?
(muṭipu) neñcē! ciṟukuṭi maṟavar cēkkōḷ taṇṇumikku eruvic cēval iruñciṟi peyarkkum veruvaru kāṉam, nammoṭu varuval eṉṟōḷ maṭanōkku, kaṅkulum māliyum kalaṅki, iṭumpi kimmika, namvayiṉ eṉṉiyākumō?
(vi-ri) kalaṅki - kalaṅka. namvayiṉ niṉiyum - nammi niṉiyum; vēṟṟumi mayakkam. nōkkuk kalaṅka, iṭumpi kimmika, namvayiṉ eṉṉi ākumō? eṉat talivaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ eṉka. eṉṉi yākumō - yātu tuṉpam nikaḻumō eṉṟapaṭi. māyttal - tīṭṭutal, `māytta pōla maḻukunuṉi tōṟṟi'1 `koṭuvil leyiṉar pakaḻi māykkum'2 eṉpavaṟṟil māyttal ipporuṭṭātal kāṇka. maṟavar tam ampiṉit tīṭṭutalāl, puṟam tēyntu eḻuttukkaḷ iṭiyiṭi maṟintiruttaliṉ, vampalar avaṟṟi iyittu nōkkalāṟṟātu viṭuttu akalvār eṉka. peyar payam - peyarum pīṭum eṉka. kuyil eḻuttu - kuyiṟṟiya eḻuttu. puṟaviṉ muṉṟilil verukiṉ piḷḷi tāyvaḻip paṭum ciṟukuṭi eṉka. veṇṇiṟamuṭiya verukiṟku matiyum ataṉic cūḻum piḷḷi kaṭku matiyic cūḻum mīṉkaḷum uvamamāyiṉa. taṇṇumi oliyik kēṭṭa eruvic cēval, āṇṭu uṇavu uḷatātal karutic cellum eṉpatu ciṟi peyarkkum eṉpataṉāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. añcattakka kāṭṭilum talivi uṭaṉpōkkut tuṇintu kūṟiya kāli avaḷatu nōkkam makiḻcciyāl malarntiruntatu, talivaṉatu neñcil nilipeṟṟiruntamiyiṉ ataṉi vitantu makiḻmaṭa nōkku eṉṟāṉ.
298. kuṟiñci
(iravukkuṟikkaṇ talimakaṟkut talimakaḷ colliyatu.)
		payaṅkeḻu tiruviṉ palkatir ñāyiṟu
		vayaṅkutoḻil, tarīiyar valaṉērpu viḷaṅki
		malkukaṭal tōṉṟi yāṅku malkupaṭa
		maṇimaruṇ māli malarnta vēṅki
	5.	oṇtaḷir avirvarum olikeḻu peruñciṉit
		taṇtuḷi acivaḷi tivarum nāṭa
		koṉṟuciṉan taṇiyātu veṉṟumuraṇ cāmpātu
		irumpiṭit toḻutiyiṉ iṉantali mayaṅkātu
		perumpeyaṟ kaṭāañ cerukki vaḷamali
	10.	iruṅkaḷiṟu iyalvarum peruṅkāṭṭu iyaviṉ
		āriruḷ tumiya 1veḷvēl ēntit
		tāḻpūṅ kōti ūtuvaṇṭu irīi
		meṉpiṇi yaviḻnta arināḷ iravivaṇ
		nīvan tataṉiṉum iṉitā kiṉṟē
	15.	tūvaṟ kaḷḷiṉ tuṉitēr enti
		kaṭiyuṭi viyaṉakar ōmpiṉaḷ uṟiyum
		yāyaṟi vuṟutal añcip pāṉāṭ
		2karaval neñcamoṭu kāmañ ceppēṉ
		yāṉniṉ koṭumi kūṟa niṉipāṅku
	20.	iṉiyal vāḻi tōḻinat tuṟantavar
		nīṭala rāki varuvar valleṉak
		kaṅkul uyavuttuṇi yākiya
		tuñcā tuṟivi ivaḷuvan tatuvē.
- maturip paṇṭavāṇikaṉ iḷantēvaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-6. payam keḻu tiruviṉ pal katir ñāyiṟu - ulakiṟkup payaṉmikka tiruviṉit tarum pala katirkaḷiyuṭiya ñāyiṟu, vayaṅku toḻil tarīiyar - viḷaṅkum toḻilkaḷi avvulakiṟkut tarumāṟu, valaṉ ērpu - valamāka eḻuntu, viḷaṅki - viḷakkamuṟṟu, malku kaṭal tōṉṟiyāṅku - periya kaṭalil tōṉṟiṉāṟ pōla, maṇi maruḷ māli - nīla maṇiyiṉi yotta mālik kālattē, malku paṭa malarnta vēṅki - niṟital uṇṭāka malarntuḷa vēṅki marattiṉ, oḷ taḷir avir varum olikeḻu peruñciṉi - oḷi poruntiya taḷirkaḷ viḷaṅkum taḻittal poruntiya periya kiḷiyiluḷḷa, taṇ tuḷi acivaḷi tivarum nāṭa - taṇṇiya tuḷikaḷi acintuvarum kāṟṟu melleṉat taṭavi utirkkum nāṭaṉē!
7-10. koṉṟu ciṉam taṇiyātu - taṉ pakiyiṉik koṉṟu taṉ ciṉattiṉip pōkkikkoḷḷāmalum, veṉṟu muraṇ cāmpātu - aṉṟi ataṉi veṉṟōṭṭi māṟupāṭṭiṉi melivikkāmalum, irumpiṭit toḻutiyiṉ iṉam tali
mayaṅkātu - periya peṇ yāṉikaḷiṉ kūṭṭamākiya iṉattuṭaṉ kalavā malum, perum peyal kaṭāam cerukki - perum maḻipōlum matattāl cerukkuṟṟu, vaḷa mali iruṅkaḷiṟu - vaḷamikka maliyiṭattatāya periya kaḷiṟu, iyalvarum peruṅ kāṭṭu iyaviṉ - iyaṅkum periya kāṭṭu neṟiyil;
11-14. ār iruḷ tumiya veḷvēl ēnti - ariya iruḷ nīṅka veḷḷiya vēlik kiyiṟ koṇṭu, mel piṇi aviḻnta - melliya arumpu malarnta, tāḻ pū kōti - tāḻnta pūmālik
kaṇ, ūtuvaṇṭu irīi - ūtum vaṇṭiṉi ōṭṭi, ari nāḷ iravu ivaṇ nī vanta ataṉiṉum - naṭu nāḷāya iravil iṅku nī vanta varukiyiṉum;
14-23. tūval kaḷḷiṉ tuṉi tēr enti - maḻi pōṉṟa kaḷḷiyum viriyum tēriṉiyumuṭiya em tantiyiṉ, kaṭi uṭi viyaṉ nakar ōmpiṉaḷ uṟiyum - kāvaliyuṭiya periya maṉiyiṉ kaṇ emmip pātukāttu akalātirukkum, yāy aṟivuṟutal añci - em tāy aṟintiṭali añci, pāl nāḷ - naṭu iravil, karaval neñcamoṭu kāmam ceppēṉ - maṟittal poruntiya maṉattiṉoṭu eṉatu kāmattiṉic ceppātu, yāṉ -, niṉ koṭumi kūṟa - nī vantili eṉṉum koṭumiyiṉi melleṉak kūṟa, āṅku niṉipu - aṅku eṉ karuttiṉi uṇarntu, tōḻi-, vāḻi-, iṉiyal - varuntātē, nam tuṟantavar - nammit tuṟanta nam talivar, nīṭalar āki val varuvar eṉa - tāḻkkātavarāki virintu varuvar eṉak kūṟi, kaṅkul uyavut tuṇiyākiya - iravil ucāttuṇiyātaṟku, tuñcātu uṟivi - tuyilātu eṉṉuṭaṉ kūṭiyirunta eṉ tōḻiyāya, ivaḷ uvantatu - ivaḷ makiḻntatu, iṉitu ākiṉṟu - eṉakku iṉiyatāka
vuḷḷatu.
(muṭipu) nāṭa! peruṅkāṭṭiyaviṉ vēl ēnti, arināḷ iravu ivaṇ ni vantataṉiṉum, tōḻi iṉiyal, nattuṟantavar nīṭalarāki varuvar eṉak kūṟi, kaṅkul uyavut tuṇiyākiya tuñcātu uṟivi ivaḷ uvantatu iṉitu ākiṉṟu.
(vi-ri) payam keḻu tiruvil pal katir - payaṉ mikka vāṉavil pōlum pala katirkaḷ eṉṟumām. vayaṅku toḻil - uyirkaḷ viḷaṅkutaṟku ētuvākiya toḻilkaḷ. ñāyiṟu tōṉṟiṉ aṉṟit toḻil nikaḻātākaliṉ, ñāyiṟu toḻil tarīiyar kaṭaṟṟōṉṟi yāṅku eṉappaṭṭatu. mālippoḻutiṉ nīlamaṇi pōlum iruḷiṟkum, ataṉkaṇ malaroṭum taḷiroṭum civantu viḷaṅkum vēṅkikkum, nīlaniṟamuṭiya kaṭalum ataṉkaṇ utikkum ñāyiṟum uvamamāyiṉa eṉka. veṉṟu - pakiyit turantu veṉṟa eṉka. koṉṟum veṉṟum eṉa ummi viritturittalumām. kaḷiṟu matac cerukkiṉālē ciṉam taṇiyātu muraṇ cāmpātu iṉam tali mayaṅkātu tiriyum kāṭu eṉka. iraviṟ pūkkum `iruḷ nāṟi' mutaliya pūkkaḷ, meṉ piṇi aviḻtta iravu eṉka. iravu nī vantataṉiṉum iṉitākiṉṟu eṉṟamiyāl, talivaṉ varuki taṉakku iṉitāyiṟṟu eṉṟāḷām. niṉ kātal mikutiyi aṟintu vittum aṉṉi aṟitaṟ kañcip pāṉāḷ ataṉi moḻiyātu, niṉ koṭumi kūṟiṉēṉāka, `nī varuntātē talivar varuvar' eṉṟu kūṟi, iravellām ucāttuṇiyākit tuyilātirunta tōḻi eytiya makiḻcci nī vanta makiḻcciyiṉum iṉitāyiṟṟu eṉat talivi talivaṟkuk kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka.
299. pāli
(iṭiccurattup pōkāniṉṟa talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.)
		1elliyum iravum viṉivayiṉ pirinta
		2uḷḷam muṉṉuṟu paṭiya uḷḷiya
		patimaṟantu uṟital 3vallunam āyiṉum
		atumaṟan tuṟital aritākiṉṟē
	5.	kaṭuvaḷi yeṭutta kālkaḻi tēkkili
		neṭuviḷip 4paruntiṉ veḷiyeḻun tāṅku
		vicumpukaṇ putiyap pāayp palavuṭaṉ
		5akaliṭañ cellunar aṟivukeṭat tāayk
		kavali karakkuṅ 6kāṭakal attañ 
	10.	ceyporuṇ maruṅkiṉ celavutaṉak kuritteṉa
		vikunili matiyam pōlap piyeṉap
		pulampukoḷ avalamoṭu putukkaviṉ iḻanta
		nalaṅkeḻu tirumukam iṟiñci nilaṅkiḷiyā
		nīroṭu poruta īritaḻ maḻikkaṇ
	15.	ikutaru teṇpaṉi ākattu uṟippak
		kālnili cellātu kaḻipaṭark kalaṅki
		nānaṭuk kuṟṟa navilāk kiḷaviyoṭu
		aṟalmaruḷ kūntaliṉ maṟiyiṉaḷ tiṟaṉmāṇṭu
		tiruntuka mātōnum celaveṉa veytuyirāp
	20.	paruvaral evvamoṭu aḻinta
		peruvitup puṟuvi pētuṟu niliyē.
- eyiṉantimakaṉār iḷaṅkīraṉār.
(co-ḷ) 1-3. viṉivayiṉ pirinta uḷḷam - viṉiyiṉ poruṭṭup pirintu pōnta uḷḷamē!  muṉ uṟupu - paṇṭu toṭṭu, elliyum iravum aṭiya uḷḷiya - pakalum iravum muḻutum niṉikkappaṭṭa, patimaṟantu uṟital vallunam āyiṉum- nam patiyi maṟantu taṅkutali māṭṭuvēmāyiṉum;
5-9. kaṭuvaḷi eṭutta kāl kaḻi tēkku ili - kaṭiya kāṟṟiṉāṟ paṟikkappeṟṟa kāmpu oḻinta tēkkiṉ ilikaḷ, neṭuviḷip paruntiṉ veṟi eḻuntāṅku - nīṇṭa kūppīṭṭiṉic ceyyum paruntiṉ vaṭṭam eḻuntatupōṉṟu, vicumpu kaṇputiyap pāay - vicumpu iṭam maṟiyap paravi, pala uṭaṉ - palavum oruṅkē cērntu, akal iṭam cellunar aṟivukeṭat tāay - akaṉṟa pāliyiṭattē celluvōriṉ aṟivu (cuvaṭu aṟiyātu) mayaṅkumpaṭi parantu, kavali karakkum kāṭu akal attam - kavartta neṟikaḷiyellām maṟittu viṭum akaṉṟa kāṭṭu neṟiyil;
10. cey poruḷ maruṅkiṉ celavu taṉakku uritteṉa - poruḷīṭṭum viṉikkaṭ cellutali avaḷukku urittēṉāka;
11-21. vikunili matiyam pōla - iravu pularum kālattu matiyiṉippōla, pulampukoḷ avalamoṭu - taṉimiyuṟum tuṉpattoṭu, piyeṉap putukkaviṉ iḻanta nalamkeḻu tirumukam iṟiñci - putiya aḻakiṉi mella iḻanta nalam poruntiya aḻakiya mukattiṉik kaviḻttu, nilam kiḷiyā - kāl viralāl nilattik kiḷittu, nīroṭu poruta īr itaḻ maḻikkaṇ - nīruṭaṉ kūṭiya īram vāynta itaḻiṉiyuṭiya kuḷirnta kaṇ, ikutaru teṇpaṉi ākattu uṟippa - corikiṉṟa teḷinta nīr mārpiṉkaṇ tuḷittu viḻa, kālnili cellātu - kāl ōriṭattil niṟṟal āṟṟātāka, kaḻi paṭarkkalaṅki - mikka tuṉpattāṟ kalakka
muṟṟu, aṟal maruḷ kūntaliṉ maṟintaṉaḷ - karumaṇali otta virinta kūntalil maṟintu, nānaṭukkuṟṟa navilāk kiḷaviyoṭu - nāvāṉatu naṭukkamuṟṟu tiruntāta coṟkaḷāl, num celavu tiṟaṉmāṇṭu tiruntuka eṉa - numatu pōkkāṉatu kūṟupāṭu māṭciyuṟṟuc ceppamuṟuka eṉṟu kūṟi, veytu uyirā - vevvitāka uyirttu, paruvaral evvamoṭu aḻinta - tuṉpattit tarum varuttattāl aḻinta, peruvituppuṟuvi pētu uṟu nili - mikka viriviṉi yuṟuvāḷāya talivi mayakkuṟṟa niliyāya;
4. atu maṟantu uṟital aritākiṉṟu - ataṉi maṟantu taṅkutal ariyatāka vuḷḷatu.
(muṭipu) viṉivayiṟ pirinta uḷḷamē! patimaṟantuṟital vallunam āyiṉum, kāṭakal attam celavu taṉakku uritteṉa, tirumukam iṟiñca, nilam kiḷiyā, maḻikkaṇ teṇpaṉi ākattu uṟippa, kāl nili cellātu, kaḻipaṭark kalaṅki, kūntaliṉ  maṟiyiṉaḷ, navilāk kiḷaviyoṭu, num celavu tiṟaṉ māṇṭu tiruntuka eṉak kūṟi aḻinta peruvituppuṟuvi pētuṟu
nili atu maṟan tuṟital aritākiṉṟu.
(vi-ri) pirinta - pirintu eṉṉuṭaṉ pōnta eṉka. uḷḷam, viḷi. piṟanta nāḷ toṭṭup pakalum iravum payiṉṟu vatintu niṉintu vanta patiyi oru kālattu maṟantuṟital eṉpatu ariteṉpāṉ, pati maṟantuṟital vallunamāyiṉum' eṉṟāṉ. tēkkili pāayt tāayk kavali karakkum attam eṉka. kaṭuṅkāṟṟiṉāl kavarappeṟṟa tēkkilikaḷ mutaṟkaṇ vicumpiṭa
mellām maṟiyumpaṭi paṭarntu, piṉ nilamici vīḻntu paravi vaḻikaḷi maṟikkum attam eṉṟāṉ. poruḷ vayiṟ celavi urittatu kēṭṭa taliviyiṉ mukam, mutalil melleṉa oḷi kuṉṟik kāṭṭiṟṟu ātaliṉ, ataṉi viṭiyaṟkāliyil oḷi kuṟintu tōṉṟum matiyattōṭu uvamittāṉ. poruta - taṭutta eṉṟumām. itaḻ - imi. tāṉ poruḷvayiṟ pirivu urippak kēṭṭapoḻutu, taliviyiṉ mukam oḷikuṉṟik kaviḻntirukka, kālkaḷ nilattik kiḷittu nilipeṟāvāka, kaṇkaḷ nīriṉic coriya, uḷḷam perun tuṉpattāl kalaṅkutaluṟa, kūntal virintu uṭampiṉi maṟikka, iṅṅaṉamākat talivi evvam uṟṟu aḻintu  vey tuyirttu, urittaṟku nā eḻātu naṭuṅka, num celavu tiruntuka eṉak kūṟiya varuttam mikka nili, talivaṉ uḷḷattē māṟātu nilipeṟṟiruntamiyiṉ, pētuṟu niliyākiya atu maṟantuṟital aritākiṉṟu eṉat talivaṉ neñciṟku urittāṉ eṉka. pētu uṟu nili varuttatti mikuvikkum nili eṉṟumām.
300. neytal
(pakaṟkuṟi vantu nīṅkun talimakaṟkut tōḻi colliyatu.)
		nāḷvali mukanta kōḷval paratavar
		nuṇaṅkumaṇal āṅkaṇ uṇaṅkap peymmār
		paṟikoḷ koḷḷiyar maṟuka ukka
		mīṉār kurukiṉ kāṉalam peruntuṟi
	5.	elli taṇpoḻiṟ ceṉṟeṉac celīiyar
		tērpūṭṭu ayara ēey vārkōl
		ceṟitoṭi tiruttip pāṟumayir nīvic
		celliṉi maṭantiniṉ tōḻiyoṭu maṉiyeṉac
		colliya aḷavi tāṉperitu kaluḻntu
	10.	tīṅkā yiṉaḷiva ḷāyiṉ tāṅkātu
		notumalar pōlap piriyiṟ katumeṉap
		piṟitoṉ ṟākalum añcuval ataṉāṟ
		cēṇiṉ varunar pōlap pēṇāy
		1iruṅkali yāṇarem ciṟukuṭit tōṉṟiṉ
	15.	valletir koṇṭu mellitiṉ viṉiit 
		tuṟiyum māṉṟiṉṟu poḻutē cuṟavum
		2ōtam malkaliṉ māṟā yiṉavē
		elliṉṟu tōṉṟal cellā tīmeṉa
		emarkuṟi kūṟat taṅki ēmuṟa
	20.	iḷiyarum puraviyum iṉpuṟa nīyum
		illuṟi nalvirun tayartal
		ollutum perumanī nalkutal peṟiṉē.
- ulōccaṉār.
(co-ḷ) 22. peruma - perumāṉē!
1-4. nāḷ vali mukanta kōḷ val paratavar - nāṭ kāliyil valiyiṉāl mīṉkaḷi mukantukoṇṭa mīṉ piṭittalil valla paratavarkaḷ, paṟikoḷ koḷḷiyar - piṉ paṟiyāl koṇṭa mikka mīṉiṉiyuṭiyarākiya avaṟṟi, nuṇaṅku maṇal āṅkaṇ - nuṇṇiya maṇaliṭattē, uṇaṅkap peymmār - pularumpaṭi peyvārāy, maṟuka ukka - varuntumāṟu corinta, mīṉ ār kurukiṉ - mīṉkaḷit tiṉṉum paṟavikaḷiyuṭiya, kāṉal am perun
tuṟi - cōliyiyuṭiya aḻakiya periya kaṭaṟṟuṟiyiṉ kaṇ;
5-9. taṇ poḻil elli ceṉṟeṉa - kuḷirnta cōliyiṟ pakaṟpoḻutu ceṉṟatāka, celīiyar - niṉ ūrkkuc cella, tēr pūṭṭu ayara ēey - tērip pūṭṭumāṟu ēvi, vār kōl ceṟi toṭi tirutti - ivaḷatu neṭiya tiraṇṭa neruṅkiya vaḷiyiṉit tirunta aṇivittu, pāṟu mayir nīvi - citaṟiya kūntalit taṭavi, maṭantiniṉ tōḻiyoṭu iṉi maṉi cel eṉa - maṭantiyē niṉ maṉikku niṉ tōḻiyuṭaṉ ippōtu celvāyāka eṉṟu, colliya aḷavi - coṉṉa aḷavilē;
9-12. ivaḷ peritu kaluḻntu tīṅkāyiṉaḷ āyiṉ - ivaḷ mikavum aḻutu varuntukiṉṟāḷātaliṉ, tāṅkātu notumalar pōlap piriyiṉ - ataṉi eṇṇātu ētilar pōlap pirintu celliṉ, katumeṉap piṟitoṉṟum ākalum añcuval ataṉāl- virintu ivaḷ vēṟu tīṅku aṭiyiṉum aṭivaḷeṉa añcukiṉṟēṉ, ataṉāl;
13-22. nī nalkutal peṟiṉē - yāṉ kūṟuvataṉi nī kēṭṭaruḷutal ceyyiṉ, cēṇiṉ varunar pōla pēṇāy - nīyum niṉ iḷiyarum cēymiyiṉiṉṟum varuvār pōlak karutāyāy, iru kali yāṇar em ciṟukuṭit tōṉṟiṉ - mikka āravāramuṭiya aḻakiya ematu cīṟūriṉ kaṇ variṉ, emar - eṅkaḷ maliyiṉar. val etir koṇṭu - viriya etirkoṇṭaḻittu, mellitiṉ viṉii - melleṉa viṉāvi, poḻutu māṉṟiṉṟu - poḻutu mayaṅkiviṭṭatu, tuṟiyum ōtam malkaliṉ cuṟavum māṟu āyiṉavē - nīrttuṟiyum aliperuki ēṟaliṉ cuṟākkaḷum pakiyāyiṉa, elliṉṟu - iruṇṭuviṭṭatu, tōṉṟal - taliva, cellā tīm eṉa - cellaṟka eṉa, kuṟi kūṟa - kuṟiyirantu vēṇṭa, ēm uṟat taṅki - aṅku iṉpamuṟat taṅkutal ceytu, iḷiyarum puraviyum nīyum iṉpu uṟa - niṉ ēvalarum kutiriyum nīyum iṉpam eyta, il uṟi nal viruntu ayartal ollutum - em illil taṅkum nalla viruntāka ēṟṟup pēṇutalip poruntuvōm.
(muṭipu) peruma! nī elli ceṉṟeṉac celīiyar tēr pūṭṭayara ēey, maṭanti! niṉ tōḻiyoṭu iṉi maṉi cel eṉac colliya aḷavi ivaḷ peritu tīṅkāyiṉaḷ āyiṉ, nī piriyiṉ piṟitoṉṟākalum añcuval; ataṉāl nī nalkutal peṟiṉ, em ciṟukuṭit tōṉṟiṉ, emar kuṟi kūṟat taṅki, ēmuṟa, nalviruntayartal ollutum.
(vi-ri) paṟi - mīṉ paṭukkum ōr karuvi. elli taṇ poḻiṟ ceṉṟeṉa eṉpatu pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ kūṭṭam muṭintami uṇarttiya vāṟām. celīiyar - ūrkkaṇ cellutaṟku. ēey - iḷiyari ēvi eṉka. iḷiyari ēvi, toṭi tirutti, mayir nīvic colliya aḷavi eṉṟiyiyum. kūṭṭattāl toṭi piṟaḻntum mayir kulintum iruntavaṟṟit tiruttiyum nīviyum cemmi ceytāṉ eṉka. tāṅkātu - uḷḷattiṟ koḷḷātu. piṟi toṉṟākal - perumpiṟi taṭital; uyirtuṟattal eṉṟataṉi veḷippaṭak kūṟutaṟkum āṟṟātu piṟitoṉṟākalum eṉṟāḷ; piṟitoṉṟā kalum kūṭumō eṉa añcuval eṉṟāḷ eṉka. cēṇiṉ varunar
pōlap pēṇātu eṉṟatu, paḻiyar pōlak karuti eṉṟapaṭiyām. aṉṟic cēymikkaṇiṉṟum putiyarāy varuvār pōla emmuṭaṉuḷḷa toṭarpu pulappaṭātu ciṟukuṭit tōṉṟiṉ eṉṟurittalum ām. poḻutu māṉṟiṉṟu tuṟiyum ōtam malkaliṉ cuṟavum māṟāyiṉa eṉak koṇṭu kūṭṭuka. kuṟi kūṟutal - kuṟiyirattal. ēmuṟat taṅki nī nalkutal peṟiṉ eṉa iyitturittalumām.
(mē-ḷ) `iruvakik kuṟipiḻip pākiya viṭattum'1 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu icceyyuḷi kāṭṭi, itaṉuḷ tāṉ peritu kaluḻntu tīṅkāyiṉaḷ eṉavē akkuṟipput talivaṉ pōkāmaṟ ṟaṭuppak `kūṟiya teṉṟu uṇarntu tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ eṉṟum, `nāṟṟamum tōṟṟamum'2 eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, vēḷāṇ peruneṟi vēṇṭiya viṭattum eṉṟa pakutiyil, icceyyuḷum atu eṉṟum kūṟiṉar, nac.
